`src/common/heap.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "common/heap.hpp"

#include <algorithm>

namespace fc6 {

std::vector<Region> writableRegions(DWORD pid) {
    std::vector<Region> regions;
    HANDLE h = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, FALSE, pid);
    if (!h) return regions;

    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
    for (std::uintptr_t a = 0;
         VirtualQueryEx(h, reinterpret_cast<LPCVOID>(a), &mbi, sizeof(mbi)) == sizeof(mbi);
         a = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(mbi.BaseAddress) + mbi.RegionSize) {
        if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && mbi.Type == MEM_PRIVATE && mbi.Protect == PAGE_READWRITE) {
            regions.push_back({reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(mbi.BaseAddress), mbi.RegionSize});
        }
    }
    CloseHandle(h);
    return regions;
}

std::vector<Region> moduleRegions(DWORD pid, const Module& module) {
    std::vector<Region> regions;
    HANDLE h = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, FALSE, pid);
    if (!h) return regions;

    constexpr DWORD kReadable = PAGE_READONLY | PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ |
                                PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY;
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
    for (std::uintptr_t a = module.base;
         a < module.base + module.size &&
         VirtualQueryEx(h, reinterpret_cast<LPCVOID>(a), &mbi, sizeof(mbi)) == sizeof(mbi);
         a = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(mbi.BaseAddress) + mbi.RegionSize) {
        if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && (mbi.Protect & kReadable) && !(mbi.Protect & PAGE_GUARD)) {
            regions.push_back({reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(mbi.BaseAddress), mbi.RegionSize});
        }
    }
    CloseHandle(h);
    return regions;
}

void forEachChunk(const Process& proc, const std::vector<Region>& regions, std::size_t overlap,
                  const std::function<void(std::uintptr_t, const std::uint8_t*, std::size_t)>& visit) {
    constexpr std::size_t kChunk = 1 << 20;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> buffer(kChunk + overlap);
    for (const Region& r : regions) {
        for (std::size_t off = 0; off < r.size; off += kChunk) {
            const std::size_t len = std::min(kChunk + overlap, r.size - off);
            if (proc.read(r.base + off, buffer.data(), len)) visit(r.base + off, buffer.data(), len);
        }
    }
}

}  // namespace fc6

```

`src/common/heap.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <vector>

namespace fc6 {

struct Region {
    std::uintptr_t base = 0;
    std::size_t size = 0;
};

// Committed, private, read-write memory: where the game's heap objects (entities, camera,
// skeletons) live. Excludes images and mapped files.
std::vector<Region> writableRegions(DWORD pid);

// Committed, readable pages of one loaded module (its .data/.rdata etc.). Static pointers to
// game singletons live here, so it is the root of every stable pointer chain.
std::vector<Region> moduleRegions(DWORD pid, const Module& module);

// Calls `visit(address, data, size)` for every readable 1 MB chunk of the given regions.
// Chunks of the same region overlap by `overlap` bytes so structures crossing a boundary are seen.
void forEachChunk(const Process& proc, const std::vector<Region>& regions, std::size_t overlap,
                  const std::function<void(std::uintptr_t, const std::uint8_t*, std::size_t)>& visit);

}  // namespace fc6

```

`src/common/math.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <cmath>
#include <numbers>

namespace fc6 {

struct Vec3 {
    float x = 0, y = 0, z = 0;

    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
    Vec3 operator*(float s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }
    float dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }
    float length() const { return std::sqrt(dot(*this)); }
};

// Row-major 3x4 affine transform, the usual layout for bone and entity world matrices.
// The real layout in Dunia must be confirmed in memory before use.
struct Mat34 {
    float m[3][4]{};

    Vec3 translation() const { return {m[0][3], m[1][3], m[2][3]}; }
    Vec3 transform(const Vec3& v) const {
        return {m[0][0] * v.x + m[0][1] * v.y + m[0][2] * v.z + m[0][3],
                m[1][0] * v.x + m[1][1] * v.y + m[1][2] * v.z + m[1][3],
                m[2][0] * v.x + m[2][1] * v.y + m[2][2] * v.z + m[2][3]};
    }
};

struct Angles {
    float yaw = 0;    // radians
    float pitch = 0;  // radians
};

// Yaw/pitch to look from `from` to `to`, assuming a Z-up world (Dunia is Z-up; to confirm).
inline Angles lookAt(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to) {
    const Vec3 d = to - from;
    return {std::atan2(d.y, d.x), std::atan2(d.z, std::sqrt(d.x * d.x + d.y * d.y))};
}

// Angle in radians between the camera's forward direction and the direction to a target.
// Used to pick the enemy closest to the crosshair inside the aim FOV.
inline float angleTo(const Vec3& forward, const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to) {
    const Vec3 d = to - from;
    const float len = d.length() * forward.length();
    if (len <= 0) return std::numbers::pi_v<float>;
    return std::acos(std::fmax(-1.f, std::fmin(1.f, forward.dot(d) / len)));
}

}  // namespace fc6

```

`src/common/pattern.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "common/pattern.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>

namespace fc6 {

namespace {

int hexValue(char c) {
    if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') return c - '0';
    c = static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
    if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') return c - 'a' + 10;
    return -1;
}

bool matchesAt(const std::uint8_t* data, const Pattern& p) {
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < p.bytes.size(); ++i) {
        if (p.mask[i] && data[i] != p.bytes[i]) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

}  // namespace

std::optional<Pattern> Pattern::parse(std::string_view text) {
    Pattern p;
    std::size_t i = 0;
    while (i < text.size()) {
        if (std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(text[i]))) {
            ++i;
            continue;
        }
        if (text[i] == '?') {
            p.bytes.push_back(0);
            p.mask.push_back(false);
            i += (i + 1 < text.size() && text[i + 1] == '?') ? 2 : 1;
            continue;
        }
        if (i + 1 >= text.size()) return std::nullopt;
        int hi = hexValue(text[i]), lo = hexValue(text[i + 1]);
        if (hi < 0 || lo < 0) return std::nullopt;
        p.bytes.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>(hi * 16 + lo));
        p.mask.push_back(true);
        i += 2;
    }
    if (p.bytes.empty()) return std::nullopt;
    return p;
}

std::vector<std::uintptr_t> scan(const Process& proc, std::uintptr_t base, std::size_t size,
                                 const Pattern& pattern, std::size_t maxHits) {
    constexpr std::size_t kChunk = 1 << 20;  // 1 MB
    const std::size_t len = pattern.bytes.size();
    std::vector<std::uintptr_t> hits;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> buffer(kChunk + len);

    for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < size && hits.size() < maxHits; offset += kChunk) {
        // Overlap chunks by the pattern length so matches across a boundary aren't missed.
        const std::size_t toRead = std::min(kChunk + len - 1, size - offset);
        if (toRead < len || !proc.read(base + offset, buffer.data(), toRead)) continue;

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i + len <= toRead && hits.size() < maxHits; ++i) {
            if (i >= kChunk) break;  // the overlap is scanned by the next chunk
            if (matchesAt(buffer.data() + i, pattern)) hits.push_back(base + offset + i);
        }
    }
    return hits;
}

std::optional<std::uintptr_t> resolveRipRelative(const Process& proc, std::uintptr_t hit,
                                                 std::size_t dispOffset, std::size_t instrLength) {
    auto disp = proc.read<std::int32_t>(hit + dispOffset);
    if (!disp) return std::nullopt;
    return hit + instrLength + static_cast<std::intptr_t>(*disp);
}

}  // namespace fc6

```

`src/common/pattern.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace fc6 {

// IDA-style byte signature, e.g. "48 8B 05 ?? ?? ?? ?? 48 85 C0". `?` and `??` are wildcards.
struct Pattern {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes;
    std::vector<bool> mask;  // true = must match

    static std::optional<Pattern> parse(std::string_view text);
};

// Scans [base, base + size) of the remote process. Reads in chunks so the 500 MB engine module
// never has to be copied in one go. Unreadable pages are skipped.
std::vector<std::uintptr_t> scan(const Process& proc, std::uintptr_t base, std::size_t size,
                                 const Pattern& pattern, std::size_t maxHits = 16);

// For a hit on an instruction with a RIP-relative operand (mov rax, [rip+disp32] ...):
// returns the absolute address it points to. `dispOffset` is where the disp32 starts inside the
// instruction, `instrLength` its total length.
std::optional<std::uintptr_t> resolveRipRelative(const Process& proc, std::uintptr_t hit,
                                                 std::size_t dispOffset, std::size_t instrLength);

}  // namespace fc6

```

`src/common/process.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <tlhelp32.h>

#include <cwctype>

namespace fc6 {

namespace {

bool iequals(const std::wstring& a, const std::wstring& b) {
    if (a.size() != b.size()) return false;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i) {
        if (std::towlower(a[i]) != std::towlower(b[i])) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

std::optional<Module> findModule(DWORD pid, const std::wstring& name) {
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPMODULE | TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32, pid);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return std::nullopt;

    std::optional<Module> found;
    MODULEENTRY32W entry{sizeof(entry)};
    for (BOOL ok = Module32FirstW(snap, &entry); ok; ok = Module32NextW(snap, &entry)) {
        if (iequals(entry.szModule, name)) {
            found = Module{reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(entry.modBaseAddr), entry.modBaseSize,
                           entry.szExePath};
            break;
        }
    }
    CloseHandle(snap);
    return found;
}

}  // namespace

std::vector<DWORD> findProcesses(const std::wstring& exeName) {
    std::vector<DWORD> pids;
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return pids;

    PROCESSENTRY32W entry{sizeof(entry)};
    for (BOOL ok = Process32FirstW(snap, &entry); ok; ok = Process32NextW(snap, &entry)) {
        if (iequals(entry.szExeFile, exeName)) pids.push_back(entry.th32ProcessID);
    }
    CloseHandle(snap);
    return pids;
}

Process::~Process() {
    if (handle_) CloseHandle(handle_);
}

bool Process::open(DWORD pid) {
    if (handle_) CloseHandle(handle_);
    handle_ = OpenProcess(PROCESS_VM_READ | PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, pid);
    pid_ = handle_ ? pid : 0;
    return handle_ != nullptr;
}

bool Process::attach(const std::wstring& exeName) {
    for (DWORD pid : findProcesses(exeName)) {
        if (open(pid)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

bool Process::attachByModule(const std::wstring& moduleName) {
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return false;

    bool attached = false;
    PROCESSENTRY32W entry{sizeof(entry)};
    for (BOOL ok = Process32FirstW(snap, &entry); ok && !attached; ok = Process32NextW(snap, &entry)) {
        if (findModule(entry.th32ProcessID, moduleName)) attached = open(entry.th32ProcessID);
    }
    CloseHandle(snap);
    return attached;
}

bool Process::isRunning() const {
    DWORD code = 0;
    return handle_ && GetExitCodeProcess(handle_, &code) && code == STILL_ACTIVE;
}

std::optional<Module> Process::module(const std::wstring& name) const {
    return pid_ ? findModule(pid_, name) : std::nullopt;
}

bool Process::read(std::uintptr_t address, void* out, std::size_t size) const {
    SIZE_T got = 0;
    return handle_ &&
           ReadProcessMemory(handle_, reinterpret_cast<LPCVOID>(address), out, size, &got) &&
           got == size;
}

std::optional<std::uintptr_t> Process::resolveChain(std::uintptr_t base,
                                                    const std::vector<std::ptrdiff_t>& offsets) const {
    std::uintptr_t addr = base;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < offsets.size(); ++i) {
        auto next = read<std::uintptr_t>(addr + offsets[i]);
        if (!next || *next == 0) return std::nullopt;
        addr = *next;
    }
    return addr;
}

}  // namespace fc6

```

`src/common/process.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace fc6 {

struct Module {
    std::uintptr_t base = 0;
    std::size_t size = 0;
    std::wstring path;
};

// Read-only handle on another process. Everything here uses ReadProcessMemory only:
// the external probe never writes to the game.
class Process {
public:
    Process() = default;
    ~Process();
    Process(const Process&) = delete;
    Process& operator=(const Process&) = delete;

    // Finds the first process with this exe name (case-insensitive) and opens it for reading.
    bool attach(const std::wstring& exeName);
    // Finds the process that has `moduleName` loaded. Useful because the engine lives in a DLL.
    bool attachByModule(const std::wstring& moduleName);

    DWORD pid() const { return pid_; }
    bool isRunning() const;

    std::optional<Module> module(const std::wstring& name) const;

    bool read(std::uintptr_t address, void* out, std::size_t size) const;

    template <typename T>
    std::optional<T> read(std::uintptr_t address) const {
        T value{};
        if (!read(address, &value, sizeof(T))) return std::nullopt;
        return value;
    }

    // Follows a pointer chain: [[base + o0] + o1] ... + oN. Returns nullopt on any bad read.
    std::optional<std::uintptr_t> resolveChain(std::uintptr_t base,
                                               const std::vector<std::ptrdiff_t>& offsets) const;

private:
    bool open(DWORD pid);

    DWORD pid_ = 0;
    HANDLE handle_ = nullptr;
};

std::vector<DWORD> findProcesses(const std::wstring& exeName);

}  // namespace fc6

```

`src/injector/main.cpp`:

```cpp
// fc6inject: loads fc6internal.dll into the running Far Cry 6 (offline single-player only).
//
//   fc6inject              inject build/Release/fc6internal.dll (next to this exe)
//   fc6inject <dll path>   inject another DLL
//
// Unloading is done from a client by setting Shared::quit = 1 (see tools/rays.py --unload).

#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdio>
#include <string>

int wmain(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    std::wstring dll;
    if (argc >= 2) {
        dll = argv[1];
    } else {
        wchar_t self[MAX_PATH];
        GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, self, MAX_PATH);
        dll = self;
        dll = dll.substr(0, dll.find_last_of(L"\\/") + 1) + L"fc6internal.dll";
    }
    wchar_t full[MAX_PATH];
    if (!GetFullPathNameW(dll.c_str(), MAX_PATH, full, nullptr) || GetFileAttributesW(full) == INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) {
        std::wprintf(L"DLL not found: %ls\n", dll.c_str());
        return 1;
    }

    fc6::Process probe;
    if (!probe.attachByModule(L"FC_m64d3d12.dll")) {
        std::printf("Far Cry 6 is not running.\n");
        return 1;
    }
    const DWORD pid = probe.pid();
    if (probe.module(L"fc6internal.dll")) {
        std::printf("fc6internal.dll is already loaded in pid %lu.\n", pid);
        return 0;
    }

    HANDLE proc = OpenProcess(PROCESS_CREATE_THREAD | PROCESS_VM_OPERATION | PROCESS_VM_WRITE |
                                  PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION | PROCESS_VM_READ,
                              FALSE, pid);
    if (!proc) {
        std::printf("OpenProcess failed (%lu)\n", GetLastError());
        return 1;
    }
    const SIZE_T bytes = (wcslen(full) + 1) * sizeof(wchar_t);
    void* remote = VirtualAllocEx(proc, nullptr, bytes, MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE, PAGE_READWRITE);
    if (!remote || !WriteProcessMemory(proc, remote, full, bytes, nullptr)) {
        std::printf("could not write the DLL path (%lu)\n", GetLastError());
        CloseHandle(proc);
        return 1;
    }
    auto loadLibrary = reinterpret_cast<LPTHREAD_START_ROUTINE>(
        GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleW(L"kernel32.dll"), "LoadLibraryW"));
    HANDLE thread = CreateRemoteThread(proc, nullptr, 0, loadLibrary, remote, 0, nullptr);
    if (!thread) {
        std::printf("CreateRemoteThread failed (%lu)\n", GetLastError());
        VirtualFreeEx(proc, remote, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
        CloseHandle(proc);
        return 1;
    }
    WaitForSingleObject(thread, 10000);
    DWORD exitCode = 0;
    GetExitCodeThread(thread, &exitCode);
    CloseHandle(thread);
    VirtualFreeEx(proc, remote, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
    CloseHandle(proc);
    std::wprintf(exitCode ? L"injected %ls into pid %lu\n" : L"LoadLibraryW failed for %ls (pid %lu)\n", full, pid);
    return exitCode ? 0 : 1;
}

```

`src/internal/aim.cpp`:

```cpp
// Game loop thread: the aimbot (port of tools/aim.py) and the view for tools/port_check.py.
//
// Hold the aim key (right mouse by default) to pull the view onto the hostile character closest
// to the crosshair inside the cone, with SendInput (nothing is written into the game). The overlay
// thread switches it on and off (F6, menu) through state.hpp. See targeting.hpp and profiles.hpp.

#include "internal/aim.hpp"

#include "internal/config.hpp"
#include "internal/game.hpp"
#include "internal/hud.hpp"
#include "internal/profiles.hpp"
#include "internal/rays.hpp"
#include "internal/state.hpp"
#include "internal/targeting.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <numbers>
#include <vector>

namespace fc6::aim {
namespace {

Shared* g_shared = nullptr;
HANDLE g_thread = nullptr;
volatile LONG g_stop = 0;

constexpr float kRateSmooth = 0.25f;  // follow: smoothing of the aim point's angular rate
constexpr float kMaxRate = 2.f;       // rad/s
// Barrel: the fire direction (camera forward + weapon sway, game::fireDirection) moves slowly (a
// loop of ~0.1 deg over seconds while aiming in a scope). Its offset from the camera, read each
// tick, jumps by 0.07-0.15 deg from one tick to the next (the camera picture and the game's fire
// direction aren't from the same instant; tools/aim_trace.py), and the aim followed that noise:
// the view shook by ~0.03 deg in scopes. So it's averaged over kBarrelTau, and ignored when
// larger than kBarrelMax (fast turns).
constexpr float kBarrelMax = 0.6f * std::numbers::pi_v<float> / 180.f;
constexpr double kBarrelTau = 0.2;
// Locked target out of sight (behind cover, a tree): wait this long for it before another.
constexpr double kHiddenWait = 0.4;
// One correction per camera picture: the camera shows a correction at the game's next frame only
// (tools/view_lag.py: ~8 ms, one matrix update, with the game at 91 fps against 120 ticks/s).
// Correcting again from the same picture counts the error twice: the view overshoots, and
// oscillates when the frame rate drops (3-4 ticks per frame). The camera matrix changes every
// frame (head motion), even with the mouse still; past this wait it's corrected anyway.
constexpr double kFrameWait = 0.05;
// Pitch from the accumulator: the camera's pitch is the accumulator's plus an offset, followed
// over kPitchTau (scope sway, head motion; -11 deg in some stances, moving by up to 0.6 deg per
// picture). A shot makes it jump up by 1-6 deg in one picture (recoil), and the game brings the
// view back by itself within ~0.3 s. The aim fought the kick and then the game's own return: the
// view bounced 3 deg past the target (tools/aim_trace.py). After such a jump the offset's value
// from before it is kept until the camera is back (kKickBack) or for kKickHold at most.
constexpr double kPitchTau = 0.06;
constexpr float kKickJump = 1.0f * std::numbers::pi_v<float> / 180.f;
constexpr float kKickBack = 0.3f * std::numbers::pi_v<float> / 180.f;
constexpr double kKickHold = 0.4;
// No correction once this close to the aim point, as a fraction of the head's angular radius: the
// error left is noise (the follow term still applies).
constexpr float kDeadband = 0.3f;

double seconds() {
    static LARGE_INTEGER freq = [] {
        LARGE_INTEGER f;
        QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f);
        return f;
    }();
    LARGE_INTEGER now;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
    return static_cast<double>(now.QuadPart) / static_cast<double>(freq.QuadPart);
}

float radians(float deg) { return deg * std::numbers::pi_v<float> / 180.f; }

// The aim point's name on the HUD (French, like the menu).
const char* partName(const char* part) {
    if (std::strcmp(part, "head") == 0) return "tête";
    if (std::strcmp(part, "neck") == 0) return "cou";
    if (std::strcmp(part, "chest") == 0) return "torse";
    if (std::strcmp(part, "pelvis") == 0) return "bassin";
    return part;
}

const char* shortName(const char* name) {
    const char* dot = std::strrchr(name, '.');
    return dot ? dot + 1 : name;
}

bool same(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) { return a.x == b.x && a.y == b.y && a.z == b.z; }

void copy3(float (&dst)[3], const Vec3& v) {
    dst[0] = v.x;
    dst[1] = v.y;
    dst[2] = v.z;
}

bool focused() {
    DWORD pid = 0;
    GetWindowThreadProcessId(GetForegroundWindow(), &pid);
    return pid == GetCurrentProcessId();
}

bool keyDown(int vk) { return (GetAsyncKeyState(vk) & 0x8000) != 0; }

void moveMouse(int dx, int dy) {
    INPUT in{};
    in.type = INPUT_MOUSE;
    in.mi.dx = dx;
    in.mi.dy = dy;
    in.mi.dwFlags = MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE;
    SendInput(1, &in, sizeof(in));
}

void publishView(game::Skeletons& skeletons) {
    Shared& s = *g_shared;
    game::Camera cam;
    s.viewCamera = game::camera(cam) ? 1 : 0;
    if (s.viewCamera) {
        copy3(s.eye, cam.m.pos);
        copy3(s.fwd, cam.m.fwd);
        s.sx = cam.sx;
        s.sy = cam.sy;
    }
    game::Weapon w;
    game::heldWeapon(game::player(), w);
    std::memcpy(s.weapon, w.name, sizeof(s.weapon));
    s.weaponSpeed = w.speed;
    s.weaponGravity = w.gravity;
    s.weaponDrop = w.dropDistance;
    s.weaponRpm = w.rpm;

    static game::Character list[256];
    const int n = game::characters(list, 256);
    const double now = seconds();
    std::uint32_t k = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n && k < kViewMax; ++i) {
        const game::Character& c = list[i];
        ViewCharacter& v = s.view[k++];
        std::memset(&v, 0, sizeof(v));
        v.entity = c.entity;
        std::memcpy(v.name, c.name, sizeof(v.name));
        const char* reason = "";
        const game::AimPoints pts = skeletons.aimPoints(c.entity, c.point, now, &reason);
        strncpy_s(v.reason, reason, _TRUNCATE);
        game::Transform m;
        if (game::transform(c.entity + 0x30, m)) copy3(v.origin, m.pos);
        copy3(v.head, pts.p[0]);
        for (int j = 0; j < pts.count; ++j) {
            if (std::strcmp(pts.label[j], "chest") == 0) copy3(v.chest, pts.p[j]);
        }
        v.source = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(pts.source);
        v.attitude = game::attitude(c.entity);
        v.death = game::deathState(c.entity);
        v.hp = v.hpMax = -1.f;
        game::health(c.entity, v.hp, v.hpMax);
        v.hostile = game::isHostile(c.entity, c.name) ? 1 : 0;
        v.dead = game::isDead(c.entity) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    s.viewCount = k;
    MemoryBarrier();
    ++s.viewSeq;
}

// Aim trace: one row per tick while the aim key is held, appended to aimtrace.csv (next to the
// DLL) when it's released, to see how the view converges (tools/aim_trace.py).
struct TraceRow {
    double t;
    float camYaw, camPitch, accYaw, accPitch;  // camera picture, accumulator (radians)
    float dy, dp;                              // error to the aim point, barrel removed
    float barrelY, barrelP;
    float sentY, sentP;                        // correction written (0 when skipped)
    float pitchBase;                           // camera - accumulator pitch, slow part
    std::uint8_t kick;                         // a recoil kick is being left to the game
    float dist;
    std::uint32_t entity;
    std::uint8_t newPicture, wrote;
};

class Trace {
public:
    void add(const TraceRow& r) {
        if (rows_.size() < 6000) rows_.push_back(r);
    }
    // Key released: writes the session (if long enough) and starts the next.
    void flush(const char* weapon, const Settings& st) {
        if (rows_.size() >= 20) write(weapon, st);
        rows_.clear();
    }
    bool empty() const { return rows_.empty(); }
    double start() const { return rows_.empty() ? 0 : rows_.front().t; }

private:
    void write(const char* weapon, const Settings& st) {
        std::wstring path = config::path();
        path = path.substr(0, path.find_last_of(L"\\/") + 1) + L"aimtrace.csv";
        FILE* f = nullptr;
        // Kept small: started again past 4 MB.
        WIN32_FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DATA fa;
        const bool big = GetFileAttributesExW(path.c_str(), GetFileExInfoStandard, &fa) && fa.nFileSizeLow > (4u << 20);
        if (_wfopen_s(&f, path.c_str(), big ? L"w" : L"a") != 0 || !f) return;
        std::fprintf(f, "# session weapon=%s smooth=%.2f frame_sync=%d direct=%d barrel=%d follow=%d recoil=%d hz=%.0f\n",
                     weapon, st.smooth, st.frameSync ? 1 : 0, st.direct ? 1 : 0, st.barrel ? 1 : 0, st.follow ? 1 : 0,
                     st.recoil ? 1 : 0, st.hz);
        std::fprintf(f, "t_ms,cam_yaw,cam_pitch,acc_yaw,acc_pitch,dy,dp,barrel_y,barrel_p,sent_y,sent_p,dist,entity,"
                        "new_picture,wrote,pitch_base,kick\n");
        const double t0 = rows_.front().t;
        for (const TraceRow& r : rows_) {
            std::fprintf(f, "%.2f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.1f,%08x,%d,%d,%.6f,%d\n",
                         (r.t - t0) * 1000.0,
                         r.camYaw, r.camPitch, r.accYaw, r.accPitch, r.dy, r.dp, r.barrelY, r.barrelP, r.sentY, r.sentP,
                         r.dist, r.entity, r.newPicture, r.wrote, r.pitchBase, r.kick);
        }
        std::fclose(f);
    }
    std::vector<TraceRow> rows_;
};

// Mouse response check after a new target: how far the camera turned against what was sent.
struct Probe {
    bool on = false;
    double t = 0;
    float yaw = 0, pitch = 0, zoom = 1, gain = 1;
    float sent[2] = {};
};

struct Aimbot {
    Settings st;
    ZoomGain zoomGain;
    Motion motion;
    game::Skeletons skeletons;
    bool enabled = true, rays = false;
    int settingsSeen = 0;
    std::uintptr_t player = 0;
    game::Weapon weapon;
    Profile prof;
    Ballistics bal;
    double weaponCheck = 0, nextVerdicts = 0;
    float restSx = 0;
    std::uintptr_t lastEntity = 0;  // the locked target while the key is held
    double lastSeen = 0;            // when it was last picked
    bool havePrev = false;
    double prevT = 0;
    std::uintptr_t prevEntity = 0;
    float prevTy = 0, prevTp = 0, rate[2] = {}, rest[2] = {};
    Probe probe;
    float barrel[2] = {};  // smoothed fire direction - camera forward (yaw, pitch), radians
    bool haveBarrel = false;
    double barrelAt = 0;
    float pitchBase = 0;  // camera pitch - accumulator pitch, slow part (kPitchTau)
    float pitchPrev = 0;  // that offset at the previous picture
    float sentPitchPrev = 0;  // the previous pitch correction
    bool havePitchBase = false;
    double kickSince = 0, pitchAt = 0;
    bool corrected = false;  // a correction was sent since the key went down
    double correctedAt = 0;
    Vec3 correctedFwd, correctedPos;  // the camera picture it was computed from
    Trace trace;
    Vec3 tickFwd;  // the camera forward at the previous tick (trace: new picture or not)

    void reload() {
        st = loadSettings();
        zoomGain.load();
        weapon = {};  // re-read the weapon profile on the next check
    }

    void updateWeaponLine() {
        hud::weapon("%s", weaponLine(weapon, bal, st.headOnly, prof.measured()).c_str());
    }

    // The held weapon: its profile applies at once, before any shot with it.
    void checkWeapon() {
        if (!player || !game::vt(player)) player = game::player();
        game::Weapon w;
        if (!player || !game::heldWeapon(player, w)) {
            player = 0;  // respawn or reload: look the player up again next time
            return;
        }
        const bool switched = std::strcmp(w.name, weapon.name) != 0;
        // Same weapon, same ammo, same attachments: nothing to do.
        if (!switched && std::strcmp(w.ammo, weapon.ammo) == 0 && w.stats == weapon.stats &&
            w.effectiveSpeed() == weapon.effectiveSpeed() && w.effectiveGravity() == weapon.effectiveGravity() &&
            w.effectiveDrop() == weapon.effectiveDrop() && w.range == weapon.range) {
            return;
        }
        weapon = w;
        prof = loadProfile(w.name);
        bal = effective(w, prof, st);
        config::log("weapon %s, ammo %s: %.0f m/s, gravity %.2f m/s2, drop after %.0f m, range %.0f m (data %.0f m/s"
                    " x%.2f attachments x%.2f ammo, %.1f m/s2 x%.2f x%.2f)",
                    shortName(w.name), w.ammo, bal.speed, bal.gravity, bal.dropDistance, w.range, w.speed, w.speedMul,
                    w.ammoSpeed, w.gravity, w.gravityMul, w.ammoGravity);
        updateWeaponLine();
    }

    // The pitch error's change when measured from the accumulator: (camera - accumulator) less its
    // slow part, which is held from a shot's jump until the camera is back (see kKickJump). A jump
    // as large as the previous correction is that correction not shown yet, not a shot.
    float pitchDeviation(float offset, double now) {
        if (!havePitchBase) {
            havePitchBase = true;
            pitchBase = pitchPrev = offset;
            pitchAt = now;
        }
        const float jump = offset - pitchPrev;
        pitchPrev = offset;
        if (jump > kKickJump && jump > 2.f * std::fabs(sentPitchPrev)) {
            if (kickSince == 0) kickSince = now;  // a burst doesn't hold it longer than kKickHold
        } else if (kickSince > 0 && (now - kickSince > kKickHold || std::fabs(offset - pitchBase) < kKickBack)) {
            kickSince = 0;
        }
        if (kickSince == 0) {
            pitchBase += (offset - pitchBase) * static_cast<float>(1.0 - std::exp(-std::min(now - pitchAt, 0.1) / kPitchTau));
        }
        pitchAt = now;
        return offset - pitchBase;
    }

    void tick(double now);
};

void Aimbot::tick(double now) {
    const bool focus = focused();
    if (const int v = state::settingsVersion.load(); v != settingsSeen) {
        settingsSeen = v;
        reload();
    }
    enabled = state::aimOn.load();
    if (!rays && rays::ready()) rays = rays::attachThread();
    if (focus && now > weaponCheck) {
        weaponCheck = now + 0.25;
        checkWeapon();
    }
    hud::beat(enabled);
    const bool aiming = enabled && focus && keyDown(st.key);
    if (!aiming) {
        hud::target("");
        lastEntity = 0;
        corrected = false;
        havePitchBase = false;
        kickSince = 0;
        if (!trace.empty()) trace.flush(weapon.name, st);
    }
    game::Camera cam;
    if (!focus || !game::camera(cam)) return;
    if (!aiming) {
        restSx = cam.sx;  // projection scale when not zoomed: what the calibration was made with
        // The ESP shows why each hostile would be taken or left: all of them on screen are
        // checked 10 times a second (line of sight included) while the key is up.
        if (state::espOn && now >= nextVerdicts) {
            nextVerdicts = now + 0.1;
            PickParams vp;
            vp.fov = radians(60.f);
            vp.maxDist = weapon.range > 0 ? std::min(st.maxDist, weapon.range) : st.maxDist;
            vp.rays = rays;
            vp.checkAll = true;
            vp.player = player;
            Vec3 fireDir;
            vp.haveOrigin = game::fireDirection(player, vp.origin, fireDir);
            Pick unused;
            pickTarget(cam, vp, skeletons, now, unused);
        }
        return;
    }
    // Zoomed in, the game turns the camera less per mouse count: scale the counts by the zoom.
    const float zoom = restSx > 0 ? std::max(1.f, cam.sx / restSx) : 1.f;
    const float gain = st.zoomComp ? zoomGain.factor(zoom) : 1.f;
    const float kx = st.kx * gain, ky = st.ky * gain;
    PickParams params;
    params.fov = radians(st.fov);
    // Beyond its range the bullet is gone: no target there.
    params.maxDist = weapon.range > 0 ? std::min(st.maxDist, weapon.range) : st.maxDist;
    params.speed = bal.speed > 0 ? bal.speed : st.speed;
    params.gravity = bal.speed > 0 ? bal.gravity : st.gravity;
    params.dropDistance = bal.dropDistance;
    game::Spread spread;
    params.spread = !st.headOnly && game::heldSpread(player, spread) ? &spread : nullptr;
    params.rays = rays;
    params.motion = st.lead ? &motion : nullptr;
    params.keep = st.sticky ? lastEntity : 0;
    params.waitHidden = st.sticky && lastEntity && now - lastSeen < kHiddenWait;
    params.threatsFirst = st.threatsFirst;
    params.player = player;
    // Barrel: where a bullet fired now would go, against the camera forward. Its origin is where the
    // line of sight is checked from.
    Vec3 fireOrigin, fireDir;
    const bool haveFire = game::fireDirection(player, fireOrigin, fireDir);
    params.haveOrigin = haveFire;
    params.origin = fireOrigin;
    if (st.barrel && haveFire) {
        float cy, cp, fy, fp;
        viewAngles(cam.m.fwd, cy, cp);
        viewAngles(fireDir, fy, fp);
        const float off[2] = {wrap(fy - cy), fp - cp};
        if (std::hypot(off[0], off[1]) < kBarrelMax) {
            const float a = static_cast<float>(1.0 - std::exp(-std::min(now - barrelAt, 0.1) / kBarrelTau));
            for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) barrel[k] = haveBarrel ? barrel[k] + (off[k] - barrel[k]) * a : off[k];
            haveBarrel = true;
            barrelAt = now;
        }
    } else {
        haveBarrel = false;
        barrel[0] = barrel[1] = 0.f;
    }
    Pick t;
    if (!pickTarget(cam, params, skeletons, now, t)) return;  // t.hidden: waiting for the locked one
    lastSeen = now;
    // Turn the camera so that the barrel, not the camera, points at the aim point.
    t.dy = wrap(t.dy - barrel[0]);
    t.dp -= barrel[1];
    float yaw, pitch;
    viewAngles(cam.m.fwd, yaw, pitch);
    const float moving = std::hypot(t.velocity.x, t.velocity.y);
    // Spread: how many shots would hit the head, when some would miss it.
    const bool spreadNote = params.spread && t.headChance < 0.995f;
    char part[64], partHud[64];
    if (spreadNote) {
        std::snprintf(part, sizeof(part), "%s (spread %.1f deg, head %.0f%%)", t.part, params.spread->s,
                      t.headChance * 100.f);
        std::snprintf(partHud, sizeof(partHud), "%s (dispersion %.1f°, tête %.0f%%)", partName(t.part),
                      params.spread->s, t.headChance * 100.f);
    } else {
        std::snprintf(part, sizeof(part), "%s", t.part);
        std::snprintf(partHud, sizeof(partHud), "%s", partName(t.part));
    }
    // Its AI aims at the player: said first on the HUD.
    char name[96];
    std::snprintf(name, sizeof(name), "%s%s", shortName(t.name), t.threat ? " · te vise" : "");
    if (t.entity != lastEntity) {
        config::log("target %s at %.1f m%s, aiming at %s, flight %.0f ms, moving %.1f m/s", shortName(t.name), t.dist,
                    t.threat ? " (targets the player)" : "", part, t.flight * 1000.f, moving);
        lastEntity = t.entity;
        probe = {true, now, yaw, pitch, zoom, gain, {0.f, 0.f}};
        hud::target("%s  %.0f m  %s  vol %.0f ms  vitesse %.1f m/s", name, t.dist, partHud, t.flight * 1000.f,
                    moving);
    } else if (haveBarrel && now - probe.t > 0.3) {
        // While on a target: the sway being compensated, in cm at the target's distance.
        hud::target("%s  %.0f m  %s  vol %.0f ms  vitesse %.1f m/s  canon %+.0f/%+.0f cm", name, t.dist, partHud,
                    t.flight * 1000.f, moving, barrel[1] * t.dist * 100.f, -barrel[0] * t.dist * 100.f);
    } else if (probe.on && now - probe.t > 0.25) {
        const float sent = std::hypot(probe.sent[0], probe.sent[1]);
        const float tv[2] = {wrap(yaw - probe.yaw), pitch - probe.pitch};
        const float turned = std::hypot(tv[0], tv[1]);
        // Not while zooming in, and only if the camera turned the way it was sent (the player
        // wasn't moving the mouse elsewhere meanwhile).
        const float norm = turned * sent;
        const bool steady = std::fabs(zoom - probe.zoom) < 0.03f * probe.zoom && norm > 0 &&
                            (tv[0] * probe.sent[0] + tv[1] * probe.sent[1]) / norm > 0.95f;
        if (sent > radians(1.f)) {
            hud::event("souris x%.2f au zoom x%.2f, gain x%.2f (1.00 = juste%s)", turned / sent, probe.zoom,
                       probe.gain, steady && st.zoomComp ? ", apprentissage" : "");
            if (steady && st.zoomComp) zoomGain.learn(probe.zoom, turned / sent);
        }
        probe.on = false;
    }
    float ty, tp;
    anglesTo(cam.m.pos, t.aim, ty, tp);
    float dt;
    if (havePrev && prevEntity == t.entity && now - prevT > 0 && now - prevT < 0.1) {
        dt = static_cast<float>(now - prevT);
        const float change[2] = {wrap(ty - prevTy), tp - prevTp};
        for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) rate[k] += (std::clamp(change[k] / dt, -kMaxRate, kMaxRate) - rate[k]) * kRateSmooth;
    } else {
        dt = 1.f / st.hz;
        rate[0] = rate[1] = 0.f;
    }
    havePrev = true;
    prevT = now;
    prevEntity = t.entity;
    prevTy = ty;
    prevTp = tp;
    TraceRow row{};
    row.t = now;
    viewAngles(cam.m.fwd, row.camYaw, row.camPitch);
    const bool haveAcc = game::viewAngles(player, row.accYaw, row.accPitch);
    row.dy = t.dy;
    row.dp = t.dp;
    row.barrelY = barrel[0];
    row.barrelP = barrel[1];
    row.dist = t.dist;
    row.entity = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(t.entity);
    row.newPicture = same(cam.m.fwd, tickFwd) ? 0 : 1;
    tickFwd = cam.m.fwd;
    // The camera still shows the picture the last correction was made from: wait for the next.
    if (st.frameSync && corrected && now - correctedAt < kFrameWait && same(cam.m.fwd, correctedFwd) &&
        same(cam.m.pos, correctedPos)) {
        trace.add(row);
        return;
    }
    // Follow over the time since the last correction (ticks skipped above included).
    if (corrected && now - correctedAt < 0.1) dt = static_cast<float>(now - correctedAt);
    corrected = true;
    correctedAt = now;
    correctedFwd = cam.m.fwd;
    correctedPos = cam.m.pos;
    const float followY = st.follow ? rate[0] * dt : 0.f, followP = st.follow ? rate[1] * dt : 0.f;
    float ey = t.dy, ep = t.dp;
    if (st.recoil && haveAcc) ep += pitchDeviation(row.camPitch - row.accPitch, now);
    row.pitchBase = pitchBase;
    row.kick = kickSince > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    if (std::hypot(ey, ep) < kDeadband * std::atan(kHeadRadius / std::max(t.dist, 1.f))) ey = ep = 0.f;
    row.sentY = ey * st.smooth + followY;
    row.sentP = ep * st.smooth + followP;
    sentPitchPrev = row.sentP;
    row.wrote = 1;
    trace.add(row);
    // Direct: add the correction to the game's view angles (exact, no mouse model). Else the mouse.
    if (st.direct && game::addViewAngles(player, row.sentY, row.sentP)) {
        probe.on = false;  // no mouse response to measure
        return;
    }
    // Mouse counts for this tick, keeping the sub-count remainder so slow corrections still move.
    const float mx = row.sentY * kx + rest[0];
    const float my = row.sentP * ky + rest[1];
    const int ix = static_cast<int>(mx), iy = static_cast<int>(my);
    rest[0] = mx - ix;
    rest[1] = my - iy;
    if (probe.on) {
        probe.sent[0] += ix / kx;
        probe.sent[1] += iy / ky;
    }
    if (ix || iy) moveMouse(ix, iy);
}

DWORD WINAPI loop(LPVOID) {
    auto* bot = new Aimbot;  // large: keep it off the thread stack
    bot->reload();
    bot->settingsSeen = state::settingsVersion.load();
    state::aimOn = bot->st.enabled;
    hud::attach(g_shared);
    config::log("aimbot thread started (%s)", bot->st.enabled ? "on" : "off");
    HANDLE timer = CreateWaitableTimerExW(nullptr, nullptr, CREATE_WAITABLE_TIMER_HIGH_RESOLUTION, TIMER_ALL_ACCESS);
    if (!timer) timer = CreateWaitableTimerW(nullptr, TRUE, nullptr);
    double nextView = 0;
    while (!g_stop) {
        LARGE_INTEGER due;
        due.QuadPart = -static_cast<LONGLONG>(1e7 / bot->st.hz);
        if (timer && SetWaitableTimer(timer, &due, 0, nullptr, nullptr, FALSE)) {
            WaitForSingleObject(timer, 100);
        } else {
            Sleep(8);
        }
        const double now = seconds();
        if (g_shared->viewOn && now >= nextView) {
            publishView(bot->skeletons);
            nextView = now + 0.1;
        }
        bot->tick(now);
    }
    if (bot->rays) rays::detachThread();
    if (timer) CloseHandle(timer);
    delete bot;
    return 0;
}

}  // namespace

void start(Shared* shared) {
    g_shared = shared;
    g_stop = 0;
    g_thread = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, loop, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
}

void stop() {
    if (!g_thread) return;
    InterlockedExchange(&g_stop, 1);
    WaitForSingleObject(g_thread, 5000);
    CloseHandle(g_thread);
    g_thread = nullptr;
}

}  // namespace fc6::aim
```

```hpp
#pragma once

// The DLL's own game loop thread (port of tools/aim.py, in steps). For now it publishes what
// game.cpp reads into Shared (the view block) for tools/port_check.py.

#include "internal/shared.hpp"

namespace fc6::aim {

void stop();  // waits for the thread to finish
```

`src/internal/canvas.cpp`:

```cpp
// Direct3D 11 + DirectComposition + Direct2D surface, see canvas.hpp.

#include "internal/canvas.hpp"

#include <d2d1_1.h>
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <dcomp.h>
#include <dxgi1_2.h>

namespace fc6::overlay {
namespace {

template <class T>
void releaseCom(T*& p) {
    if (p) p->Release();
    p = nullptr;
}

}  // namespace

bool Canvas::init(HWND hwnd, int width, int height) {
    if (FAILED(D3D11CreateDevice(nullptr, D3D_DRIVER_TYPE_HARDWARE, nullptr, D3D11_CREATE_DEVICE_BGRA_SUPPORT, nullptr,
                                 0, D3D11_SDK_VERSION, &d3d_, nullptr, nullptr))) {
        return false;
    }
    IDXGIDevice* dxgi = nullptr;
    IDXGIFactory2* dxgiFactory = nullptr;
    bool ok = SUCCEEDED(d3d_->QueryInterface(__uuidof(IDXGIDevice), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dxgi))) &&
              SUCCEEDED(CreateDXGIFactory2(0, __uuidof(IDXGIFactory2), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dxgiFactory)));
    if (ok) {
        DXGI_SWAP_CHAIN_DESC1 desc{};
        desc.Width = static_cast<UINT>(width);
        desc.Height = static_cast<UINT>(height);
        desc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_B8G8R8A8_UNORM;
        desc.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
        desc.BufferUsage = DXGI_USAGE_RENDER_TARGET_OUTPUT;
        desc.BufferCount = 2;
        desc.SwapEffect = DXGI_SWAP_EFFECT_FLIP_SEQUENTIAL;
        desc.AlphaMode = DXGI_ALPHA_MODE_PREMULTIPLIED;
        D2D1_FACTORY_OPTIONS opts{};
        ok = SUCCEEDED(dxgiFactory->CreateSwapChainForComposition(dxgi, &desc, nullptr, &swap_)) &&
             SUCCEEDED(D2D1CreateFactory(D2D1_FACTORY_TYPE_SINGLE_THREADED, __uuidof(ID2D1Factory1), &opts,
                                         reinterpret_cast<void**>(&factory_))) &&
             SUCCEEDED(factory_->CreateDevice(dxgi, &d2d_)) &&
             SUCCEEDED(d2d_->CreateDeviceContext(D2D1_DEVICE_CONTEXT_OPTIONS_NONE, &dc_)) && bindTarget() &&
             SUCCEEDED(DCompositionCreateDevice(dxgi, __uuidof(IDCompositionDevice), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dcomp_))) &&
             SUCCEEDED(dcomp_->CreateTargetForHwnd(hwnd, TRUE, &dcompTarget_)) &&
             SUCCEEDED(dcomp_->CreateVisual(&visual_)) && SUCCEEDED(visual_->SetContent(swap_)) &&
             SUCCEEDED(dcompTarget_->SetRoot(visual_)) && SUCCEEDED(dcomp_->Commit());
    }
    releaseCom(dxgiFactory);
    releaseCom(dxgi);
    return ok;
}

bool Canvas::bindTarget() {
    IDXGISurface* surface = nullptr;
    if (FAILED(swap_->GetBuffer(0, __uuidof(IDXGISurface), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&surface)))) return false;
    const D2D1_BITMAP_PROPERTIES1 props = D2D1::BitmapProperties1(
        D2D1_BITMAP_OPTIONS_TARGET | D2D1_BITMAP_OPTIONS_CANNOT_DRAW,
        D2D1::PixelFormat(DXGI_FORMAT_B8G8R8A8_UNORM, D2D1_ALPHA_MODE_PREMULTIPLIED), 96.f, 96.f);
    const bool ok = SUCCEEDED(dc_->CreateBitmapFromDxgiSurface(surface, &props, &target_));
    surface->Release();
    if (ok) dc_->SetTarget(target_);
    return ok;
}

bool Canvas::resize(int width, int height) {
    if (!swap_) return false;
    dc_->SetTarget(nullptr);
    releaseCom(target_);
    return SUCCEEDED(swap_->ResizeBuffers(0, static_cast<UINT>(width), static_cast<UINT>(height), DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN, 0)) &&
           bindTarget();
}

ID2D1DeviceContext* Canvas::begin() {
    dc_->BeginDraw();
    dc_->Clear(D2D1::ColorF(0, 0.f));
    return dc_;
}

void Canvas::end() {
    dc_->EndDraw();
    swap_->Present(1, 0);
}

void Canvas::release() {
    if (dc_) dc_->SetTarget(nullptr);
    releaseCom(visual_);
    releaseCom(dcompTarget_);
    releaseCom(dcomp_);
    releaseCom(target_);
    releaseCom(dc_);
    releaseCom(d2d_);
    releaseCom(factory_);
    releaseCom(swap_);
    releaseCom(d3d_);
}

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/canvas.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// GPU drawing surface of the overlay window: a Direct3D 11 flip-model swap chain with
// premultiplied alpha, shown through DirectComposition, drawn with a Direct2D device context.
// Present waits for the display's refresh, which paces the overlay loop. (UpdateLayeredWindow
// copied a full-screen bitmap from the CPU each frame and kept the ESP near 20 fps.)

#include <windows.h>

struct ID3D11Device;
struct IDXGISwapChain1;
struct ID2D1Factory1;
struct ID2D1Device;
struct ID2D1DeviceContext;
struct ID2D1Bitmap1;
struct IDCompositionDevice;
struct IDCompositionTarget;
struct IDCompositionVisual;

namespace fc6::overlay {

class Canvas {
public:
    ~Canvas() { release(); }
    bool init(HWND hwnd, int width, int height);
    bool resize(int width, int height);
    ID2D1DeviceContext* begin();  // cleared to transparent
    void end();                   // draws and presents (waits for the next refresh)
    ID2D1Factory1* factory() const { return factory_; }
    void release();

private:
    bool bindTarget();
    ID3D11Device* d3d_ = nullptr;
    IDXGISwapChain1* swap_ = nullptr;
    ID2D1Factory1* factory_ = nullptr;
    ID2D1Device* d2d_ = nullptr;
    ID2D1DeviceContext* dc_ = nullptr;
    ID2D1Bitmap1* target_ = nullptr;
    IDCompositionDevice* dcomp_ = nullptr;
    IDCompositionTarget* dcompTarget_ = nullptr;
    IDCompositionVisual* visual_ = nullptr;
};

}  // namespace fc6::overlay
```

`src/internal/config.cpp`:

```cpp
// fc6hack.ini access, see config.hpp.

#include "internal/config.hpp"

#include <share.h>
#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <mutex>

namespace fc6::config {
namespace {

std::wstring widen(const char* s) {
    std::wstring out;
    for (; *s; ++s) out += static_cast<wchar_t>(static_cast<unsigned char>(*s));
    return out;
}

}  // namespace

const std::wstring& path() {
    static const std::wstring p = [] {
        HMODULE self = nullptr;
        GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                           reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(&path), &self);
        wchar_t buf[MAX_PATH] = {};
        GetModuleFileNameW(self, buf, MAX_PATH);
        std::wstring dir = buf;
        return dir.substr(0, dir.find_last_of(L"\\/") + 1) + L"fc6hack.ini";
    }();
    return p;
}

std::string getString(const char* section, const char* key, const char* fallback) {
    wchar_t buf[2048];
    GetPrivateProfileStringW(widen(section).c_str(), widen(key).c_str(), widen(fallback).c_str(), buf, 2048,
                             path().c_str());
    std::string out;
    for (const wchar_t* p = buf; *p; ++p) out += static_cast<char>(*p < 128 ? *p : '?');
    return out;
}

float getFloat(const char* section, const char* key, float fallback) {
    const std::string s = getString(section, key);
    if (s.empty()) return fallback;
    char* end = nullptr;
    const float v = std::strtof(s.c_str(), &end);
    return end != s.c_str() ? v : fallback;
}

void setString(const char* section, const char* key, const std::string& value) {
    WritePrivateProfileStringW(widen(section).c_str(), widen(key).c_str(), widen(value.c_str()).c_str(),
                               path().c_str());
}

void setFloat(const char* section, const char* key, float value, int decimals) {
    char buf[64];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%.*f", decimals, value);
    setString(section, key, buf);
}

void log(const char* format, ...) {
    static std::FILE* f = [] {
        std::wstring p = path();
        p = p.substr(0, p.size() - 3) + L"log";
        return _wfsopen(p.c_str(), L"a", _SH_DENYNO);
    }();
    if (!f) return;
    static std::mutex lock;  // the aimbot and overlay threads both log
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> hold(lock);
    SYSTEMTIME t;
    GetLocalTime(&t);
    std::fprintf(f, "%02d:%02d:%02d.%03d ", t.wHour, t.wMinute, t.wSecond, t.wMilliseconds);
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, format);
    std::vfprintf(f, format, args);
    va_end(args);
    std::fputc('\n', f);
    std::fflush(f);
}

}  // namespace fc6::config

```

`src/internal/config.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Settings and learned values of the DLL aimbot: fc6hack.ini next to fc6internal.dll.
//   [aim]     enabled, key (virtual-key code), fov, smooth, max_dist, hz, follow, lead, head_only,
//             zoom_comp, auto, barrel, direct, sticky, threats_first, speed, gravity
//   [overlay] esp, esp_dist, hud               [keys] aim_toggle (F6), esp_toggle (F7), menu (Insert)
//   [mouse]   yaw_counts_per_rad, pitch_counts_per_rad (tools/calibrate.py measures them)
//   [zoom]    learned mouse gain per zoom level ("1.00=1.597")
//   [weapon <archetype>]  measured speed, gravity, speed_x, gravity_x (only without the game's data)
// Read with GetPrivateProfileString: the file can be edited while the game runs; values are
// read when the aimbot starts or the held weapon changes.

#include <string>

namespace fc6::config {

std::string getString(const char* section, const char* key, const char* fallback = "");
float getFloat(const char* section, const char* key, float fallback);
void setString(const char* section, const char* key, const std::string& value);
void setFloat(const char* section, const char* key, float value, int decimals = 4);

// Appends a timestamped line to fc6hack.log next to the DLL (what tools/aim.py printed).
void log(const char* format, ...);

}  // namespace fc6::config

```

`src/internal/dllmain.cpp`:

```cpp
// fc6internal.dll: runs inside Far Cry 6 (offline single-player only).
//
// Ray casts through the game's own query path, by record and replay:
// 1. A hardware execute breakpoint (debug register Dr0, no code patch) on the game's per-ray
//    query function (engine + 0x47921c0) catches one real call. Its arguments are copied:
//    rcx = query object (broadphase), rdx = ray entry, r8 = collector prepared by the caller.
// 2. Ray requests from shared memory (shared.hpp) are served by calling that same function with a
//    copy of the captured entry and collector, our own from/to, and our own output buffer.
//    The captured collision filter is kept, so rays see what the game's queries see.
//
// Write watch: a client sets watchAddr/watchLen/watchMs; a hardware data breakpoint (Dr1) records
// every instruction that writes there, with registers and callers (tools/watch.py). With
// watchLen = 0, execute breakpoints (Dr1-Dr3) on execAddr record who runs that code, and with what.
//
// Trace: a client sets traceFilter/traceMs; the Dr0 breakpoint stays armed and every game ray query
// with that filter is recorded with from/to, time and caller (tools/ballistics.py).
//
// Unload: a client sets Shared::quit = 1; the worker thread frees the DLL itself.

#include "internal/aim.hpp"
#include "internal/config.hpp"
#include "internal/game.hpp"
#include "internal/gamethread.hpp"
#include "internal/overlay.hpp"
#include "internal/rays.hpp"
#include "internal/shared.hpp"

#include <windows.h>
#include <tlhelp32.h>

#include <cmath>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

namespace {

// Engine offsets (Steam build 11359732), see docs/reversing.md.
constexpr std::uintptr_t kRayQuery = 0x47921c0;  // (queryObject, entry, collector, secondary)
constexpr std::uintptr_t kHavokTlsIndex = 0x7095a98;  // u32 TLS slot of the Havok memory router

// Havok's per-thread memory router starts with an hkLifoAllocator (stack allocator):
//   +0x10 int slab size, +0x18 cur, +0x20 end, +0x28 first non-LIFO end.
// Our worker thread has none, so it gets a copy of a game thread's router with its own stack.
constexpr std::size_t kRouterCopySize = 0x200;
constexpr std::size_t kLifoStackSize = 16 << 20;

// Ray entry used by the per-ray query (only the fields we touch).
constexpr std::size_t kEntrySize = 0x50;      // from +0x00, to +0x10, flag +0x20, filter +0x24,
                                              // results* +0x30, capacity +0x38, count +0x3c,
                                              // secondary flag +0x40
constexpr std::size_t kCollectorSize = 0x90;  // vtable +0x614c018, context +0x08, ...
constexpr std::size_t kOutputSize = 0x60;     // one result: normal +0x00, fraction +0x10, object +0x40
constexpr std::size_t kOutputSlots = 4;       // spare room; the entry says capacity 1 (closest hit)

HMODULE g_self = nullptr;
std::uintptr_t g_engine = 0;
fc6::Shared* g_shared = nullptr;

volatile LONG g_captured = 0;
std::uint64_t g_queryObject = 0;
alignas(16) std::uint8_t g_entryTemplate[kEntrySize];
alignas(16) std::uint8_t g_collectorTemplate[kCollectorSize];
alignas(16) std::uint8_t g_router[kRouterCopySize];  // a game thread's router, as captured
bool g_haveRouter = false;

// Each of our threads that casts rays gets its own copy of the router with a private LIFO stack.
struct ThreadRouter {
    alignas(16) std::uint8_t copy[kRouterCopySize];
    std::uint8_t* stack = nullptr;
};
thread_local ThreadRouter t_router;

// Filter sampling: the hardware breakpoint stays armed for a while and every game query is counted.
volatile LONG g_sampling = 0;
volatile LONG g_sampleLock = 0;
struct SampleAcc {
    std::uint32_t filter, flags, count;
    double lengthSum;
};
SampleAcc g_acc[fc6::kMaxFilterSamples];
std::uint32_t g_accCount = 0, g_accTotal = 0;

void recordSample(const std::uint8_t* entry) {
    std::uint32_t filter = 0;
    std::memcpy(&filter, entry + 0x24, 4);
    const std::uint32_t flags = entry[0x20];
    float from[3], to[3];
    std::memcpy(from, entry, 12);
    std::memcpy(to, entry + 0x10, 12);
    const double dx = to[0] - from[0], dy = to[1] - from[1], dz = to[2] - from[2];
    const double len = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    while (InterlockedExchange(&g_sampleLock, 1)) YieldProcessor();
    ++g_accTotal;
    std::uint32_t i = 0;
    while (i < g_accCount && !(g_acc[i].filter == filter && g_acc[i].flags == flags)) ++i;
    if (i == g_accCount && g_accCount < fc6::kMaxFilterSamples) g_acc[g_accCount++] = {filter, flags, 0, 0.0};
    if (i < g_accCount) {
        ++g_acc[i].count;
        g_acc[i].lengthSum += len;
    }
    InterlockedExchange(&g_sampleLock, 0);
}

// Trace: every matching game query, with its time and caller, straight into g_shared->trace.
volatile LONG g_tracing = 0;
volatile LONG g_traceNext = 0;
volatile LONG g_traceTotal = 0;
volatile LONG g_traceRing = 0;  // bullet monitor: trace[] is a ring, no snapshots
std::uint32_t g_traceFilter = 0;

void walkStack(const CONTEXT& start, std::uint64_t* frames, int count, std::uint64_t (*regs)[8]);

// Copies memory that may not be mapped (separate function: __try can't sit next to C++ objects).
bool safeCopy(void* dst, const void* src, std::size_t n) {
    __try {
        std::memcpy(dst, src, n);
        return true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

void recordTrace(const CONTEXT* ctx) {
    const auto* entry = reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(ctx->Rdx);
    std::uint32_t filter = 0;
    std::memcpy(&filter, entry + 0x24, 4);
    if (g_traceFilter && filter != g_traceFilter) return;
    InterlockedIncrement(&g_traceTotal);
    const LONG i = InterlockedIncrement(&g_traceNext) - 1;
    const bool ring = g_traceRing != 0;
    if (!ring && i >= fc6::kMaxTraceRays) return;
    fc6::TraceRay& t = g_shared->trace[static_cast<std::uint32_t>(i) % fc6::kMaxTraceRays];
    std::memcpy(t.from, entry, 12);
    std::memcpy(t.to, entry + 0x10, 12);
    t.filterInfo = filter;
    t.flags = entry[0x20];
    LARGE_INTEGER now;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
    t.qpc = now.QuadPart;
    t.threadId = GetCurrentThreadId();
    // Execute breakpoint on the first instruction: [rsp] is still the return address.
    t.caller = *reinterpret_cast<const std::uint64_t*>(ctx->Rsp) - g_engine;
    if (ring) {
        // monitorHead = max(monitorHead, i + 1): threads may finish out of order.
        MemoryBarrier();
        auto* head = reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(&g_shared->monitorHead);
        for (LONG cur = *head; cur < i + 1; cur = *head) {
            if (InterlockedCompareExchange(head, i + 1, cur) == cur) break;
        }
        return;
    }
    if (i < fc6::kTraceSnaps) {
        fc6::TraceSnap& s = g_shared->snaps[i];
        s.index = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(i);
        s.entry = ctx->Rdx;
        std::memcpy(&s.results, entry + 0x30, 8);
        s.batch = ctx->Rsi;
        std::memset(s.aroundResults, 0, sizeof(s.aroundResults));
        std::memset(s.batchBytes, 0, sizeof(s.batchBytes));
        safeCopy(s.aroundResults, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(s.results - fc6::kSnapBytes / 2), fc6::kSnapBytes);
        safeCopy(s.batchBytes, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(s.batch), sizeof(s.batchBytes));
        std::memset(s.frames, 0, sizeof(s.frames));
        std::memset(s.frameRegs, 0, sizeof(s.frameRegs));
        walkStack(*ctx, s.frames, fc6::kSnapFrames, s.frameRegs);
    }
}

using RayQueryFn = void(__fastcall*)(void* queryObject, void* entry, void* collector, void* secondary);

void setMessage(const char* text) {
    if (g_shared) strncpy_s(g_shared->message, text, _TRUNCATE);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Capture with a hardware execute breakpoint on every thread.

LONG CALLBACK onException(EXCEPTION_POINTERS* info) {
    if (info->ExceptionRecord->ExceptionCode != EXCEPTION_SINGLE_STEP) return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
    const auto addr = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(info->ExceptionRecord->ExceptionAddress);
    if (addr != g_engine + kRayQuery) return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
    auto* ctx = info->ContextRecord;
    if (g_sampling || g_tracing) {
        if (g_sampling) recordSample(reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(ctx->Rdx));
        if (g_tracing) recordTrace(ctx);
        ctx->EFlags |= 0x10000;  // keep the breakpoint armed, just step over it
        return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_EXECUTION;
    }
    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&g_captured, 1, 0) == 0) {
        g_queryObject = ctx->Rcx;
        std::memcpy(g_entryTemplate, reinterpret_cast<void*>(ctx->Rdx), kEntrySize);
        std::memcpy(g_collectorTemplate, reinterpret_cast<void*>(ctx->R8), kCollectorSize);
        // We run on a game thread here: keep a copy of its Havok memory router.
        const DWORD slot = *reinterpret_cast<std::uint32_t*>(g_engine + kHavokTlsIndex);
        if (void* router = TlsGetValue(slot)) {
            std::memcpy(g_router, router, kRouterCopySize);
            g_haveRouter = true;
        }
        MemoryBarrier();
        InterlockedExchange(&g_captured, 2);
    }
    ctx->Dr0 = 0;
    ctx->Dr7 &= ~1ull;
    ctx->EFlags |= 0x10000;  // RF: don't re-trigger on resume
    return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_EXECUTION;
}

void setBreakpointOnAllThreads(bool enable) {
    const DWORD self = GetCurrentThreadId(), pid = GetCurrentProcessId();
    const std::uintptr_t target = g_engine + kRayQuery;
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    THREADENTRY32 te{sizeof(te)};
    for (BOOL ok = Thread32First(snap, &te); ok; ok = Thread32Next(snap, &te)) {
        if (te.th32OwnerProcessID != pid || te.th32ThreadID == self) continue;
        HANDLE t = OpenThread(THREAD_GET_CONTEXT | THREAD_SET_CONTEXT | THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME, FALSE, te.th32ThreadID);
        if (!t) continue;
        if (SuspendThread(t) != static_cast<DWORD>(-1)) {
            CONTEXT ctx{};
            ctx.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS;
            if (GetThreadContext(t, &ctx)) {
                if (enable) {
                    ctx.Dr0 = target;
                    ctx.Dr7 = (ctx.Dr7 & ~0xF0003ull) | 1ull;  // L0 = 1, RW0/LEN0 = 00 (execute)
                } else if (ctx.Dr0 == target) {
                    ctx.Dr0 = 0;
                    ctx.Dr7 &= ~1ull;
                }
                SetThreadContext(t, &ctx);
            }
            ResumeThread(t);
        }
        CloseHandle(t);
    }
    CloseHandle(snap);
}

bool capture(DWORD timeoutMs) {
    PVOID veh = AddVectoredExceptionHandler(1, onException);
    setBreakpointOnAllThreads(true);
    for (DWORD waited = 0; g_captured != 2 && waited < timeoutMs; waited += 20) Sleep(20);
    setBreakpointOnAllThreads(false);
    Sleep(50);  // let any in-flight exception finish before removing the handler
    RemoveVectoredExceptionHandler(veh);
    return g_captured == 2;
}

void runSampling(std::uint32_t ms) {
    g_accCount = g_accTotal = 0;
    PVOID veh = AddVectoredExceptionHandler(1, onException);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_sampling, 1);
    setBreakpointOnAllThreads(true);
    Sleep(ms > 30000 ? 30000 : ms);
    setBreakpointOnAllThreads(false);
    Sleep(50);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_sampling, 0);
    RemoveVectoredExceptionHandler(veh);
    while (InterlockedExchange(&g_sampleLock, 1)) YieldProcessor();
    g_shared->sampleDistinct = g_accCount;
    g_shared->sampleTotal = g_accTotal;
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < g_accCount; ++i) {
        g_shared->samples[i] = {g_acc[i].filter, g_acc[i].flags, g_acc[i].count,
                                g_acc[i].count ? float(g_acc[i].lengthSum / g_acc[i].count) : 0.f};
    }
    InterlockedExchange(&g_sampleLock, 0);
}

// Bullet monitor: the trace breakpoint stays armed while the client asks for it (see Shared).
struct Monitor {
    bool on = false;
    PVOID veh = nullptr;
    DWORD armedAt = 0, beatAt = 0;
    std::uint32_t beat = 0;
};

void stepMonitor(Monitor& m) {
    const DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    const bool wanted = g_shared->monitorOn && g_shared->status == fc6::kReady;
    if (wanted && !m.on) {
        LARGE_INTEGER freq;
        QueryPerformanceFrequency(&freq);
        g_shared->qpcFrequency = freq.QuadPart;
        g_traceFilter = g_shared->traceFilter;
        g_traceNext = g_traceTotal = 0;
        g_shared->monitorHead = 0;
        InterlockedExchange(&g_traceRing, 1);
        m.veh = AddVectoredExceptionHandler(1, onException);
        InterlockedExchange(&g_tracing, 1);
        setBreakpointOnAllThreads(true);
        m.on = true;
        m.armedAt = m.beatAt = now;
        m.beat = g_shared->monitorBeat;
        return;
    }
    if (!m.on) return;
    if (g_shared->monitorBeat != m.beat) {
        m.beat = g_shared->monitorBeat;
        m.beatAt = now;
    }
    if (wanted && now - m.beatAt > 3000) g_shared->monitorOn = 0;  // the client is gone
    if (g_shared->monitorOn) {
        if (now - m.armedAt > 5000) {  // threads created since the last pass
            setBreakpointOnAllThreads(true);
            m.armedAt = now;
        }
        return;
    }
    setBreakpointOnAllThreads(false);
    Sleep(50);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_tracing, 0);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_traceRing, 0);
    RemoveVectoredExceptionHandler(m.veh);
    m.on = false;
}

void runTrace(std::uint32_t ms) {
    LARGE_INTEGER freq;
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&freq);
    g_shared->qpcFrequency = freq.QuadPart;
    g_traceFilter = g_shared->traceFilter;
    g_traceNext = g_traceTotal = 0;
    PVOID veh = AddVectoredExceptionHandler(1, onException);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_tracing, 1);
    setBreakpointOnAllThreads(true);
    Sleep(ms > 30000 ? 30000 : ms);
    setBreakpointOnAllThreads(false);
    Sleep(50);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_tracing, 0);
    RemoveVectoredExceptionHandler(veh);
    g_shared->traceTotal = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(g_traceTotal);
    g_shared->traceCount = g_traceNext < fc6::kMaxTraceRays ? static_cast<std::uint32_t>(g_traceNext)
                                                            : fc6::kMaxTraceRays;
    g_shared->snapCount = g_traceNext < fc6::kTraceSnaps ? static_cast<std::uint32_t>(g_traceNext)
                                                         : fc6::kTraceSnaps;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Write watch: hardware data breakpoint (Dr1, no code patch) to find which code writes a value.

volatile LONG g_watching = 0;
volatile LONG g_watchExec = 0;  // execute watch: one entry per distinct register set
volatile LONG g_watchLock = 0;
fc6::WatchHit g_watchAcc[fc6::kMaxWatchHits];
std::uint32_t g_watchCount = 0, g_watchTotal = 0;

// Caller chain from the faulting context. Engine code mostly has unwind info (.pdata); for code
// without it (leaf or virtualized) the return address is assumed at [rsp].
// Return addresses of the callers; with regs, also the non-volatile registers as they were in
// each caller (rbx rbp rsi rdi r12 r13 r14 r15), restored by the unwinder.
void walkStack(const CONTEXT& start, std::uint64_t* frames, int count, std::uint64_t (*regs)[8]) {
    CONTEXT c = start;
    __try {
        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            DWORD64 imageBase = 0;
            PRUNTIME_FUNCTION fn = RtlLookupFunctionEntry(c.Rip, &imageBase, nullptr);
            if (fn) {
                void* handlerData = nullptr;
                DWORD64 establisher = 0;
                RtlVirtualUnwind(UNW_FLAG_NHANDLER, imageBase, c.Rip, fn, &c, &handlerData, &establisher, nullptr);
            } else {
                c.Rip = *reinterpret_cast<DWORD64*>(c.Rsp);
                c.Rsp += 8;
            }
            if (!c.Rip) break;
            frames[i] = c.Rip;
            if (regs) {
                const std::uint64_t r[8] = {c.Rbx, c.Rbp, c.Rsi, c.Rdi, c.R12, c.R13, c.R14, c.R15};
                std::memcpy(regs[i], r, sizeof(r));
            }
        }
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
    }
}

// Same call site with the same objects: rip and the non-volatile and argument registers match.
bool sameCall(const fc6::WatchHit& h, const DWORD64* regs) {
    static constexpr int kKey[] = {1, 2, 3, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 12, 13, 14, 15};  // rcx rdx rbx rbp rsi rdi r8 r9 r12-r15
    for (int k : kKey) {
        if (h.regs[k] != regs[k]) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Same path for a data watch: the instruction and its first three callers (one instruction can
// serve several callers, e.g. a getter and a setter of the same value).
bool samePath(const fc6::WatchHit& h, const std::uint64_t* frames) {
    return h.frames[0] == frames[0] && h.frames[1] == frames[1] && h.frames[2] == frames[2];
}

void recordWrite(const CONTEXT* ctx) {
    const DWORD64 regs[16] = {ctx->Rax, ctx->Rcx, ctx->Rdx, ctx->Rbx, ctx->Rsp, ctx->Rbp, ctx->Rsi, ctx->Rdi,
                              ctx->R8,  ctx->R9,  ctx->R10, ctx->R11, ctx->R12, ctx->R13, ctx->R14, ctx->R15};
    std::uint64_t frames[fc6::kWatchFrames] = {};
    if (!g_watchExec) walkStack(*ctx, frames, fc6::kWatchFrames, nullptr);
    while (InterlockedExchange(&g_watchLock, 1)) YieldProcessor();
    ++g_watchTotal;
    std::uint32_t i = 0;
    while (i < g_watchCount && !(g_watchAcc[i].rip == ctx->Rip &&
                                 (g_watchExec ? sameCall(g_watchAcc[i], regs) : samePath(g_watchAcc[i], frames)))) {
        ++i;
    }
    if (i == g_watchCount && g_watchCount < fc6::kMaxWatchHits) {
        fc6::WatchHit& h = g_watchAcc[g_watchCount++];
        std::memset(&h, 0, sizeof(h));
        h.rip = ctx->Rip;
        h.threadId = GetCurrentThreadId();
        std::memcpy(h.regs, regs, sizeof(regs));
        if (g_watchExec) {
            walkStack(*ctx, h.frames, fc6::kWatchFrames, nullptr);
        } else {
            std::memcpy(h.frames, frames, sizeof(frames));
        }
    }
    if (i < g_watchCount) ++g_watchAcc[i].count;
    InterlockedExchange(&g_watchLock, 0);
}

LONG CALLBACK onWatch(EXCEPTION_POINTERS* info) {
    if (info->ExceptionRecord->ExceptionCode != EXCEPTION_SINGLE_STEP) return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
    auto* ctx = info->ContextRecord;
    if (!(ctx->Dr6 & 0xE)) return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;  // not Dr1-Dr3
    ctx->Dr6 &= ~0xFull;  // status bits are sticky
    if (g_watching) recordWrite(ctx);
    // Data breakpoints trap after the write: just resume. Execute ones trap before the
    // instruction: RF lets it run once without trapping again.
    if (g_watchExec) ctx->EFlags |= 0x10000;
    return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_EXECUTION;
}

// Execute breakpoints on up to 3 code addresses: Dr1-Dr3, RW = 00, LEN = 00.
void setExecOnAllThreads(const std::uintptr_t* addrs, bool enable) {
    std::uint64_t mask = 0, bits = 0;
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
        const int n = k + 1;
        mask |= (3ull << (2 * n)) | (0xFull << (16 + 4 * n));
        if (addrs[k]) bits |= 1ull << (2 * n);
    }
    const DWORD self = GetCurrentThreadId(), pid = GetCurrentProcessId();
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    THREADENTRY32 te{sizeof(te)};
    for (BOOL ok = Thread32First(snap, &te); ok; ok = Thread32Next(snap, &te)) {
        if (te.th32OwnerProcessID != pid || te.th32ThreadID == self) continue;
        HANDLE t = OpenThread(THREAD_GET_CONTEXT | THREAD_SET_CONTEXT | THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME, FALSE, te.th32ThreadID);
        if (!t) continue;
        if (SuspendThread(t) != static_cast<DWORD>(-1)) {
            CONTEXT ctx{};
            ctx.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS;
            if (GetThreadContext(t, &ctx)) {
                ctx.Dr1 = enable ? addrs[0] : 0;
                ctx.Dr2 = enable ? addrs[1] : 0;
                ctx.Dr3 = enable ? addrs[2] : 0;
                ctx.Dr7 = (ctx.Dr7 & ~mask) | (enable ? bits : 0);
                SetThreadContext(t, &ctx);
            }
            ResumeThread(t);
        }
        CloseHandle(t);
    }
    CloseHandle(snap);
}

void setWatchOnAllThreads(std::uintptr_t addr, std::uint32_t len, bool enable) {
    // Dr7: L1 = bit 2, RW1 = bits 20-21 (01 = write, 11 = read or write), LEN1 = bits 22-23
    // (00/01/11/10 = 1/2/4/8 bytes)
    const std::uint64_t lenBits = len == 8 ? 2 : len == 4 ? 3 : len == 2 ? 1 : 0;
    const std::uint64_t rwBits = g_shared->watchAccess ? 3 : 1;
    const std::uint64_t mask = (1ull << 2) | (0xFull << 20);
    const std::uint64_t bits = (1ull << 2) | (rwBits << 20) | (lenBits << 22);
    const DWORD self = GetCurrentThreadId(), pid = GetCurrentProcessId();
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    THREADENTRY32 te{sizeof(te)};
    for (BOOL ok = Thread32First(snap, &te); ok; ok = Thread32Next(snap, &te)) {
        if (te.th32OwnerProcessID != pid || te.th32ThreadID == self) continue;
        HANDLE t = OpenThread(THREAD_GET_CONTEXT | THREAD_SET_CONTEXT | THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME, FALSE, te.th32ThreadID);
        if (!t) continue;
        if (SuspendThread(t) != static_cast<DWORD>(-1)) {
            CONTEXT ctx{};
            ctx.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS;
            if (GetThreadContext(t, &ctx)) {
                if (enable) {
                    ctx.Dr1 = addr;
                    ctx.Dr7 = (ctx.Dr7 & ~mask) | bits;
                } else if (ctx.Dr1 == addr) {
                    ctx.Dr1 = 0;
                    ctx.Dr7 &= ~mask;
                }
                SetThreadContext(t, &ctx);
            }
            ResumeThread(t);
        }
        CloseHandle(t);
    }
    CloseHandle(snap);
}

// len == 0: execute breakpoints on g_shared->execAddr instead of a write watch on addr.
void runWatch(std::uintptr_t addr, std::uint32_t len, std::uint32_t ms) {
    g_watchCount = g_watchTotal = 0;
    std::uintptr_t exec[3] = {};
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) exec[k] = static_cast<std::uintptr_t>(g_shared->execAddr[k]);
    PVOID veh = AddVectoredExceptionHandler(1, onWatch);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_watchExec, len == 0);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_watching, 1);
    if (len) setWatchOnAllThreads(addr, len, true);
    else setExecOnAllThreads(exec, true);
    Sleep(ms > 30000 ? 30000 : ms);
    if (len) setWatchOnAllThreads(addr, len, false);
    else setExecOnAllThreads(exec, false);
    Sleep(50);  // let in-flight exceptions finish before removing the handler
    InterlockedExchange(&g_watching, 0);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_watchExec, 0);
    RemoveVectoredExceptionHandler(veh);
    while (InterlockedExchange(&g_watchLock, 1)) YieldProcessor();
    g_shared->watchDistinct = g_watchCount;
    g_shared->watchTotal = g_watchTotal;
    std::memcpy(g_shared->watch, g_watchAcc, sizeof(fc6::WatchHit) * g_watchCount);
    InterlockedExchange(&g_watchLock, 0);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Inventory commands (version 15): the game's item functions, on the game's thread.

struct InvJob {
    std::uint32_t cmd;
    std::uintptr_t item;
    int delta;
    int result;
    std::uint32_t count;
    bool ok;
};

void invJob(void* arg) {
    auto* j = static_cast<InvJob*>(arg);
    if (j->cmd == 3) j->result = fc6::game::changeItemCount(j->item, j->delta);
    j->ok = fc6::game::itemCount(j->item, j->count);
}

void runInventoryCommand() {
    fc6::Shared& s = *g_shared;
    s.invOk = 0;
    s.invResult = 0;
    s.invValue = 0;
    s.invThread = 0;
    s.invMs = 0;
    if (s.invCmd == 1) {
        static fc6::game::InventoryItem list[fc6::kInvMax];
        const std::uintptr_t player = fc6::game::player();
        const int n = player ? fc6::game::inventoryItems(player, list, fc6::kInvMax) : 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            fc6::InvEntry& e = s.inv[i];
            std::memset(&e, 0, sizeof(e));
            e.item = list[i].item;
            e.id = list[i].id;
            e.maxStack = list[i].maxStack;
            std::memcpy(e.name, list[i].name, sizeof(e.name));
        }
        s.invCount = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(n);
        s.invOk = 1;
        return;
    }
    if ((s.invCmd != 2 && s.invCmd != 3) || !s.invItem || !s.invHook) return;
    InvJob job{s.invCmd, static_cast<std::uintptr_t>(s.invItem), s.invDelta, 0, 0, false};
    unsigned thread = 0;
    double ms = 0;
    if (!fc6::gamethread::run(static_cast<std::uintptr_t>(s.invHook), invJob, &job, 2000, thread, ms)) return;
    s.invResult = job.result;
    s.invValue = job.count;
    s.invThread = thread;
    s.invMs = ms;
    s.invOk = job.ok ? 1 : 0;
    fc6::config::log("inventory: cmd %u item %llx delta %d -> applied %d, count %u (%s), thread %u, %.3f ms", s.invCmd,
                     static_cast<unsigned long long>(s.invItem), s.invDelta, job.result, job.count,
                     job.ok ? "read" : "not read", thread, ms);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Replay.

int recordFault(EXCEPTION_POINTERS* info) {
    if (g_shared) {
        const auto* rec = info->ExceptionRecord;
        const auto addr = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(rec->ExceptionAddress);
        const bool inEngine = addr >= g_engine && addr < g_engine + 0x20000000;
        char text[128];
        sprintf_s(text, "fault %08lx at %s%llx (access %llx)", rec->ExceptionCode, inEngine ? "engine+" : "",
                  static_cast<unsigned long long>(inEngine ? addr - g_engine : addr),
                  static_cast<unsigned long long>(rec->NumberParameters > 1 ? rec->ExceptionInformation[1] : 0));
        setMessage(text);
    }
    return EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER;
}

// Separate function: __try cannot live next to objects that need unwinding.
bool callRayQuery(void* entry, void* collector) {
    __try {
        reinterpret_cast<RayQueryFn>(g_engine + kRayQuery)(reinterpret_cast<void*>(g_queryObject), entry, collector,
                                                           nullptr);
        return true;
    } __except (recordFault(GetExceptionInformation())) {
        return false;
    }
}

// Gives the calling thread its own Havok router with a private LIFO stack.
bool installRouter() {
    if (!g_haveRouter) return false;
    if (t_router.stack) return true;
    t_router.stack = static_cast<std::uint8_t*>(VirtualAlloc(nullptr, kLifoStackSize, MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE,
                                                             PAGE_READWRITE));
    if (!t_router.stack) return false;
    std::memcpy(t_router.copy, g_router, kRouterCopySize);
    const std::int32_t slab = 0x40000000;
    void* cur = t_router.stack;
    void* end = t_router.stack + kLifoStackSize;
    void* none = nullptr;
    std::memcpy(t_router.copy + 0x10, &slab, 4);
    std::memcpy(t_router.copy + 0x18, &cur, 8);
    std::memcpy(t_router.copy + 0x20, &end, 8);
    std::memcpy(t_router.copy + 0x28, &none, 8);
    const DWORD slot = *reinterpret_cast<std::uint32_t*>(g_engine + kHavokTlsIndex);
    return TlsSetValue(slot, t_router.copy) != FALSE;
}

void uninstallRouter() {
    if (!t_router.stack) return;
    TlsSetValue(*reinterpret_cast<std::uint32_t*>(g_engine + kHavokTlsIndex), nullptr);
    VirtualFree(t_router.stack, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
    t_router.stack = nullptr;
}

void resetLifo() {
    void* cur = t_router.stack;
    std::memcpy(t_router.copy + 0x18, &cur, 8);
}

// filter: collision filter info for this ray, 0 = the captured game query's.
void castOne(const fc6::Ray& ray, std::uint32_t filter, fc6::Hit& out) {
    alignas(16) std::uint8_t entry[kEntrySize];
    alignas(16) std::uint8_t collector[kCollectorSize];
    alignas(16) std::uint8_t output[kOutputSize * kOutputSlots] = {};
    std::memcpy(entry, g_entryTemplate, kEntrySize);
    std::memcpy(collector, g_collectorTemplate, kCollectorSize);

    float from[4] = {ray.from[0], ray.from[1], ray.from[2], 0.f};
    float to[4] = {ray.to[0], ray.to[1], ray.to[2], 0.f};
    std::memcpy(entry + 0x00, from, 16);
    std::memcpy(entry + 0x10, to, 16);
    void* outPtr = output;
    std::memcpy(entry + 0x30, &outPtr, 8);
    // One result slot: when full, the collector keeps the closest hit.
    const std::int32_t capacity = 1, count = 0;
    std::memcpy(entry + 0x38, &capacity, 4);
    std::memcpy(entry + 0x3c, &count, 4);
    entry[0x40] = 0;  // no secondary collector
    if (filter) std::memcpy(entry + 0x24, &filter, 4);

    std::memset(&out, 0, sizeof(out));
    if (!callRayQuery(entry, collector)) {
        out.fault = 1;
        return;
    }
    std::int32_t hits = 0;
    std::memcpy(&hits, entry + 0x3c, 4);
```
