```cpp
    float fraction = 1.f;
    if (hits > 0) std::memcpy(&fraction, output + 0x10, 4);
    out.fraction = fraction;
    out.hit = hits > 0 && fraction < 1.f;
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) out.position[k] = from[k] + (to[k] - from[k]) * fraction;
    std::memcpy(out.normal, output + 0x00, 16);
    std::memcpy(&out.object, output + 0x40, 8);
    std::memcpy(out.raw, output, sizeof(out.raw));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

DWORD WINAPI worker(LPVOID) {
    HANDLE mapping = CreateFileMappingW(INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE, nullptr, PAGE_READWRITE, 0, fc6::kSharedBytes,
                                       fc6::kSharedName);
    if (!mapping) FreeLibraryAndExitThread(g_self, 1);
    g_shared = static_cast<fc6::Shared*>(MapViewOfFile(mapping, FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS, 0, 0, sizeof(fc6::Shared)));
    if (!g_shared) {
        // A client still holds the smaller block of an older DLL (the header and message are at
        // the same place in every version): say so there.
        if (auto* old = static_cast<fc6::Shared*>(MapViewOfFile(mapping, FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS, 0, 0, 0))) {
            old->status = fc6::kStopped;
            strncpy_s(old->message, "close the tools still open (aim.py...) and inject again", _TRUNCATE);
            UnmapViewOfFile(old);
        }
        CloseHandle(mapping);
        FreeLibraryAndExitThread(g_self, 1);
    }
    std::memset(g_shared, 0, sizeof(fc6::Shared));
    g_shared->magic = fc6::kSharedMagic;
    g_shared->version = fc6::kSharedVersion;
    // Sleep(1) lasts a whole timer tick (~15.6 ms) unless something raised the timer resolution:
    // ray requests wake the worker through an event instead.
    HANDLE request = CreateEventW(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, fc6::kRequestEvent);
    HANDLE response = CreateEventW(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, fc6::kResponseEvent);

    g_engine = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(GetModuleHandleW(L"FC_m64d3d12.dll"));
    if (!g_engine) {
        setMessage("engine module not found");
        g_shared->status = fc6::kNoWorld;
    } else {
        const auto* dos = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(g_engine);
        const auto* nt = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS64*>(g_engine + dos->e_lfanew);
        fc6::game::init(g_engine, nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage);
        fc6::gamethread::start(g_engine);
        fc6::config::log("shared: inventory block at +0x%llx",
                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(offsetof(fc6::Shared, invSeq)));
        fc6::aim::start(g_shared);  // game reads don't need the ray capture below
        fc6::overlay::start(g_shared);
        g_shared->status = fc6::kWaitingForWorld;
        setMessage("waiting for the game to make a ray query");
        if (capture(30000) && installRouter()) {
            g_shared->queryObject = g_queryObject;
            g_shared->queryVtable = *reinterpret_cast<std::uint64_t*>(g_queryObject) - g_engine;
            std::memcpy(&g_shared->filterInfo, g_entryTemplate + 0x24, 4);
            g_shared->entryFlags = g_entryTemplate[0x20];
            g_shared->status = fc6::kReady;
            setMessage("ready");
        } else {
            g_shared->status = fc6::kNoWorld;
            setMessage(g_captured == 2 ? "query captured but no Havok router to copy"
                                        : "no ray query seen within 30 s (menu? paused?)");
        }
    }

    Monitor monitor;
    while (!g_shared->quit) {
        stepMonitor(monitor);
        if (monitor.on && (g_shared->sampleMs || g_shared->traceMs)) {
            setMessage("sampling and traces wait while the bullet monitor runs");
            g_shared->sampleMs = g_shared->traceMs = 0;
        }
        if (g_shared->sampleMs) {
            runSampling(g_shared->sampleMs);
            g_shared->sampleMs = 0;
        }
        if (g_shared->watchMs) {
            const std::uint32_t len = g_shared->watchLen;
            const std::uintptr_t addr = g_shared->watchAddr;
            if (len == 0 || ((len == 1 || len == 2 || len == 4 || len == 8) && addr % len == 0)) {
                runWatch(addr, len, g_shared->watchMs);
            } else {
                g_shared->watchDistinct = g_shared->watchTotal = 0;
                setMessage("watch: length must be 1/2/4/8 and the address aligned to it");
            }
            g_shared->watchMs = 0;
        }
        if (g_shared->traceMs) {
            runTrace(g_shared->traceMs);
            g_shared->traceMs = 0;
        }
        if (g_shared->invSeq != g_shared->invRespSeq) {
            const std::uint32_t seq = g_shared->invSeq;
            runInventoryCommand();
            MemoryBarrier();
            g_shared->invRespSeq = seq;
        }
        if (g_shared->status == fc6::kReady && g_shared->reqSeq != g_shared->respSeq) {
            const std::uint32_t seq = g_shared->reqSeq;
            const std::uint32_t n = g_shared->count > fc6::kMaxRays ? fc6::kMaxRays : g_shared->count;
            for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                castOne(g_shared->rays[i], g_shared->filterOverride, g_shared->hits[i]);
                resetLifo();
            }
            g_shared->served += n;
            MemoryBarrier();
            g_shared->respSeq = seq;
            if (response) SetEvent(response);
        }
        if (request) {
            WaitForSingleObject(request, 10);  // timeout: quit, sampling, watch and polling clients
        } else {
            Sleep(1);
        }
    }

    fc6::overlay::stop();
    fc6::aim::stop();
    fc6::gamethread::stop();
    g_shared->monitorOn = 0;
    stepMonitor(monitor);
    g_shared->status = fc6::kStopped;
    uninstallRouter();
    UnmapViewOfFile(g_shared);
    CloseHandle(mapping);
    if (request) CloseHandle(request);
    if (response) CloseHandle(response);
    FreeLibraryAndExitThread(g_self, 0);
}

}  // namespace

namespace fc6::rays {

bool ready() { return g_shared && g_shared->status == kReady; }

bool attachThread() { return ready() && installRouter(); }

void detachThread() { uninstallRouter(); }

bool cast(const Ray& ray, std::uint32_t filter, Hit& out) {
    if (!t_router.stack) return false;
    castOne(ray, filter, out);
    resetLifo();
    return !out.fault;
}

}  // namespace fc6::rays

BOOL APIENTRY DllMain(HMODULE module, DWORD reason, LPVOID) {
    if (reason == DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH) {
        g_self = module;
        DisableThreadLibraryCalls(module);
        HANDLE t = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, worker, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
        if (t) CloseHandle(t);
    }
    return TRUE;
}

```

`src/internal/esp.cpp`:

```cpp
// Skeleton ESP, see esp.hpp.

#include "internal/esp.hpp"

#include "internal/targeting.hpp"

#include <windows.h>
#include <intrin.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

namespace fc6::overlay {
namespace {

constexpr std::uint32_t kTarget = 0xFF4040, kAlly = 0x40FF60, kOther = 0xB0B0B0, kNotSkeleton = 0xFFD000,
                        kThreat = 0xFF6B2C;

// Bone chains drawn as lines (names as in the game's skeletons).
constexpr const char* kChains[][7] = {
    {"Hips", "Spine", "Spine1", "Spine2", "Neck", "Head", "HeadEnd"},
    {"Spine2", "LeftShoulder", "LeftArm", "LeftForeArm", "LeftHand"},
    {"Spine2", "RightShoulder", "RightArm", "RightForeArm", "RightHand"},
    {"Hips", "LeftUpLeg", "LeftLeg", "LeftFoot"},
    {"Hips", "RightUpLeg", "RightLeg", "RightFoot"},
};
constexpr int kChainCount = sizeof(kChains) / sizeof(kChains[0]);
constexpr int kChainLen = 7;
constexpr int kMaxNpcs = 64;

struct ChainBones {
    std::uint32_t crc[kChainCount * kChainLen] = {};
    ChainBones() {
        for (int c = 0; c < kChainCount; ++c) {
            for (int k = 0; k < kChainLen; ++k) {
                if (kChains[c][k]) crc[c * kChainLen + k] = game::crc32(kChains[c][k]);
            }
        }
    }
};

// The aimbot's last verdict on this NPC (targeting.hpp), as a label suffix.
const char* verdictText(aim::Verdict v) {
    switch (v) {
        case aim::Verdict::kTarget: return " · CIBLE";
        case aim::Verdict::kVisible: return " · visible";
        case aim::Verdict::kHidden: return " · caché";
        case aim::Verdict::kOutOfCone: return " · hors cône";
        case aim::Verdict::kTooFar: return " · trop loin";
        case aim::Verdict::kNotEnemy: return " · pas ennemi";
        default: return "";
    }
}

const char* mood(int attitude) {
    switch (attitude) {
        case 1: return " calme";
        case 2: return " hostile";
        case 4: return " allié";
        default: return " ?";
    }
}

// One NPC as it will be drawn, in screen pixels.
struct NpcView {
    std::uint32_t colour = 0;
    int lines = 0;
    float seg[kChainCount * kChainLen][4];  // x1, y1, x2, y2
    float x = 0, y = 0, r = 0;
    bool skeleton = false, threat = false;
    char label[128];
};

double wall();

bool gatherNpc(const Projector& proj, game::Skeletons& skeletons, const game::Character& c, const Vec3& eye,
               std::uintptr_t player, double now, NpcView& v, EspTiming& t, const char* verdict) {
    static const ChainBones chains;
    double t0 = wall();
    const int attitude = game::attitude(c.entity);
    v.colour = game::isHostile(c.entity, c.name) ? kTarget : attitude == game::kAttitudeAlly ? kAlly : kOther;
    const char* reason = "";
    double t1 = wall();
    t.rest += t1 - t0;
    const game::AimPoints pts = skeletons.aimPoints(c.entity, c.point, now, &reason);
    t0 = wall();
    t.points += t0 - t1;
    float x, y;
    if (!proj(pts.p[0], x, y)) return false;
    v.skeleton = pts.source == game::PointSource::kSkeleton;
    v.lines = 0;
    int bones = 0;
    if (v.skeleton) {
        Vec3 world[kChainCount * kChainLen];
        bool have[kChainCount * kChainLen];
        bones = skeletons.namedBones(c.entity, chains.crc, kChainCount * kChainLen, world, have);
        t1 = wall();
        t.bones += t1 - t0;
        t0 = t1;
        for (int ch = 0; ch < kChainCount; ++ch) {
            bool prev = false;
            float px = 0, py = 0;
            for (int k = 0; k < kChainLen && kChains[ch][k]; ++k) {
                float bx, by;
                const int i = ch * kChainLen + k;
                if (!have[i] || !proj(world[i], bx, by)) continue;  // skip: join the next one found
                if (prev) {
                    float* s = v.seg[v.lines++];
                    s[0] = px;
                    s[1] = py;
                    s[2] = bx;
                    s[3] = by;
                }
                prev = true;
                px = bx;
                py = by;
            }
        }
    }
    const float dist = game::dist(eye, pts.p[0]);
    v.x = x;
    v.y = y;
    v.r = std::clamp(120.f / std::max(dist, 1.f), 3.f, 14.f);
    char life[32] = "";
    float hp, hpMax;
    if (game::health(c.entity, hp, hpMax)) std::snprintf(life, sizeof(life), " %.0f/%.0fhp", hp, hpMax);
    char what[32];
    if (v.skeleton) {
        std::snprintf(what, sizeof(what), "%db%s", bones, std::strcmp(reason, "live") == 0 ? "" : " new");
    } else {
        std::snprintf(what, sizeof(what), "%s [%s]", pts.source == game::PointSource::kRagdoll ? "ragdoll" : "estimate",
                      reason);
    }
    const bool calmCivilian = std::strstr(c.name, "_Neutral") && attitude == 1;
    std::snprintf(v.label, sizeof(v.label), "%.0fm%s%s %s%s", dist, calmCivilian ? "" : mood(attitude), life, what,
                  verdict);
    v.threat = player && game::aiTarget(c.entity) == player;  // its AI aims at the player
    t.rest += wall() - t0;
    return true;
}

void drawNpc(Painter& paint, const NpcView& v) {
    for (int i = 0; i < v.lines; ++i) paint.line(v.seg[i][0], v.seg[i][1], v.seg[i][2], v.seg[i][3], v.colour);
    if (v.skeleton) {
        paint.circle(v.x, v.y, v.r, v.colour);
    } else {
        paint.rect(v.x - v.r, v.y - v.r, 2 * v.r, 2 * v.r, kNotSkeleton);
    }
    paint.text(v.x, v.y - v.r - 16, v.label, v.colour, Font::kSmall, true);
    if (v.threat) paint.text(v.x, v.y - v.r - 32, "TE VISE", kThreat, Font::kSmall, true);
}

double wall() {
    LARGE_INTEGER f, n;
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f);
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&n);
    return static_cast<double>(n.QuadPart) / static_cast<double>(f.QuadPart);
}

// This thread's CPU time: its cycle count over the cycle counter's rate (measured once).
double cpu() {
    static const double rate = [] {
        const double w0 = wall();
        const unsigned long long c0 = __rdtsc();
        Sleep(20);
        return static_cast<double>(__rdtsc() - c0) / (wall() - w0);
    }();
    ULONG64 cycles = 0;
    QueryThreadCycleTime(GetCurrentThread(), &cycles);
    return static_cast<double>(cycles) / rate;
}

}  // namespace

bool Projector::operator()(const Vec3& p, float& x, float& y) const {
    const Vec3 d = p - cam.m.pos;
    const float z = d.dot(cam.m.fwd);
    if (z < 0.1f) return false;
    x = (1.f + d.dot(cam.m.right) / z * cam.sx) * w / 2;
    y = (1.f - d.dot(cam.m.up) / z * cam.sy) * h / 2;
    return true;
}

EspTiming drawEsp(Painter& paint, const Projector& proj, game::Skeletons& skeletons, float maxDist, double now) {
    EspTiming t;
    const double w0 = wall(), c0 = cpu();
    const game::ReadStats r0 = game::readStats();
    static game::Character list[256];
    static NpcView views[kMaxNpcs];
    const int n = game::characters(list, 256);
    t.list = wall() - w0;
    static aim::VerdictNote notes[256];
    const int nv = aim::verdicts(notes, 256);
    std::uintptr_t player = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n && !player; ++i) {
        if (std::strstr(list[i].name, "player:")) player = list[i].entity;
    }
    const Vec3 eye = proj.cam.m.pos;
    for (int i = 0; i < n && t.drawn < kMaxNpcs; ++i) {
        const float d = game::dist(eye, list[i].point);
        if (!std::strstr(list[i].name, "NPC") || d > maxDist) continue;
        // Behind the camera or well off screen (the body is within 2 m of the point): nothing read.
        float x, y;
        const float mw = proj.w * 0.5f, mh = proj.h * 0.5f;
        if (d > 3.f && (!proj(list[i].point, x, y) || x < -mw || x > proj.w + mw || y < -mh || y > proj.h + mh)) {
            continue;
        }
        const double k0 = wall();
        const bool dead = game::isDead(list[i].entity);
        t.checks += wall() - k0;
        if (dead) continue;
        const char* verdict = "";
        for (int k = 0; k < nv; ++k) {
            if (notes[k].entity == list[i].entity) verdict = verdictText(notes[k].verdict);
        }
        if (gatherNpc(proj, skeletons, list[i], eye, player, now, views[t.drawn], t, verdict)) ++t.drawn;
    }
    const game::ReadStats r1 = game::readStats();
    t.reads = r1.reads - r0.reads;
    t.queries = r1.queries - r0.queries;
    const double w1 = wall(), c1 = cpu();
    for (int i = 0; i < t.drawn; ++i) drawNpc(paint, views[i]);
    const double w2 = wall(), c2 = cpu();
    t.gather = w1 - w0;
    t.gatherCpu = c1 - c0;
    t.draw = w2 - w1;
    t.drawCpu = c2 - c1;
    return t;
}

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/esp.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Skeleton ESP (port of tools/esp.py draw_npc): for each living NPC, its skeleton and the head
// point the aimbot would pick, coloured by the aimbot's decision (red target, green ally, grey
// other; yellow square when the point doesn't come from the skeleton), and a label, with "TE VISE"
// above it when the NPC's AI targets the player (game::aiTarget). NPCs off screen and dead ones are
// skipped before any skeleton read (bodies pile up after a fight).
//
// Two passes: gather (reads the game, projects to the screen) then draw (Direct2D), timed apart.

#include "internal/game.hpp"
#include "internal/painter.hpp"

namespace fc6::overlay {

struct Projector {
    game::Camera cam;
    float w = 0, h = 0;
    // Screen position of a world point; false behind the camera.
    bool operator()(const Vec3& p, float& x, float& y) const;
};

// Wall time and CPU time of this thread (QueryThreadCycleTime), in seconds, per pass.
struct EspTiming {
    int drawn = 0;
    double gather = 0, gatherCpu = 0, draw = 0, drawCpu = 0;
    std::uint64_t reads = 0, queries = 0;  // game::readStats() during the gather pass
    // The gather pass by step (wall time, seconds): the character list, the dead / on-screen
    // checks, the skeleton's aim points, the drawn bones, the rest (faction, health, AI target).
    double list = 0, checks = 0, points = 0, bones = 0, rest = 0;
};

EspTiming drawEsp(Painter& paint, const Projector& proj, game::Skeletons& skeletons, float maxDist, double now);

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/game.cpp`:

```cpp
// Game state reads (port of tools/targets.py), see game.hpp.

#include "internal/game.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
#include <map>
#include <numbers>

namespace fc6::game {
namespace {

std::uintptr_t g_base = 0;
std::size_t g_size = 0;

// Memory known to be readable / writable, per thread: a stray pointer is caught by VirtualQuery
// before it faults (another module's crash handler could see the fault first). VirtualQuery scans
// the page table from the address to the end of its region, and the game's heaps are regions of
// 200-430 MB: one call took up to 4.5 ms (VirtualQueryEx timed from outside), and the checks of
// each page, renewed every second, cost the ESP 3.5-4 ms per frame. One call now covers the rest
// of the region, kept for kRegionLife; a read or write that faults anyway forgets its region.
struct Region {
    std::uintptr_t end;
    DWORD until;  // tick of the next check
    bool read, write;
};
thread_local std::map<std::uintptr_t, Region> t_regions;  // start -> region (disjoint)
thread_local ReadStats t_readStats;
constexpr DWORD kRegionLife = 20000, kBadRegionLife = 1000;

const Region* region(std::uintptr_t addr) {
    const DWORD now = GetTickCount();
    auto it = t_regions.upper_bound(addr);
    if (it != t_regions.begin()) {
        --it;
        if (addr < it->second.end && static_cast<LONG>(it->second.until - now) > 0) return &it->second;
    }
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi;
    ++t_readStats.queries;
    if (!VirtualQuery(reinterpret_cast<void*>(addr & ~0xFFFull), &mbi, sizeof(mbi))) return nullptr;
    const DWORD p = mbi.Protect;
    const bool usable = mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && !(p & (PAGE_GUARD | PAGE_NOACCESS));
    Region r;
    r.read = usable && (p & (PAGE_READONLY | PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ |
                             PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY)) != 0;
    r.write = usable && (p & (PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)) != 0;
    // From the queried page to the end of the run of pages with the same attributes.
    const std::uintptr_t start = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(mbi.BaseAddress);
    r.end = start + mbi.RegionSize;
    r.until = now + (r.read ? kRegionLife : kBadRegionLife);
    if (t_regions.size() > 4096) t_regions.clear();
    // Older entries inside it (the same region queried from higher up) are covered by this one.
    t_regions.erase(t_regions.lower_bound(start), t_regions.lower_bound(r.end));
    return &(t_regions[start] = r);
}

void forgetRegion(std::uintptr_t addr) {
    auto it = t_regions.upper_bound(addr);
    if (it != t_regions.begin() && addr < std::prev(it)->second.end) t_regions.erase(std::prev(it));
}

// Components found, per thread: a lookup scans the entity's 96 slots and reads each one's vtable
// (~100 reads), several times per NPC and frame for the ESP. An entry is trusted while the
// entity keeps its component array and the slot still holds that component with that vtable
// (3 reads). Not found: remembered for a second.
struct ComponentEntry {
    std::uintptr_t entity, vtable, array, component;
    int slot;
    DWORD until;  // not found: tick of the next scan
};
thread_local std::unordered_map<std::uintptr_t, ComponentEntry> t_components;

bool readable(std::uintptr_t addr, std::size_t n) {
    for (const std::uintptr_t last = addr + n; addr < last;) {
        const Region* r = region(addr);
        if (!r || !r->read) return false;
        addr = r->end;
    }
    return true;
}

bool writable(std::uintptr_t addr, std::size_t n) {
    for (const std::uintptr_t last = addr + n; addr < last;) {
        const Region* r = region(addr);
        if (!r || !r->write) return false;
        addr = r->end;
    }
    return true;
}

int copyFilter(DWORD code) {
    return code == EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION || code == EXCEPTION_IN_PAGE_ERROR || code == STATUS_GUARD_PAGE_VIOLATION
               ? EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER
               : EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
}

// Separate function: __try can't sit next to objects that need unwinding.
bool guardedCopy(void* dst, std::uintptr_t src, std::size_t n) {
    __try {
        std::memcpy(dst, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(src), n);
        return true;
    } __except (copyFilter(GetExceptionCode())) {
        return false;
    }
}

bool contains(const char* s, const char* part) { return std::strstr(s, part) != nullptr; }

using FireDirectionFn = void(__fastcall*)(void* fire, void* shooter, float* origin, float* direction);

// Separate function: __try can't sit next to objects that need unwinding.
bool callFireDirection(std::uintptr_t fn, std::uintptr_t fire, std::uintptr_t shooter, float* origin, float* dir) {
    __try {
        reinterpret_cast<FireDirectionFn>(fn)(reinterpret_cast<void*>(fire), reinterpret_cast<void*>(shooter), origin,
                                              dir);
        return true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

using ItemCountFn = void(__fastcall*)(void* value, std::uint32_t* out);
using ItemChangeFn = std::uint32_t(__fastcall*)(void* item, std::uint32_t n);

// Only access violations are caught: other exceptions may be the game's own (its protected code)
// and must reach its handlers.
int accessFilter(DWORD code) {
    return code == EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION ? EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER : EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
}

bool callItemCount(std::uintptr_t fn, std::uintptr_t value, std::uint32_t* out) {
    __try {
        reinterpret_cast<ItemCountFn>(fn)(reinterpret_cast<void*>(value), out);
        return true;
    } __except (accessFilter(GetExceptionCode())) {
        return false;
    }
}

bool callItemChange(std::uintptr_t fn, std::uintptr_t item, std::uint32_t n, std::uint32_t& done) {
    __try {
        done = reinterpret_cast<ItemChangeFn>(fn)(reinterpret_cast<void*>(item), n);
        return true;
    } __except (accessFilter(GetExceptionCode())) {
        return false;
    }
}

bool g_itemsFaulted = false;  // a game item call faulted: no more of them this session

// Still a CInventoryItemStackable (the tree is read from another thread than the game's).
bool isStackable(std::uintptr_t item) {
    return item && vt(item) == off::kStackableVt && vt(item + 8) == off::kStackableIfaceVt;
}

bool guardedStore(std::uintptr_t dst, const void* src, std::size_t n) {
    __try {
        std::memcpy(reinterpret_cast<void*>(dst), src, n);
        return true;
    } __except (copyFilter(GetExceptionCode())) {
        return false;
    }
}

}  // namespace

void init(std::uintptr_t engineBase, std::size_t engineSize) {
    g_base = engineBase;
    g_size = engineSize;
}

std::uintptr_t engine() { return g_base; }

ReadStats readStats() { return t_readStats; }

bool read(std::uintptr_t addr, void* dst, std::size_t n) {
    ++t_readStats.reads;
    if (addr >= 0x10000 && addr + n > addr && addr + n <= 0x7FFFFFFF0000ull && readable(addr, n)) {
        if (guardedCopy(dst, addr, n)) return true;
        forgetRegion(addr);  // changed since it was checked
    }
    std::memset(dst, 0, n);
    return false;
}

bool write(std::uintptr_t addr, const void* src, std::size_t n) {
    if (addr < 0x10000 || addr + n <= addr || addr + n > 0x7FFFFFFF0000ull || !writable(addr, n)) return false;
    if (guardedStore(addr, src, n)) return true;
    forgetRegion(addr);
    return false;
}

std::uintptr_t vt(std::uintptr_t addr) {
    const std::uint64_t v = addr ? u64(addr) : 0;
    return v >= g_base && v < g_base + g_size ? v - g_base : 0;
}

bool transform(std::uintptr_t addr, Transform& out) {
    float f[16];
    if (!read(addr, f, sizeof(f))) return false;
    for (float x : f) {
        if (!std::isfinite(x)) return false;
    }
    out.right = {f[0], f[1], f[2]};
    out.fwd = {f[4], f[5], f[6]};
    out.up = {f[8], f[9], f[10]};
    out.pos = {f[12], f[13], f[14]};
    return true;
}

bool camera(Camera& out) {
    for (const auto& c : off::kCameraChains) {
        const std::uint64_t p = u64(g_base + c.root);
        const std::uint64_t q = p ? u64(p + c.o1) : 0;
        if (!q) continue;
        const std::uintptr_t addr = q + c.o2;
        Transform m;
        if (!transform(addr, m) || std::fabs(m.up.z) > 1.0001f || std::fabs(m.fwd.dot(m.fwd) - 1.f) >= 1e-2f) continue;
        const float sx = f32(addr + 0x40), sy = f32(addr + 0x54);
        if (!(sx > 0.1f && sx < 50.f && sy > 0.1f && sy < 50.f)) continue;
        out = {m, sx, sy};
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

void archetype(std::uintptr_t entity, char (&name)[kNameLen]) {
    name[0] = 0;
    const std::uint64_t s = entity ? u64(entity + 0x18) : 0;
    const std::uint32_t n = s ? u32(s) : 0;
    if (!n) return;
    const std::size_t len = n < kNameLen - 1 ? n : kNameLen - 1;
    if (read(s + 0xC, name, len)) name[len] = 0;
}

int characters(Character* out, int max) {
    const std::uint64_t mgr = u64(g_base + off::kAimAssistStatic);
    if (!mgr || vt(mgr) != off::kAimAssistVt) return 0;
    const std::uint64_t arr = u64(mgr + 0x30);
    const std::uint32_t count = u32(mgr + 0x3C);
    int n = 0;
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count && i < 256 && n < max; ++i) {
        const std::uint64_t p = u64(arr + 8 * i);
        if (!p || vt(p) != off::kPointVt) continue;
        const std::uint64_t handle = u64(p + 8);
        const std::uint64_t entity = handle ? u64(handle + 8) : 0;
        Transform m;
        if (!entity || !transform(p + 0x30, m)) continue;
        Character& c = out[n++];
        c.entity = entity;
        c.point = m.pos;
        archetype(entity, c.name);
    }
    return n;
}

std::uintptr_t component(std::uintptr_t entity, std::uintptr_t vtable) {
    const std::uint64_t arr = entity ? u64(entity + 0xA8) : 0;
    if (!arr) return 0;
    const std::uintptr_t key = entity * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ull ^ vtable;
    const auto it = t_components.find(key);
    if (it != t_components.end() && it->second.entity == entity && it->second.vtable == vtable &&
        it->second.array == arr) {
        const ComponentEntry& e = it->second;
        if (!e.component && static_cast<LONG>(e.until - GetTickCount()) > 0) return 0;
        if (e.component && u64(arr + 8ull * e.slot) == e.component && vt(e.component) == vtable) return e.component;
    }
    std::uint64_t comps[96];
    if (!read(arr, comps, sizeof(comps))) return 0;
    if (t_components.size() > 16384) t_components.clear();
    for (int i = 0; i < 96; ++i) {
        if (comps[i] && vt(comps[i]) == vtable) {
            t_components[key] = {entity, vtable, arr, comps[i], i, 0};
            return comps[i];
        }
    }
    t_components[key] = {entity, vtable, arr, 0, -1, GetTickCount() + 1000};
    return 0;
}

int attitude(std::uintptr_t entity) {
    const std::uintptr_t c = component(entity, off::kFactionComponentVt);
    return c ? static_cast<int>(u32(c + 0x2C)) : -1;
}

int deathState(std::uintptr_t entity) {
    const std::uintptr_t c = component(entity, off::kDeathComponentVt);
    return c ? static_cast<int>(u32(c + 0x50)) : 0;
}

bool health(std::uintptr_t entity, float& current, float& maximum) {
    const std::uintptr_t c = component(entity, off::kDamageComponentVt);
    const std::uint64_t h = c ? u64(c + 0x48) : 0;
    if (!h || vt(h) != off::kHealthObjectVt) return false;
    current = f32(h + 0x18);
    maximum = f32(h + 0x1C);
    return true;
}

bool isDead(std::uintptr_t entity) {
    if (deathState(entity)) return true;
    float cur, max;
    return health(entity, cur, max) && cur <= 0.f;
}

// Enemy human: hostile now (attitude 2), or an FND soldier / elite by archetype name who isn't
// hostile yet. Never an ally (attitude 4). See targets.is_hostile.
bool isHostile(std::uintptr_t entity, const char* name) {
    if (!contains(name, "NPC:") || contains(name, "_Friendly") || contains(name, "_Neutral")) return false;
    const int a = attitude(entity);
    if (a == kAttitudeAlly) return false;
    return a == kAttitudeHostile || contains(name, "_Hostile") || contains(name, ".NPC.Elite.");
}

std::uintptr_t player() {
    Character list[256];
    const int n = characters(list, 256);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (contains(list[i].name, "player:")) return list[i].entity;
    }
    return 0;
}

std::uintptr_t aiTarget(std::uintptr_t npc) {
    const std::uintptr_t c = component(npc, off::kNpcPawnComponentVt);
    const std::uint64_t data = c ? u64(c + 0x2AE8) : 0;
    const std::uint64_t handle = data ? u64(data + off::kAiAimData + 0xE8) : 0;
    return handle ? u64(handle + 8) : 0;
}

std::uintptr_t heldWeaponObject(std::uintptr_t playerEntity) {
    const std::uintptr_t c = component(playerEntity, off::kAimComponentVt);
    return c ? u64(c + 0x248) : 0;
}

bool heldWeapon(std::uintptr_t playerEntity, Weapon& out) {
    out = {};
    const std::uintptr_t w = heldWeaponObject(playerEntity);
    const std::uint64_t h = w ? u64(w + 8) : 0;
    const std::uint64_t e = h ? u64(h + 8) : 0;
    archetype(e, out.name);
    if (std::strncmp(out.name, "weapons:", 8) != 0) {
        out.name[0] = 0;
        return false;
    }
    // Fire properties: bUseBulletPhysic +0x324, fBulletSpeed +0x328, fBulletGravity +0x32c
    // (negative), fBulletDropDistance +0x330.
    const std::uint64_t t = u64(w + 0x168);
    const std::uint64_t p = t ? u64(t + 0x10) : 0;
    struct {
        std::uint8_t physics[4];
        float speed, gravity, drop;
    } raw{};
    if (!p || vt(p) != off::kFirePropsVt || !read(p + 0x324, &raw, sizeof(raw)) || !(raw.speed > 1.f) ||
        !(raw.speed < 100000.f)) {
        return true;
    }
    out.stats = true;
    out.physics = raw.physics[0] == 1;
    out.speed = raw.speed;
    out.gravity = std::fabs(raw.gravity);
    out.dropDistance = raw.drop;
    out.rpm = f32(w + 0x170);
    // Spread data (deg): BulletSpread +0x2e4 (hip min, max), BulletSpread_ironSight +0x2f4.
    read(p + 0x2E4, out.hipSpread, sizeof(out.hipSpread));
    read(p + 0x2F4, out.aimSpread, sizeof(out.aimSpread));
    // Attachments: range, speed, gravity and drop multipliers on the weapon object.
    struct {
        float range, unknown, speed, gravity, drop;
    } mods{};
    if (read(w + 0xEC4, &mods, sizeof(mods))) {
        auto sane = [](float m) { return m > 0.01f && m < 100.f ? m : 1.f; };
        out.range = mods.range > 0.f && mods.range < 100000.f ? mods.range : 0.f;
        out.speedMul = sane(mods.speed);
        out.gravityMul = sane(mods.gravity);
        out.dropMul = sane(mods.drop);
    }
    // Loaded ammo: its id in the fire component, looked up in the weapon's supported ammo list.
    const std::uint64_t list = u64(p + 0x6F0);
    const std::uint32_t count = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(u64(p + 0x6F8) >> 32) & 0x7FFFFFFF;
    if (!list || count == 0 || count > 64) return true;
    const std::uint64_t fire = u64(w + 0x198);
    const std::uint64_t id = fire ? u64(fire + 0xF98) : 0;
    std::uint32_t index = count;
    for (std::uint32_t k = 0; k < count && id; ++k) {
        if (u64(list + k * 0xD8ull + 0x10) == id) index = k;
    }
    if (index == count) {
        const std::uint32_t def = u32(p + 0x700);
        index = def < count ? def : 0;
    }
    const std::uint64_t entry = list + index * 0xD8ull;
    float factors[2];  // +0x38 gravity, +0x3c speed
    if (read(entry + 0x38, factors, sizeof(factors))) {
        auto sane = [](float m) { return m > 0.01f && m < 100.f ? m : 1.f; };
        out.ammoGravity = sane(factors[0]);
        out.ammoSpeed = sane(factors[1]);
    }
    // Name: the descriptor [entry + 0x18] -> [+0x10] holds "Ammo/<name>" near its start.
    const std::uint64_t desc = u64(entry + 0x18);
    const std::uint64_t buf = desc ? u64(desc + 0x10) : 0;
    char raw96[97] = {};
    if (buf && read(buf, raw96, 96)) {
        for (int i = 0; i + 5 <= 96; ++i) {
            if (std::memcmp(raw96 + i, "Ammo/", 5) == 0) {
                strncpy_s(out.ammo, raw96 + i + 5, _TRUNCATE);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

float Spread::hitChance(float radius, float dist) const {
    const float half = s * 0.5f * std::numbers::pi_v<float> / 180.f;
    if (!(half > 0.f) || dist <= 0.f) return 1.f;
    // P(angle < x) = (x / half) ^ (2 / p).
    const float x = std::atan2(radius, dist) / half;
    return x >= 1.f ? 1.f : std::pow(x, 2.f / p);
}

float Spread::median() const {
    return s * 0.5f * std::numbers::pi_v<float> / 180.f * std::pow(0.5f, p / 2.f);
}

bool heldSpread(std::uintptr_t playerEntity, Spread& out) {
    out = {};
    const std::uintptr_t w = heldWeaponObject(playerEntity);
    const std::uint64_t t = w ? u64(w + 0x168) : 0;
    const std::uint64_t p = t ? u64(t + 0x10) : 0;
    if (!p || vt(p) != off::kFirePropsVt) return false;
    const float exponent = f32(p + 0x2C4);
    out.p = exponent > 0.1f && exponent < 10.f ? exponent : 2.f;
    std::uint8_t angle = 0;
    read(p + 0x2B1, &angle, 1);
    out.angle = angle != 0;
    if (out.angle) {
        float yawPitch[2] = {};
        read(p + 0x2B4, yawPitch, sizeof(yawPitch));
        out.s = std::max(yawPitch[0], yawPitch[1]);
        if (f32(w + 0xBE8) >= 0.75f) out.s *= f32(p + 0x2C8);  // aimed (same test as the game)
    } else {
        out.s = f32(w + 0xB10);
    }
    if (!(out.s >= 0.f && out.s < 90.f)) out.s = 0.f;
    return true;
}

int inventoryItems(std::uintptr_t playerEntity, InventoryItem* out, int max) {
    const std::uintptr_t pouch = component(playerEntity, off::kItemPouchVt);
    if (!pouch) return 0;
    // In-order walk with an explicit stack; the tree has a few hundred nodes.
    std::uintptr_t stack[64];
    int depth = 0, n = 0, visited = 0;
    std::uintptr_t node = u64(pouch + 0x208);
    while ((node || depth) && visited < 4096) {
        while (node && depth < 64) {
            stack[depth++] = node;
            node = u64(node);
        }
        if (!depth) break;
        node = stack[--depth];
        ++visited;
        const std::uintptr_t item = u64(node + 0x28);
        if (n < max && isStackable(item)) {
            InventoryItem& it = out[n];
            it = {};
            it.item = item;
            it.id = u64(node + 0x20);
            it.maxStack = u32(item + 0x68);
            const std::uint64_t desc = u64(item + 0x28);
            const std::uint64_t text = desc ? u64(desc + 0x10) : 0;
            const std::uint32_t len = text ? u32(text) : 0;
            if (len > 0 && len < sizeof(it.name)) read(text + 0xC, it.name, len);
            if (it.name[0]) ++n;
        }
        node = u64(node + 8);
    }
    return n;
}

bool itemCount(std::uintptr_t item, std::uint32_t& count) {
    if (g_itemsFaulted || !isStackable(item)) return false;
    alignas(16) std::uint32_t out[4] = {};
    if (!callItemCount(g_base + off::kItemCountGet, item + 0x58, out)) {
        g_itemsFaulted = true;
        return false;
    }
    count = out[0];
    return true;
}

int changeItemCount(std::uintptr_t item, int delta) {
    if (g_itemsFaulted || !delta || !isStackable(item)) return 0;
    // The game's own add / remove, called through the item's vtable like the pickups do.
    const std::uintptr_t slot = delta > 0 ? 0x208 : 0x210;
    const std::uintptr_t fn = u64(u64(item) + slot);
    if (fn != g_base + (delta > 0 ? off::kItemAdd : off::kItemRemove)) return 0;
    std::uint32_t done = 0;
    if (!callItemChange(fn, item, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(delta > 0 ? delta : -delta), done)) {
        g_itemsFaulted = true;
        return 0;
    }
    return delta > 0 ? static_cast<int>(done) : -static_cast<int>(done);
}

bool addViewAngles(std::uintptr_t playerEntity, float dYaw, float dPitch) {
    const std::uintptr_t pawn = component(playerEntity, off::kPawnComponentVt);
    if (!pawn) return false;
    const std::uintptr_t view = pawn + off::kViewAngles;
    float a[3];  // pitch, roll, yaw
    if (!read(view + 0x10, a, sizeof(a))) return false;
    for (float x : a) {
        if (!std::isfinite(x) || std::fabs(x) > 7.f) return false;
    }
    constexpr float pi = 3.14159265f;
    a[0] = std::clamp(a[0] + dPitch, -1.5f, 1.5f);
    a[2] = std::remainder(a[2] + dYaw, 2.f * pi);
    return write(view + 0x10, &a[0], 4) && write(view + 0x18, &a[2], 4);
}

bool viewAngles(std::uintptr_t playerEntity, float& yaw, float& pitch) {
    const std::uintptr_t pawn = component(playerEntity, off::kPawnComponentVt);
    float a[3];  // pitch, roll, yaw
    if (!pawn || !read(pawn + off::kViewAngles + 0x10, a, sizeof(a))) return false;
    pitch = a[0];
    yaw = a[2];
    return std::isfinite(pitch) && std::isfinite(yaw);
}

bool fireDirection(std::uintptr_t playerEntity, Vec3& origin, Vec3& direction) {
    static bool faulted = false;  // once it faulted, never again in this session
    if (faulted) return false;
    const std::uintptr_t w = heldWeaponObject(playerEntity);
    const std::uint64_t fire = w ? u64(w + 0x198) : 0;
    const std::uintptr_t shooter = component(playerEntity, off::kPawnComponentVt);
    // The fire component points back to the weapon (+0x50) and its fire properties (+0x330).
    if (!fire || !shooter || u64(fire + 0x50) != w || !u64(fire + 0x330)) return false;
    alignas(16) float o[8] = {}, d[8] = {};
    if (!callFireDirection(g_base + off::kFireOriginDirection, fire, shooter, o, d)) {
        faulted = true;
        return false;
    }
    origin = {o[0], o[1], o[2]};
    direction = {d[0], d[1], d[2]};
    const float n = direction.length();
    if (!(n > 0.5f && n < 1.5f)) return false;
    direction = direction * (1.f / n);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace fc6::game

```

`src/internal/game.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// In-process reads of Far Cry 6 game state (offline single-player only). C++ port of
// tools/targets.py and tools/skeleton.py; offsets and their meaning are in docs/reversing.md.
// Every read goes through read(), which survives freed or unmapped memory (an entity can be
// destroyed while we read it): a failed read gives zeros / false, never a crash.

#include "common/math.hpp"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <unordered_map>

namespace fc6::game {

// Engine offsets (Steam build 11359732), relative to FC_m64d3d12.dll.
namespace off {
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kAimAssistStatic = 0x6E3D2D0;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kAimAssistVt = 0x5BB5788;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kPointVt = 0x5BB2638;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kPhysComponentVt = 0x5933868;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kFactionComponentVt = 0x5BF9C58;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kDeathComponentVt = 0x59336E8;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kDamageComponentVt = 0x5A74D68;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kHealthObjectVt = 0x5A49C68;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kAimComponentVt = 0x5BB55E8;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kFirePropsVt = 0x5C59958;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kSkeletonVt = 0x5951DC8;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kBodyComponentVt = 0x595F6E8;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kPawnComponentVt = 0x5D53D88;  // the shooter: +0x2ae8 -> pawn data
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kNpcPawnComponentVt = 0x5FCD820;  // an NPC's: +0x2ae8 -> pawn data
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kAiAimData = 0xD40;               // in the pawn data (+0x23d1dd0)
// Inventory (docs/reversing.md "Inventory"): the item pouch component and the stackable items.
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kItemPouchVt = 0x5CCC300;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kStackableVt = 0x5CC8600;       // CInventoryItemStackable
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kStackableIfaceVt = 0x5CC8828;  // its reflection interface, at +8
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kItemCountGet = 0x366CF0;       // (item + 0x58, &count)
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kItemAdd = 0x2B0C2F0;           // vtable +0x208 (item, n): added
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kItemRemove = 0x2B65300;        // vtable +0x210 (item, n): removed
// The view integrator: every frame on the game's thread, the one that runs the inventory too
// (gamethread.hpp hooks it to call the item functions there).
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kGameThreadHook = 0x228DF90;
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kFireOriginDirection = 0x2848C40;  // weapon fire component vtable +0x340
inline constexpr std::uintptr_t kViewAngles = 0x2F20;  // in the pawn component: the view accumulator
struct Chain {
    std::uintptr_t root, o1, o2;
};
inline constexpr Chain kCameraChains[] = {{0x6A66EA0, 0x2F8, 0x260}, {0x6A678E8, 0x348, 0x260}, {0x6B1B1B0, 0x38, 0x350}};
}  // namespace off

inline constexpr int kAttitudeHostile = 2;
inline constexpr int kAttitudeAlly = 4;

void init(std::uintptr_t engineBase, std::size_t engineSize);
std::uintptr_t engine();

// Copies n bytes from addr; false (and dst zeroed) if any of it is unreadable.
bool read(std::uintptr_t addr, void* dst, std::size_t n);
// This thread's reads so far, and the VirtualQuery calls they needed (pages re-checked each second).
struct ReadStats {
    std::uint64_t reads = 0, queries = 0;
};
ReadStats readStats();

// Writes n bytes to addr (game memory: the view angles only); false if it isn't writable.
bool write(std::uintptr_t addr, const void* src, std::size_t n);

template <class T>
T rd(std::uintptr_t addr) {
    T v{};
    read(addr, &v, sizeof(v));
    return v;
}

inline std::uint64_t u64(std::uintptr_t a) { return rd<std::uint64_t>(a); }
inline std::uint32_t u32(std::uintptr_t a) { return rd<std::uint32_t>(a); }
inline float f32(std::uintptr_t a) { return rd<float>(a); }

// Engine-relative vtable offset of the object at addr, 0 if it has none in the engine.
std::uintptr_t vt(std::uintptr_t addr);

inline float dist(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) { return (a - b).length(); }

// R44 world transform: right, forward, up, position (docs/reversing.md "World conventions").
struct Transform {
    Vec3 right, fwd, up, pos;
};
bool transform(std::uintptr_t addr, Transform& out);

struct Camera {
    Transform m;  // eye = m.pos, view direction = m.fwd
    float sx = 0, sy = 0;  // projection x / y scale (grow when aiming down sights)
};
bool camera(Camera& out);

inline constexpr int kNameLen = 128;

struct Character {
    std::uintptr_t entity = 0;
    Vec3 point;  // the aiming point (~0.85 m above the feet)
    char name[kNameLen] = {};  // archetype, e.g. "NPC:FCX_NPC:Base.NPC.Assaulter.Cuban_Hostile"
};
// The game's aim-assist list (player included). Returns the number written.
int characters(Character* out, int max);

void archetype(std::uintptr_t entity, char (&name)[kNameLen]);
std::uintptr_t component(std::uintptr_t entity, std::uintptr_t vtable);
int attitude(std::uintptr_t entity);  // -1 without a faction component
int deathState(std::uintptr_t entity);
bool health(std::uintptr_t entity, float& current, float& maximum);
bool isDead(std::uintptr_t entity);
bool isHostile(std::uintptr_t entity, const char* name);

std::uintptr_t player();  // the player's own entity, 0 if not found

// The NPC's AI target: the entity its weapon aims at (the NPC path of the fire direction,
// +0x28485f0, reads the aim data A = [pawn + 0x2ae8] + 0xd40 from +0x23d1dd0: [A + 0xe8] is the
// target's entity handle, A + 0x34 the aim point, [A + 0xf0] / [A + 0xf4] a bone and a time to
// track it). Seen switching between the player, allies and animals during fights; 0 when the NPC
// isn't fighting anyone.
std::uintptr_t aiTarget(std::uintptr_t npc);
std::uintptr_t heldWeaponObject(std::uintptr_t player);

// The held weapon's ballistics as the game computes them when firing (+0x284ae60, +0x2879da0):
//   ammo     = [[weapon + 0x198] + 0xf98] (id) -> index in SupportedAmmoList (fire props + 0x6f0,
//              entries of 0xd8, id at +0x10), else iDefaultAmmoIndex (+0x700)   (+0x27d71e0)
//   speed    = fBulletSpeed (+0x328)         x [weapon + 0xecc] x entry[+0x3c]    (+0x2955120)
//   gravity  = -fBulletGravity (+0x32c)      x [weapon + 0xed0] x entry[+0x38]    (+0x2910f20)
//   drop     = fBulletDropDistance (+0x330)  x [weapon + 0xed4]
//   range    = [weapon + 0xec4]
// [weapon + 0xec4..0xed4] are the attachments' modifiers (fields +0x2b0..0x2c0 of the reflected
// modifier struct, +0x15ceb37); entry[+0x3c] / [+0x38] are the ammo's (FMJ, shown in game as the
// armour-piercing round, speed x8; hollow point gravity x1.1).
struct Weapon {
    char name[kNameLen] = {};  // "weapons:Weapons:Rifles.FAL"
    char ammo[64] = {};        // "Rifle_SpecialAmmo_FullMetalJacket"
    bool stats = false;        // the fire properties below were read
    bool physics = false;      // bUseBulletPhysic
    float speed = 0, gravity = 0, dropDistance = 0, rpm = 0;  // game data, before mods
    float speedMul = 1, gravityMul = 1, dropMul = 1;          // attachments
    float ammoSpeed = 1, ammoGravity = 1;                     // loaded ammo
    float range = 0;                                          // m, 0 if unknown
    float hipSpread[2] = {}, aimSpread[2] = {};               // BulletSpread{,_ironSight}: min, max (deg)
    float effectiveSpeed() const { return speed * speedMul * ammoSpeed; }
    float effectiveGravity() const { return gravity * gravityMul * ammoGravity; }
    float effectiveDrop() const { return dropDistance * dropMul; }
};
bool heldWeapon(std::uintptr_t player, Weapon& out);

// The held weapon's bullet spread right now. The fire setup (+0x284ae60) turns the fire direction
// by +0x27a57b0: an angle of (s / 2) x max(u1, u2) ^ p degrees in a random direction (u1, u2 from
// the weapon's own subtractive generator at fire component + 0x358, the direction from the global
// one), with s = CurrentBulletSpread [weapon + 0xb10] (degrees, written each frame by +0x286b6a0
// between [weapon + 0xb18] and [weapon + 0xb14] from the stance, the movement, the aim blend
// [weapon + 0xbe8] and the shots in a row) and p = [fire props + 0x2c4] (2: the angle is uniform
// in [0, s / 2]). Weapons with bUseAngleSpread (fire props + 0x2b1) use fAngleYaw / Pitch
// BulletSpread (+0x2b4 / +0x2b8, x fAngleSpreadIronsightFactor +0x2c8 when aimed) instead.
struct Spread {
    float s = 0, p = 2;  // degrees (full cone), exponent
    bool angle = false;  // bUseAngleSpread: s is the larger of the yaw / pitch spreads
    // Fraction of the shots that land within radius (m) of the aim point at dist (m).
    float hitChance(float radius, float dist) const;
    float median() const;  // radians: half the shots land closer to the aim point
};
bool heldSpread(std::uintptr_t player, Spread& out);

// Where a bullet fired now would start and go, as the game computes it when the player fires
// (+0x2848c40 = [weapon + 0x198]->vtable[0x340] with the player's pawn component, called by the
// fire code at +0x2883683 before +0x284ae60): the camera forward plus the weapon's sway and
// recoil. Calls the game's own function (no state of ours is passed in); false if it can't or
// it faulted.
bool fireDirection(std::uintptr_t player, Vec3& origin, Vec3& direction);

// The player's view angle accumulator: pawn component (+0x5d53d88) + 0x2f20. The mouse look is
// integrated into it each frame by +0x228df90 (called from the look controller +0x24d0580 with
// the input actions): +0x10 pitch, +0x18 yaw relative to the body (wrapped to +-pi), radians, same
// signs as viewAngles(). Writing it turns the camera at once (unlike the per-frame outputs).
// Adds the given angles; false if the accumulator wasn't found or doesn't look right.
bool addViewAngles(std::uintptr_t player, float dYaw, float dPitch);
bool viewAngles(std::uintptr_t player, float& yaw, float& pitch);  // reads it

std::uint32_t crc32(const char* text);  // bone names are looked up by their crc32

// The player's stackable items: resources, materials, Moneda, consumable keys. The pouch component
// (+0x5ccc300) keeps every item in a tree: root [pouch + 0x208], nodes {left +0, right +8, item
// id +0x20, item +0x28}. A CInventoryItemStackable has its descriptor at +0x28 (item id at +0x20,
// name string at [+0x10]: "LootItem/Economic/Metal") and its max stack at +0x68.
// The count is protected by the game: it is only read and changed through the game's own
// functions, the ones the pickups use (the getter +0x366cf0 on item + 0x58, the virtual add
// +0x208 and remove +0x210, which clamp to [0, max stack]); nothing is decoded here.
// itemCount and changeItemCount must run on the game's own thread (gamethread.hpp): called from
// another thread, the getter took ~50 ms per item and the game crashed soon after.
struct InventoryItem {
    std::uintptr_t item = 0;
    std::uint64_t id = 0;
    char name[96] = {};  // "Tetra/CraftingMaterials/TradeMaterials/LookMaterial"
    std::uint32_t maxStack = 0;
};
int inventoryItems(std::uintptr_t player, InventoryItem* out, int max);  // sorted by item id
bool itemCount(std::uintptr_t item, std::uint32_t& count);
// Adds (delta > 0) or removes (delta < 0) items through the game; returns the change applied,
// 0 when it can't (not a stackable any more, or a call faulted: then never again this session).
int changeItemCount(std::uintptr_t item, int delta);

enum class PointSource { kSkeleton, kRagdoll, kEstimate };

struct AimPoints {
    int count = 0;
    PointSource source = PointSource::kEstimate;
    const char* label[4] = {};  // "head", "neck", "chest", "pelvis"
    Vec3 p[4];
};

// The character's animated skeleton, with the caches of tools/skeleton.py (bone tables and the
// liveness of each pose). Not thread safe: one instance per thread.
class Skeletons {
public:
    // Head / neck / chest / pelvis from the skeleton, else the ragdoll, else fixed heights.
    // reason (optional): what fromSkeleton() said.
    AimPoints aimPoints(std::uintptr_t entity, const Vec3& fallback, double now, const char** reason = nullptr);
    // Skeleton only: false with why in reason (for the overlay and the port check).
    bool fromSkeleton(std::uintptr_t entity, double now, AimPoints& out, const char** reason);
    // World positions of the bones with these crc32 names in the skeleton's current pose (no
    // checks: call fromSkeleton first). have[i] false for a bone the model lacks. Returns the
    // model's bone count, 0 if the pose can't be read.
    int namedBones(std::uintptr_t entity, const std::uint32_t* crcs, int n, Vec3* out, bool* have);

private:
    struct Live {
        std::uint32_t digest;
        double changed, first;
    };
    struct Bones {
        int head = -1, headEnd = -1, neck = -1, spine2 = -1, hips = -1;
    };
    std::unordered_map<std::uintptr_t, Bones> tables_;  // bone table address -> indices
    std::unordered_map<std::uintptr_t, Live> live_;     // pose address -> liveness
    std::unordered_map<std::uintptr_t, std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, int>> names_;  // table -> crc -> index
    const Bones& bones(std::uintptr_t table, std::uint32_t count);
};

}  // namespace fc6::game
```

`src/internal/gamethread.cpp`:

```cpp
// Work on the game's thread, see gamethread.hpp.

#include "internal/gamethread.hpp"

#include <windows.h>
#include <tlhelp32.h>

#include <atomic>
#include <mutex>

namespace fc6::gamethread {
namespace {

enum State : LONG { kIdle, kArmed, kRunning, kDone };

std::uintptr_t g_base = 0;
PVOID g_veh = nullptr;
HANDLE g_done = nullptr;
volatile LONG g_state = kIdle;
std::atomic<std::uintptr_t> g_hook{0};  // last armed address, kept after the job
Job g_job = nullptr;
void* g_arg = nullptr;
unsigned g_threadId = 0;
double g_ms = 0;
std::mutex g_runMutex;  // one job at a time (the menu's worker and tools/inventory.py)

// Dr3 (execute: RW3 = LEN3 = 00) on every thread but ours.
void armOnAllThreads(std::uintptr_t addr, bool enable) {
    constexpr std::uint64_t mask = (1ull << 6) | (0xFull << 28);
    const DWORD self = GetCurrentThreadId(), pid = GetCurrentProcessId();
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    THREADENTRY32 te{sizeof(te)};
    for (BOOL ok = Thread32First(snap, &te); ok; ok = Thread32Next(snap, &te)) {
        if (te.th32OwnerProcessID != pid || te.th32ThreadID == self) continue;
        HANDLE t = OpenThread(THREAD_GET_CONTEXT | THREAD_SET_CONTEXT | THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME, FALSE, te.th32ThreadID);
        if (!t) continue;
        if (SuspendThread(t) != static_cast<DWORD>(-1)) {
            CONTEXT ctx{};
            ctx.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS;
            if (GetThreadContext(t, &ctx)) {
                if (enable) {
                    ctx.Dr3 = addr;
                    ctx.Dr7 = (ctx.Dr7 & ~mask) | (1ull << 6);
                    SetThreadContext(t, &ctx);
                } else if (ctx.Dr3 == addr) {
                    ctx.Dr3 = 0;
                    ctx.Dr7 &= ~mask;
                    SetThreadContext(t, &ctx);
                }
            }
            ResumeThread(t);
        }
        CloseHandle(t);
    }
    CloseHandle(snap);
}

double seconds() {
    LARGE_INTEGER f, n;
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f);
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&n);
    return static_cast<double>(n.QuadPart) / static_cast<double>(f.QuadPart);
}

LONG CALLBACK onHook(EXCEPTION_POINTERS* info) {
    if (info->ExceptionRecord->ExceptionCode != EXCEPTION_SINGLE_STEP) return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
    const auto addr = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(info->ExceptionRecord->ExceptionAddress);
    const std::uintptr_t hook = g_hook.load();
    if (!hook || addr != hook) return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
    CONTEXT* ctx = info->ContextRecord;
    // One pass is enough on this thread: disarm here, then let the function run.
    ctx->Dr3 = 0;
    ctx->Dr7 &= ~((1ull << 6) | (0xFull << 28));
    ctx->Dr6 &= ~0xFull;
    ctx->EFlags |= 0x10000;  // RF
    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&g_state, kRunning, kArmed) == kArmed) {
        const double t0 = seconds();
        g_job(g_arg);
        g_ms = (seconds() - t0) * 1000.0;
        g_threadId = GetCurrentThreadId();
        InterlockedExchange(&g_state, kDone);
        SetEvent(g_done);
    }
    return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_EXECUTION;
}

}  // namespace

void start(std::uintptr_t engineBase) {
    g_base = engineBase;
    if (!g_done) g_done = CreateEventW(nullptr, TRUE, FALSE, nullptr);
    if (!g_veh) g_veh = AddVectoredExceptionHandler(1, onHook);
}

void stop() {
    // Disarm first; the handler keeps recognising the address meanwhile.
    if (const std::uintptr_t hook = g_hook.load()) armOnAllThreads(hook, false);
    Sleep(50);  // let an in-flight exception finish before removing the handler
    if (g_veh) RemoveVectoredExceptionHandler(g_veh);
    g_veh = nullptr;
    if (g_done) CloseHandle(g_done);
    g_done = nullptr;
}

bool run(std::uintptr_t hookOffset, Job job, void* arg, unsigned timeoutMs, unsigned& threadId, double& ms) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_runMutex);
    if (!g_veh || !g_done || InterlockedCompareExchange(&g_state, kIdle, kIdle) != kIdle) return false;
    const std::uintptr_t hook = g_base + hookOffset;
    g_job = job;
    g_arg = arg;
    g_threadId = 0;
    g_ms = 0;
    ResetEvent(g_done);
    g_hook = hook;
    InterlockedExchange(&g_state, kArmed);
    armOnAllThreads(hook, true);
    WaitForSingleObject(g_done, timeoutMs);
    // Not started yet: drop it. Started: it must finish (the game thread is inside it).
    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&g_state, kIdle, kArmed) != kArmed) {
        while (InterlockedCompareExchange(&g_state, kDone, kDone) != kDone) Sleep(1);
    }
    armOnAllThreads(hook, false);  // g_hook stays: a thread still in the dispatch is recognised
    const bool done = InterlockedExchange(&g_state, kIdle) == kDone;
    threadId = done ? g_threadId : 0;
    ms = done ? g_ms : 0;
    return done;
}

}  // namespace fc6::gamethread

```

`src/internal/gamethread.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Runs work on the game's own thread, without patching code: a hardware execute breakpoint (debug
// register Dr3) on a function that thread runs every frame. The breakpoint is armed for one job;
// its exception handler runs the job on that thread, at that function's entry, disarms itself and
// lets the function run (RF). For the game functions that must not be called from another thread
// (the inventory's, game.hpp).
//
// Dr3 is shared with the execute watch (watch.py --exec): don't run both at once.

#include <cstdint>

namespace fc6::gamethread {

void start(std::uintptr_t engineBase);  // installs the exception handler
void stop();

using Job = void (*)(void* arg);
// Runs job(arg) at the next pass of the game thread through the hook function (engine offset),
// waiting up to timeoutMs (jobs run one at a time). False on timeout (the job is then dropped,
// never run later).
// threadId: the thread that ran it; ms: the time it took there.
bool run(std::uintptr_t hookOffset, Job job, void* arg, unsigned timeoutMs, unsigned& threadId, double& ms);

}  // namespace fc6::gamethread

```

`src/internal/hud.cpp`:

```cpp
// HUD lines in shared memory, see hud.hpp.

#include "internal/hud.hpp"

#include "internal/config.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

namespace fc6::hud {
namespace {

Shared* g_shared = nullptr;

void format(char (&dst)[kHudText], const char* fmt, va_list args) {
    char buf[kHudText];
    std::vsnprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), fmt, args);
    std::memcpy(dst, buf, sizeof(buf));
}

}  // namespace

void attach(Shared* shared) { g_shared = shared; }

void beat(bool aimOn) {
    if (!g_shared) return;
    g_shared->hudAimOn = aimOn ? 1 : 0;
    g_shared->hudTick = GetTickCount64();
}

void weapon(const char* fmt, ...) {
    if (!g_shared) return;
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    format(g_shared->hudWeapon, fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
}

void target(const char* fmt, ...) {
    if (!g_shared) return;
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    format(g_shared->hudTarget, fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
}

void event(const char* fmt, ...) {
    if (!g_shared) return;
    HudEvent* e = g_shared->hudEvents;
    std::memmove(e, e + 1, sizeof(HudEvent) * (kHudEvents - 1));
    HudEvent& last = e[kHudEvents - 1];
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    format(last.text, fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
    last.tick = GetTickCount64();
    config::log("%s", last.text);
}

}  // namespace fc6::hud
```

`src/internal/hud_view.cpp`:

```cpp
// HUD drawing, see hud_view.hpp.

#include "internal/hud_view.hpp"

#include "internal/state.hpp"

#include <windows.h>
#include <dwrite.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <numbers>

namespace fc6::overlay {
namespace {

// A HUD line from the aimbot ("FAL FullMetalJacket  4920 m/s  g 15.0  ..."): its first part as the
// card's title, the others below it, three per line, joined by dots.
struct Card {
    char title[kHudText] = {};
    char lines[3][kHudText] = {};
    int count = 0;
};

Card split(const char* text) {
    Card c;
    int part = 0, inLine = 0;
    const char* p = text;
    while (*p) {
        const char* end = std::strstr(p, "  ");
        const std::size_t n = end ? static_cast<std::size_t>(end - p) : std::strlen(p);
        if (n > 0) {
            if (part == 0) {
                std::snprintf(c.title, sizeof(c.title), "%.*s", static_cast<int>(n), p);
            } else if (c.count < 3 || inLine < 3) {
                if (inLine == 3) inLine = 0;
                if (inLine == 0) ++c.count;
                char* line = c.lines[c.count - 1];
                const std::size_t len = std::strlen(line);
                std::snprintf(line + len, kHudText - len, "%s%.*s", inLine ? "  ·  " : "", static_cast<int>(n), p);
                ++inLine;
            }
            ++part;
        }
        if (!end) break;
        p = end;
        while (*p == ' ') ++p;
    }
    return c;
}

// Returns the card's height.
float drawCard(Painter& paint, float x, float y, const char* label, const char* text, std::uint32_t accent) {
    const Card c = split(text);
    const float labelW = paint.measure(label, Font::kTiny);
    float w = labelW + 12 + paint.measure(c.title, Font::kHud);
    for (int i = 0; i < c.count; ++i) w = std::max(w, paint.measure(c.lines[i], Font::kTiny));
    const float h = 34.f + 17.f * c.count;
    paint.panel(x, y, w + 28, h, 8, theme::kPanel, 0.72f);
    paint.fill(x, y + 6, 3, h - 12, accent);
    paint.text(x + 14, y + 10, label, accent, Font::kTiny, false, 1.f, false);
    paint.text(x + 14 + labelW + 12, y + 7, c.title, theme::kText, Font::kHud, false, 1.f, false);
    for (int i = 0; i < c.count; ++i) {
        paint.text(x + 14, y + 30 + 17.f * i, c.lines[i], theme::kMuted, Font::kTiny, false, 1.f, false);
    }
    return h;
}

}  // namespace

void drawHud(Painter& paint, const Shared& s, float fps, float ms, float x) {
    float y = paint.height * 0.3f;
    float px = x;
    px += paint.pill(px, y, "AIM  F6", state::aimOn ? theme::kOn : theme::kOff) + 6;
    px += paint.pill(px, y, "ESP  F7", state::espOn ? theme::kOn : theme::kOff) + 10;
    char line[48];
    std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%.0f fps   %.1f ms   menu: Inser", fps, ms);
    paint.text(px, y + 3, line, theme::kDim, Font::kTiny);
    y += 30;
    if (s.hudWeapon[0]) y += drawCard(paint, x, y, "ARME", s.hudWeapon, theme::kAccent) + 6;
    if (s.hudTarget[0]) y += drawCard(paint, x, y, "CIBLE", s.hudTarget, theme::kOff) + 6;
    // Events: the newest first, fading out over their last 5 of 20 s.
    const std::uint64_t now = GetTickCount64();
    for (int i = kHudEvents - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        const HudEvent& e = s.hudEvents[i];
        if (!e.tick || now - e.tick > 20000) continue;
        const float alpha = std::clamp((20000.f - static_cast<float>(now - e.tick)) / 5000.f, 0.f, 1.f);
        const std::uint32_t c = std::strstr(e.text, " HIT") ? theme::kOn : theme::kWarn;
        const float w = paint.measure(e.text, Font::kTiny);
        paint.panel(x, y, w + 26, 22, 6, theme::kPanel, 0.6f * alpha);
        paint.disc(x + 9, y + 11, 3, c, alpha);
        paint.text(x + 17, y + 3, e.text, c, Font::kTiny, false, alpha, false);
        y += 26;
    }
}

// The aimbot's cone around the crosshair: an angle a from the view lands tan(a) x sx x width / 2
// pixels from the centre (esp.hpp Projector). Orange while a target is locked.
void drawCone(Painter& paint, const game::Camera& cam, float fovDegrees, bool locked) {
    const float r = std::tan(fovDegrees * std::numbers::pi_v<float> / 180.f) * cam.sx * paint.width / 2;
    if (!(r > 4.f) || r > paint.width) return;
    paint.circle(paint.width / 2, paint.height / 2, r, locked ? theme::kAccent : 0xFFFFFF, 1.5f, locked ? 0.6f : 0.22f);
}

bool createFonts(IDWriteFactory* dwrite, IDWriteTextFormat** out) {
    // In the order of Font: HUD, small (ESP), menu, title, tiny.
    const float sizes[] = {15.f, 12.f, 15.f, 22.f, 12.f};
    const DWRITE_FONT_WEIGHT weights[] = {DWRITE_FONT_WEIGHT_SEMI_BOLD, DWRITE_FONT_WEIGHT_SEMI_BOLD,
                                          DWRITE_FONT_WEIGHT_NORMAL, DWRITE_FONT_WEIGHT_BOLD, DWRITE_FONT_WEIGHT_NORMAL};
    for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(Font::kCount); ++i) {
        if (FAILED(dwrite->CreateTextFormat(L"Segoe UI", nullptr, weights[i], DWRITE_FONT_STYLE_NORMAL,
                                            DWRITE_FONT_STRETCH_NORMAL, sizes[i], L"fr-fr", &out[i]))) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

}  // namespace fc6::overlay
```
