`src/internal/inventory.cpp`:

```cpp
// Inventory worker, see inventory.hpp.

#include "internal/inventory.hpp"

#include "internal/gamethread.hpp"
#include "internal/hud.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <utility>

namespace fc6::overlay {
namespace {

constexpr unsigned kTimeoutMs = 1500;  // the hook runs every frame unless the game is paused
constexpr double kRefresh = 5.0;
constexpr int kMaxItems = 128;

double seconds() {
    LARGE_INTEGER f, n;
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f);
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&n);
    return static_cast<double>(n.QuadPart) / static_cast<double>(f.QuadPart);
}

// Jobs run on the game's thread.
struct CountsJob {
    InventoryRow* rows;
    std::size_t n;
};

void countsJob(void* arg) {
    auto* j = static_cast<CountsJob*>(arg);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < j->n; ++i) j->rows[i].haveCount = game::itemCount(j->rows[i].item.item, j->rows[i].count);
}

struct ChangeJob {
    std::uintptr_t item;
    int delta;
    int done;
    std::uint32_t count;
    bool ok;
};

void changeJob(void* arg) {
    auto* j = static_cast<ChangeJob*>(arg);
    j->done = game::changeItemCount(j->item, j->delta);
    j->ok = game::itemCount(j->item, j->count);
}

}  // namespace

void InventoryWorker::touch(double now) {
    touched_ = now;
    if (!thread_.joinable()) {
        stop_ = false;
        thread_ = std::thread(&InventoryWorker::run, this);
    }
}

void InventoryWorker::request(std::uintptr_t item, int delta, const char* label) {
    Change c{item, delta, {}};
    strncpy_s(c.label, label, _TRUNCATE);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    pending_.push_back(c);
    snap_.busy = true;
}

InventorySnapshot InventoryWorker::snapshot() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return snap_;
}

void InventoryWorker::stop() {
    stop_ = true;
    if (thread_.joinable()) thread_.join();
}

void InventoryWorker::run() {
    double nextRefresh = 0;
    bool shown = false;
    while (!stop_) {
        const double now = seconds();
        if (now - touched_.load() > 0.5) {  // the tab isn't shown
            shown = false;
            Sleep(50);
            continue;
        }
        if (!shown) {
            shown = true;
            nextRefresh = 0;
        }
        Change c{};
        bool have = false;
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
            if (!pending_.empty()) {
                c = pending_.front();
                pending_.erase(pending_.begin());
                have = true;
            }
        }
        if (have) {
            apply(c);
        } else if (now >= nextRefresh) {
            refresh();
            nextRefresh = seconds() + kRefresh;
        } else {
            Sleep(20);
        }
    }
}

// The list from memory here, the counts on the game's thread (one job for all of them).
void InventoryWorker::refresh() {
    static game::InventoryItem list[kMaxItems];
    const std::uintptr_t player = game::player();
    const int n = player ? game::inventoryItems(player, list, kMaxItems) : 0;
    std::vector<InventoryRow> rows;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (list[i].maxStack > 1) rows.push_back({list[i], 0, false});  // unlock tokens (max 1) left alone
    }
    CountsJob job{rows.data(), rows.size()};
    unsigned thread = 0;
    double ms = 0;
    const bool ran = !rows.empty() && gamethread::run(game::off::kGameThreadHook, countsJob, &job, kTimeoutMs, thread, ms);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (ran || snap_.rows.empty()) {
        snap_.rows = std::move(rows);
        ++snap_.version;
    }
    if (!ran && !snap_.rows.empty()) {
        std::snprintf(snap_.status, sizeof(snap_.status), "Quantités non lues : le jeu ne tourne pas (pause ?).");
    }
}

void InventoryWorker::apply(const Change& c) {
    ChangeJob job{c.item, c.delta, 0, 0, false};
    unsigned thread = 0;
    double ms = 0;
    const bool ran = gamethread::run(game::off::kGameThreadHook, changeJob, &job, kTimeoutMs, thread, ms);
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        if (!ran) {
            std::snprintf(snap_.status, sizeof(snap_.status), "%s : pas appliqué, le jeu ne tourne pas (pause ?).",
                          c.label);
        } else {
            for (InventoryRow& r : snap_.rows) {
                if (r.item.item == c.item) {
                    r.count = job.count;
                    r.haveCount = job.ok;
                }
            }
            ++snap_.version;
            if (job.done == 0) {
                std::snprintf(snap_.status, sizeof(snap_.status), "%s : %s", c.label,
                              c.delta > 0 ? "déjà au maximum." : "déjà à zéro.");
            } else {
                std::snprintf(snap_.status, sizeof(snap_.status), "%s %+d  →  %u", c.label, job.done, job.count);
            }
        }
        snap_.busy = !pending_.empty();
    }
    if (ran && job.done != 0) hud::event("inventaire : %s %+d -> %u", c.label, job.done, job.count);
}

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/inventory.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Inventory worker of the overlay menu: a thread of its own lists the player's stackable items
// (memory reads, game::inventoryItems) and has their counts read and changed by the game's own
// functions on the game's thread (gamethread.hpp, hooked on the view integrator). The overlay
// never calls the game: it draws the last snapshot and posts changes. Counts are read when the
// tab opens, every 5 s while it's shown, and for an item right after it changed (each read costs
// the game's thread ~2 ms).

#include "internal/game.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

namespace fc6::overlay {

struct InventoryRow {
    game::InventoryItem item;
    std::uint32_t count = 0;
    bool haveCount = false;
};

struct InventorySnapshot {
    std::vector<InventoryRow> rows;
    std::uint32_t version = 0;  // changes when rows change
    bool busy = false;          // a change is waiting or running
    char status[128] = {};      // last change, or why it didn't happen
};

class InventoryWorker {
public:
    ~InventoryWorker() { stop(); }
    void touch(double now);  // the inventory tab is shown (call every frame); starts the thread
    void request(std::uintptr_t item, int delta, const char* label);
    InventorySnapshot snapshot();
    void stop();

private:
    struct Change {
        std::uintptr_t item;
        int delta;
        char label[48];
    };
    void run();
    void refresh();
    void apply(const Change& c);

    std::thread thread_;
    std::atomic<bool> stop_{false};
    std::atomic<double> touched_{0};
    std::mutex mutex_;
    InventorySnapshot snap_;
    std::vector<Change> pending_;
};

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/menu.cpp`:

```cpp
// Settings menu, see menu.hpp.

#include "internal/menu.hpp"

#include "internal/config.hpp"
#include "internal/state.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

namespace fc6::overlay {
namespace {

enum class Kind { kHeader, kToggle, kNumber, kChoice };

struct Choice {
    float value;
    const char* label;
};

constexpr Choice kKeys[] = {{2, "clic droit"}, {5, "souris 4"}, {6, "souris 5"}, {0xA4, "Alt gauche"},
                            {0x14, "Verr. maj"}};
constexpr Choice kAimAt[] = {{0, "tête, torse si dispersion"}, {1, "toujours la tête"}};
constexpr Choice kOrder[] = {{1, "menaces d'abord"}, {0, "plus proche du réticule"}};

struct Item {
    const char* label;
    const char* section;
    const char* key;
    Kind kind;
    float fallback;
    const char* help = "";
    float min = 0, max = 0, step = 0;
    const char* format = "%.0f";
    const Choice* choices = nullptr;
    int nChoices = 0;
    std::atomic<bool>* live = nullptr;  // switch the threads read directly
};

Item header(const char* title) { return {title, "", "", Kind::kHeader, 0}; }
Item toggle(const char* label, const char* section, const char* key, float fallback, const char* help,
            std::atomic<bool>* live = nullptr) {
    return {label, section, key, Kind::kToggle, fallback, help, 0, 0, 0, "", nullptr, 0, live};
}
Item number(const char* label, const char* section, const char* key, float fallback, const char* help, float min,
            float max, float step, const char* format) {
    return {label, section, key, Kind::kNumber, fallback, help, min, max, step, format};
}
template <int N>
Item choice(const char* label, const char* section, const char* key, float fallback, const char* help,
            const Choice (&choices)[N]) {
    return {label, section, key, Kind::kChoice, fallback, help, 0, 0, 0, "", choices, N};
}

const Item kItems[] = {
    header("AIMBOT"),
    toggle("Aimbot  (F6)", "aim", "enabled", 1, "Active ou coupe l'aimbot.", &state::aimOn),
    choice("Touche de visée", "aim", "key", 2, "Maintenir cette touche pour viser.", kKeys),
    number("Cône de visée", "aim", "fov", 12, "Angle autour du réticule dans lequel une cible est prise.", 2, 45, 1,
           "%.0f°"),
    number("Lissage", "aim", "smooth", 0.35f, "Part de l'écart corrigée à chaque image du jeu (1 = collé à la cible).", 0.05f,
           1, 0.05f, "%.2f"),
    number("Distance max", "aim", "max_dist", 250, "Au-delà, pas de cible (limitée aussi par la portée de l'arme).",
           50, 600, 25, "%.0f m"),
    header("CIBLE"),
    choice("Viser", "aim", "head_only", 0, "Le torse quand moins d'une balle sur deux toucherait la tête.", kAimAt),
    toggle("Rester sur la cible", "aim", "sticky", 1, "Garde la cible jusqu'à sa mort ; relâcher la touche pour changer."),
    choice("Ordre des cibles", "aim", "threats_first", 1,
           "D'abord ceux qui te visent, puis ceux en alerte, puis les autres.", kOrder),
    toggle("Anticiper le mouvement", "aim", "lead", 1, "Vise devant une cible qui court (temps de vol de la balle)."),
    toggle("Suivre le point de visée", "aim", "follow", 1, "Ajoute la vitesse de la cible pour ne pas traîner derrière."),
    header("TECHNIQUE"),
    toggle("Viser avec le canon", "aim", "barrel", 1, "Compense le balancement de l'arme (lunettes)."),
    toggle("Vue directe", "aim", "direct", 1, "Tourne la vue du jeu directement, sans simuler la souris."),
    toggle("Une correction par image", "aim", "frame_sync", 1,
           "Corrige une fois par image du jeu, pas sur une image déjà corrigée."),
    toggle("Laisser le recul au jeu", "aim", "recoil", 1,
           "Après un tir, le jeu ramène la vue lui-même : l'aimbot ne lutte pas contre le recul."),
    toggle("Compensation du zoom", "aim", "zoom_comp", 1, "Mode souris seulement : adapte le gain au zoom."),
    header("AFFICHAGE"),
    toggle("ESP  (F7)", "overlay", "esp", 0, "Squelettes et infos des PNJ vivants.", &state::espOn),
    number("Distance ESP", "overlay", "esp_dist", 300, "Les PNJ plus loin ne sont pas dessinés.", 50, 600, 25,
           "%.0f m"),
    toggle("HUD", "overlay", "hud", 1, "Statut, arme, cible et événements à gauche de l'écran.", &state::hudOn),
    toggle("Cercle du cône", "overlay", "cone", 1, "Montre le cône de visée autour du réticule."),
    number("Images/s de l'overlay", "overlay", "max_fps", 60, "Plafond de l'ESP et du HUD : moins = moins de charge.",
           30, 240, 10, "%.0f"),
};
constexpr int kCount = sizeof(kItems) / sizeof(kItems[0]);

constexpr float kWidth = 560.f, kRowH = 30.f, kHeaderH = 66.f, kFooterH = 58.f, kPad = 24.f;
constexpr float kSlider = 140.f;

float value(const Item& it) {
    if (it.live) return it.live->load() ? 1.f : 0.f;
    return config::getFloat(it.section, it.key, it.fallback);
}

const char* choiceLabel(const Item& it, float v) {
    for (int i = 0; i < it.nChoices; ++i) {
        if (it.choices[i].value == v) return it.choices[i].label;
    }
    return "?";
}

constexpr int kSteps[] = {1, 10, 100, 1000};

// Inventory sections, from the item's path.
const char* section(const char* name) {
    if (std::strncmp(name, "Money/", 6) == 0 || std::strncmp(name, "LootItem/", 9) == 0) return "RESSOURCES";
    if (std::strncmp(name, "Tetra/CraftingMaterials/", 24) == 0) return "MATÉRIAUX";
    if (std::strncmp(name, "Keys/", 5) == 0) return "CLÉS";
    return "AUTRES";
}

// Shown name: the last part of the path, in French when known.
const char* label(const char* name) {
    const char* last = std::strrchr(name, '/');
    last = last ? last + 1 : name;
    struct Known {
        const char *id, *fr;
    };
    static constexpr Known kKnown[] = {{"Money", "Moneda"},
                                       {"Metal", "Métal"},
                                       {"Medicine", "Médicaments"},
                                       {"DamagedAnimalParts", "Viande abîmée"}};
    for (const Known& k : kKnown) {
        if (std::strcmp(last, k.id) == 0) return k.fr;
    }
    return last;
}

}  // namespace

float Menu::right() { return 36.f + kWidth; }

Menu::Menu() {
    while (selected_ < kCount && kItems[selected_].kind == Kind::kHeader) ++selected_;
}

bool Menu::pressed(int vk, double now) {
    const int slot = vk == VK_UP ? 0 : vk == VK_DOWN ? 1 : vk == VK_LEFT ? 2 : 3;
    Repeat& r = repeat_[slot];
    const bool down = (GetAsyncKeyState(vk) & 0x8000) != 0;
    bool hit = false;
    if (down && !r.down) {
        hit = true;
        r.next = now + 0.35;  // held: repeat after a pause
    } else if (down && now >= r.next) {
        hit = true;
        r.next = now + 0.08;
    }
    r.down = down;
    return hit;
}

void Menu::move(int dir) {
    do {
        selected_ = (selected_ + dir + kCount) % kCount;
    } while (kItems[selected_].kind == Kind::kHeader);
}

void Menu::change(int dir) {
    const Item& it = kItems[selected_];
    float v = value(it);
    if (it.kind == Kind::kToggle) {
        v = v != 0 ? 0.f : 1.f;
    } else if (it.kind == Kind::kChoice) {
        int i = 0;
        while (i < it.nChoices && it.choices[i].value != v) ++i;
        i = ((i < it.nChoices ? i : 0) + dir + it.nChoices) % it.nChoices;
        v = it.choices[i].value;
    } else {
        v = std::clamp(std::round((v + dir * it.step) / it.step) * it.step, it.min, it.max);
    }
    if (it.live) it.live->store(v != 0);
    config::setFloat(it.section, it.key, v, it.kind == Kind::kNumber && it.step < 1 ? 2 : 0);
    state::settingsVersion.fetch_add(1);
}

bool Menu::edge(int vk, bool& down) {
    const bool d = (GetAsyncKeyState(vk) & 0x8000) != 0;
    const bool hit = d && !down;
    down = d;
    return hit;
}

void Menu::keys(double now) {
    if (edge(VK_PRIOR, pgUp_) || edge(VK_NEXT, pgDown_)) {
        tab_ = 1 - tab_;
        highlight_ = -1.f;
        if (tab_ == 1) refreshInventory(now);
        return;
    }
    if (tab_ == 1) {
        inventoryKeys(now);
        return;
    }
    if (pressed(VK_UP, now)) move(-1);
    if (pressed(VK_DOWN, now)) move(+1);
    if (pressed(VK_LEFT, now)) change(-1);
    if (pressed(VK_RIGHT, now)) change(+1);
}

// The worker's last snapshot (it does the game calls); rows rebuilt when it changed.
void Menu::refreshInventory(double now) {
    worker_.touch(now);
    InventorySnapshot snap = worker_.snapshot();
    std::snprintf(invStatus_, sizeof(invStatus_), "%s", snap.status);
    if (snap.version == invVersion_) return;
    invVersion_ = snap.version;
    items_ = std::move(snap.rows);
    std::stable_sort(items_.begin(), items_.end(), [](const InventoryRow& a, const InventoryRow& b) {
        const int c = std::strcmp(section(a.item.name), section(b.item.name));
        return c != 0 ? c > 0 : std::strcmp(label(a.item.name), label(b.item.name)) < 0;
    });
    // Rows: the step, then each section with its items.
    invRows_.clear();
    invRows_.push_back({nullptr, -1});
    const char* current = nullptr;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < items_.size(); ++i) {
        const char* s = section(items_[i].item.name);
        if (!current || std::strcmp(s, current) != 0) invRows_.push_back({s, -1});
        current = s;
        invRows_.push_back({nullptr, static_cast<int>(i)});
    }
    invSelected_ = std::clamp(invSelected_, 0, static_cast<int>(invRows_.size()) - 1);
    while (invSelected_ > 0 && invRows_[invSelected_].header) --invSelected_;
}

void Menu::inventoryKeys(double now) {
    refreshInventory(now);
    const int n = static_cast<int>(invRows_.size());
    for (int dir : {-1, +1}) {
        if (!pressed(dir < 0 ? VK_UP : VK_DOWN, now) || n == 0) continue;
        do {
            invSelected_ = (invSelected_ + dir + n) % n;
        } while (invRows_[invSelected_].header);
        showStatus_ = false;
    }
    const int dir = pressed(VK_LEFT, now) ? -1 : pressed(VK_RIGHT, now) ? +1 : 0;
    if (!dir || invSelected_ >= n) return;
    const InvRow& row = invRows_[invSelected_];
    if (row.item < 0) {
        step_ = std::clamp(step_ + dir, 0, static_cast<int>(std::size(kSteps)) - 1);
        return;
    }
    const game::InventoryItem& it = items_[row.item].item;
    worker_.request(it.item, dir * kSteps[step_], label(it.name));
    showStatus_ = true;
}

void Menu::draw(Painter& paint, double now) {
    if (tab_ == 1) refreshInventory(now);
    const int count = tab_ == 0 ? kCount : std::max(1, static_cast<int>(invRows_.size()));
    const int selected = tab_ == 0 ? selected_ : invSelected_;
    const float height = kHeaderH + kRowH * count + kFooterH;
    const float x = right() - kWidth, y = std::max(16.f, (paint.height - height) / 2);
    const float right = x + kWidth - kPad;
    paint.panel(x, y, kWidth, height, 12, theme::kPanel, 0.93f);
    paint.frame(x, y, kWidth, height, 12, theme::kLine, 1.f);

    // Title, tabs and status.
    paint.text(x + kPad, y + 12, "FC6HACK", theme::kAccent, Font::kTitle, false, 1.f, false);
    const float t1 = paint.text(x + kPad, y + 40, "Réglages", tab_ == 0 ? theme::kText : theme::kDim, Font::kTiny,
                                false, 1.f, false);
    paint.text(x + kPad + t1 + 16, y + 40, "Inventaire", tab_ == 1 ? theme::kText : theme::kDim, Font::kTiny, false,
               1.f, false);
    const float ux = tab_ == 0 ? x + kPad : x + kPad + t1 + 16;
    const float uw = tab_ == 0 ? t1 : paint.measure("Inventaire", Font::kTiny);
    paint.fill(ux, y + 56, uw, 2, theme::kAccent);
    const float espX = right - paint.pillWidth("ESP");
    paint.pill(espX, y + 22, "ESP", state::espOn ? theme::kOn : theme::kOff);
    paint.pill(espX - 8 - paint.pillWidth("AIM"), y + 22, "AIM", state::aimOn ? theme::kOn : theme::kOff);
    paint.line(x + 1, y + kHeaderH - 6, x + kWidth - 1, y + kHeaderH - 6, theme::kLine, 1.f);

    // Selection highlight, eased towards the selected row.
    const float rows = y + kHeaderH;
    const float target = rows + kRowH * selected;
    const float dt = lastDraw_ > 0 ? static_cast<float>(std::min(0.1, now - lastDraw_)) : 1.f;
    lastDraw_ = now;
    highlight_ = highlight_ < 0 ? target : highlight_ + (target - highlight_) * std::min(1.f, dt * 18.f);
    paint.panel(x + 8, highlight_ + 1, kWidth - 16, kRowH - 2, 6, theme::kRow, 0.95f);
    paint.fill(x + 8, highlight_ + 6, 3, kRowH - 12, theme::kAccent);

    if (tab_ == 0) {
        drawSettings(paint, x, rows, right);
    } else {
        drawInventory(paint, x, rows, right);
    }
    const float fy = rows + kRowH * count + 4;
    paint.line(x + 1, fy, x + kWidth - 1, fy, theme::kLine, 1.f);
    if (tab_ == 0) {
        paint.text(x + kPad, fy + 10, kItems[selected_].help, theme::kMuted, Font::kTiny, false, 1.f, false);
    } else {
        const InvRow* row = invSelected_ < static_cast<int>(invRows_.size()) ? &invRows_[invSelected_] : nullptr;
        char help[160];
        if (showStatus_ && invStatus_[0]) {
            std::snprintf(help, sizeof(help), "%s", invStatus_);
        } else if (row && row->item >= 0) {
            std::snprintf(help, sizeof(help), "%s  (max %u)", items_[row->item].item.name,
                          items_[row->item].item.maxStack);
        } else {
            std::snprintf(help, sizeof(help), "Quantité ajoutée ou retirée à chaque appui sur ← →.");
        }
        paint.text(x + kPad, fy + 10, help, theme::kMuted, Font::kTiny, false, 1.f, false);
    }
    paint.text(x + kPad, fy + 31, "↑↓  choisir     ←→  modifier     PgUp/PgDn  onglet     Inser  fermer", theme::kDim,
               Font::kTiny, false, 1.f, false);
}

void Menu::drawInventory(Painter& paint, float x, float rows, float right) {
    if (items_.empty()) {
        paint.text(x + kPad + 4, rows + 5 + kRowH, "Aucun objet lu (pas de joueur ou inventaire indisponible).",
                   theme::kDim, Font::kMenu, false, 1.f, false);
    }
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < invRows_.size(); ++i) {
        const InvRow& row = invRows_[i];
        const float ry = rows + kRowH * static_cast<float>(i);
        const bool sel = static_cast<int>(i) == invSelected_;
        const std::uint32_t colour = sel ? theme::kText : theme::kMuted;
        char buf[64];
        if (row.header) {
            const float w = paint.text(x + kPad, ry + 9, row.header, theme::kAccent, Font::kTiny, false, 0.9f, false);
            paint.line(x + kPad + w + 10, ry + 18, right, ry + 18, theme::kLine, 1.f);
        } else if (row.item < 0) {
            paint.text(x + kPad + 4, ry + 5, "Pas", colour, Font::kMenu, false, 1.f, false);
            std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), sel ? "‹  %d  ›" : "%d", kSteps[step_]);
            paint.text(right - paint.measure(buf, Font::kMenu), ry + 5, buf, colour, Font::kMenu, false, 1.f, false);
        } else {
            const InventoryRow& it = items_[row.item];
            paint.text(x + kPad + 4, ry + 5, label(it.item.name), colour, Font::kMenu, false, 1.f, false);
            if (it.haveCount) {
                std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), sel ? "‹  %u  ›" : "%u", it.count);
            } else {
                std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "?");
            }
            paint.text(right - paint.measure(buf, Font::kMenu), ry + 5, buf, colour, Font::kMenu, false, 1.f, false);
        }
    }
}

void Menu::drawSettings(Painter& paint, float x, float rows, float right) {
    for (int i = 0; i < kCount; ++i) {
        const Item& it = kItems[i];
        const float ry = rows + kRowH * i;
        if (it.kind == Kind::kHeader) {
            const float w = paint.text(x + kPad, ry + 9, it.label, theme::kAccent, Font::kTiny, false, 0.9f, false);
            paint.line(x + kPad + w + 10, ry + 18, right, ry + 18, theme::kLine, 1.f);
            continue;
        }
        const bool sel = i == selected_;
        const float v = value(it);
        paint.text(x + kPad + 4, ry + 5, it.label, sel ? theme::kText : theme::kMuted, Font::kMenu, false, 1.f, false);
        const float mid = ry + kRowH / 2;
        char buf[64];
        if (it.kind == Kind::kToggle) {
            const bool on = v != 0;
            const float sw = 40.f, sh = 20.f, sx = right - sw;
            paint.panel(sx, mid - sh / 2, sw, sh, sh / 2, on ? theme::kOn : theme::kTrack);
            paint.disc(on ? sx + sw - 10 : sx + 10, mid, 7, 0xFFFFFF);
        } else if (it.kind == Kind::kNumber) {
            const float tx = right - kSlider;
            const float f = std::clamp((v - it.min) / (it.max - it.min), 0.f, 1.f);
            paint.panel(tx, mid - 2, kSlider, 4, 2, theme::kTrack);
            paint.panel(tx, mid - 2, kSlider * f, 4, 2, theme::kAccent);
            paint.disc(tx + kSlider * f, mid, sel ? 7.f : 6.f, sel ? 0xFFFFFF : theme::kMuted);
            std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), it.format, v);
            paint.text(tx - 14 - paint.measure(buf, Font::kMenu), ry + 5, buf, sel ? theme::kText : theme::kMuted,
                       Font::kMenu, false, 1.f, false);
        } else {
            std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), sel ? "‹  %s  ›" : "%s", choiceLabel(it, v));
            paint.text(right - paint.measure(buf, Font::kMenu), ry + 5, buf, sel ? theme::kText : theme::kMuted,
                       Font::kMenu, false, 1.f, false);
        }
    }
}

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/menu.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Settings menu of the overlay, driven by the keyboard (Insert opens it; up / down choose, left /
// right change, PgUp / PgDn switch the tab). Two tabs:
// - Réglages: sections of switches, sliders and choices, with a help line for the selected one.
//   Each change is written to fc6hack.ini at once and bumps state::settingsVersion.
// - Inventaire: the player's stackable items (resources, materials, Moneda, consumable keys) with
//   their counts; left / right remove / add the chosen step through the game's own functions on
//   the game's thread (inventory.hpp: the menu itself never calls the game).
// The keyboard only, since the overlay never takes the focus: the game pauses without it.

#include "internal/inventory.hpp"
#include "internal/painter.hpp"

#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

namespace fc6::overlay {

class Menu {
public:
    Menu();
    void keys(double now);  // call every frame while the menu is open and the game has focus
    void draw(Painter& paint, double now);
    static float right();  // x of the panel's right edge (the HUD moves beside it while it's open)

private:
    struct InvRow {
        const char* header = nullptr;  // section title, or null for an item / the step row
        int item = -1;                 // index in items_, -1 for the step row
    };

    bool pressed(int vk, double now);
    bool edge(int vk, bool& down);
    void change(int dir);
    void move(int dir);
    void refreshInventory(double now);
    void inventoryKeys(double now);
    void drawSettings(Painter& paint, float x, float rows, float right);
    void drawInventory(Painter& paint, float x, float rows, float right);

    int tab_ = 0;  // 0 settings, 1 inventory
    int selected_ = 0;
    int invSelected_ = 0;
    int step_ = 2;  // index in the steps (1, 10, 100, 1000)
    float highlight_ = -1.f;  // y of the selection highlight, eased towards the selected row
    double lastDraw_ = 0;
    bool pgUp_ = false, pgDown_ = false;
    InventoryWorker worker_;
    std::uint32_t invVersion_ = ~0u;
    std::vector<InventoryRow> items_;  // the worker's snapshot, sorted by section and name
    std::vector<InvRow> invRows_;
    char invStatus_[128] = {};
    bool showStatus_ = false;  // the worker's status since the last change asked here
    struct Repeat {
        bool down = false;
        double next = 0;
    } repeat_[4];
};

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/overlay.cpp`:

```cpp
// Overlay window, see overlay.hpp.

#include "internal/overlay.hpp"

#include "internal/canvas.hpp"
#include "internal/config.hpp"
#include "internal/esp.hpp"
#include "internal/hud.hpp"
#include "internal/hud_view.hpp"
#include "internal/menu.hpp"
#include "internal/painter.hpp"
#include "internal/state.hpp"

#include <windows.h>
#include <d2d1_1.h>
#include <dwrite.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

namespace fc6::overlay {
namespace {

Shared* g_shared = nullptr;
HANDLE g_thread = nullptr;
volatile LONG g_stop = 0;
constexpr wchar_t kClass[] = L"fc6hack_overlay";

double seconds() {
    LARGE_INTEGER f, n;
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f);
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&n);
    return static_cast<double>(n.QuadPart) / static_cast<double>(f.QuadPart);
}

bool focused() {
    DWORD pid = 0;
    GetWindowThreadProcessId(GetForegroundWindow(), &pid);
    return pid == GetCurrentProcessId();
}

int setting(const char* section, const char* key, int fallback) {
    if (config::getString(section, key).empty()) config::setFloat(section, key, static_cast<float>(fallback), 0);
    return static_cast<int>(config::getFloat(section, key, static_cast<float>(fallback)));
}

// The game's main window: the largest visible window of this process that isn't ours.
struct GameWindow {
    HWND hwnd = nullptr;
    RECT rect{};  // client area in screen pixels
};

BOOL CALLBACK enumWindow(HWND hwnd, LPARAM param) {
    auto* best = reinterpret_cast<GameWindow*>(param);
    DWORD pid = 0;
    GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, &pid);
    wchar_t cls[64] = {};
    GetClassNameW(hwnd, cls, 64);
    if (pid != GetCurrentProcessId() || !IsWindowVisible(hwnd) || std::wcscmp(cls, kClass) == 0) return TRUE;
    RECT r;
    GetClientRect(hwnd, &r);
    const LONG area = r.right * r.bottom, bestArea = (best->rect.right - best->rect.left) * (best->rect.bottom - best->rect.top);
    if (area > 0 && area > bestArea) {
        POINT p{0, 0};
        ClientToScreen(hwnd, &p);
        best->hwnd = hwnd;
        best->rect = {p.x, p.y, p.x + r.right, p.y + r.bottom};
    }
    return TRUE;
}

LRESULT CALLBACK windowProc(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wp, LPARAM lp) {
    if (msg == WM_NCHITTEST) return HTTRANSPARENT;
    return DefWindowProcW(hwnd, msg, wp, lp);
}

struct Keys {
    int aim = 0x75, esp = 0x76, menu = 0x2D;
    bool down[3] = {};
    bool edge(int i, int vk) {
        const bool d = (GetAsyncKeyState(vk) & 0x8000) != 0;
        const bool hit = d && !down[i];
        down[i] = d;
        return hit;
    }
};

void hotkeys(Keys& k) {
    if (k.edge(0, k.aim)) {
        const bool on = !state::aimOn.load();
        state::aimOn = on;
        config::setFloat("aim", "enabled", on ? 1.f : 0.f, 0);
        hud::event("aimbot %s", on ? "activé" : "coupé");
        Beep(on ? 1200 : 600, 80);
    }
    if (k.edge(1, k.esp)) {
        const bool on = !state::espOn.load();
        state::espOn = on;
        config::setFloat("overlay", "esp", on ? 1.f : 0.f, 0);
        Beep(on ? 1000 : 500, 60);
    }
    if (k.edge(2, k.menu)) state::menuOpen = !state::menuOpen.load();
}

// Frames per second and drawing time, averaged over a second.
struct FrameStats {
    double since = 0, work = 0;
    int frames = 0;
    float fps = 0, ms = 0;
    void add(double now, double workSeconds) {
        ++frames;
        work += workSeconds;
        if (now - since >= 1.0) {
            fps = static_cast<float>(frames / (now - since));
            ms = static_cast<float>(1000.0 * work / frames);
            since = now;
            work = 0;
            frames = 0;
        }
    }
};

// The ESP's time per frame, NPCs drawn and game reads, logged every 10 s while it's on: wall time
// against this thread's CPU time tells work from waiting (GPU, display, or no free core).
struct EspPerf {
    double since = 0, gather = 0, gatherCpu = 0, draw = 0, drawCpu = 0, worst = 0;
    double reads = 0, queries = 0, npcs = 0;
    double list = 0, checks = 0, points = 0, bones = 0, rest = 0;
    int frames = 0;
    void add(double now, const EspTiming& t, float fps) {
        if (since == 0) since = now;
        ++frames;
        gather += t.gather;
        gatherCpu += t.gatherCpu;
        draw += t.draw;
        drawCpu += t.drawCpu;
        worst = std::max(worst, t.gather + t.draw);
        reads += static_cast<double>(t.reads);
        queries += static_cast<double>(t.queries);
        npcs += t.drawn;
        list += t.list;
        checks += t.checks;
        points += t.points;
        bones += t.bones;
        rest += t.rest;
        if (now - since < 10.0) return;
        const double k = 1000.0 / frames;
        config::log("overlay: ESP gather by step (ms per frame): list %.2f, dead checks %.2f, aim points %.2f,"
                    " bones %.2f, rest %.2f", list * k, checks * k, points * k, bones * k, rest * k);
        config::log("overlay: %.0f fps; ESP per frame: gather %.2f ms (cpu %.2f), draw %.2f ms (cpu %.2f), worst %.1f ms;"
                    " %.1f NPCs, %.0f reads, %.1f VirtualQuery",
                    fps, gather * k, gatherCpu * k, draw * k, drawCpu * k, 1000.0 * worst, npcs / frames,
                    reads / frames, queries / frames);
        *this = {};
        since = now;
    }
};

// Text formats and the brush; the drawing surface is the Canvas.
struct Graphics {
    ID2D1SolidColorBrush* brush = nullptr;
    IDWriteFactory* dwrite = nullptr;
    IDWriteTextFormat* fonts[static_cast<int>(Font::kCount)] = {};

    bool init() {
        if (FAILED(DWriteCreateFactory(DWRITE_FACTORY_TYPE_SHARED, __uuidof(IDWriteFactory),
                                       reinterpret_cast<IUnknown**>(&dwrite)))) {
            return false;
        }
        return createFonts(dwrite, fonts);
    }
    void release() {
        Painter::releaseLayouts();  // they hold the fonts
        for (auto*& f : fonts) {
            if (f) f->Release();
            f = nullptr;
        }
        if (brush) brush->Release();
        if (dwrite) dwrite->Release();
        brush = nullptr;
        dwrite = nullptr;
    }
};

DWORD WINAPI loop(LPVOID) {
    SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext(DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT_PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2);  // screen pixels
    HINSTANCE inst = nullptr;
    GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                       reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(&windowProc), &inst);
    WNDCLASSEXW wc{sizeof(wc)};
    wc.lpfnWndProc = windowProc;
    wc.hInstance = inst;
    wc.lpszClassName = kClass;
    RegisterClassExW(&wc);
    // Layered + transparent: clicks go through to the game. No redirection bitmap: the content is
    // the DirectComposition visual (canvas.hpp).
    HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(WS_EX_NOREDIRECTIONBITMAP | WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT | WS_EX_TOPMOST |
                                    WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW | WS_EX_NOACTIVATE,
                                kClass, L"", WS_POPUP, 0, 0, 1, 1, nullptr, nullptr, inst, nullptr);
    if (hwnd) SetLayeredWindowAttributes(hwnd, 0, 255, LWA_ALPHA);
    Graphics gfx;
    if (!hwnd || !gfx.init()) {
        config::log("overlay: window or Direct2D setup failed");
        gfx.release();
        if (hwnd) DestroyWindow(hwnd);
        UnregisterClassW(kClass, inst);
        return 1;
    }
    Keys keys;
    keys.aim = setting("keys", "aim_toggle", keys.aim);
    keys.esp = setting("keys", "esp_toggle", keys.esp);
    keys.menu = setting("keys", "menu", keys.menu);
    state::espOn = setting("overlay", "esp", 0) != 0;
    state::hudOn = setting("overlay", "hud", 1) != 0;
    float espDist = static_cast<float>(setting("overlay", "esp_dist", 300));
    bool cone = setting("overlay", "cone", 1) != 0;
    float fov = config::getFloat("aim", "fov", 12.f);
    // Frames per second at most: Present waits for the display (144 Hz and more), and each frame
    // reads every NPC's skeleton and draws on the game's GPU.
    double maxFps = std::clamp(setting("overlay", "max_fps", 60), 20, 240);
    double nextFrame = 0;
    int settingsSeen = state::settingsVersion.load();
    config::log("overlay started: keys aimbot 0x%x, ESP 0x%x, menu 0x%x", keys.aim, keys.esp, keys.menu);

    Canvas canvas;
    bool canvasOk = false;
    int width = 0, height = 0;
    FrameStats stats;
    EspPerf espPerf;
    Menu menu;
    game::Skeletons skeletons;  // this thread's own caches
    GameWindow game;
    double nextFind = 0;
    bool shown = false, blank = false;
    while (!g_stop) {
        MSG msg;
        while (PeekMessageW(&msg, nullptr, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) DispatchMessageW(&msg);
        const double now = seconds();
        if (now >= nextFind) {
            nextFind = now + 1.0;
            GameWindow found;
            EnumWindows(enumWindow, reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(&found));
            const int w = found.rect.right - found.rect.left, h = found.rect.bottom - found.rect.top;
            if (found.hwnd && w > 0 && h > 0 && (std::memcmp(&found.rect, &game.rect, sizeof(RECT)) != 0 || !canvasOk)) {
                SetWindowPos(hwnd, HWND_TOPMOST, found.rect.left, found.rect.top, w, h, SWP_NOACTIVATE);
                if (!canvasOk) {
                    canvasOk = canvas.init(hwnd, w, h) &&
                               SUCCEEDED(canvas.begin()->CreateSolidColorBrush(D2D1::ColorF(0xFFFFFF), &gfx.brush));
                    if (canvasOk) canvas.end();
                    if (!canvasOk) config::log("overlay: Direct3D / DirectComposition setup failed");
                } else if (w != width || h != height) {
                    canvasOk = canvas.resize(w, h);
                }
                width = w;
                height = h;
            }
            game = found;
        }
        if (const int v = state::settingsVersion.load(); v != settingsSeen) {
            settingsSeen = v;
            espDist = config::getFloat("overlay", "esp_dist", espDist);
            cone = config::getFloat("overlay", "cone", 1.f) != 0;
            fov = config::getFloat("aim", "fov", fov);
            maxFps = std::clamp(config::getFloat("overlay", "max_fps", static_cast<float>(maxFps)), 20.f, 240.f);
        }
        const bool focus = focused();
        if (focus) hotkeys(keys);
        if (!focus || !game.hwnd || !canvasOk) {
            if (shown) ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_HIDE);
            shown = false;
            Sleep(50);
            continue;
        }
        if (now < nextFrame) {
            Sleep(1);
            continue;
        }
        nextFrame = std::max(nextFrame + 1.0 / maxFps, now);
        const bool menuOpen = state::menuOpen.load();
        if (menuOpen) menu.keys(now);
        const bool showCone = cone && state::aimOn;
        const bool empty = !state::hudOn && !state::espOn && !menuOpen && !showCone;
        if (empty && blank && shown) {  // nothing to draw and already cleared
            Sleep(30);
            continue;
        }
        ID2D1DeviceContext* dc = canvas.begin();
        Painter paint(dc, gfx.brush, gfx.dwrite, gfx.fonts, static_cast<float>(width), static_cast<float>(height));
        game::Camera cam;
        if ((state::espOn || showCone) && game::camera(cam)) {
            if (state::espOn) {
                espPerf.add(now, drawEsp(paint, Projector{cam, paint.width, paint.height}, skeletons, espDist, now),
                            stats.fps);
            }
            if (showCone) drawCone(paint, cam, fov, g_shared->hudTarget[0] != 0);
        }
        if (state::hudOn) drawHud(paint, *g_shared, stats.fps, stats.ms, menuOpen ? Menu::right() + 20 : 14.f);
        if (menuOpen) menu.draw(paint, now);
        stats.add(now, seconds() - now);
        canvas.end();  // waits for the next refresh of the display
        blank = empty;
        if (!shown) ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
        shown = true;
    }
    gfx.release();
    canvas.release();
    DestroyWindow(hwnd);
    UnregisterClassW(kClass, inst);
    return 0;
}

}  // namespace

void start(Shared* shared) {
    g_shared = shared;
    g_stop = 0;
    g_thread = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, loop, nullptr, 0, nullptr);
}

void stop() {
    if (!g_thread) return;
    InterlockedExchange(&g_stop, 1);
    WaitForSingleObject(g_thread, 5000);
    CloseHandle(g_thread);
    g_thread = nullptr;
}

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

```hpp
#pragma once

// The overlay: a transparent, click-through, topmost window over the game's client area (the
// game must run windowed or borderless), drawn with Direct2D from its own thread. Nothing is
// hooked in the game's renderer. Shows the HUD (aimbot status, from hud.hpp), the ESP and the
// settings menu; handles the hotkeys ([keys] in fc6hack.ini: aim_toggle F6, esp_toggle F7,
// menu Insert).

#include "internal/shared.hpp"

namespace fc6::overlay {

void start(Shared* shared);
void stop();

}  // namespace fc6::overlay
```

`src/internal/painter.cpp`:

```cpp
// Direct2D drawing helpers, see painter.hpp.

#include "internal/painter.hpp"

#include <windows.h>
#include <d2d1.h>
#include <dwrite.h>

#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>

namespace fc6::overlay {
namespace {

std::wstring widen(const char* s) {
    const int n = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s, -1, nullptr, 0);
    std::wstring w(n > 0 ? n - 1 : 0, L'\0');
    if (n > 1) MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s, -1, w.data(), n);
    return w;
}

// Text layouts kept between frames: the ESP labels and the HUD lines mostly repeat from one frame
// to the next, and building a layout (CreateTextLayout) was the costly part of drawing text, two
// per NPC each frame. A layout unused for kKeepFrames frames is dropped.
struct CachedLayout {
    IDWriteTextLayout* layout;
    float width;
    unsigned used;
};
std::unordered_map<std::string, CachedLayout> g_layouts;
unsigned g_frame = 0;
constexpr unsigned kKeepFrames = 30;
constexpr std::size_t kMaxLayouts = 2048;

}  // namespace

Painter::Painter(ID2D1RenderTarget* rt, ID2D1SolidColorBrush* brush, IDWriteFactory* dwrite,
                 IDWriteTextFormat* const* fonts, float canvasWidth, float canvasHeight)
    : width(canvasWidth), height(canvasHeight), rt_(rt), brush_(brush), dwrite_(dwrite), fonts_(fonts) {
    // One painter per frame: age the cached layouts.
    if (++g_frame % kKeepFrames == 0 || g_layouts.size() > kMaxLayouts) {
        for (auto it = g_layouts.begin(); it != g_layouts.end();) {
            if (g_frame - it->second.used > kKeepFrames || g_layouts.size() > kMaxLayouts) {
                it->second.layout->Release();
                it = g_layouts.erase(it);
            } else {
                ++it;
            }
        }
    }
}

void Painter::releaseLayouts() {
    for (auto& [key, c] : g_layouts) c.layout->Release();
    g_layouts.clear();
}

IDWriteTextLayout* Painter::layout(const char* s, Font font, float& textWidth) {
    IDWriteTextFormat* format = fonts_[static_cast<int>(font)];
    std::string key(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&format), sizeof(format));
    key += s;
    if (const auto it = g_layouts.find(key); it != g_layouts.end()) {
        it->second.used = g_frame;
        textWidth = it->second.width;
        return it->second.layout;
    }
    const std::wstring w = widen(s);
    IDWriteTextLayout* l = nullptr;
    if (FAILED(dwrite_->CreateTextLayout(w.c_str(), static_cast<UINT32>(w.size()), format, 4096.f, 256.f, &l))) {
        return nullptr;
    }
    DWRITE_TEXT_METRICS m{};
    l->GetMetrics(&m);
    g_layouts.emplace(std::move(key), CachedLayout{l, m.width, g_frame});
    textWidth = m.width;
    return l;
}

void Painter::colour(std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha) {
    brush_->SetColor(D2D1::ColorF(rgb, alpha));
}

void Painter::line(float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2, std::uint32_t rgb, float w, float alpha) {
    colour(rgb, alpha);
    rt_->DrawLine({x1, y1}, {x2, y2}, brush_, w);
}

void Painter::rect(float x, float y, float w, float h, std::uint32_t rgb, float lineWidth) {
    colour(rgb);
    rt_->DrawRectangle({x, y, x + w, y + h}, brush_, lineWidth);
}

void Painter::fill(float x, float y, float w, float h, std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha) {
    colour(rgb, alpha);
    rt_->FillRectangle({x, y, x + w, y + h}, brush_);
}

void Painter::panel(float x, float y, float w, float h, float radius, std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha) {
    colour(rgb, alpha);
    rt_->FillRoundedRectangle({{x, y, x + w, y + h}, radius, radius}, brush_);
}

void Painter::frame(float x, float y, float w, float h, float radius, std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha,
                    float lineWidth) {
    colour(rgb, alpha);
    rt_->DrawRoundedRectangle({{x, y, x + w, y + h}, radius, radius}, brush_, lineWidth);
}

void Painter::circle(float x, float y, float r, std::uint32_t rgb, float w, float alpha) {
    colour(rgb, alpha);
    rt_->DrawEllipse(D2D1::Ellipse({x, y}, r, r), brush_, w);
}

void Painter::disc(float x, float y, float r, std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha) {
    colour(rgb, alpha);
    rt_->FillEllipse(D2D1::Ellipse({x, y}, r, r), brush_);
}

float Painter::text(float x, float y, const char* s, std::uint32_t rgb, Font font, bool centred, float alpha,
                    bool shadow) {
    float w = 0;
    IDWriteTextLayout* l = layout(s, font, w);
    if (!l) return 0.f;
    const float left = centred ? x - w / 2 : x;
    if (shadow) {
        colour(0x000000, 0.85f * alpha);
        rt_->DrawTextLayout({left + 1, y + 1}, l, brush_);
    }
    colour(rgb, alpha);
    rt_->DrawTextLayout({left, y}, l, brush_);
    return w;
}

float Painter::measure(const char* s, Font font) {
    float w = 0;
    return layout(s, font, w) ? w : 0.f;
}

float Painter::pill(float x, float y, const char* label, std::uint32_t rgb) {
    const float w = pillWidth(label);
    panel(x, y, w, 22, 11, rgb, 0.16f);
    disc(x + 11, y + 11, 4, rgb);
    text(x + 20, y + 3, label, rgb, Font::kTiny, false, 1.f, false);
    return w;
}

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/painter.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// 2D drawing for the overlay window (Direct2D, see canvas.hpp).
// Coordinates in pixels of the game's client area; colours 0xRRGGBB; text in UTF-8.

#include <cstdint>

struct ID2D1RenderTarget;
struct ID2D1SolidColorBrush;
struct IDWriteFactory;
struct IDWriteTextFormat;
struct IDWriteTextLayout;

namespace fc6::overlay {

// kHud: HUD titles, kSmall: ESP labels, kMenu: menu rows, kTitle: menu title, kTiny: details.
enum class Font { kHud, kSmall, kMenu, kTitle, kTiny, kCount };

// Colours of the menu and the HUD.
namespace theme {
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kPanel = 0x0D1117, kRow = 0x1C2533, kLine = 0x2A3342;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kText = 0xE6EAF0, kDim = 0x8B95A5, kMuted = 0xC9D1DC;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kAccent = 0xFF6B2C, kOn = 0x3DD68C, kOff = 0xE5484D, kWarn = 0xFFC233;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kTrack = 0x394150;
}  // namespace theme

class Painter {
public:
    Painter(ID2D1RenderTarget* rt, ID2D1SolidColorBrush* brush, IDWriteFactory* dwrite,
            IDWriteTextFormat* const* fonts, float canvasWidth, float canvasHeight);

    void line(float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2, std::uint32_t rgb, float w = 2.f, float alpha = 1.f);
    void rect(float x, float y, float w, float h, std::uint32_t rgb, float lineWidth = 2.f);
    void fill(float x, float y, float w, float h, std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha = 1.f);
    // Rounded rectangle, filled or outlined.
    void panel(float x, float y, float w, float h, float radius, std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha = 1.f);
    void frame(float x, float y, float w, float h, float radius, std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha = 1.f,
               float lineWidth = 1.f);
    void circle(float x, float y, float r, std::uint32_t rgb, float w = 2.f, float alpha = 1.f);
    void disc(float x, float y, float r, std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha = 1.f);
    // Text, with a 1 px shadow unless shadow is false; centred: x is the middle. Returns its width.
    float text(float x, float y, const char* s, std::uint32_t rgb, Font font = Font::kHud, bool centred = false,
               float alpha = 1.f, bool shadow = true);
    float measure(const char* s, Font font);  // width of the text
    // Status pill "● label" in a tinted rounded box at (x, y), 22 px high; returns its width.
    float pill(float x, float y, const char* label, std::uint32_t rgb);
    float pillWidth(const char* label) { return measure(label, Font::kTiny) + 30.f; }

    const float width, height;

    // Text layouts are kept between frames (see painter.cpp); released with the fonts.
    static void releaseLayouts();

private:
    void colour(std::uint32_t rgb, float alpha = 1.f);
    IDWriteTextLayout* layout(const char* s, Font font, float& textWidth);
    ID2D1RenderTarget* rt_;
    ID2D1SolidColorBrush* brush_;
    IDWriteFactory* dwrite_;
    IDWriteTextFormat* const* fonts_;
};

}  // namespace fc6::overlay

```

`src/internal/profiles.cpp`:

```cpp
// Settings and learned values, see profiles.hpp.

#include "internal/profiles.hpp"

#include "internal/config.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

namespace fc6::aim {
namespace {

constexpr float kAimBaseGain = 1.33f;

float setting(const char* section, const char* key, float fallback, int decimals = 2) {
    if (config::getString(section, key).empty()) config::setFloat(section, key, fallback, decimals);
    return config::getFloat(section, key, fallback);
}

bool flag(const char* key, bool fallback) { return setting("aim", key, fallback ? 1.f : 0.f, 0) != 0.f; }

std::string section(const char* weapon) { return std::string("weapon ") + weapon; }

}  // namespace

Settings loadSettings() {
    Settings s;
    s.enabled = flag("enabled", s.enabled);
    s.key = static_cast<int>(setting("aim", "key", static_cast<float>(s.key), 0));
    s.fov = setting("aim", "fov", s.fov);
    s.smooth = setting("aim", "smooth", s.smooth);
    s.maxDist = setting("aim", "max_dist", s.maxDist, 0);
    s.hz = std::clamp(setting("aim", "hz", s.hz, 0), 20.f, 500.f);
    s.follow = flag("follow", s.follow);
    s.lead = flag("lead", s.lead);
    s.headOnly = flag("head_only", s.headOnly);
    s.zoomComp = flag("zoom_comp", s.zoomComp);
    s.autoBallistics = flag("auto", s.autoBallistics);
    s.barrel = flag("barrel", s.barrel);
    s.direct = flag("direct", s.direct);
    s.frameSync = flag("frame_sync", s.frameSync);
    s.recoil = flag("recoil", s.recoil);
    s.sticky = flag("sticky", s.sticky);
    s.threatsFirst = flag("threats_first", s.threatsFirst);
    s.speed = setting("aim", "speed", s.speed, 1);
    s.gravity = setting("aim", "gravity", s.gravity, 2);
    s.kx = setting("mouse", "yaw_counts_per_rad", s.kx, 4);
    s.ky = setting("mouse", "pitch_counts_per_rad", s.ky, 4);
    return s;
}

float ZoomGain::model(float zoom) { return kAimBaseGain * std::max(1.f, zoom); }

void ZoomGain::load() {
    table_.clear();
    for (int q = 4; q <= 80; ++q) {
        char key[16];
        std::snprintf(key, sizeof(key), "%.2f", q / 4.0);
        const float v = config::getFloat("zoom", key, 0.f);
        if (v > 0.f) table_[q] = v;
    }
}

float ZoomGain::factor(float zoom) const {
    const auto it = table_.find(static_cast<int>(std::lround(zoom * 4.f)));
    return it != table_.end() ? it->second : model(zoom);
}

// The player's own mouse moves spoil some checks: each one moves the factor half a step (in
// log), and the factor stays within -20 % / +25 % of the model.
void ZoomGain::learn(float zoom, float response) {
    if (!(response > 0.5f && response < 2.f)) return;
    const float m = model(zoom);
    const float f = std::clamp(factor(zoom) / std::sqrt(response), 0.8f * m, 1.25f * m);
    const int q = static_cast<int>(std::lround(zoom * 4.f));
    table_[q] = std::round(f * 1000.f) / 1000.f;
    char key[16];
    std::snprintf(key, sizeof(key), "%.2f", q / 4.0);
    config::setFloat("zoom", key, table_[q], 3);
}

Profile loadProfile(const char* weapon) {
    const std::string sec = section(weapon);
    Profile p;
    p.speed = config::getFloat(sec.c_str(), "speed", 0.f);
    p.gravity = config::getFloat(sec.c_str(), "gravity", 0.f);
    p.speedX = config::getFloat(sec.c_str(), "speed_x", 0.f);
    p.gravityX = config::getFloat(sec.c_str(), "gravity_x", 0.f);
    return p;
}

void saveProfile(const char* weapon, const Profile& p) {
    const std::string sec = section(weapon);
    config::setFloat(sec.c_str(), "speed", p.speed, 2);
    config::setFloat(sec.c_str(), "gravity", p.gravity, 3);
    if (p.speedX > 0) config::setFloat(sec.c_str(), "speed_x", p.speedX, 4);
    if (p.gravityX > 0) config::setFloat(sec.c_str(), "gravity_x", p.gravityX, 4);
}

Ballistics effective(const game::Weapon& base, const Profile& prof, const Settings& st) {
    if (base.stats) return {base.effectiveSpeed(), base.effectiveGravity(), base.effectiveDrop()};
    if (prof.measured()) return {prof.speed, prof.gravity, 0.f};
    return {st.speed, st.gravity, 0.f};
}

std::string weaponLine(const game::Weapon& w, const Ballistics& b, bool headOnly, bool measured) {
    const char* dot = std::strrchr(w.name, '.');
    const char* ammo = std::strrchr(w.ammo, '_');
    char buf[256];
    int n = std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%s %s  %.0f m/s  g %.1f", w.name[0] ? (dot ? dot + 1 : w.name) : "?",
                          ammo ? ammo + 1 : w.ammo, b.speed, b.gravity);
    if (w.stats) {
        n += std::snprintf(buf + n, sizeof(buf) - n, "  portée %.0f m  (jeu %.0f x%.2f x%.2f, g %.1f x%.2f x%.2f)",
                           w.range, w.speed, w.speedMul, w.ammoSpeed, w.gravity, w.gravityMul, w.ammoGravity);
    }
    if (w.stats) {
        n += std::snprintf(buf + n, sizeof(buf) - n, "  dispersion %.1f-%.1f°, en visée %.1f-%.1f°%s", w.hipSpread[0],
                           w.hipSpread[1], w.aimSpread[0], w.aimSpread[1], headOnly ? " (toujours la tête)" : "");
    }
    if (!measured && !w.stats) std::snprintf(buf + n, sizeof(buf) - n, "  (non mesurée : tirer une fois à 40 m+)");
    return buf;
}

}  // namespace fc6::aim

```

`src/internal/profiles.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Settings, learned mouse gain per zoom and per-weapon ballistics (port of the corresponding parts
// of tools/aim.py), kept in fc6hack.ini (config.hpp).

#include "internal/game.hpp"

#include <map>
#include <string>

namespace fc6::aim {

struct Settings {
    bool enabled = true;
    int key = 0x02;        // hold to aim: right mouse button
    float fov = 12.f;      // cone half-angle, degrees
    float smooth = 0.35f;  // fraction of the error corrected per tick
    float maxDist = 250.f;
    float hz = 120.f;
    bool follow = true, lead = true, headOnly = false, zoomComp = true, autoBallistics = true;
    bool barrel = true;  // aim the barrel (fire direction with sway), not the camera
    bool direct = true;  // turn the view by writing its angles (game::addViewAngles), not the mouse
    bool frameSync = true;  // one correction per camera picture (aim.cpp kFrameWait)
    bool recoil = true;     // pitch from the accumulator: a shot's kick is left to the game (aim.cpp kKick)
    bool sticky = true;        // stay on the target while it lives (re-press the key to switch)
    bool threatsFirst = true;  // NPCs targeting the player first, then the alerted ones
    float speed = 614.f, gravity = 16.5f;  // without game data or measurement
    float kx = -806.3f, ky = -806.3f;      // mouse counts per radian (tools/calibrate.py)
};
Settings loadSettings();  // and writes the defaults of missing keys back, so the file shows them

// Mouse counts multiplier per zoom level while aiming: 1.33 x zoom, learned per zoom (+-20 %).
class ZoomGain {
public:
    void load();
    float factor(float zoom) const;
    void learn(float zoom, float response);  // response: camera turn / turn sent
    static float model(float zoom);

private:
    std::map<int, float> table_;  // key: zoom in quarters
};

struct Profile {
    float speed = 0, gravity = 0, speedX = 0, gravityX = 0;  // 0 = not measured
    bool measured() const { return speed > 0; }
};
Profile loadProfile(const char* weapon);
void saveProfile(const char* weapon, const Profile& p);

struct Ballistics {
    float speed = 0, gravity = 0, dropDistance = 0;
};
// The game's own effective values (data x attachments x loaded ammo, game.hpp), else the values
// measured on the player's shots, else the defaults.
Ballistics effective(const game::Weapon& base, const Profile& prof, const Settings& st);

std::string weaponLine(const game::Weapon& w, const Ballistics& b, bool headOnly, bool measured);

}  // namespace fc6::aim
```

```hpp
#pragma once

// Ray casts through the game's own query (implemented in dllmain.cpp, see its header comment),
// for threads of the DLL other than the shared-memory worker.

#include "internal/shared.hpp"

#include <cstdint>

namespace fc6::rays {

bool ready();          // a game query was captured
bool attachThread();   // gives the calling thread its own Havok memory router; false if not ready
void detachThread();   // before the thread exits
// filter: collision filter info for this ray, 0 = the captured game query's.
bool cast(const Ray& ray, std::uint32_t filter, Hit& out);
```
