`src/internal/shared.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Shared memory between fc6internal.dll (inside the game) and the external tools.
// Python opens the same block with mmap(-1, sizeof(Shared), tagname="Local\\fc6hack").

#include <cstdint>

namespace fc6 {

inline constexpr wchar_t kSharedName[] = L"Local\\fc6hack";
// Auto-reset events: a client sets kRequestEvent after writing reqSeq, the DLL sets kResponseEvent
// after writing respSeq. Optional for clients: the DLL also wakes every few ms to poll.
inline constexpr wchar_t kRequestEvent[] = L"Local\\fc6hack_req";
inline constexpr wchar_t kResponseEvent[] = L"Local\\fc6hack_resp";
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kSharedMagic = 0x36434646;  // "FFC6"
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kSharedVersion = 15;
inline constexpr int kMaxRays = 64;

enum Status : std::uint32_t {
    kStarting = 0,
    kWaitingForWorld = 1,  // hardware breakpoint armed on the game's per-ray query
    kReady = 2,            // a game query was captured, ray requests are served
    kNoWorld = 3,          // the game made no ray query while we waited
    kStopped = 4,
};

struct Ray {
    float from[4];
    float to[4];
};

struct Hit {
    std::uint32_t hit;      // 1 if something was hit between from and to
    std::uint32_t fault;    // 1 if the call raised an exception (result invalid)
    float fraction;         // 0..1 along the ray (1 = nothing hit)
    std::uint32_t pad;
    float position[4];      // from + (to - from) * fraction
    float normal[4];        // surface normal at the hit
    std::uint64_t object;   // engine collidable that was hit (0 if none)
    std::uint8_t raw[0x60]; // the game's raw result record, for reverse engineering
};

inline constexpr int kMaxFilterSamples = 64;

struct FilterSample {
    std::uint32_t filterInfo;  // entry + 0x24 of a game ray query
    std::uint32_t flags;       // entry + 0x20
    std::uint32_t count;       // calls seen during the sampling window
    float meanLength;          // mean ray length in metres
};

inline constexpr int kMaxWatchHits = 32;
inline constexpr int kWatchFrames = 8;

// One instruction that wrote to the watched address (hardware data breakpoint, Dr1).
struct WatchHit {
    std::uint64_t rip;                    // instruction *after* the write (data breakpoints trap after)
    std::uint32_t count;                  // writes seen from this rip
    std::uint32_t threadId;               // thread of the first write
    std::uint64_t regs[16];               // rax rcx rdx rbx rsp rbp rsi rdi r8..r15 at the first write
    std::uint64_t frames[kWatchFrames];   // caller chain (return addresses) at the first write
};

inline constexpr int kMaxTraceRays = 16384;

// One game ray query seen by the trace.
struct TraceRay {
    float from[3];
    float to[3];
    std::uint32_t filterInfo;  // entry + 0x24
    std::uint32_t flags;       // entry + 0x20
    std::int64_t qpc;          // QueryPerformanceCounter at the call
    std::uint32_t threadId;
    std::uint32_t pad;
    std::uint64_t caller;      // return address of the query call, minus the engine base
};

inline constexpr int kTraceSnaps = 64;
inline constexpr int kSnapBytes = 0x400;
inline constexpr int kSnapFrames = 6;

// Memory around one traced call: the query is batched (the dispatcher +0x47923e0 walks an array
// of entries), so the requester is found through the data, not the call stack.
struct TraceSnap {
    std::uint32_t index;                     // index of the call in Shared::trace
    std::uint32_t pad;
    std::uint64_t entry;                     // rdx: the query entry
    std::uint64_t results;                   // [entry + 0x30]: where the hits are written
    std::uint64_t batch;                     // rsi of the dispatcher: the batch job
    std::uint64_t frames[kSnapFrames];       // callers (return addresses), from the dispatcher up
    std::uint64_t frameRegs[kSnapFrames][8]; // rbx rbp rsi rdi r12 r13 r14 r15 in each caller
    std::uint8_t aroundResults[kSnapBytes];  // results - kSnapBytes / 2 .. results + kSnapBytes / 2
    std::uint8_t batchBytes[0x100];          // batch .. batch + 0x100
};

inline constexpr int kViewMax = 64;

// One character of the aim-assist list as the DLL reads it (version 12).
struct ViewCharacter {
    std::uint64_t entity;
    char name[128];           // archetype
    float head[3];            // first aim point (head centre, or the ragdoll / estimate head)
    float chest[3];           // the chest point (0 if none)
    std::int32_t attitude;    // -1 without a faction component
    std::int32_t death;       // death state, 0 = alive
    float hp, hpMax;          // -1 without a health object
    std::uint32_t hostile;    // game::isHostile
    std::uint32_t dead;       // game::isDead
    std::uint32_t source;     // 0 skeleton, 1 ragdoll, 2 estimate
    float origin[3];          // entity position (entity + 0x30 row 3), read with the aim points
    char reason[20];          // why the skeleton was used or not ("live", "frozen", "away"...)
    std::uint32_t pad;
};

inline constexpr int kHudEvents = 5;
inline constexpr int kInvMax = 64;

// One of the player's stackable items (version 15, game::InventoryItem).
struct InvEntry {
    std::uint64_t item;  // the item object
    std::uint64_t id;    // item id
    std::uint32_t maxStack;
    std::uint32_t pad;
    char name[96];       // "LootItem/Economic/Metal"
};
inline constexpr int kHudText = 200;

struct HudEvent {
    std::uint64_t tick;  // GetTickCount64 when it happened
    char text[kHudText];
};

struct Shared {
    std::uint32_t magic;
    std::uint32_t version;
    std::uint64_t queryObject;        // rcx of the game's per-ray query (+0x47921c0)
    volatile std::uint32_t status;    // Status
    volatile std::uint32_t quit;      // set to 1 by a client to unload the DLL
    volatile std::uint32_t reqSeq;    // client bumps this after filling rays/count
    volatile std::uint32_t respSeq;   // DLL copies reqSeq here once hits are written
    std::uint32_t count;              // number of rays in this request
    std::uint32_t served;             // total rays cast since load
    std::uint64_t queryVtable;        // engine offset of the query object's vtable
    std::uint32_t filterInfo;         // collision filter info of the captured game query
    std::uint32_t entryFlags;         // byte at entry + 0x20 of the captured query
    volatile std::uint32_t filterOverride;  // non-zero: use this filter info for our rays
    volatile std::uint32_t sampleMs;        // client sets > 0 to sample the game's filters; reset to 0 when done
    std::uint32_t sampleDistinct;           // number of entries in samples[]
    std::uint32_t sampleTotal;              // total calls seen
    FilterSample samples[kMaxFilterSamples];
    Ray rays[kMaxRays];
    Hit hits[kMaxRays];
    char message[128];
    // Write watch (version 6): who writes to watchAddr during watchMs milliseconds.
    volatile std::uint64_t watchAddr;  // must be aligned to watchLen
    volatile std::uint32_t watchLen;   // 1, 2, 4 or 8 bytes
    volatile std::uint32_t watchMs;    // client sets > 0 to start; reset to 0 when done
    std::uint32_t watchDistinct;       // number of entries in watch[]
    std::uint32_t watchTotal;          // total writes seen
    WatchHit watch[kMaxWatchHits];
    // Trace (version 7): every game ray query with filterInfo == traceFilter (0 = all) during
    // traceMs milliseconds, in call order per thread.
    volatile std::uint32_t traceMs;      // client sets > 0 to start; reset to 0 when done
    volatile std::uint32_t traceFilter;  // collision filter info to keep, 0 = every query
    std::uint32_t traceCount;            // entries in trace[]
    std::uint32_t traceTotal;            // matching calls seen (more than traceCount if full)
    std::int64_t qpcFrequency;           // QueryPerformanceFrequency, for TraceRay::qpc
    TraceRay trace[kMaxTraceRays];
    // Execute watch (version 8): with watchLen = 0, watchMs starts execute breakpoints (Dr1-Dr3)
    // on these code addresses (0 = unused) instead of the write watch. Each distinct register set
    // seen there becomes a WatchHit (rip = the instruction itself).
    volatile std::uint64_t execAddr[3];
    // Trace snapshots (version 9, callers in 10): memory around the first kTraceSnaps traced calls.
    std::uint32_t snapCount;
    std::uint32_t snapPad;
    TraceSnap snaps[kTraceSnaps];
    // Bullet monitor (version 11): while monitorOn is 1 and the client keeps changing monitorBeat
    // (3 s without a change stops it), the Dr0 breakpoint stays armed and every query with
    // traceFilter is written to trace[] as a ring: entry k goes to trace[k % kMaxTraceRays] and
    // monitorHead counts the entries written. qpcFrequency is set. No snapshots in this mode.
    volatile std::uint32_t monitorOn;
    volatile std::uint32_t monitorBeat;
    volatile std::uint32_t monitorHead;
    std::uint32_t monitorPad;
    // Game view (version 12): what the DLL's own reads (game.cpp) see, published while viewOn is
    // 1, to check the C++ port against the Python tools (tools/port_check.py). viewSeq changes
    // after each complete update.
    volatile std::uint32_t viewOn;
    volatile std::uint32_t viewSeq;
    std::uint32_t viewCount;  // entries in view[]
    std::uint32_t viewCamera; // 1 if eye/fwd/sx/sy were read
    float eye[3], fwd[3], sx, sy;
    char weapon[128];         // held weapon archetype, "" if none
    float weaponSpeed, weaponGravity, weaponDrop, weaponRpm;  // game data (0 without stats)
    ViewCharacter view[kViewMax];
    // HUD (version 13): status of the DLL's own aimbot (aim.cpp) for the overlay (tools/esp.py).
    volatile std::uint64_t hudTick;  // GetTickCount64 of the last update: the aimbot is running
    std::uint32_t hudAimOn;          // 1 while the aimbot is switched on (toggle key)
    std::uint32_t hudPad;
    char hudWeapon[kHudText];
    char hudTarget[kHudText];
    HudEvent hudEvents[kHudEvents];  // oldest first, tick 0 = empty
    // Read watch (version 14): set to 1 before watchMs and the write watch's data breakpoint
    // traps reads too (Dr7 RW = 11): who reads watchAddr.
    volatile std::uint32_t watchAccess;
    std::uint32_t watchAccessPad;
    // Inventory commands (version 15, tools/inventory.py): the client fills invCmd (1 list the
    // items, 2 read the count of invItem, 3 add invDelta to invItem, negative removes) and
    // invHook (engine offset of a function the game's thread runs every frame), then bumps
    // invSeq; the DLL runs 2 and 3 on that thread (gamethread.hpp) and copies invSeq to invRespSeq.
    volatile std::uint32_t invSeq;
    volatile std::uint32_t invRespSeq;
    std::uint32_t invCmd;
    std::int32_t invDelta;
    std::uint64_t invItem;
    std::uint64_t invHook;
    std::int32_t invResult;   // 3: the change applied
    std::uint32_t invValue;   // 2, 3: the count after
    std::uint32_t invOk;      // 1 done, 0 not run (timeout: the hook didn't run, game paused?)
    std::uint32_t invThread;  // 2, 3: the thread that ran it
    double invMs;             // 2, 3: the time it took there
    std::uint32_t invCount;   // 1: entries in inv[]
    std::uint32_t invPad;
    InvEntry inv[kInvMax];
};

// The block is created with this fixed size so that fields added at the end later don't make it
// larger than a block a running client still holds (which would stop a newer DLL from mapping it).
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kSharedBytes = 4u << 20;
static_assert(sizeof(Shared) <= kSharedBytes);

}  // namespace fc6

```

`src/internal/skeleton.cpp`:

```cpp
// Animated skeleton and aim points (port of tools/skeleton.py and targets.aim_points).
//
// S = [[entity + 0x28] + 0xe0] (fallback [body + 0xa0]), vtable +0x5951dc8: model-space pose at
// [S + 0x78] (stride 0x20, position at +0x10), local pose at [S + 0x68], bone table
// {u32 crc32(name), u32 index} at [S + 0xe0], bone count at S + 0xec.

#include "internal/game.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

namespace fc6::game {
namespace {

constexpr double kLiveWindow = 0.3;  // seconds without change before a pose counts as frozen

std::uint32_t crc32(const void* data, std::size_t n, std::uint32_t c = 0) {
    c = ~c;
    const auto* p = static_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(data);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        c ^= p[i];
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) c = (c >> 1) ^ (0xEDB88320u & (0u - (c & 1u)));
    }
    return ~c;
}

std::uintptr_t skeletonInstance(std::uintptr_t entity) {
    const std::uint64_t c = u64(entity + 0x28);
    std::uint64_t s = c ? u64(c + 0xE0) : 0;
    if (!s) {
        const std::uintptr_t b = component(entity, off::kBodyComponentVt);
        s = b ? u64(b + 0xA0) : 0;
    }
    return s && vt(s) == off::kSkeletonVt ? s : 0;
}

Vec3 mid(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) { return (a + b) * 0.5f; }

bool flatNear(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, float r) { return std::hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) < r; }

// Ragdoll bodies (hit zones) when the ragdoll is active and sits on its owner.
bool fromRagdoll(std::uintptr_t entity, const Transform& m, AimPoints& out) {
    const std::uintptr_t phys = component(entity, off::kPhysComponentVt);
    const std::uint64_t y = phys ? u64(phys + 0x70) : 0;
    const std::uint64_t x = y ? u64(y + 0x08) : 0;
    const std::uint64_t a = x ? u64(x + 0x88) : 0;
    const std::uint64_t c = a ? u64(a + 0x128) : 0;
    if (!c) return false;
    const std::uint64_t arr = u64(c + 0xD8);
    const std::uint32_t count = u32(c + 0xE0);
    // Names lack the leading 'R' ("agdoll_Head").
    static constexpr const char* kParts[4] = {"agdoll_Head", "agdoll_Neck", "agdoll_Spine1", "agdoll_Pelvis"};
    static constexpr const char* kLabels[4] = {"head", "neck", "chest", "pelvis"};
    bool have[4] = {};
    Vec3 pos[4];
    for (std::uint32_t k = 0; k < count && k < 64; ++k) {
        const std::uint64_t b = u64(arr + 8 * k);
        const std::uint64_t s = b ? u64(b + 0xB8) : 0;
        char name[32] = {};
        if (!s || !read(s, name, sizeof(name) - 1) || u32(b + 0x13C) == 0xFFFFFFFFu) continue;
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
            if (std::strcmp(name, kParts[i]) == 0) {
                have[i] = read(b + 0x1A0, &pos[i], sizeof(Vec3));
            }
        }
    }
    if (!have[3] || !flatNear(pos[3], m.pos, 1.f)) return false;
    out = {};
    out.source = PointSource::kRagdoll;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        if (!have[i]) continue;
        out.label[out.count] = kLabels[i];
        out.p[out.count++] = pos[i];
    }
    return out.count > 0;
}

}  // namespace

std::uint32_t crc32(const char* s) {
    std::uint32_t c = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    for (; *s; ++s) {
        c ^= static_cast<std::uint8_t>(*s);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) c = (c >> 1) ^ (0xEDB88320u & (0u - (c & 1u)));
    }
    return ~c;
}

int Skeletons::namedBones(std::uintptr_t entity, const std::uint32_t* crcs, int n, Vec3* out, bool* have) {
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) have[i] = false;
    Transform m;
    const std::uintptr_t s = skeletonInstance(entity);
    if (!s || !transform(entity + 0x30, m)) return 0;
    const std::uint64_t model = u64(s + 0x78), table = u64(s + 0xE0);
    const std::uint32_t count = u32(s + 0xEC);
    if (!model || !table || count == 0 || count > 1024) return 0;
    auto it = names_.find(table);
    if (it == names_.end()) {
        if (names_.size() > 2048) names_.clear();
        std::vector<std::uint32_t> raw(count * 2);
        auto& map = names_[table];
        if (read(table, raw.data(), raw.size() * 4)) {
            for (std::uint32_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
                if (raw[2 * k + 1] < count) map[raw[2 * k]] = static_cast<int>(raw[2 * k + 1]);
            }
        }
        it = names_.find(table);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const auto b = it->second.find(crcs[i]);
        float p[3];
        if (b == it->second.end() || !read(model + b->second * 0x20ull + 0x10, p, sizeof(p))) continue;
        out[i] = m.pos + m.right * p[0] + m.fwd * p[1] + m.up * p[2];
        have[i] = true;
    }
    return static_cast<int>(count);
}

const Skeletons::Bones& Skeletons::bones(std::uintptr_t table, std::uint32_t count) {
    auto it = tables_.find(table);
    if (it != tables_.end()) return it->second;
    if (tables_.size() > 2048) tables_.clear();
    Bones b;
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> raw(count * 2);
    if (read(table, raw.data(), raw.size() * 4)) {
        static const std::uint32_t kHead = crc32("Head"), kHeadEnd = crc32("HeadEnd"), kNeck = crc32("Neck"),
                                   kSpine2 = crc32("Spine2"), kHips = crc32("Hips");
        for (std::uint32_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
            const std::uint32_t h = raw[2 * k], idx = raw[2 * k + 1];
            const int i = idx < count ? static_cast<int>(idx) : -1;
            if (h == kHead) b.head = i;
            else if (h == kHeadEnd) b.headEnd = i;
            else if (h == kNeck) b.neck = i;
            else if (h == kSpine2) b.spine2 = i;
            else if (h == kHips) b.hips = i;
        }
    }
    return tables_[table] = b;
}

bool Skeletons::fromSkeleton(std::uintptr_t entity, double now, AimPoints& out, const char** reason) {
    *reason = "no skeleton";
    Transform m;
    const std::uintptr_t s = skeletonInstance(entity);
    if (!s || !transform(entity + 0x30, m)) return false;
    const std::uint64_t local = u64(s + 0x68), model = u64(s + 0x78), table = u64(s + 0xE0);
    const std::uint32_t count = u32(s + 0xEC);
    // The pose buffers are allocated back to back: their spacing confirms the bone count.
    if (!model || !local || !table || count == 0 || count > 1024 || model - local != count * 0x20ull) return false;
    struct Bone {
        float quat[4];
        float pos[4];
    };
    std::vector<Bone> pose(count);
    if (!read(model, pose.data(), count * sizeof(Bone))) {
        *reason = "unreadable";
        return false;
    }
    for (const Bone& b : pose) {
        for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
            if (!std::isfinite(b.pos[k]) || std::fabs(b.pos[k]) >= 10.f) {
                *reason = "garbage";
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    const Bones& idx = bones(table, count);
    // Liveness digest over the aim bones, in index order (as tools/skeleton.py).
    int used[5] = {idx.head, idx.headEnd, idx.neck, idx.spine2, idx.hips};
    std::sort(used, used + 5);
    std::uint32_t digest = 0;
    for (int i : used) {
        if (i >= 0) digest = crc32(&pose[i], sizeof(Bone), digest);
    }
    const char* state = "new";
    auto it = live_.find(model);
    if (it == live_.end()) {
        if (live_.size() > 4096) live_.clear();
        live_[model] = {digest, now, now};
    } else if (it->second.digest != digest) {
        it->second.digest = digest;
        it->second.changed = now;
        state = "live";
    } else if (now - it->second.changed < kLiveWindow) {
        state = "live";
    } else if (now - it->second.first >= kLiveWindow) {
        state = "frozen";
    }
    auto world = [&](int i) {
        const float* p = pose[i].pos;
        return m.pos + m.right * p[0] + m.fwd * p[1] + m.up * p[2];
    };
    if (idx.hips < 0 || idx.neck < 0 || idx.head < 0) {
        *reason = "missing bones";
        return false;
    }
    const Vec3 hips = world(idx.hips), neck = world(idx.neck), head = world(idx.head);
    const Vec3 centre = idx.headEnd >= 0 ? mid(head, world(idx.headEnd)) : head + (head - neck) * 0.7f;
    const Vec3 chest = idx.spine2 >= 0 ? world(idx.spine2) : mid(hips, neck);
    const float spine = dist(hips, neck), skull = dist(neck, head);
    const float top = idx.headEnd >= 0 ? dist(head, world(idx.headEnd)) : 0.1f;
    if (!(spine > 0.3f && spine < 0.8f && skull < 0.3f && top < 0.3f)) {
        *reason = "anatomy";
        return false;
    }
    if (dist(hips, m.pos) > 1.5f) {
        *reason = "away";
        return false;
    }
    if (std::strcmp(state, "frozen") == 0) {
        *reason = "frozen";
        return false;
    }
    *reason = state;
    out = {};
    out.source = PointSource::kSkeleton;
    out.count = 4;
    static constexpr const char* kLabels[4] = {"head", "neck", "chest", "pelvis"};
    const Vec3 pts[4] = {centre, neck, chest, hips};
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        out.label[i] = kLabels[i];
        out.p[i] = pts[i];
    }
    return true;
}

AimPoints Skeletons::aimPoints(std::uintptr_t entity, const Vec3& fallback, double now, const char** reason) {
    AimPoints out;
    const char* why;
    if (reason == nullptr) reason = &why;
    if (fromSkeleton(entity, now, out, reason)) return out;
    Transform m;
    const bool haveM = transform(entity + 0x30, m);
    if (haveM && fromRagdoll(entity, m, out)) return out;
    const Vec3 base = haveM ? m.pos : fallback;
    const Vec3 up = haveM ? m.up : Vec3{0, 0, 1};
    static constexpr const char* kLabels[3] = {"head", "chest", "pelvis"};
    static constexpr float kHeights[3] = {1.6f, 1.25f, 0.9f};
    out = {};
    out.source = PointSource::kEstimate;
    out.count = 3;
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        out.label[i] = kLabels[i];
        out.p[i] = base + up * kHeights[i];
    }
    return out;
}

}  // namespace fc6::game

```

`src/internal/state.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Switches shared by the DLL's threads: the overlay thread (hotkeys, menu) sets them, the aimbot
// thread reads them. Settings live in fc6hack.ini; a change bumps settingsVersion and the
// aimbot reloads them on its next tick.

#include <atomic>

namespace fc6::state {

inline std::atomic<bool> aimOn{true};
inline std::atomic<bool> espOn{false};
inline std::atomic<bool> hudOn{true};
inline std::atomic<bool> menuOpen{false};
inline std::atomic<int> settingsVersion{0};

}  // namespace fc6::state

```

`src/internal/targeting.cpp`:

```cpp
// Target choice and ballistics, see targeting.hpp.

#include "internal/targeting.hpp"

#include "internal/rays.hpp"

#include "internal/config.hpp"

#include <windows.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
#include <mutex>
#include <numbers>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

namespace fc6::aim {
namespace {

constexpr std::uint32_t kBulletFilter = 0x11FBB;  // collision filter of the game's bullet traces
constexpr float kRayStart = 1.0f;       // m in front of the eye: clear the player's body and weapon
// The camera that far from the bullets' origin is a third-person one (driving): rays start further
// out, past the player's own vehicle, which the game's bullets go through.
constexpr float kDetachedCamera = 1.0f, kRayStartDetached = 2.0f;
// A seated target (its head this close above its feet: in a vehicle) with the ray stopping this
// close to its axis: its vehicle's window or door, which bullets go through (hit log: occupants
// shot through their window, the ray stopping 0.7-1.1 m short of the head at seat height).
constexpr float kSeatedTop = 1.05f, kVehicleRadius = 1.6f;
constexpr float kSelfHitMargin = 0.45f;  // a ray stopping this close to the point hit the target
constexpr float kTargetRadius = 0.9f;   // m around the target's vertical axis: its own body
constexpr float kMaxTargetSpeed = 40.f;  // m/s; faster is a teleport, not motion
constexpr double kMotionWindow = 0.1;

// Where a ray stopped against the target's axis: radial distance and height above its feet.
void axisPosition(const Vec3& hit, const Vec3& base, const Vec3& up, float& radial, float& height) {
    const Vec3 rel = hit - base;
    height = rel.dot(up);
    radial = std::sqrt(std::max(0.f, rel.dot(rel) - height * height));
}

bool hitsTarget(const Vec3& hit, const Vec3& base, const Vec3& up, float top) {
    float radial, h;
    axisPosition(hit, base, up, radial, h);
    return radial < kTargetRadius && h > -0.2f && h < top + 0.4f;
}

// The first blocked ray of a candidate, for the log (why a character in the cone was left).
struct RayNote {
    bool blocked = false, ownBody = false;  // ownBody: stopped on the target's cylinder, counted visible
    float stop = 0, point = 0, radial = 0, height = 0;
    std::uint64_t object = 0;
    // The game's result record (layout unknown past the fraction at +0x00..+0x10): the fields that
    // may tell the surface (glass, foliage...) from a wall; see the "hit" log lines.
    std::uint32_t w18 = 0, w28 = 0;
    std::uint64_t obj50 = 0;
    std::uintptr_t objVt = 0;
};

// Index of the first point with a clear line from the eye, -1 if none. base / up: the target's
// feet and up axis (hasBase false: unknown), to recognise rays that stop on the target itself.
int visiblePoint(bool useRays, const Vec3& eye, float rayStart, const game::AimPoints& pts, const int* order,
                 bool hasBase, const Vec3& base, const Vec3& up, RayNote& note) {
    if (!useRays) return order[0];
    float top = 0.f;
    if (hasBase) {
        for (int i = 0; i < pts.count; ++i) top = std::max(top, (pts.p[i] - base).dot(up));
    }
    const bool seated = hasBase && top > 0.f && top < kSeatedTop;
    for (int k = 0; k < pts.count; ++k) {
        const Vec3& p = pts.p[order[k]];
        const Vec3 d = p - eye;
        const float n = d.length();
        const Vec3 start = eye + d * (rayStart / (n > 0 ? n : 1.f));
        Ray ray{{start.x, start.y, start.z, 0.f}, {p.x, p.y, p.z, 0.f}};
        Hit h{};
        if (!rays::cast(ray, kBulletFilter, h)) {
            if (!h.fault) return order[0];  // no router on this thread: don't block aiming
            continue;
        }
        if (!h.hit) return order[k];
        const Vec3 at{h.position[0], h.position[1], h.position[2]};
        if (game::dist(start, at) >= game::dist(start, p) - kSelfHitMargin) return order[k];
        bool own = hasBase && hitsTarget(at, base, up, top);
        if (!own && seated) {
            float radial, height;
            axisPosition(at, base, up, radial, height);
            own = radial < kVehicleRadius && height > -0.3f && height < top + 0.6f;
        }
        if (!note.blocked) {
            note.blocked = true;
            note.ownBody = own;
            note.stop = game::dist(eye, at);
            note.point = n;
            note.object = h.object;
            std::memcpy(&note.w18, h.raw + 0x18, 4);
            std::memcpy(&note.w28, h.raw + 0x28, 4);
            std::memcpy(&note.obj50, h.raw + 0x50, 8);
            note.objVt = game::vt(note.obj50);
            if (hasBase) axisPosition(at, base, up, note.radial, note.height);
        }
        if (own) return order[k];
    }
    return -1;
}

std::mutex g_verdictMutex;
std::vector<VerdictNote> g_verdicts;
double g_verdictTime = 0;

double seconds() {
    LARGE_INTEGER f, n;
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f);
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&n);
    return static_cast<double>(n.QuadPart) / static_cast<double>(f.QuadPart);
}

const char* shortName(const char* name) {
    const char* dot = std::strrchr(name, '.');
    return dot ? dot + 1 : name;
}

// A blocked ray in the log, once a second per character: what stopped it and where.
void logRay(const game::Character& c, const RayNote& note, double now) {
    static std::unordered_map<std::uintptr_t, double> last;
    if (last.size() > 512) last.clear();
    double& t = last[c.entity];
    if (now - t < 1.0) return;
    t = now;
    config::log("line of sight: %s at %.1f m %s: ray stopped at %.1f m (%.2f m short), %.2f m from its axis at"
                " %.2f m high; record +18 %08x +28 %08x +50 %llx (vt +%llx)",
                shortName(c.name), note.point, note.ownBody ? "counted visible (stopped on its own cylinder)" : "hidden",
                note.stop, note.point - note.stop, note.radial, note.height, note.w18, note.w28,
                static_cast<unsigned long long>(note.obj50), static_cast<unsigned long long>(note.objVt));
}

// The oracle for the line of sight: a hostile that loses health was hit, so when the last ray to
// it had stopped on something, the bullet went through that surface. Logged with the surface's
// record fields (aim thread only).
struct Track {
    float hp = -1;
    RayNote block;     // its last blocked ray
    double blockAt = 0;
    bool visible = false;  // its last line-of-sight check
    double checkedAt = 0;
};
std::unordered_map<std::uintptr_t, Track> g_track;

void checked(std::uintptr_t entity, const RayNote& rn, bool visible, double now) {
    Track& t = g_track[entity];
    t.visible = visible;
    t.checkedAt = now;
    if (rn.blocked) {
        t.block = rn;
        t.blockAt = now;
    }
}

void watchHealth(const game::Character& c, double now) {
    if (g_track.size() > 1024) g_track.clear();
    float hp, hpMax;
    if (!game::health(c.entity, hp, hpMax)) return;
    Track& t = g_track[c.entity];
    if (t.hp >= 0 && hp < t.hp - 0.01f) {
        const bool recent = now - t.blockAt < 1.0;
        config::log("hit: %s lost %.0f hp (%.0f -> %.0f); last check %.1f s ago said %s%s", shortName(c.name),
                    t.hp - hp, t.hp, hp, now - t.checkedAt, t.visible ? "visible" : "hidden",
                    recent ? "" : " (no blocked ray in the last second)");
        if (recent) {
            const RayNote& b = t.block;
            config::log("hit:   its ray had stopped %.2f m short of the point (%.2f m from its axis, %.2f m high%s);"
                        " record +18 %08x +28 %08x +50 %llx (vt +%llx)",
                        b.point - b.stop, b.radial, b.height, b.ownBody ? ", counted as its body" : "", b.w18, b.w28,
                        static_cast<unsigned long long>(b.obj50), static_cast<unsigned long long>(b.objVt));
        }
    }
    t.hp = hp;
}

}  // namespace

int verdicts(VerdictNote* out, int max) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_verdictMutex);
    if (seconds() - g_verdictTime > 0.5) return 0;
    const int n = std::min(max, static_cast<int>(g_verdicts.size()));
    std::copy(g_verdicts.begin(), g_verdicts.begin() + n, out);
    return n;
}

namespace {

}  // namespace

float wrap(float a) {
    constexpr float pi = std::numbers::pi_v<float>;
    a = std::fmod(a + pi, 2 * pi);
    if (a < 0) a += 2 * pi;
    return a - pi;
}

void viewAngles(const Vec3& fwd, float& yaw, float& pitch) {
    yaw = std::atan2(-fwd.x, fwd.y);
    pitch = std::asin(std::clamp(fwd.z, -1.f, 1.f));
}

void anglesTo(const Vec3& eye, const Vec3& p, float& yaw, float& pitch) {
    const Vec3 d = p - eye;
    yaw = std::atan2(-d.x, d.y);
    pitch = std::atan2(d.z, std::hypot(d.x, d.y));
}

Vec3 Motion::velocity(std::uintptr_t entity, const Vec3& pos, double now) {
    auto& h = hist_[entity];
    if (!h.empty() && now - h.back().first > 0.5) h.clear();  // unseen for a while
    h.emplace_back(now, pos);
    while (h.size() > 2 && now - h[1].first >= kMotionWindow) h.pop_front();
    const double dt = now - h.front().first;
    if (dt < 0.03) return {};
    const Vec3 v = (pos - h.front().second) * static_cast<float>(1.0 / dt);
    return v.length() < kMaxTargetSpeed ? v : Vec3{};
}

void Motion::prune(double now) {
    for (auto it = hist_.begin(); it != hist_.end();) {
        it = it->second.empty() || now - it->second.back().first > 2.0 ? hist_.erase(it) : std::next(it);
    }
}

Vec3 leadPoint(const Vec3& eye, const Vec3& point, const Vec3& velocity, float speed, float gravity,
               float dropDistance, float& flight) {
    flight = 0.f;
    if (speed <= 0.f) return point;
    float t = game::dist(eye, point) / speed;
    Vec3 aim = point;
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        aim = point + velocity * t;
        const float fall = std::max(0.f, t - dropDistance / speed);
        aim.z += 0.5f * gravity * fall * fall;
        t = game::dist(eye, aim) / speed;
    }
    flight = t;
    return aim;
}

bool pickTarget(const game::Camera& cam, const PickParams& params, game::Skeletons& skeletons, double now,
                Pick& out) {
    const Vec3 eye = cam.m.pos;
    float yaw, pitch;
    viewAngles(cam.m.fwd, yaw, pitch);
    static game::Character list[256];
    const int n = game::characters(list, 256);
    struct Cand {
        float off, dist, score, headChance;
        int index;
        bool kept, threat;
        game::AimPoints pts;
        int order[4];
        Vec3 vel, base, up;
        bool hasBase;
    };
    std::vector<Cand> cands;
    std::vector<VerdictNote> notes;
    auto note = [&](std::uintptr_t entity, Verdict v) { notes.push_back({entity, v}); };
    // Published on every return.
    struct Publish {
        std::vector<VerdictNote>& notes;
        double now;
        ~Publish() {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_verdictMutex);
            g_verdicts.swap(notes);
            g_verdictTime = seconds();
        }
    } publish{notes, now};
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const game::Character& c = list[i];
        if (!std::strstr(c.name, "NPC")) continue;
        watchHealth(c, now);  // before the dead are skipped: a kill is a hit too
        if (game::isDead(c.entity)) continue;
        if (!game::isHostile(c.entity, c.name)) {
            note(c.entity, Verdict::kNotEnemy);
            continue;
        }
        Cand cd{};
        cd.index = i;
        cd.kept = params.keep && c.entity == params.keep;
        // Far outside the cone or the range: skipped before the skeleton is read (120 times a
        // second for every hostile otherwise). The aim points are within 2 m of c.point.
        const float rough = game::dist(eye, c.point);
        if (rough > params.maxDist + 3.f) {
            note(c.entity, Verdict::kTooFar);
            continue;
        }
        float ay, ap;
        anglesTo(eye, c.point, ay, ap);
        if (std::hypot(wrap(ay - yaw), ap - pitch) - std::atan2(2.f, std::max(rough, 0.5f)) >
            params.fov * (cd.kept ? kKeepCone : 1.f)) {
            note(c.entity, Verdict::kOutOfCone);
            continue;
        }
        cd.pts = skeletons.aimPoints(c.entity, c.point, now);
        game::Transform m;
        cd.hasBase = game::transform(c.entity + 0x30, m);
        cd.base = cd.hasBase ? m.pos : Vec3{};
        cd.up = cd.hasBase ? m.up : Vec3{0, 0, 1};
        cd.vel = params.motion ? params.motion->velocity(c.entity, cd.hasBase ? m.pos : c.point, now) : Vec3{};
        cd.dist = game::dist(eye, cd.pts.p[0]);
        if (cd.dist > params.maxDist || cd.dist < 1.f) {
            note(c.entity, Verdict::kTooFar);
            continue;
        }
        for (int k = 0; k < 4; ++k) cd.order[k] = k;
        cd.headChance = params.spread ? params.spread->hitChance(kHeadRadius, cd.dist) : 1.f;
        if (cd.headChance < 0.5f) {
            std::stable_sort(cd.order, cd.order + cd.pts.count,
                             [&](int a, int b) { return std::strcmp(cd.pts.label[a], "chest") == 0 &&
                                                        std::strcmp(cd.pts.label[b], "chest") != 0; });
        }
        float ty, tp;
        anglesTo(eye, cd.pts.p[0], ty, tp);
        cd.off = std::hypot(wrap(ty - yaw), tp - pitch);
        // Its AI aims at the player: first. Not alerted yet (attitude 1 with a hostile archetype):
        // after the ones fighting now.
        cd.threat = params.player && game::aiTarget(c.entity) == params.player;
        if (params.threatsFirst) {
            cd.score = cd.off * (cd.threat                                             ? 0.25f
                                 : game::attitude(c.entity) == game::kAttitudeHostile ? 1.f
                                                                                      : 3.f);
        } else {
            cd.score = cd.off;
        }
        if (cd.off <= params.fov * (cd.kept ? kKeepCone : 1.f)) {
            cands.push_back(cd);
        } else {
            note(c.entity, Verdict::kOutOfCone);
        }
    }
    if (params.motion) params.motion->prune(now);
    std::sort(cands.begin(), cands.end(), [](const Cand& a, const Cand& b) {
        return a.kept != b.kept ? a.kept : a.score < b.score;
    });
    out = {};
    bool found = false;
    // Rays from where the bullets start.
    const Vec3 from = params.haveOrigin ? params.origin : eye;
    const float rayStart = game::dist(from, eye) > kDetachedCamera ? kRayStartDetached : kRayStart;
    for (std::size_t ci = 0; ci < cands.size(); ++ci) {
        const Cand& cd = cands[ci];
        const game::Character& c = list[cd.index];
        if (found) {  // checkAll: the rest only for their verdicts
            RayNote rn;
            const bool seen = visiblePoint(params.rays, from, rayStart, cd.pts, cd.order, cd.hasBase, cd.base, cd.up,
                                           rn) >= 0;
            checked(c.entity, rn, seen, now);
            note(c.entity, seen ? Verdict::kVisible : Verdict::kHidden);
            continue;
        }
        RayNote rn;
        const int v = visiblePoint(params.rays, from, rayStart, cd.pts, cd.order, cd.hasBase, cd.base, cd.up, rn);
        checked(c.entity, rn, v >= 0, now);
        if (rn.blocked && !params.checkAll) logRay(c, rn, now);
        if (v < 0) {
            note(c.entity, Verdict::kHidden);
            if (cd.kept && params.waitHidden) {
                out.hidden = true;
                for (std::size_t k = ci + 1; k < cands.size(); ++k) note(list[cands[k].index].entity, Verdict::kUnchecked);
                return false;
            }
            continue;
        }
        note(c.entity, Verdict::kTarget);
        found = true;
        out = {};
        out.off = cd.off;
        out.headChance = cd.headChance;
        out.threat = cd.threat;
        out.dist = cd.dist;
        out.entity = list[cd.index].entity;
        std::memcpy(out.name, list[cd.index].name, sizeof(out.name));
        out.part = cd.pts.label[v];
        out.velocity = cd.vel;
        out.partPos = cd.pts.p[v];
        out.aim = leadPoint(eye, out.partPos, cd.vel, params.speed, params.gravity, params.dropDistance, out.flight);
        float ty, tp;
        anglesTo(eye, out.aim, ty, tp);
        out.dy = wrap(ty - yaw);
        out.dp = tp - pitch;
        if (params.checkAll) continue;
        for (std::size_t k = ci + 1; k < cands.size(); ++k) note(list[cands[k].index].entity, Verdict::kUnchecked);
        return true;
    }
    return found;
}

}  // namespace fc6::aim

```

`src/internal/targeting.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

// Target choice and ballistics (port of tools/aim.py: Motion, lead_point, visible_point,
// pick_target). Angles in radians; yaw = atan2(-fwd.x, fwd.y), pitch = asin(fwd.z).

#include "internal/game.hpp"

#include <deque>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <utility>

namespace fc6::aim {

inline constexpr float kHeadRadius = 0.10f;  // m: chest first when most shots would miss it
inline constexpr float kKeepCone = 2.f;      // the locked target stays locked within this x the cone

float wrap(float a);
void viewAngles(const Vec3& fwd, float& yaw, float& pitch);
void anglesTo(const Vec3& eye, const Vec3& p, float& yaw, float& pitch);

// Velocity of each target from its root position over the last 0.1 s.
class Motion {
public:
    Vec3 velocity(std::uintptr_t entity, const Vec3& pos, double now);
    void prune(double now);

private:
    std::unordered_map<std::uintptr_t, std::deque<std::pair<double, Vec3>>> hist_;
};

// Where to aim so a bullet fired now from the eye meets a target at point moving at velocity:
// straight flight at speed, falling by gravity t^2 / 2 past dropDistance.
Vec3 leadPoint(const Vec3& eye, const Vec3& point, const Vec3& velocity, float speed, float gravity,
               float dropDistance, float& flight);

struct PickParams {
    float fov = 0, maxDist = 0;
    float speed = 0, gravity = 0, dropDistance = 0;
    const game::Spread* spread = nullptr;  // the weapon's spread now; null = head only
    bool rays = false;                     // line of sight with the game's collision
    Motion* motion = nullptr;              // null: no lead
    std::uintptr_t keep = 0;   // locked target: chosen first while alive, visible, within kKeepCone x the cone
    bool waitHidden = false;   // the locked target is out of sight: no other target (it may reappear)
    bool threatsFirst = false;  // the NPCs whose AI targets the player first, then the alerted ones
    std::uintptr_t player = 0;  // for threatsFirst
    bool checkAll = false;      // line of sight of every candidate (verdicts for the ESP), not just
                                // until the first visible one
    // Where the player's bullets start (game::fireDirection): the line of sight is checked from
    // there, not from the camera, which sits behind the player's vehicle when driving.
    bool haveOrigin = false;
    Vec3 origin;
};

// What the last target choice decided for each NPC, shown by the ESP: why the aimbot takes or
// leaves a character.
enum class Verdict : std::uint8_t { kNone, kTarget, kVisible, kHidden, kUnchecked, kOutOfCone, kTooFar, kNotEnemy };
struct VerdictNote {
    std::uintptr_t entity = 0;
    Verdict verdict = Verdict::kNone;
};
// The last choice's verdicts (any thread); none when older than half a second.
int verdicts(VerdictNote* out, int max);

struct Pick {
    float off = 0, dy = 0, dp = 0, dist = 0, flight = 0;  // dy / dp: yaw / pitch error to the aim point
    float headChance = 1;  // fraction of the shots that would hit the head (spread)
    bool threat = false;   // its AI targets the player
    bool hidden = false;   // keep was a candidate but out of sight (pickTarget returned false)
    std::uintptr_t entity = 0;
    char name[game::kNameLen] = {};
    const char* part = "";
    Vec3 velocity, partPos, aim;
};

// Hostile, alive characters in the cone: the locked one (params.keep) first, then the closest to
// the crosshair (with threatsFirst: those targeting the player, then the alerted ones, then the
// others); the first one with an exposed part wins.
// The head is aimed at unless most shots would miss it (spread), then the chest first.
bool pickTarget(const game::Camera& cam, const PickParams& params, game::Skeletons& skeletons, double now,
                Pick& out);

}  // namespace fc6::aim

```

`src/preview/main.cpp`:

```cpp
// fc6preview: draws the overlay's menu, HUD and cone into a PNG, with sample aimbot lines, to check
// the layout without the game.
//
//   fc6preview out.png [background.png]
//
// The menu shows the values of fc6hack.ini next to the executable (build/Release), like the DLL.

#include "internal/hud_view.hpp"
#include "internal/menu.hpp"
#include "internal/painter.hpp"
#include "internal/state.hpp"

#include <windows.h>
#include <d2d1.h>
#include <dwrite.h>
#include <wincodec.h>

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>

namespace {

template <class T>
void release(T*& p) {
    if (p) p->Release();
    p = nullptr;
}

IWICBitmapSource* loadPng(IWICImagingFactory* wic, const wchar_t* file) {
    IWICBitmapDecoder* decoder = nullptr;
    IWICBitmapFrameDecode* frame = nullptr;
    IWICFormatConverter* converter = nullptr;
    if (FAILED(wic->CreateDecoderFromFilename(file, nullptr, GENERIC_READ, WICDecodeMetadataCacheOnLoad, &decoder)) ||
        FAILED(decoder->GetFrame(0, &frame)) || FAILED(wic->CreateFormatConverter(&converter)) ||
        FAILED(converter->Initialize(frame, GUID_WICPixelFormat32bppPBGRA, WICBitmapDitherTypeNone, nullptr, 0.0,
                                     WICBitmapPaletteTypeCustom))) {
        release(converter);
        converter = nullptr;
    }
    release(frame);
    release(decoder);
    return converter;
}

bool savePng(IWICImagingFactory* wic, IWICBitmap* bitmap, const wchar_t* file) {
    IWICStream* stream = nullptr;
    IWICBitmapEncoder* encoder = nullptr;
    IWICBitmapFrameEncode* frame = nullptr;
    WICPixelFormatGUID format = GUID_WICPixelFormat32bppPBGRA;
    const bool ok = SUCCEEDED(wic->CreateStream(&stream)) &&
                    SUCCEEDED(stream->InitializeFromFilename(file, GENERIC_WRITE)) &&
                    SUCCEEDED(wic->CreateEncoder(GUID_ContainerFormatPng, nullptr, &encoder)) &&
                    SUCCEEDED(encoder->Initialize(stream, WICBitmapEncoderNoCache)) &&
                    SUCCEEDED(encoder->CreateNewFrame(&frame, nullptr)) && SUCCEEDED(frame->Initialize(nullptr)) &&
                    SUCCEEDED(frame->SetPixelFormat(&format)) && SUCCEEDED(frame->WriteSource(bitmap, nullptr)) &&
                    SUCCEEDED(frame->Commit()) && SUCCEEDED(encoder->Commit());
    release(frame);
    release(encoder);
    release(stream);
    return ok;
}

void sample(fc6::Shared& s) {
    std::snprintf(s.hudWeapon, sizeof(s.hudWeapon), "%s",
                  "FAL FullMetalJacket  4920 m/s  g 15.0  portée 288 m  (jeu 615 x1.00 x8.00, g 15.0 x1.00 x1.00)"
                  "  dispersion 2.5-4.0°, en visée 0.0-0.0°");
    std::snprintf(s.hudTarget, sizeof(s.hudTarget), "%s",
                  "Cuban_Hostile  64 m  torse (dispersion 3.0°, tête 38%)  vol 13 ms  vitesse 2.1 m/s  canon +1/-2 cm");
    const std::uint64_t now = GetTickCount64();
    const char* events[] = {"aimbot activé", "souris x1.02 au zoom x2.17, gain x2.88 (1.00 = juste)", "tir HIT tête 64 m"};
    const std::uint64_t ages[] = {17000, 9000, 1000};
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        fc6::HudEvent& e = s.hudEvents[fc6::kHudEvents - 3 + i];
        e.tick = now - ages[i];
        std::snprintf(e.text, sizeof(e.text), "%s", events[i]);
    }
}

}  // namespace

int wmain(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    using namespace fc6::overlay;
    if (argc < 2) {
        std::fwprintf(stderr, L"usage: fc6preview out.png [background.png]\n");
        return 2;
    }
    if (FAILED(CoInitializeEx(nullptr, COINIT_MULTITHREADED))) return 1;
    IWICImagingFactory* wic = nullptr;
    ID2D1Factory* d2d = nullptr;
    IDWriteFactory* dwrite = nullptr;
    if (FAILED(CoCreateInstance(CLSID_WICImagingFactory, nullptr, CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER, IID_PPV_ARGS(&wic))) ||
        FAILED(D2D1CreateFactory(D2D1_FACTORY_TYPE_SINGLE_THREADED, &d2d)) ||
        FAILED(DWriteCreateFactory(DWRITE_FACTORY_TYPE_SHARED, __uuidof(IDWriteFactory),
                                   reinterpret_cast<IUnknown**>(&dwrite)))) {
        std::fwprintf(stderr, L"WIC / Direct2D / DirectWrite unavailable\n");
        return 1;
    }
    IWICBitmapSource* background = argc > 2 ? loadPng(wic, argv[2]) : nullptr;
    UINT width = 1920, height = 1080;
    if (background) background->GetSize(&width, &height);
    IWICBitmap* bitmap = nullptr;
    ID2D1RenderTarget* rt = nullptr;
    ID2D1SolidColorBrush* brush = nullptr;
    IDWriteTextFormat* fonts[static_cast<int>(Font::kCount)] = {};
    if (FAILED(wic->CreateBitmap(width, height, GUID_WICPixelFormat32bppPBGRA, WICBitmapCacheOnLoad, &bitmap)) ||
        FAILED(d2d->CreateWicBitmapRenderTarget(bitmap, D2D1::RenderTargetProperties(), &rt)) ||
        FAILED(rt->CreateSolidColorBrush(D2D1::ColorF(0xFFFFFF), &brush)) || !createFonts(dwrite, fonts)) {
        std::fwprintf(stderr, L"drawing setup failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    auto shared = std::make_unique<fc6::Shared>();
    std::memset(shared.get(), 0, sizeof(fc6::Shared));
    sample(*shared);
    fc6::state::aimOn = true;
    fc6::state::espOn = false;

    rt->BeginDraw();
    rt->Clear(D2D1::ColorF(0x4A5A48));
    if (background) {
        ID2D1Bitmap* bg = nullptr;
        if (SUCCEEDED(rt->CreateBitmapFromWicBitmap(background, &bg))) rt->DrawBitmap(bg);
        release(bg);
    }
    Painter paint(rt, brush, dwrite, fonts, static_cast<float>(width), static_cast<float>(height));
    fc6::game::Camera cam;
    cam.sx = 1.f;  // 90 degrees across, unzoomed
    drawCone(paint, cam, 12.f, true);
    drawHud(paint, *shared, 118.f, 3.1f, Menu::right() + 20);
    Menu menu;
    menu.draw(paint, 1.0);
    const bool drawn = SUCCEEDED(rt->EndDraw());
    const bool saved = drawn && savePng(wic, bitmap, argv[1]);
    std::fwprintf(saved ? stdout : stderr, saved ? L"wrote %ls (%ux%u)\n" : L"failed to write %ls\n", argv[1], width,
                  height);
    for (auto*& f : fonts) release(f);
    release(brush);
    release(rt);
    release(bitmap);
    release(background);
    release(dwrite);
    release(d2d);
    release(wic);
    CoUninitialize();
    return saved ? 0 : 1;
}

```

`src/probe/main.cpp`:

```cpp
// fc6probe: external, read-only probe for Far Cry 6 (offline only).
//
//   fc6probe                         attach and print the engine module
//   fc6probe scan "48 8B 05 ?? ??"   find a byte signature in the engine module
//   fc6probe read <hex addr> [size]  hex dump memory (addr can be "+offset" from the module base)
//   fc6probe matrices <out.tsv>      dump every world transform (rotation + position) in the heap
//   fc6probe chain <static> <o1> .. <oN>   resolve a pointer chain and print the transform there
//   fc6probe chains <file>           same for one chain per line, all at the same instant
//   fc6probe refs <targets.txt> [hex maxoff]   who points at any of these addresses
//   fc6probe dump <out.bin>          copy the unpacked engine module from memory (local use only)
//   fc6probe near <x> <y> <z> <radius> <out.tsv>   float triples near a world point
//   fc6probe values <out.tsv> <v1> [v2..] [--tol t]   floats close to any value
//   fc6probe ints <out.tsv> <v1> [v2..] [--u16]   u32 (or u16) equal to any value
//   fc6probe world                   find hknpWorld candidates by structure
//   fc6probe ptrscan <hex addr> [levels] [hex maxoff]   static pointer chains to a heap address

#include "common/heap.hpp"
#include "common/pattern.hpp"
#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <fstream>
#include <iterator>
#include <sstream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace {

constexpr wchar_t kEngineModule[] = L"FC_m64d3d12.dll";

}  // namespace

int runMatrices(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine, const char* outPath);
int runPtrScan(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine, std::uintptr_t target, int levels,
               std::size_t maxOff);
int runNear(const fc6::Process& proc, float x, float y, float z, float radius, const char* outPath);
int runValues(const fc6::Process& proc, const char* outPath, const std::vector<float>& values, float tol);
int runInts(const fc6::Process& proc, const char* outPath, const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& values, int width);
int runWorld(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine);
int runRefs(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine, const std::vector<std::uintptr_t>& targets,
            std::size_t maxOff);

namespace {

std::uintptr_t parseAddress(const char* text, std::uintptr_t moduleBase) {
    if (text[0] == '+') return moduleBase + std::strtoull(text + 1, nullptr, 16);
    return std::strtoull(text, nullptr, 16);
}

void hexDump(const fc6::Process& proc, std::uintptr_t addr, std::size_t size) {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> buf(size);
    if (!proc.read(addr, buf.data(), size)) {
        std::printf("read failed at %llx\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(addr));
        return;
    }
    for (std::size_t row = 0; row < size; row += 16) {
        std::printf("%016llx  ", static_cast<unsigned long long>(addr + row));
        for (std::size_t i = row; i < row + 16; ++i) {
            if (i < size) std::printf("%02x ", buf[i]);
            else std::printf("   ");
        }
        std::printf(" ");
        for (std::size_t i = row; i < row + 16 && i < size; ++i) {
            std::printf("%c", (buf[i] >= 32 && buf[i] < 127) ? buf[i] : '.');
        }
        std::printf("\n");
    }
}

// <static off> <o1> ... <oN>: p = [module+static]; p = [p+o1] ...; final = p + oN.
// Prints the transform at the final address and the vtable of the object that holds it.
bool printChain(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine, const std::vector<std::string>& tokens) {
    auto hex = [](const std::string& t) { return std::strtoull(t.c_str(), nullptr, 16); };
    auto p = proc.read<std::uintptr_t>(engine.base + hex(tokens[0]));
    for (std::size_t i = 1; p && i + 1 < tokens.size(); ++i) {
        p = *p ? proc.read<std::uintptr_t>(*p + hex(tokens[i])) : std::nullopt;
    }
    float m[16];
    if (!p || !*p || !proc.read(*p + hex(tokens.back()), m, sizeof(m))) {
        std::printf("chain broken\n");
        return false;
    }
    const auto vt = proc.read<std::uintptr_t>(*p).value_or(0);
    const bool inModule = vt >= engine.base && vt < engine.base + engine.size;
    std::printf("obj vt %s%llx | right %.4f %.4f %.4f | fwd %.4f %.4f %.4f | up %.4f %.4f %.4f | pos %.3f %.3f %.3f\n",
                inModule ? "+" : "?", static_cast<unsigned long long>(inModule ? vt - engine.base : vt), m[0], m[1],
                m[2], m[4], m[5], m[6], m[8], m[9], m[10], m[12], m[13], m[14]);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    fc6::Process proc;
    if (!proc.attachByModule(kEngineModule)) {
        std::printf("Far Cry 6 is not running (no process has %ls loaded), or access was denied.\n",
                    kEngineModule);
        return 1;
    }

    auto engine = proc.module(kEngineModule);
    if (!engine) {
        std::printf("attached to pid %lu but could not read the module list\n", proc.pid());
        return 1;
    }
    std::printf("pid %lu\n%ls\n  base %016llx  size %zu MB\n", proc.pid(), engine->path.c_str(),
                static_cast<unsigned long long>(engine->base), engine->size >> 20);

    if (argc >= 3 && std::string(argv[1]) == "scan") {
        auto pattern = fc6::Pattern::parse(argv[2]);
        if (!pattern) {
            std::printf("bad pattern\n");
            return 2;
        }
        auto hits = fc6::scan(proc, engine->base, engine->size, *pattern);
        std::printf("%zu hit(s)\n", hits.size());
        for (auto hit : hits) {
            std::printf("  %016llx  (+%llx)\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(hit),
                        static_cast<unsigned long long>(hit - engine->base));
        }
    } else if (argc >= 3 && std::string(argv[1]) == "matrices") {
        return runMatrices(proc, *engine, argv[2]);
    } else if (argc >= 3 && std::string(argv[1]) == "ptrscan") {
        const int levels = argc >= 4 ? std::atoi(argv[3]) : 3;
        const std::size_t maxOff = argc >= 5 ? std::strtoull(argv[4], nullptr, 16) : 0x800;
        return runPtrScan(proc, *engine, parseAddress(argv[2], engine->base), levels, maxOff);
    } else if (argc >= 4 && std::string(argv[1]) == "chain") {
        std::vector<std::string> tokens(argv + 2, argv + argc);
        return printChain(proc, *engine, tokens) ? 0 : 3;
    } else if (argc >= 3 && std::string(argv[1]) == "dump") {
        // Writes the engine module as it is in memory (unpacked), at its in-memory layout.
        // Unreadable pages stay zero. The file is for local analysis only: never share it.
        std::ofstream out(argv[2], std::ios::binary);
        std::vector<std::uint8_t> zeros(engine->size, 0);
        out.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(zeros.data()), static_cast<std::streamsize>(zeros.size()));
        std::size_t copied = 0;
        fc6::forEachChunk(proc, fc6::moduleRegions(proc.pid(), *engine), 0,
                          [&](std::uintptr_t base, const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
                              out.seekp(static_cast<std::streamoff>(base - engine->base));
                              out.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(data), static_cast<std::streamsize>(len));
                              copied += len;
                          });
        std::printf("dumped %zu MB of %zu MB to %s\n", copied >> 20, engine->size >> 20, argv[2]);
    } else if (argc >= 2 && std::string(argv[1]) == "world") {
        return runWorld(proc, *engine);
    } else if (argc >= 4 && std::string(argv[1]) == "values") {
        std::vector<float> values;
        float tol = 1e-4f;
        for (int i = 3; i < argc; ++i) {
            if (std::string(argv[i]) == "--tol" && i + 1 < argc) tol = std::strtof(argv[++i], nullptr);
            else values.push_back(std::strtof(argv[i], nullptr));
        }
        return runValues(proc, argv[2], values, tol);
    } else if (argc >= 4 && std::string(argv[1]) == "ints") {
        std::vector<std::uint32_t> values;
        int width = 4;
        for (int i = 3; i < argc; ++i) {
            if (std::string(argv[i]) == "--u16") width = 2;
            else values.push_back(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::strtoul(argv[i], nullptr, 10)));
        }
        return runInts(proc, argv[2], values, width);
    } else if (argc >= 7 && std::string(argv[1]) == "near") {
        return runNear(proc, std::strtof(argv[2], nullptr), std::strtof(argv[3], nullptr), std::strtof(argv[4], nullptr),
                       std::strtof(argv[5], nullptr), argv[6]);
    } else if (argc >= 3 && std::string(argv[1]) == "refs") {
        std::ifstream in(argv[2]);
        std::vector<std::uintptr_t> targets;
        for (std::string line; std::getline(in, line);) {
            if (!line.empty()) targets.push_back(std::strtoull(line.c_str(), nullptr, 16));
        }
        return runRefs(proc, *engine, targets, argc >= 4 ? std::strtoull(argv[3], nullptr, 16) : 0);
    } else if (argc >= 3 && std::string(argv[1]) == "chains") {
        // One chain per line, all resolved in the same instant so their values can be compared.
        std::ifstream in(argv[2]);
        for (std::string line; std::getline(in, line);) {
            std::istringstream ss(line);
            std::vector<std::string> tokens{std::istream_iterator<std::string>(ss), {}};
            if (tokens.size() < 2) continue;
            std::printf("%-28s :: ", line.c_str());
            printChain(proc, *engine, tokens);
        }
    } else if (argc >= 2 && std::string(argv[1]) == "regions") {
        HANDLE h = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, FALSE, proc.pid());
        MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
        std::size_t count = 0, total = 0, biggest = 0;
        for (std::uintptr_t a = 0;
             VirtualQueryEx(h, reinterpret_cast<LPCVOID>(a), &mbi, sizeof(mbi)) == sizeof(mbi);
             a = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(mbi.BaseAddress) + mbi.RegionSize) {
            if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && mbi.Type == MEM_PRIVATE && mbi.Protect == PAGE_READWRITE) {
                ++count;
                total += mbi.RegionSize;
                biggest = std::max<std::size_t>(biggest, mbi.RegionSize);
            }
        }
        CloseHandle(h);
        std::printf("private RW regions: %zu, total %zu MB, biggest %zu MB\n", count, total >> 20,
                    biggest >> 20);
    } else if (argc >= 3 && std::string(argv[1]) == "read") {
        const std::size_t size = argc >= 4 ? std::strtoull(argv[3], nullptr, 0) : 64;
        hexDump(proc, parseAddress(argv[2], engine->base), size);
    }
    return 0;
}

```

`src/probe/matrices.cpp`:

```cpp
// `fc6probe matrices <out.tsv>`: finds world transforms in the game heap.
//
// Entities, the camera and bones all store a rotation + position. A 3x3 rotation is easy to
// recognise in raw memory (three unit rows, mutually orthogonal), so we scan every 4-byte
// aligned address of the heap for it, in two layouts:
//   R44: 4x4 row-major, translation in row 3, w column = (0,0,0,1)   (DirectX style)
//   R34: 3x4 row-major, translation in column 3
// For each hit we also look backwards for the owning object's vtable (a pointer into the engine
// module), so hits can later be grouped by object type.

#include "common/heap.hpp"
#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <unordered_set>

namespace {

constexpr float kEps = 2e-3f;

bool unit(const float* v) {
    const float n = v[0] * v[0] + v[1] * v[1] + v[2] * v[2];
    return std::fabs(n - 1.f) < kEps;
}

bool ortho(const float* a, const float* b) {
    return std::fabs(a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2]) < kEps;
}

bool finite(const float* f, int n) {
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (!std::isfinite(f[i])) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool isRotation(const float* r0, const float* r1, const float* r2) {
    if (!unit(r0) || !unit(r1) || !unit(r2)) return false;
    if (!ortho(r0, r1) || !ortho(r0, r2) || !ortho(r1, r2)) return false;
    // Reject exact identity: millions of default-initialised transforms look like this.
    return !(r0[0] == 1.f && r1[1] == 1.f && r2[2] == 1.f);
}

bool plausiblePosition(float x, float y, float z) {
    const float m = std::fabs(x) + std::fabs(y) + std::fabs(z);
    return m > 1.f && std::fabs(x) < 1e5f && std::fabs(y) < 1e5f && std::fabs(z) < 1e5f;
}

}  // namespace

int runMatrices(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine, const char* outPath) {
    FILE* out = std::fopen(outPath, "w");
    if (!out) {
        std::printf("cannot write %s\n", outPath);
        return 1;
    }
    std::fprintf(out, "addr\tlayout\tpx\tpy\tpz\tr0x\tr0y\tr0z\tr1x\tr1y\tr1z\tr2x\tr2y\tr2z\tvtable\tvt_dist\n");

    const auto regions = fc6::writableRegions(proc.pid());
    std::unordered_set<std::uintptr_t> seen;
    std::size_t hits = 0;

    auto vtableOf = [&](std::uintptr_t addr, std::uintptr_t& vt, std::size_t& dist) {
        constexpr std::size_t kBack = 0x400;
        std::uintptr_t buf[kBack / 8];
        const std::uintptr_t start = (addr & ~std::uintptr_t{7}) - kBack;
        vt = 0;
        dist = 0;
        if (!proc.read(start, buf, sizeof(buf))) return;
        for (int i = kBack / 8 - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
            if (buf[i] >= engine.base && buf[i] < engine.base + engine.size) {
                vt = buf[i] - engine.base;
                dist = addr - (start + i * 8);
                return;
            }
        }
    };

    fc6::forEachChunk(proc, regions, 64, [&](std::uintptr_t base, const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 64 <= len; i += 4) {
            float f[16];
            std::memcpy(f, data + i, sizeof(f));
            // Cheap prefilter on the first row before the full check.
            if (!(std::fabs(f[0]) <= 1.001f) || !unit(f)) continue;
            if (!finite(f, 16)) continue;

            const char* layout = nullptr;
            float px = 0, py = 0, pz = 0;
            if (isRotation(f, f + 4, f + 8)) {
                if (f[3] == 0 && f[7] == 0 && f[11] == 0 && f[15] == 1.f) {
                    layout = "R44";
                    px = f[12], py = f[13], pz = f[14];
                } else {
                    layout = "R34";
                    px = f[3], py = f[7], pz = f[11];
                }
            }
            if (!layout || !plausiblePosition(px, py, pz)) continue;

            const std::uintptr_t addr = base + i;
            if (!seen.insert(addr).second) continue;

            std::uintptr_t vt;
            std::size_t dist;
            vtableOf(addr, vt, dist);
            std::fprintf(out,
                         "%llx\t%s\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.4f\t%.4f\t%.4f\t%.4f\t%.4f\t%.4f\t%.4f\t%.4f\t%.4f\t%llx\t%zx\n",
                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(addr), layout, px, py, pz, f[0], f[1], f[2], f[4],
                         f[5], f[6], f[8], f[9], f[10], static_cast<unsigned long long>(vt), dist);
            ++hits;
        }
    });

    std::fclose(out);
    std::printf("%zu transform(s) written to %s\n", hits, outPath);
    return 0;
}

```

`src/probe/near.cpp`:

```cpp
// `fc6probe near <x> <y> <z> <radius> <out.tsv>`: every float triple in the heap within `radius`
// of a world point. Bones, hitboxes and attachment points of a character all sit within ~1 m of
// it, so long runs of hits with a constant stride point at its skeleton in world space.
// Output: address, stride to the previous hit, the triple.

#include "common/heap.hpp"
#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

int runNear(const fc6::Process& proc, float x, float y, float z, float radius, const char* outPath) {
    FILE* out = std::fopen(outPath, "w");
    if (!out) return 1;
    const float r2 = radius * radius;
    std::size_t hits = 0;
    std::uintptr_t last = 0;
    fc6::forEachChunk(proc, fc6::writableRegions(proc.pid()), 0,
                      [&](std::uintptr_t base, const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
                          for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 12 <= len; i += 4) {
                              float v[3];
                              std::memcpy(v, data + i, sizeof(v));
                              // Cheap reject on x first.
                              const float dx = v[0] - x;
                              if (!(dx * dx <= r2)) continue;
                              const float dy = v[1] - y, dz = v[2] - z;
                              if (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz > r2) continue;
                              const std::uintptr_t addr = base + i;
                              std::fprintf(out, "%llx\t%llx\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(addr),
                                           static_cast<unsigned long long>(last ? addr - last : 0), v[0], v[1], v[2]);
                              last = addr;
                              ++hits;
                          }
                      });
    std::fclose(out);
    std::printf("%zu hit(s) written to %s\n", hits, outPath);
    return 0;
}

```

`src/probe/ptrscan.cpp`:

```cpp
// `fc6probe ptrscan <hex addr> [levels] [hex maxoff]`: finds pointer chains from the engine
// module's static memory to a heap address, so the object can be found again after a restart.
//
// Breadth-first: level 1 finds every qword (heap or module) that points into
// [target - maxoff, target]. Hits inside the engine module are static roots and are printed as
// "FC_m64d3d12.dll+off -> +o1 -> +o2 ...". Heap hits become the targets of the next level.

#include "common/heap.hpp"
#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

namespace {

struct Node {
    std::uintptr_t addr;    // where this object starts (the pointed-to address)
    std::ptrdiff_t offset;  // offset from this node to the parent target
    int parent;             // index in the previous level, -1 for the target itself
};

constexpr std::size_t kMaxPerLevel = 20000;

}  // namespace

int runPtrScan(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine, std::uintptr_t target, int levels,
               std::size_t maxOff) {
    const auto heap = fc6::writableRegions(proc.pid());
    const auto image = fc6::moduleRegions(proc.pid(), engine);

    std::vector<std::vector<Node>> tree{{Node{target, 0, -1}}};
    std::size_t roots = 0;

    auto printChain = [&](std::uintptr_t staticAddr, std::ptrdiff_t off, int level, int idx) {
        std::printf("  FC_m64d3d12.dll+%llx", static_cast<unsigned long long>(staticAddr - engine.base));
        std::printf(" -> +%llx", static_cast<unsigned long long>(off));
        for (int l = level; l > 0; --l) {
            const Node& n = tree[l][idx];
            std::printf(" -> +%llx", static_cast<unsigned long long>(n.offset));
            idx = n.parent;
        }
        std::printf("\n");
    };

    for (int level = 0; level < levels && !tree[level].empty(); ++level) {
        // Sorted targets for binary search: a qword v hits target t when t - maxOff <= v <= t.
        std::vector<std::pair<std::uintptr_t, int>> targets;
        for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(tree[level].size()); ++i) targets.push_back({tree[level][i].addr, i});
        std::sort(targets.begin(), targets.end());

        std::vector<Node> next;
        std::size_t levelRoots = 0;
        auto visit = [&](bool isStatic) {
            return [&, isStatic](std::uintptr_t base, const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
                for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 8 <= len; i += 8) {
                    std::uintptr_t v;
                    std::memcpy(&v, data + i, 8);
                    auto it = std::lower_bound(targets.begin(), targets.end(), std::make_pair(v, -1));
                    if (it == targets.end() || it->first - v > maxOff) continue;
                    const std::ptrdiff_t off = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(it->first - v);
                    if (isStatic) {
                        if (roots < 200) printChain(base + i, off, level, it->second);
                        ++roots;
                        ++levelRoots;
                    } else if (next.size() < kMaxPerLevel) {
                        // The containing object starts somewhere before base+i; the next level
                        // searches for pointers into [base+i - maxOff, base+i].
                        next.push_back(Node{base + i, off, it->second});
                    }
                }
            };
        };
        std::printf("level %d: %zu target(s)\n", level + 1, targets.size());
        fc6::forEachChunk(proc, image, 0, visit(true));
        fc6::forEachChunk(proc, heap, 0, visit(false));
        std::printf("level %d: %zu static root(s), %zu heap pointer(s)%s\n", level + 1, levelRoots, next.size(),
                    next.size() >= kMaxPerLevel ? " (capped)" : "");
        tree.push_back(std::move(next));
    }
    std::printf("%zu static chain(s) in total\n", roots);
    return 0;
}

// `fc6probe refs <targets.txt> [hex maxoff]`: every qword in the heap or the engine module that
// points into [t - maxoff, t] for any target t listed (one hex address per line).
// Prints "location<TAB>target<TAB>offset<TAB>static|heap". Used to find arrays and lists that
// hold many known objects (e.g. the entity list).
int runRefs(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine, const std::vector<std::uintptr_t>& list,
            std::size_t maxOff) {
    std::vector<std::uintptr_t> targets(list);
    std::sort(targets.begin(), targets.end());
    std::size_t hits = 0;
    auto visit = [&](bool isStatic) {
        return [&, isStatic](std::uintptr_t base, const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
            for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 8 <= len; i += 8) {
                std::uintptr_t v;
                std::memcpy(&v, data + i, 8);
                auto it = std::lower_bound(targets.begin(), targets.end(), v);
                if (it == targets.end() || *it - v > maxOff) continue;
                std::printf("%llx\t%llx\t%llx\t%s\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(base + i),
                            static_cast<unsigned long long>(*it), static_cast<unsigned long long>(*it - v),
                            isStatic ? "static" : "heap");
                ++hits;
            }
        };
    };
    fc6::forEachChunk(proc, fc6::moduleRegions(proc.pid(), engine), 0, visit(true));
    fc6::forEachChunk(proc, fc6::writableRegions(proc.pid()), 0, visit(false));
    std::fprintf(stderr, "%zu reference(s)\n", hits);
    return 0;
}
```

`src/probe/values.cpp`:

```cpp
// `fc6probe values <out.tsv> <v1> [v2 ...] [--tol t]`: every 4-byte aligned float in the heap
// within `tol` of any of the given values. Used to find the view angles: the camera matrix gives
// the current yaw/pitch, so we look for them in radians and degrees, then narrow the candidates
// down after the mouse moves (tools/find_angles.py).

#include "common/heap.hpp"
#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

int runValues(const fc6::Process& proc, const char* outPath, const std::vector<float>& values, float tol) {
    FILE* out = std::fopen(outPath, "w");
    if (!out) return 1;
    std::size_t hits = 0;
    fc6::forEachChunk(proc, fc6::writableRegions(proc.pid()), 0,
                      [&](std::uintptr_t base, const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
                          for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 4 <= len; i += 4) {
                              float v;
                              std::memcpy(&v, data + i, 4);
                              for (std::size_t k = 0; k < values.size(); ++k) {
                                  if (std::fabs(v - values[k]) <= tol) {
                                      std::fprintf(out, "%llx\t%zu\t%.6f\n",
                                                   static_cast<unsigned long long>(base + i), k, v);
                                      ++hits;
                                      break;
                                  }
                              }
                          }
                      });
    std::fclose(out);
    std::printf("%zu hit(s) written to %s\n", hits, outPath);
    return 0;
}

// `fc6probe ints <out.tsv> <v1> [v2 ...] [--u16]`: every 4-byte aligned u32 (2-byte aligned u16 with
// --u16) in the heap equal to one of the values (item counts: tools/inv_hunt.py narrows them down
// after the count changes in game).
int runInts(const fc6::Process& proc, const char* outPath, const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& values, int width) {
    FILE* out = std::fopen(outPath, "w");
    if (!out) return 1;
    std::size_t hits = 0;
    fc6::forEachChunk(proc, fc6::writableRegions(proc.pid()), 0,
                      [&](std::uintptr_t base, const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
                          for (std::size_t i = 0; i + width <= len; i += width) {
                              std::uint32_t v = 0;
                              std::memcpy(&v, data + i, width);
                              for (std::size_t k = 0; k < values.size(); ++k) {
                                  if (v == values[k]) {
                                      std::fprintf(out, "%llx\t%zu\t%u\n",
                                                   static_cast<unsigned long long>(base + i), k, v);
                                      ++hits;
                                      break;
                                  }
                              }
                          }
                      });
    std::fclose(out);
    std::printf("%zu hit(s) written to %s\n", hits, outPath);
    return 0;
}

```

`src/probe/world.cpp`:

```cpp
// `fc6probe world`: finds hknpWorld candidates in the heap by structure.
//
// From hknpWorld::castRay (+0x48983a0) and its callers (docs/reversing.md):
//   world + 0x000 : vtable (hkReferencedObject)
//   world + 0x028 : hknpBody array (stride 0xb0, hkTransform at the start of each body)
//   world + 0x4b0 : query interface object
// The body manager embedded near the start of the world keeps a back pointer to the world, so a
// candidate must contain its own address somewhere in +0x08..+0x40.

#include "common/heap.hpp"
#include "common/process.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

namespace {

bool heapPtr(std::uint64_t v) { return v > 0x10000000000ull && v < 0x7f0000000000ull && !(v & 7); }

bool unit(const float* v) { return std::fabs(v[0] * v[0] + v[1] * v[1] + v[2] * v[2] - 1.f) < 2e-3f; }

bool bodyLooksValid(const fc6::Process& proc, std::uintptr_t body) {
    float m[12];
    if (!proc.read(body, m, sizeof(m))) return false;
    // three rotation columns of 16 bytes each
    return unit(m) && unit(m + 4) && unit(m + 8) &&
           std::fabs(m[0] * m[4] + m[1] * m[5] + m[2] * m[6]) < 2e-3f;
}

}  // namespace

int runWorld(const fc6::Process& proc, const fc6::Module& engine) {
    std::size_t found = 0;
    const auto inEngine = [&](std::uint64_t v) { return v >= engine.base && v < engine.base + engine.size; };
    fc6::forEachChunk(proc, fc6::writableRegions(proc.pid()), 0xb00,
                      [&](std::uintptr_t base, const std::uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
                          for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 0xa80 <= len; i += 8) {
                              const std::uintptr_t w = base + i;
                              std::uint64_t own, self, bodies, iface;
                              std::memcpy(&own, data + i, 8);
                              if (!inEngine(own)) continue;
                              bool back = false;
                              for (std::size_t o = 0x8; o <= 0x40 && !back; o += 8) {
                                  std::memcpy(&self, data + i + o, 8);
                                  back = self == w;
                              }
                              if (!back) continue;
                              std::memcpy(&bodies, data + i + 0x28, 8);
                              std::memcpy(&iface, data + i + 0x4b0, 8);
                              if (!heapPtr(bodies)) continue;
                              int valid = 0;
                              for (int b = 0; b < 64; ++b) valid += bodyLooksValid(proc, bodies + b * 0xb0);
                              std::printf("world? %llx  vt +%llx  bodies %llx (%d/64 valid)  +0x4b0 %llx\n",
                                          static_cast<unsigned long long>(w),
                                          static_cast<unsigned long long>(own - engine.base),
                                          static_cast<unsigned long long>(bodies), valid,
                                          static_cast<unsigned long long>(iface));
                              ++found;
                          }
                      });
    std::printf("%zu candidate(s)\n", found);
    return 0;
}

```

`tools/aim.py`:

```py
"""Aim prototype for Far Cry 6, offline single-player only.

    python tools/calibrate.py     # once (or after changing the in-game sensitivity)
    python tools/aim.py           # hold the aim key to pull the view onto the nearest enemy head

Reads the game's own aim-assist target list (tools/targets.py), keeps hostile characters inside
a cone around the crosshair, picks the one closest to the crosshair and moves the mouse towards
its head with SendInput. Nothing is written into the game.

Line of sight: when fc6internal.dll is loaded (build/Release/fc6inject.exe), rays are cast from
the camera to the head, neck, chest and pelvis with the game's own collision query; the first
exposed part is aimed at and enemies with no exposed part are skipped. Without the DLL every
enemy in the cone is a candidate.

Targets: enemy humans (live attitude 2 or an FND soldier archetype, never an ally); dead ones
are skipped (death state, see targets.is_dead).

Ballistics (measured with tools/ballistics.py): a bullet starts at the eye along the view
direction, flies at constant speed and falls with gravity. The aim point is moved to where the
target will be when the bullet arrives (velocity of its root over the last 0.1 s), then raised
by the drop over that flight time.
"""

import argparse
import collections
import json
import math
import os
import time

import mouse
from mem import Game
from targets import (aim_points, camera, camera_with_projection, held_weapon, is_dead, is_hostile, player_entity,
                     targets, weapon_ballistics)

from ballistics import BulletMonitor, qpc
from rays import BULLET_FILTER, Rays

# A ray that stops this close to the aim point hit the target itself (capsule, ragdoll), not cover.
SELF_HIT_MARGIN = 0.45
# A ray that stops within this horizontal distance of the target's vertical axis (between its
# feet and above its head) hit the target's own body: wide characters (heavies) need it.
TARGET_RADIUS = 0.9
RAY_START = 1.0  # metres in front of the eye, to clear the player's own body and weapon
# Traced bullets (docs/reversing.md "Ballistics"): 614 m/s, gravity 16.5 m/s2, gone after 287.5 m.
BULLET_SPEED = 614.0
BULLET_GRAVITY = 16.5
MAX_TARGET_SPEED = 40.0  # m/s; a faster root movement is a teleport or a glitch, not motion
# Follow: the aim direction's own angular rate is added to each tick's correction, so the view
# keeps up with a moving aim point instead of trailing it. Smoothed over a few ticks (jitter).
RATE_SMOOTH = 0.25
MAX_RATE = 2.0  # rad/s
# Weapons scatter their bullets at random around the camera forward (SR-A Yaran aiming: 0.07-0.24
```
