```py
# deg; FAL: none measured). When a median shot would land farther than this from the head
# centre at the target's distance (fewer than half the shots on the head), the chest is aimed at.
HEAD_RADIUS = 0.10  # m


def angle(a, b):
    na = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in a)) or 1.0
    nb = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in b)) or 1.0
    return math.acos(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(a[k] * b[k] for k in range(3)) / (na * nb))))


class Spread:
    """The current weapon's spread: angle between each bullet and the camera forward when it was
    fired, over the last shots fired while aiming. radius() is the median, in radians."""

    def __init__(self, devs=()):
        self.devs = collections.deque(devs, maxlen=8)

    def reset(self):
        self.devs.clear()

    STILL = math.radians(0.05)  # camera turn allowed within +-25 ms of the shot

    def add(self, chain, eyes, freq):
        """Adds one shot, unless the camera was turning: the camera sample nearest the shot is
        a few ms off, and a turning camera would pass for spread."""
        t0 = chain[0]["qpc"]
        near = [e for e in eyes if abs(e[0] - t0) <= 0.025 * freq]
        if len(near) < 2 or angle(near[0][2], near[-1][2]) > self.STILL:
            return False
        d = [chain[0]["to"][k] - chain[0]["from"][k] for k in range(3)]
        cam = min(near, key=lambda e: abs(e[0] - t0))
        self.devs.append(angle(d, cam[2]))
        return True

    def radius(self):
        if len(self.devs) < 3:
            return None
        s = sorted(self.devs)
        return s[len(s) // 2]

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
# Per weapon (archetype name of the held weapon): speed, gravity and spread measured on the
# player's shots, kept across sessions. The two seeds were measured with tools/ballistics.py.
PROFILES = os.path.join(HERE, "weapons.json")
SEED_PROFILES = {
    "weapons:Weapons:Rifles.FAL": {"speed": 614.0, "gravity": 16.5, "spread": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0003]},
    "weapons:Weapons:Rifles.CUBAN-SRA": {"speed": 500.0, "gravity": 15.0,
                                         "spread": [0.0021, 0.0017, 0.0012, 0.0042, 0.0019]},
}


# What the ESP overlay (tools/esp.py) shows in game, since this console isn't visible there.
STATUS = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "captures", "aim_status.json")


class Hud:
    """Status written for the ESP overlay: weapon line, current target, last events."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.state = {"weapon": "", "target": "", "events": []}
        self.dirty, self.written = True, 0.0

    def set(self, key, text):
        if self.state.get(key) != text:
            self.state[key] = text
            self.dirty = True

    def event(self, text):
        self.state["events"] = (self.state["events"] + [[time.time(), text]])[-5:]
        self.dirty = True

    def flush(self):
        now = time.time()
        if not (self.dirty and now - self.written > 0.2) and now - self.written < 1.0:
            return
        self.state["t"] = now  # heartbeat: the overlay says "not running" without it
        try:
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(STATUS), exist_ok=True)
            with open(STATUS + ".tmp", "w") as f:
                json.dump(self.state, f)
            os.replace(STATUS + ".tmp", STATUS)
            self.dirty, self.written = False, now
        except OSError:
            pass  # the overlay is reading it: next time


def effective(base, prof, args):
    """(speed, gravity, drop distance) for the held weapon: the game's data (base) times the
    correction measured on the player's shots (attachments: the FAL with its mod fell x1.1 faster
    than its fBulletGravity), else the measured values alone, else the command line defaults."""
    if base:  # the game's own values, attachments and loaded ammo included (exact)
        return base["speed"], base["gravity"], base["drop_distance"]
    if "speed" in prof:
        return prof["speed"], prof["gravity"], 0.0
    return args.speed, args.gravity, 0.0


def weapon_line(weapon, speed, gravity, spread, head_only, measured, base=None):
    text = f"{weapon.split('.')[-1] if weapon else '?'}  {speed:.0f} m/s  g {gravity:.1f}"
    if base:
        text += f"  (game {base['speed']:.0f} m/s g {base['gravity']:.1f}"
        if measured and base["gravity"]:
            text += f", x{speed / base['speed']:.2f} x{gravity / base['gravity']:.2f} measured"
        text += ")"
    r = spread.radius()
    if r:
        text += f"  spread {math.degrees(r):.2f} deg -> " + ("head" if head_only else f"chest > {HEAD_RADIUS / r:.0f} m")
    return text + ("" if measured or base else "  (not measured: fire once at 40 m+)")


ZOOM_GAINS = os.path.join(HERE, "zoom_gain.json")
# First guess: while aiming the camera turned ~0.75x of the calibration unmagnified, and ~0.75x
# again once the counts were scaled by the zoom (x2.17): an aiming multiplier times the zoom.
AIM_BASE_GAIN = 1.33


class ZoomGain:
    """Mouse counts multiplier per zoom level. While aiming the game turns the camera less per
    count than calibration.json says (0.7-0.8x even unmagnified, 0.36x at zoom x2.17), so the
    factor starts at AIM_BASE_GAIN * zoom and learns from each response check at that zoom
    (camera turn / turn sent, aiming for 1.0). Kept in zoom_gain.json."""

    def __init__(self):
        try:
            with open(ZOOM_GAINS) as f:
                self.table = {float(k): v for k, v in json.load(f).items()}
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            self.table = {}

    @staticmethod
    def key(zoom):
        return round(zoom * 4) / 4

    @staticmethod
    def model(zoom):
        return AIM_BASE_GAIN * max(1.0, zoom)

    def factor(self, zoom):
        return self.table.get(self.key(zoom), self.model(zoom))

    def learn(self, zoom, response):
        """response: camera turn / turn sent, measured with factor(zoom) applied. The player's own
        mouse moves spoil some checks, so each one only moves the factor half a step (in log),
        and the factor stays within -20 % / +25 % of the model."""
        if not 0.5 < response < 2.0:
            return
        m = self.model(zoom)
        f = min(1.25 * m, max(0.8 * m, self.factor(zoom) / math.sqrt(response)))
        self.table[self.key(zoom)] = round(f, 3)
        try:
            with open(ZOOM_GAINS, "w") as fh:
                json.dump({str(k): v for k, v in sorted(self.table.items())}, fh, indent=1)
        except OSError:
            pass


def load_profiles():
    try:
        with open(PROFILES) as f:
            return {**SEED_PROFILES, **json.load(f)}
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return dict(SEED_PROFILES)


def save_profiles(profiles):
    try:
        with open(PROFILES, "w") as f:
            json.dump(profiles, f, indent=1, sort_keys=True)
    except OSError:
        pass


def wrap(a):
    return (a + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi


def view_angles(fwd):
    return math.atan2(-fwd[0], fwd[1]), math.asin(max(-1.0, min(1.0, fwd[2])))


def angles_to(eye, point):
    d = [point[i] - eye[i] for i in range(3)]
    return math.atan2(-d[0], d[1]), math.atan2(d[2], math.hypot(d[0], d[1]))


class Motion:
    """Velocity of each target from its root position over the last WINDOW seconds."""

    WINDOW = 0.1

    def __init__(self):
        self.hist = {}

    def velocity(self, entity, pos, now):
        h = self.hist.setdefault(entity, collections.deque())
        if h and now - h[-1][0] > 0.5:
            h.clear()  # not seen for a while: old samples would average over a stop
        h.append((now, pos))
        while len(h) > 2 and now - h[1][0] >= self.WINDOW:
            h.popleft()
        t0, p0 = h[0]
        dt = now - t0
        if dt < 0.03:
            return (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        v = tuple((pos[k] - p0[k]) / dt for k in range(3))
        return v if math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in v)) < MAX_TARGET_SPEED else (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def prune(self, now):
        for e in [e for e, h in self.hist.items() if not h or now - h[-1][0] > 2.0]:
            del self.hist[e]


def lead_point(eye, point, velocity, speed, gravity, drop_distance=0.0):
    """Where to aim so that a bullet fired now from the eye meets a target at `point` moving at
    `velocity`: the bullet flies straight at `speed` and, past drop_distance (the weapon's
    fBulletDropDistance), falls by gravity * t^2 / 2. Returns (aim point, flight time)."""
    if not speed:
        return point, 0.0
    t = math.dist(eye, point) / speed
    for _ in range(3):
        aim = [point[k] + velocity[k] * t for k in range(3)]
        t_fall = max(0.0, t - drop_distance / speed)
        aim[2] += 0.5 * gravity * t_fall * t_fall
        t = math.dist(eye, aim) / speed
    return aim, t


def hits_target(hit, base, up, top):
    """True if a hit position lies on the target's own body (near its vertical axis)."""
    rel = [hit[k] - base[k] for k in range(3)]
    h = sum(rel[k] * up[k] for k in range(3))
    radial = math.sqrt(max(0.0, sum(x * x for x in rel) - h * h))
    return radial < TARGET_RADIUS and -0.2 < h < top + 0.4


def visible_point(rays, eye, fwd, points, base=None, up=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)):
    """First (label, xyz) in points with a clear line from the eye, or None.
    base: the target's feet position, to recognise rays that stop on the target itself."""
    if rays is None:
        return points[0]
    batch = []
    for _label, p in points:
        d = [p[i] - eye[i] for i in range(3)]
        n = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in d)) or 1.0
        start = tuple(eye[i] + d[i] / n * RAY_START for i in range(3))
        batch.append((start, p))
    res = rays.cast(batch)
    if res is None:
        return points[0]  # DLL busy or gone: don't block aiming
    top = max(sum((p[k] - base[k]) * up[k] for k in range(3)) for _l, p in points) if base else 0.0
    for (label, p), (start, _), h in zip(points, batch, res):
        if h["fault"]:
            continue
        if not h["hit"]:
            return label, p
        hit_dist = math.dist(start, h["position"])
        if hit_dist >= math.dist(start, p) - SELF_HIT_MARGIN:
            return label, p
        if base and hits_target(h["position"], base, up, top):
            return label, p
    return None


def pick_target(g, cam, fov, max_dist, rays=None, motion=None, speed=0.0, gravity=0.0, spread=None,
                drop_distance=0.0):
    """Best target as (offset, yaw error, pitch error, distance, name, part, flight time, velocity,
    part position now, aim point).
    With motion and speed, the errors point at the lead and drop compensated aim point. With a
    spread (radians) wider than the head at the target's distance, the chest comes first."""
    eye, fwd = cam[3], cam[1]
    yaw, pitch = view_angles(fwd)
    _, pts = targets(g)
    now = time.perf_counter()
    cands = []
    for _i, _p, entity, pos, name in pts:
        if not is_hostile(g, entity, name) or is_dead(g, entity):
            continue
        points = aim_points(g, entity, pos)
        m = g.mat(entity + 0x30)
        vel = motion.velocity(entity, tuple(m[3][:3]) if m else tuple(pos[:3]), now) if motion else (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        head = points[0][1]
        dist = math.dist(eye, head)
        if dist > max_dist or dist < 1.0:
            continue
        if spread and spread * dist > HEAD_RADIUS:
            points = sorted(points, key=lambda lp: not lp[0].startswith("chest"))
        ty, tp = angles_to(eye, head)
        off = math.hypot(wrap(ty - yaw), tp - pitch)
        if off <= fov:
            cands.append((off, dist, name, points, vel, (m[3], m[2]) if m else (None, (0.0, 0.0, 1.0))))
    if motion:
        motion.prune(now)
    # Closest to the crosshair first; the first one with an exposed part wins.
    for off, dist, name, points, vel, (base, up) in sorted(cands, key=lambda c: c[0]):
        vis = visible_point(rays, eye, fwd, points, base, up)
        if vis is None:
            continue
        label, p = vis
        aim, flight = lead_point(eye, p, vel, speed, gravity, drop_distance)
        ty, tp = angles_to(eye, aim)
        return off, wrap(ty - yaw), tp - pitch, dist, name, label, flight, vel, p, aim
    return None


def split_offset(off, d):
    """(up, left) components of an offset, relative to a direction of travel d."""
    hn = math.hypot(d[0], d[1]) or 1.0
    return off[2], (off[0] * -d[1] + off[1] * d[0]) / hn


def describe_offset(up, left):
    return (f"{abs(up) * 100:3.0f} cm {'above' if up >= 0 else 'below'},"
            f" {abs(left) * 100:3.0f} cm {'left' if left >= 0 else 'right'}")


def report_shot(chain, aims, freq):
    """For one of the player's bullets fired while aiming, prints:
    - at fire: where the bullet's direction pointed against the aim point aim.py wanted (the
      mouse hadn't converged if this is large; the bullet leaves along the camera forward);
    - at arrival: where the bullet passed against the target part, at the same instants (the
      lead and drop model if this is large while the first is small)."""
    t0 = chain[0]["qpc"]
    before = [a for a in aims if t0 - 0.1 * freq <= a[0] <= t0]
    if not before:
        return None  # not fired while aiming
    _t, name, label, _part, aim, err_yaw, err_pitch = before[-1]
    track = [a for a in aims if a[1] == name and t0 - 0.1 * freq <= a[0] <= chain[-1]["qpc"] + 0.05 * freq]
    start = chain[0]["from"]
    d0 = [chain[0]["to"][k] - start[k] for k in range(3)]
    n0 = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in d0)) or 1.0
    to_aim = [aim[k] - start[k] for k in range(3)]
    rng = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in to_aim)) or 1.0
    along = [start[k] + d0[k] / n0 * rng for k in range(3)]  # where the barrel pointed, at the aim's range
    up0, left0 = split_offset([along[k] - aim[k] for k in range(3)], d0)
    best = None
    for r in chain:
        a = min(track, key=lambda x: abs(x[0] - r["qpc"]))
        p = a[3]
        f, to = r["from"], r["to"]
        d = [to[k] - f[k] for k in range(3)]
        l2 = sum(x * x for x in d) or 1e-9
        u = max(0.0, min(1.0, sum((p[k] - f[k]) * d[k] for k in range(3)) / l2))
        c = [f[k] + u * d[k] for k in range(3)]
        dist = math.dist(c, p)
        if best is None or dist < best[0]:
            best = (dist, c, p, d)
    dist, c, p, d = best
    up, left = split_offset([c[k] - p[k] for k in range(3)], d)
    travelled = math.dist(start, chain[-1]["to"])
    if travelled < rng - 2.0:
        print(f"shot at {name.split('.')[-1]} {label} ({rng:.0f} m): blocked at {travelled:.0f} m"
              f" (barrel {describe_offset(up0, left0)} of the aim point)")
        return f"shot {label} {rng:.0f} m: blocked at {travelled:.0f} m (cover between); barrel {describe_offset(up0, left0)}"
    print(f"shot at {name.split('.')[-1]} {label} ({rng:.0f} m): at fire the barrel was"
          f" {describe_offset(up0, left0)} of the aim point (aim error read from the camera:"
          f" {abs(err_pitch) * rng * 100:.0f} cm {'low' if err_pitch > 0 else 'high'},"
          f" {abs(err_yaw) * rng * 100:.0f} cm {'right' if err_yaw > 0 else 'left'});"
          f" the bullet passed {dist * 100:.0f} cm from the {label} ({describe_offset(up, left)})")
    verdict = "HIT" if dist < (HEAD_RADIUS if label.startswith("head") else 0.2) else "miss"
    return (f"shot {label} {rng:.0f} m: {verdict}, {dist * 100:.0f} cm"
            f" ({describe_offset(up, left).replace('  ', ' ')}); barrel {describe_offset(up0, left0)}")


def calibrate(speed, gravity, est):
    """New (speed, gravity) after one of the player's shots (ballistics.estimate()). Each shot
    weighs by what it can tell: the speed by its number of segments (frame timing jitter: shots of
    4 segments with 3600 m/s armor-piercing rounds read 3378-3746), the gravity by its horizontal
    extent (curvature). A jump of x1.5 or more is another ammo or attachment and is taken at once;
    smaller differences are averaged in."""
    def update(old, new, weight):
        if not old or new > 1.5 * old or new < old / 1.5:
            return new
        return old + (new - old) * weight

    changed = []
    n = est["n"]
    if est["extent"] >= 15 and n >= 4 and 50 < est["speed"] < 20000:
        speed = update(speed, est["speed"], min(0.5, 0.02 * n))
        changed.append(f"{speed:.0f} m/s (measured {est['speed']:.0f})")
    if est["extent"] >= 60 and n >= 6 and est["gravity"] is not None and 0 < est["gravity"] < 100:
        gravity = update(gravity, est["gravity"], min(0.5, est["extent"] / 600))
        changed.append(f"gravity {gravity:.2f} m/s2 (measured {est['gravity']:.2f})")
    if changed:
        print(f"ballistics from your shot ({n} segments over {est['extent']:.0f} m): {', '.join(changed)}")
    return speed, gravity


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--key", default="rbutton", choices=sorted(mouse.VK), help="hold to aim (default: right mouse)")
    ap.add_argument("--fov", type=float, default=12.0, help="cone half-angle in degrees")
    ap.add_argument("--smooth", type=float, default=0.35, help="fraction of the error corrected per tick (0-1)")
    ap.add_argument("--max-dist", type=float, default=250.0)
    ap.add_argument("--speed", type=float, default=BULLET_SPEED, help="bullet speed in m/s (0 = no ballistics)")
    ap.add_argument("--gravity", type=float, default=BULLET_GRAVITY, help="bullet gravity in m/s2")
    ap.add_argument("--no-lead", action="store_true", help="don't lead moving targets")
    ap.add_argument("--no-follow", action="store_true", help="don't add the aim point's own motion to the mouse")
    ap.add_argument("--head-only", action="store_true", help="aim at the head even when the spread is wider")
    ap.add_argument("--no-zoom-comp", action="store_true", help="don't scale the mouse by the zoom when aiming")
    ap.add_argument("--no-auto", action="store_true",
                    help="keep --speed/--gravity instead of measuring them on your own shots")
    ap.add_argument("--hz", type=float, default=120.0)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    cal = json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "calibration.json")))
    kx, ky = cal["yaw_counts_per_rad"], cal["pitch_counts_per_rad"]
    g = Game()
    rays = monitor = None
    try:
        rays = Rays()
        rays.set_filter(BULLET_FILTER)
        print("line of sight: on (fc6internal.dll, bullet filter)" if rays.ready() else "line of sight: DLL loaded but not ready")
    except RuntimeError:
        print("line of sight: off (inject build/Release/fc6inject.exe to enable)")
    if rays and not args.no_auto:
        try:
            monitor = BulletMonitor()
            print("ballistics: measured on your own shots (fire once with each weapon)")
        except RuntimeError:
            pass
    speed, gravity = args.speed, args.gravity
    eyes = collections.deque(maxlen=240)
    fov = math.radians(args.fov)
    print(f"aim prototype running: hold '{args.key}' in game. Ctrl+C to stop.")
    last_name = None
    motion = Motion()
    # (qpc, target name, part, part position, aim point, yaw error, pitch error) while aiming
    aims = collections.deque(maxlen=1200)
    prev_aim = None  # (time, target name, yaw, pitch) of the aim direction at the last tick
    rate = [0.0, 0.0]
    probe = None  # mouse response check after a new target
    spread = Spread()
    profiles = load_profiles() if monitor else {}
    weapon = player = base = None
    drop_distance = 0.0
    weapon_check = 0.0
    hud = Hud()
    rest_sx = None
    zoom_gain = ZoomGain()
    rest_x = rest_y = 0.0
    while True:
        time.sleep(1.0 / args.hz)
        focused = mouse.foreground_pid() == g.pid
        if monitor and focused:
            cam = camera(g)
            if cam:
                eyes.append((qpc(), tuple(cam[3][:3]), tuple(cam[1][:3])))
            if time.perf_counter() > weapon_check:
                # The held weapon: its profile applies at once, before any shot with it.
                weapon_check = time.perf_counter() + 0.25
                player = player if player and g.vt(player) else player_entity(g)
                name = held_weapon(g, player) if player else None
                if not name:
                    player = None  # respawn or reload: look the player up again next time
                new_base = weapon_ballistics(g, player) if name else None
                if name and (name != weapon or new_base != base):
                    # Another weapon, or the same one with other stats (workbench): the game's
                    # values times this weapon's measured correction, before any shot.
                    switched = name != weapon
                    weapon, base, prof = name, new_base, profiles.get(name) or {}
                    speed, gravity, drop_distance = effective(base, prof, args)
                    if switched:
                        spread = Spread(prof.get("spread", ()))
                    r = spread.radius()
                    print(f"weapon {name.split('.')[-1]}: {speed:.0f} m/s, gravity {gravity:.2f} m/s2"
                          + (f" (game data {base['speed']:.0f} m/s, {base['gravity']:.1f} m/s2,"
                             f" drop after {base['drop_distance']:.0f} m)" if base else " (no game data)")
                          + (f", spread {math.degrees(r):.2f} deg" if r else ""))
            for est in monitor.poll(eyes):
                old_speed = speed
                speed, gravity = calibrate(speed, gravity, est)
                if abs(speed - old_speed) > 0.03 * old_speed:
                    spread.reset()  # attachments or ammo changed the bullet
                shot = report_shot(est["chain"], aims, monitor.freq)
                if shot:
                    hud.event(shot)
                    spread.add(est["chain"], eyes, monitor.freq)
                    r = spread.radius()
                    if r:
                        print(f"spread of this weapon while aiming: {math.degrees(r):.2f} deg ->"
                              f" {'head always' if args.head_only else f'chest beyond {HEAD_RADIUS / r:.0f} m'}")
                if weapon:
                    prof = {"speed": round(speed, 2), "gravity": round(gravity, 3),
                            "spread": [round(x, 6) for x in spread.devs]}
                    if base:
                        prof["speed_x"] = round(speed / base["speed"], 4)
                        if base["gravity"]:
                            prof["gravity_x"] = round(gravity / base["gravity"], 4)
                    profiles[weapon] = prof
                    save_profiles(profiles)
        if monitor:
            hud.set("weapon", weapon_line(weapon, speed, gravity, spread, args.head_only,
                                          bool(weapon and weapon in profiles), base))
        aiming = focused and mouse.key_down(args.key)
        if not aiming:
            hud.set("target", "")
        hud.flush()
        cp = camera_with_projection(g) if focused else None
        if cp and not aiming:
            rest_sx = cp[1]  # projection scale when not zoomed: what calibration.json was made with
        if not aiming:
            last_name = None
            continue
        if not cp:
            continue
        cam, sx = cp[0], cp[1]
        # Zoomed in, the game turns the camera less per mouse count (measured 0.36-0.66x while
        # aiming with the SR-A Yaran): scale the counts by the zoom, unless --no-zoom-comp.
        zoom = max(1.0, sx / rest_sx) if rest_sx else 1.0
        gain = 1.0 if args.no_zoom_comp else zoom_gain.factor(zoom)
        kxz, kyz = kx * gain, ky * gain
        t = pick_target(g, cam, fov, args.max_dist, rays if rays and rays.ready() else None,
                        None if args.no_lead else motion, speed, gravity,
                        None if args.head_only else spread.radius(), drop_distance)
        if not t:
            continue
        _off, dy, dp, dist, name, src, flight, vel, part, aim = t
        aims.append((qpc(), name, src, tuple(part), tuple(aim), dy, dp))
        now = time.perf_counter()
        yaw, pitch = view_angles(cam[1])
        if name != last_name:
            print(f"target {name.split('.')[-1]} at {dist:.1f} m, aiming at {src}, flight {flight * 1000:.0f} ms,"
                  f" moving {math.hypot(vel[0], vel[1]):.1f} m/s")
            last_name = name
            probe = {"t": now, "yaw": yaw, "pitch": pitch, "sent": [0.0, 0.0], "zoom": zoom, "gain": gain}
            hud.set("target", f"{name.split('.')[-1]}  {dist:.0f} m  {src}  flight {flight * 1000:.0f} ms"
                              f"  moving {math.hypot(vel[0], vel[1]):.1f} m/s")
        elif probe and now - probe["t"] > 0.25:
            # How far the camera turned against what was sent: 1.0 if the calibration holds here.
            sent = math.hypot(*probe["sent"])
            turned = math.hypot(wrap(yaw - probe["yaw"]), pitch - probe["pitch"])
            steady = abs(zoom - probe["zoom"]) < 0.03 * probe["zoom"]  # not while zooming in
            # Turned the way it was sent: the player wasn't moving the mouse elsewhere meanwhile.
            tv = (wrap(yaw - probe["yaw"]), pitch - probe["pitch"])
            sv = probe["sent"]
            norm_ts = math.hypot(*tv) * math.hypot(*sv)
            steady = steady and norm_ts > 0 and (tv[0] * sv[0] + tv[1] * sv[1]) / norm_ts > 0.95
            if sent > math.radians(1.0):
                print(f"mouse response {turned / sent:.2f}x (camera turned {math.degrees(turned):.1f} deg"
                      f" for {math.degrees(sent):.1f} deg sent in 0.25 s, zoom x{probe['zoom']:.2f},"
                      f" gain x{probe['gain']:.2f}{'' if steady else ', zoom changing or player moving: not learned'})")
                hud.event(f"mouse x{turned / sent:.2f} at zoom x{probe['zoom']:.2f} (1.00 = right"
                          f"{', learning' if steady and not args.no_zoom_comp else ''})")
                if steady and not args.no_zoom_comp:
                    zoom_gain.learn(probe["zoom"], turned / sent)
            probe = None
        ty, tp = angles_to(cam[3], aim)
        if prev_aim and prev_aim[1] == name and 0 < now - prev_aim[0] < 0.1:
            dt = now - prev_aim[0]
            for k, change in enumerate((wrap(ty - prev_aim[2]), tp - prev_aim[3])):
                rate[k] += (max(-MAX_RATE, min(MAX_RATE, change / dt)) - rate[k]) * RATE_SMOOTH
        else:
            dt, rate = 1.0 / args.hz, [0.0, 0.0]
        prev_aim = (now, name, ty, tp)
        follow_y, follow_p = (0.0, 0.0) if args.no_follow else (rate[0] * dt, rate[1] * dt)
        # Mouse counts for this tick, keeping the sub-count remainder so slow corrections still move.
        mx = (dy * args.smooth + follow_y) * kxz + rest_x
        my = (dp * args.smooth + follow_p) * kyz + rest_y
        ix, iy = int(mx), int(my)
        rest_x, rest_y = mx - ix, my - iy
        if probe:
            probe["sent"][0] += ix / kxz
            probe["sent"][1] += iy / kyz
        if ix or iy:
            mouse.move(ix, iy)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass

```

`tools/aim_trace.py`:

```py
"""Reads build/Release/aimtrace.csv (written by the DLL each time the aim key is released).

    python tools/aim_trace.py [--last N]

Per aim session: the settings, ticks and new camera pictures, and how much the view shakes once
on the target (after the first 0.3 s): the error to the aim point (dy/dp, barrel removed), the same
error against the camera alone (dy + barrel), the barrel offset's change per tick, and whether the
error swings back and forth (lag-1 autocorrelation < 0). Angles in thousandths of a degree.
"""

import argparse
import math
import os
import statistics

TRACE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "build", "Release", "aimtrace.csv")
MDEG = 180000.0 / math.pi  # radians -> thousandths of a degree


def sessions(path):
    out, cur, header = [], None, None
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            line = line.strip()
            if line.startswith("# session"):
                cur = {"settings": dict(kv.split("=", 1) for kv in line[len("# session "):].split()), "rows": []}
                out.append(cur)
            elif line.startswith("t_ms"):
                header = line.split(",")
            elif line and cur is not None:
                vals = line.split(",")
                row = dict(zip(header, vals))
                cur["rows"].append({k: (v if k == "entity" else float(v)) for k, v in row.items()})
    return out


def rms(xs):
    return math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in xs) / len(xs)) if xs else float("nan")


def autocorr(xs):
    if len(xs) < 3:
        return float("nan")
    m = statistics.fmean(xs)
    num = sum((a - m) * (b - m) for a, b in zip(xs, xs[1:]))
    den = sum((a - m) ** 2 for a in xs)
    return num / den if den else float("nan")


def report(s):
    st, rows = s["settings"], s["rows"]
    w = st.get("weapon", "?").split(".")[-1]
    t_end = rows[-1]["t_ms"]
    steady = [r for r in rows if r["t_ms"] >= 300 and r["entity"] == rows[-1]["entity"]]
    pics = sum(int(r["new_picture"]) for r in rows) / len(rows)
    print(f"{w:10s} smooth {st.get('smooth')} sync {st.get('frame_sync')} barrel {st.get('barrel')}: "
          f"{len(rows)} ticks in {t_end / 1000:.1f} s, new picture {pics:.0%}, {rows[-1]['dist']:.0f} m")
    if len(steady) < 10:
        print("    (too short once on the target)")
        return
    dy = [r["dy"] * MDEG for r in steady]
    dp = [r["dp"] * MDEG for r in steady]
    cam_y = [(r["dy"] + r["barrel_y"]) * MDEG for r in steady]
    cam_p = [(r["dp"] + r["barrel_p"]) * MDEG for r in steady]
    db_y = [(b["barrel_y"] - a["barrel_y"]) * MDEG for a, b in zip(steady, steady[1:])]
    db_p = [(b["barrel_p"] - a["barrel_p"]) * MDEG for a, b in zip(steady, steady[1:])]
    sent_y = [r["sent_y"] * MDEG for r in steady if r["wrote"]]
    sent_p = [r["sent_p"] * MDEG for r in steady if r["wrote"]]
    print(f"    error yaw/pitch rms {rms(dy):5.0f} / {rms(dp):5.0f}, swings {autocorr(dy):+.2f} / {autocorr(dp):+.2f};"
          f" vs camera alone {rms(cam_y):5.0f} / {rms(cam_p):5.0f}")
    print(f"    barrel change per tick rms {rms(db_y):5.0f} / {rms(db_p):5.0f};"
          f" corrections rms {rms(sent_y):5.0f} / {rms(sent_p):5.0f} (thousandths of a degree)")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--last", type=int, default=0)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    ss = sessions(TRACE)
    for s in ss[-a.last:] if a.last else ss:
        report(s)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/ammo_hunt.py`:

```py
"""Finds where the loaded ammo type is kept (exploration, read-only).

    python tools/ammo_hunt.py

Each F8 in game (holding the weapon) records every field that refers to one of the weapon's
supported ammo types (SupportedAmmoList at props + 0x6f0, entries of 0xd8: id at +0x10, objects
at +0x8 and +0x18, the name via [entry + 0x18] -> [+0x10]) by id, entry address or object
address, in the weapon's object graph (see mod_hunt.graph) and the player's components and their
pointees. Switch ammo in game between presses (no workbench needed); after 4 presses (or F7 after
2) prints the fields whose ammo followed the switches, with the sequence of names.
"""

import struct
import time
import winsound

import mouse
from mem import Game
from mod_hunt import SIZE, entity_graph, graph
from targets import archetype, held_weapon_object, player_entity


def ammo_table(g, w):
    """{value: (index, name)} for each supported ammo of the held weapon: its id, its list entry
    and the two objects the entry points to."""
    props = g.u64(g.u64(w + 0x168) + 0x10)
    lst, n = g.u64(props + 0x6F0), g.u32(props + 0x6F8)
    out = {}
    for k in range(min(n or 0, 16)):
        ent = lst + k * 0xD8
        desc = g.u64(ent + 0x18)
        buf = g.u64(desc + 0x10) if desc else None
        raw = g.read(buf, 96) if buf else b""
        i = raw.find(b"Ammo/") if raw else -1
        name = raw[i:].split(bytes(1))[0].decode(errors="replace") if i >= 0 else f"#{k}"
        for kind, v in (("id", g.u64(ent + 0x10)), ("entry", ent), ("obj", g.u64(ent + 0x8)), ("desc", desc)):
            if v:
                out[v] = (k, f"{name} ({kind})")
    return out


def record(g, w, ids, player):
    hits = {}
    objs = {**graph(g, w), **entity_graph(g, player, "P")}
    for label, a in objs.items():
        b = g.read(a, SIZE) or g.read(a, 0x200) or b""
        for o in range(0, len(b) - 7, 8):
            v = struct.unpack_from("<Q", b, o)[0]
            if v in ids:
                hits[(label, o)] = v
    return hits


def main():
    g = Game()
    state, snaps = {}, []
    print("hold the weapon and press F8; switch ammo in game between presses (F7 = done after 2)",
          flush=True)
    ids = {}
    while len(snaps) < 4:
        time.sleep(0.02)
        if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
            continue
        if mouse.user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x76) & 0x8000 and len(snaps) >= 2:
            break
        down = mouse.user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x77) & 0x8000 != 0
        hit, state["f8"] = down and not state.get("f8"), down
        if not hit:
            continue
        player = player_entity(g)
        w = held_weapon_object(g, player) if player else None
        e = g.u64(g.u64(w + 8) + 8) if w and g.u64(w + 8) else None
        if not e:
            winsound.Beep(400, 300)
            continue
        ids.update(ammo_table(g, w))
        hits = record(g, w, ids, player)
        snaps.append(hits)
        print(f"snapshot {len(snaps)}: {archetype(g, e)}, {len(hits)} fields refer to an ammo", flush=True)
        winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    keys = set.intersection(*(set(s) for s in snaps))
    moving = [k for k in keys if len({ids[s[k]][0] for s in snaps}) > 1]
    print(f"{len(moving)} fields followed the ammo switches:")
    for label, o in sorted(moving):
        print(f"  {label:40} +{o:#05x}  " + " -> ".join(ids[s[(label, o)]][1] for s in snaps))
    print(f"{len(keys) - len(moving)} fields kept the same ammo (lists, defaults)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/ballistics.py`:

```py
"""Bullet trajectories from the game's own bullet traces (offline only, read-only for the game).

    python tools/ballistics.py [--ms 10000] [--filter 0x11fbb]
    python tools/ballistics.py --load captures/ballistics_XXXX.json     # analyse a saved capture

Press F8 in game (one beep), then fc6internal.dll (v7 trace: the Dr0 breakpoint on the per-ray
query stays armed, no code patch) records for --ms milliseconds every game ray query with the
bullet collision filter, while this side logs the camera and the left mouse button (two beeps at
the end). Fire single shots at distant ground or at the sky during that time.

A projectile in flight queries one segment per frame, from its previous position to its next
one: segments that continue each other form a trajectory. Prints per trajectory where it starts
(against the eye), its speed, vertical acceleration (gravity) and horizontal slow-down (drag);
a shot that is a single straight ray is a hitscan. Saves everything to captures/ballistics_*.json.
"""

import argparse
import ctypes
import json
import math
import mmap
import os
import struct
import time
import winsound

import mouse
from mem import Game
from rays import BULLET_FILTER, MAGIC, MSG_OFF, NAME
from targets import camera

WATCH_OFF = MSG_OFF + 128
TRACE_OFF = WATCH_OFF + 24 + 32 * 208  # watch header (<QIIII) and 32 WatchHit
TRACE_HDR = struct.Struct("<IIIIq")  # ms, filter, count, total, qpcFrequency
TRACE_RAY = struct.Struct("<6fIIqIIQ")  # from, to, filter, flags, qpc, thread, pad, caller
TRACE_RAYS_OFF = TRACE_OFF + TRACE_HDR.size
MAX_TRACE = 16384
SIZE = TRACE_RAYS_OFF + MAX_TRACE * TRACE_RAY.size
EXEC_OFF = SIZE  # execAddr[3] (v8)
SNAP_HDR_OFF = EXEC_OFF + 24  # snapCount, pad (v9)
SNAP_FRAMES = 6
# index, pad, entry, results, batch, frames, frame registers, around results, batch bytes (v10)
SNAP = struct.Struct(f"<IIQQQ{SNAP_FRAMES}Q{SNAP_FRAMES * 8}Q1024s256s")
FRAME_REGS = ("rbx", "rbp", "rsi", "rdi", "r12", "r13", "r14", "r15")
SNAPS_OFF = SNAP_HDR_OFF + 8
MAX_SNAPS = 64
SNAP_HALF = 0x200
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
CAPTURES = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "captures")

kernel32 = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32")
user32 = ctypes.WinDLL("user32")
VK_F8 = 0x77


def qpc():
    c = ctypes.c_int64()
    kernel32.QueryPerformanceCounter(ctypes.byref(c))
    return c.value


def record(ms, filt):
    g = Game()
    m = mmap.mmap(-1, MSG_OFF, tagname=NAME)
    magic, version = struct.unpack_from("<II", m, 0)
    if magic != MAGIC or version < 7:
        raise SystemExit("fc6internal.dll v7 is not loaded (rebuild, then build/Release/fc6inject.exe)")
    m = mmap.mmap(-1, SNAPS_OFF + MAX_SNAPS * SNAP.size if version >= 10 else SIZE, tagname=NAME)
    print("in game, press F8 to start recording (one beep; two beeps at the end)...", flush=True)
    while not (mouse.foreground_pid() == g.pid and user32.GetAsyncKeyState(VK_F8) & 0x8000):
        time.sleep(0.02)
    struct.pack_into("<I", m, TRACE_OFF + 4, filt)
    struct.pack_into("<I", m, TRACE_OFF, ms)  # last: the DLL starts on this
    winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    print(f"recording {ms / 1000:.0f} s: fire single shots at distant ground or at the sky", flush=True)
    cams, clicks, was_down = [], [], False
    while struct.unpack_from("<I", m, TRACE_OFF)[0]:
        t = qpc()
        cam = camera(g)
        if cam:
            cams.append((t, cam[3][:3], cam[1][:3]))
        down = mouse.key_down("lbutton")
        if down and not was_down:
            clicks.append(t)
        was_down = down
        time.sleep(0.004)
    for _ in range(2):
        winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    _ms, _f, count, total, freq = TRACE_HDR.unpack_from(m, TRACE_OFF)
    rays = []
    for i in range(count):
        fx, fy, fz, tx, ty, tz, f, flags, t, thread, _pad, caller = TRACE_RAY.unpack_from(
            m, TRACE_RAYS_OFF + i * TRACE_RAY.size)
        rays.append({"from": (fx, fy, fz), "to": (tx, ty, tz), "filter": f, "flags": flags, "qpc": t,
                     "thread": thread, "caller": caller})
    snaps = []
    if version >= 10:
        for i in range(struct.unpack_from("<I", m, SNAP_HDR_OFF)[0]):
            index, _pad, entry, results, batch, *rest = SNAP.unpack_from(m, SNAPS_OFF + i * SNAP.size)
            frames, regs = rest[:SNAP_FRAMES], rest[SNAP_FRAMES:SNAP_FRAMES * 9]
            around, batch_bytes = rest[SNAP_FRAMES * 9:]
            snaps.append({"index": index, "entry": entry, "results": results, "batch": batch,
                          "frames": list(frames), "frame_regs": [list(regs[k * 8:k * 8 + 8]) for k in range(SNAP_FRAMES)],
                          "around": around.hex(), "batch_bytes": batch_bytes.hex()})
    print(f"{count} traced queries ({total} matching calls), {len(clicks)} clicks, {len(cams)} camera samples,"
          f" {len(snaps)} snapshots")
    return {"filter": filt, "freq": freq, "rays": rays, "cams": cams, "clicks": clicks, "snaps": snaps}


def analyse_snaps(data, limit=6):
    """Looks for the bullet's state in the memory around each traced query's results pointer:
    its position (the query's from or to), a velocity vector along the segment, and lone floats
    that look like a bullet speed or gravity. Offsets are relative to the results pointer."""
    rays = data["rays"]
    for s in data.get("snaps", [])[:limit]:
        r = rays[s["index"]]
        raw = bytes.fromhex(s["around"])
        fl = [struct.unpack_from("<f", raw, o)[0] for o in range(0, len(raw) - 3, 4)]
        step = sub(r["to"], r["from"])
        n = norm(step) or 1.0
        d = [x / n for x in step]
        found = []
        for k in range(len(fl) - 2):
            v = fl[k:k + 3]
            if not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in v):
                continue
            off = k * 4 - SNAP_HALF
            if norm(sub(v, r["from"])) < 0.02:
                found.append(f"from@{off:+#x}")
            elif norm(sub(v, r["to"])) < 0.02:
                found.append(f"to@{off:+#x}")
            else:
                vn = norm(v)
                if 50 < vn < 3000 and sum(v[i] * d[i] for i in range(3)) / vn > 0.995:
                    found.append(f"vel@{off:+#x}={vn:.2f}")
        for k, x in enumerate(fl):
            off = k * 4 - SNAP_HALF
            if math.isfinite(x) and (590 < x < 640 or 15 < x < 18.5 or 280 < x < 295):
                found.append(f"{x:.4f}@{off:+#x}")
        ptrs = sorted({struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, o)[0] for o in range(0, len(raw) - 7, 8)} - {0})
        print(f"query {s['index']}: entry {s['entry']:#x} results {s['results']:#x} batch {s['batch']:#x}"
              f"  segment {n:.2f} m\n   " + "  ".join(found[:40]))
        print(f"   {len(ptrs)} distinct qwords; batch bytes {s['batch_bytes'][:96]}...")
        for f, regs in zip(s.get("frames", []), s.get("frame_regs", [])):
            print(f"   caller {f:#x}  " + " ".join(f"{n}={v:#x}" for n, v in zip(FRAME_REGS, regs) if v))


def scan_frame_objects(data, g, size=0x1000):
    """Reads the live objects that the callers of the bullet queries held in their registers and
    prints floats that look like the bullet's speed (590-640) or gravity (15-18.5)."""
    seen = {}
    for s in data.get("snaps", []):
        for fi, (f, regs) in enumerate(zip(s.get("frames", []), s.get("frame_regs", []))):
            for n, v in zip(FRAME_REGS, regs):
                if 0x10000000000 <= v < 0x7FF000000000 and not g.in_engine(v):
                    seen.setdefault(v, f"frame{fi}({f - g.base:+#x}).{n}")
    for addr, where in sorted(seen.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]):
        raw = g.read(addr, size)
        if not raw:
            continue
        hits = []
        for o in range(0, len(raw) - 3, 4):
            x = struct.unpack_from("<f", raw, o)[0]
            if math.isfinite(x) and (590 < x < 640 or 15 < x < 18.5):
                hits.append(f"{x:.4f}@{o:#x}")
        vt = g.vt(addr)
        print(f"{where:34} {addr:#x} vt {('+%x' % vt) if vt else '-':10} " + " ".join(hits[:12]))


MONITOR_OFF = SNAPS_OFF + MAX_SNAPS * SNAP.size
MONITOR = struct.Struct("<IIII")  # on, beat, head, pad (v11)


class BulletMonitor:
    """The player's bullets, live (fc6internal.dll v11: continuous trace of the bullet filter).

    poll(eyes) returns an estimate() for each trajectory of the player that just ended; eyes are
    recent (qpc, eye position) camera samples, since the player's bullets start at the eye."""

    QUIET = 0.15  # seconds without a new segment: the trajectory is over
    # A ring slot older than the previous entry by this much still holds an old entry: another
    # thread is writing it right now, read it on the next poll.
    STALE = 0.5

    def __init__(self, filt=BULLET_FILTER):
        m = mmap.mmap(-1, MSG_OFF, tagname=NAME)
        magic, version = struct.unpack_from("<II", m, 0)
        if magic != MAGIC or version < 11:
            raise RuntimeError("fc6internal.dll v11 is not loaded")
        self.m = mmap.mmap(-1, MONITOR_OFF + MONITOR.size, tagname=NAME)
        self.filt = filt
        self.beat = 0
        self.next = None  # next ring entry to read
        self.open = []
        self.start()

    def start(self):
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, TRACE_OFF + 4, self.filt)
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, MONITOR_OFF, 1)
        self.next = self.last = None

    def close(self):
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, MONITOR_OFF, 0)

    def poll(self, eyes):
        self.beat = (self.beat + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, MONITOR_OFF + 4, self.beat)
        on, _beat, head, _pad = MONITOR.unpack_from(self.m, MONITOR_OFF)
        if not on:  # the DLL stopped it (no beat for 3 s, e.g. the game was paused)
            self.start()
            return []
        freq = TRACE_HDR.unpack_from(self.m, TRACE_OFF)[4]
        now = qpc()
        if self.next is None or head < self.next:
            self.next = head
        self.next = max(self.next, head - MAX_TRACE)
        while self.next < head:
            fx, fy, fz, tx, ty, tz, _f, _flags, t, _thread, _pad, _caller = TRACE_RAY.unpack_from(
                self.m, TRACE_RAYS_OFF + (self.next % MAX_TRACE) * TRACE_RAY.size)
            if self.last is not None and (self.last - t) / freq > self.STALE:
                break
            self.last = t if self.last is None else max(self.last, t)
            self.add({"from": (fx, fy, fz), "to": (tx, ty, tz), "qpc": t})
            self.next += 1
        done = [c for c in self.open if (now - c[-1]["qpc"]) / freq > self.QUIET]
        self.open = [c for c in self.open if c not in done]
        self.freq = freq
        out = []
        for c in done:
            eye = min(eyes, key=lambda e: abs(e[0] - c[0]["qpc"]), default=None)
            if eye and norm(sub(c[0]["from"], eye[1])) < 1.0:
                est = estimate(c, freq)
                if est:
                    est["chain"] = c
                    out.append(est)
        return out

    def add(self, r):
        for c in self.open:
            if r["qpc"] > c[-1]["qpc"] and norm(sub(r["from"], c[-1]["to"])) < 0.05:
                c.append(r)
                return
        self.open.append([r])


def sub(a, b):
    return [a[i] - b[i] for i in range(3)]


def norm(v):
    return math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in v))


def chains(rays, tol=0.05, gap=0.5, freq=1):
    """Groups segments that continue each other (end of one = start of the next) into trajectories."""
    out, open_ = [], []
    for r in sorted(rays, key=lambda r: r["qpc"]):
        open_ = [c for c in open_ if (r["qpc"] - c[-1]["qpc"]) / freq < gap]
        for c in open_:
            if r["qpc"] > c[-1]["qpc"] and norm(sub(r["from"], c[-1]["to"])) < tol:
                c.append(r)
                break
        else:
            c = [r]
            open_.append(c)
            out.append(c)
    return out


def solve3(a, b):
    """Solves a 3x3 linear system (Cramer)."""
    def det(m):
        return (m[0][0] * (m[1][1] * m[2][2] - m[1][2] * m[2][1]) - m[0][1] * (m[1][0] * m[2][2] - m[1][2] * m[2][0])
                + m[0][2] * (m[1][0] * m[2][1] - m[1][1] * m[2][0]))
    d = det(a)
    if abs(d) < 1e-12:
        return None
    out = []
    for k in range(3):
        m = [row[:] for row in a]
        for i in range(3):
            m[i][k] = b[i]
        out.append(det(m) / d)
    return out


def fit_quadratic(ts, ys):
    """Least squares y = c0 + c1 t + c2 t^2; returns (c0, c1, c2) or None."""
    s = [sum(t ** k for t in ts) for k in range(5)]
    a = [[s[0], s[1], s[2]], [s[1], s[2], s[3]], [s[2], s[3], s[4]]]
    b = [sum(y * t ** k for t, y in zip(ts, ys)) for k in range(3)]
    return solve3(a, b)


def nearest_cam(cams, t):
    return min(cams, key=lambda c: abs(c[0] - t)) if cams else None


def describe(chain, data):
    freq = data["freq"]
    t0 = chain[0]["qpc"]
    first = chain[0]
    seg0 = sub(first["to"], first["from"])
    lengths = [norm(sub(r["to"], r["from"])) for r in chain]
    cam = nearest_cam(data["cams"], t0)
    row = {"n": len(chain), "t": (t0 - data["t_start"]) / freq, "length": sum(lengths),
           "duration": (chain[-1]["qpc"] - t0) / freq, "callers": sorted({hex(r["caller"]) for r in chain}),
           "threads": len({r["thread"] for r in chain})}
    if cam:
        eye, fwd = cam[1], cam[2]
        row["start_from_eye"] = norm(sub(first["from"], eye))
        d = [x / (norm(seg0) or 1) for x in seg0]
        row["angle_to_view_deg"] = math.degrees(math.acos(max(-1, min(1, sum(d[i] * fwd[i] for i in range(3))))))
        row["elevation_deg"] = math.degrees(math.asin(max(-1, min(1, d[2]))))
        # Signed: where the bullet went against the camera forward, in cm at 100 m.
        hn = math.hypot(fwd[0], fwd[1]) or 1.0
        left = (-fwd[1] / hn, fwd[0] / hn, 0.0)
        row["off_up_cm100"] = 1e4 * (math.asin(max(-1, min(1, d[2]))) - math.asin(max(-1, min(1, fwd[2]))))
        row["off_left_cm100"] = 1e4 * sum(d[i] * left[i] for i in range(3))
    clicks = [(t0 - c) / freq for c in data["clicks"] if 0 <= (t0 - c) / freq < 0.3]
    row["after_click_s"] = min(clicks) if clicks else None
    est = estimate(chain, freq)
    if est:
        row.update(est)
        half = len(chain) // 2
        ts = [(r["qpc"] - t0) / freq for r in chain]
        # Drag check: speed over the first half against the second half.
        if half >= 2 and ts[-1] > ts[half] > 0:
            row["speed_halves"] = (sum(lengths[:half]) / ts[half],
                                   sum(lengths[half:-1]) / (ts[-1] - ts[half]))
        row["step_mean"] = sum(lengths[:-1]) / max(1, len(lengths) - 1)
        row["frame_ms"] = 1000 * ts[-1] / (len(chain) - 1)
    return row


def estimate(chain, freq):
    """Speed, gravity and horizontal extent of one trajectory, or None if it is too short.
    Speed = path length over flight time (averages out frame timing). Gravity comes from the
    curvature of height against horizontal distance, z = a + b x + c x^2, so g = -2 c v_h^2: the
    shape doesn't depend on frame timing, only the speed does."""
    if len(chain) < 4:
        return None
    ts = [(r["qpc"] - chain[0]["qpc"]) / freq for r in chain]
    seg = [norm(sub(r["to"], r["from"])) for r in chain]
    if ts[-1] <= 0:
        return None
    speed = sum(seg[:-1]) / ts[-1]
    d0 = sub(chain[0]["to"], chain[0]["from"])
    hn = math.hypot(d0[0], d0[1]) or 1.0
    h = (d0[0] / hn, d0[1] / hn)
    p0 = chain[0]["from"]
    pts = [r["from"] for r in chain] + [chain[-1]["to"]]
    xs = [(p[0] - p0[0]) * h[0] + (p[1] - p0[1]) * h[1] for p in pts]
    zs = [p[2] - p0[2] for p in pts]
    out = {"speed": speed, "gravity": None, "extent": xs[-1], "n": len(chain), "length": sum(seg),
           "elevation_deg": math.degrees(math.atan2(d0[2], hn))}
    fit = fit_quadratic(xs, zs)
    if fit:
        vh = speed * math.cos(math.atan(fit[1]))
        out["gravity"] = -2 * fit[2] * vh * vh
        out["residual"] = max(abs(z - (fit[0] + fit[1] * x + fit[2] * x * x)) for x, z in zip(xs, zs))
    return out


def analyse(data):
    rays = data["rays"]
    if not rays:
        print("no bullet query recorded: was a shot fired while recording?")
        return
    data["t_start"] = min(r["qpc"] for r in rays + [{"qpc": c[0]} for c in data["cams"]])
    cs = chains(rays, freq=data["freq"])
    print(f"{len(cs)} trajectories from {len(rays)} segments")
    for c in cs:
        row = describe(c, data)
        who = "player?" if row.get("start_from_eye", 99) < 3.0 else "other"
        line = (f"t={row['t']:6.2f}s n={row['n']:4} {who:7} len={row['length']:7.1f} m dur={row['duration']:5.2f}s"
                f" eye+{row.get('start_from_eye', float('nan')):5.2f} m view{row.get('angle_to_view_deg', float('nan')):5.2f}deg")
        if row["after_click_s"] is not None:
            line += f" click+{row['after_click_s'] * 1000:3.0f}ms"
        if "off_up_cm100" in row:
            up, left = row["off_up_cm100"], row["off_left_cm100"]
            line += (f"  bullet vs view at 100 m: {abs(up):3.0f} cm {'up' if up >= 0 else 'down'},"
                     f" {abs(left):3.0f} cm {'left' if left >= 0 else 'right'}")
        if "speed" in row:
            g = row["gravity"]
            halves = row.get("speed_halves")
            line += (f"\n      speed {row['speed']:7.1f} m/s"
                     + (f" (halves {halves[0]:.0f}/{halves[1]:.0f})" if halves else "")
                     + (f"  gravity {g:6.2f} m/s2 (fit residual {row['residual'] * 100:.1f} cm)" if g is not None else "")
                     + f"  over {row['extent']:.0f} m  elev {row['elevation_deg']:5.1f}deg"
                     f"  step {row['step_mean']:5.2f} m / {row['frame_ms']:4.1f} ms")
        line += f"  caller {','.join(row['callers'])}"
        print(line)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--ms", type=int, default=10000)
    ap.add_argument("--filter", type=lambda s: int(s, 0), default=BULLET_FILTER, help="0 = every query")
    ap.add_argument("--load", help="analyse a saved capture instead of recording")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.load:
        data = json.load(open(args.load))
    else:
        data = record(args.ms, args.filter)
        os.makedirs(CAPTURES, exist_ok=True)
        path = os.path.join(CAPTURES, time.strftime("ballistics_%Y%m%d_%H%M%S.json"))
        json.dump(data, open(path, "w"))
        print(f"saved {os.path.normpath(path)}")
    analyse(data)
    analyse_snaps(data)
    if not args.load:
        scan_frame_objects(data, Game())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/calibrate.py`:

```py
"""Measures how many mouse counts turn the camera by one radian (yaw and pitch).

    python tools/calibrate.py

Waits until Far Cry 6 is the foreground window, sends small relative mouse moves and reads the
camera matrix before/after. Saves the result to tools/calibration.json for aim.py and to the [mouse] section of
build/Release/fc6hack.ini for the aimbot inside fc6internal.dll.
"""

import configparser
import json
import math
import os
import time

import mouse
from mem import Game
from targets import camera

OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "calibration.json")
INI = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "build", "Release", "fc6hack.ini")


def save_ini(result):
    """The DLL reads fc6hack.ini when it starts or when F6 switches the aimbot back on."""
    cfg = configparser.ConfigParser(interpolation=None)
    cfg.optionxform = str
    cfg.read(INI)
    if not cfg.has_section("mouse"):
        cfg.add_section("mouse")
    for key in ("yaw_counts_per_rad", "pitch_counts_per_rad"):
        if result[key]:
            cfg["mouse"][key] = f"{result[key]:.4f}"
    with open(INI, "w") as f:
        cfg.write(f, space_around_delimiters=False)


def angles(g):
    f = camera(g)[1]
    return math.atan2(-f[0], f[1]), math.asin(max(-1.0, min(1.0, f[2])))


def wrap(a):
    return (a + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi


def measure(g, dx, dy, steps=10):
    y0, p0 = angles(g)
    for _ in range(steps):
        mouse.move(dx, dy)
        time.sleep(0.01)
    time.sleep(0.3)
    y1, p1 = angles(g)
    # put the view back
    for _ in range(steps):
        mouse.move(-dx, -dy)
        time.sleep(0.01)
    time.sleep(0.3)
    return wrap(y1 - y0), p1 - p0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    g = Game()
    print("Click into the game window (offline, on foot)...")
    while mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
        time.sleep(0.2)
    time.sleep(1.0)
    dyaw, _ = measure(g, 20, 0)      # 200 counts right
    _, dpitch = measure(g, 0, 20)    # 200 counts down
    result = {
        "yaw_counts_per_rad": 200 / dyaw if abs(dyaw) > 1e-4 else None,
        "pitch_counts_per_rad": 200 / dpitch if abs(dpitch) > 1e-4 else None,
    }
    json.dump(result, open(OUT, "w"), indent=2)
    save_ini(result)
    print(f"yaw change {dyaw:+.4f} rad, pitch change {dpitch:+.4f} rad for 200 counts")
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

```

`tools/compare_transforms.py`:

```py
"""Compare `fc6probe matrices` samples to single out the player and the camera.

    python tools/compare_transforms.py still.tsv looked.tsv walked.tsv

still  -> player idle, mouse untouched
looked -> only the camera turned (no movement)
walked -> walked ~10 m forward and stopped

Camera candidates: rotation changed between still and looked, position barely moved.
Player candidates: world-space, moved 5-20 m between looked and walked, did not move between
still and looked. Results are grouped by (vtable, distance from vtable, layout) = object type.
"""

import collections
import csv
import math
import sys


def load(path):
    with open(path) as f:
        return {r["addr"]: r for r in csv.DictReader(f, delimiter="\t")}


def pos(r):
    return tuple(float(r[k]) for k in ("px", "py", "pz"))


def rot(r):
    return [float(r[k]) for k in ("r0x", "r0y", "r0z", "r1x", "r1y", "r1z", "r2x", "r2y", "r2z")]


def kind(r):
    return (r["vtable"], r["vt_dist"], r["layout"])


def main(still_path, looked_path, walked_path):
    still, looked, walked = load(still_path), load(looked_path), load(walked_path)
    common = set(still) & set(looked) & set(walked)
    world = [a for a in common if math.hypot(*pos(walked[a])[:2]) > 50]

    camera = []
    for a in world:
        drot = max(abs(x - y) for x, y in zip(rot(still[a]), rot(looked[a])))
        dpos = math.dist(pos(still[a]), pos(looked[a]))
        if drot > 0.2 and dpos < 1.0:
            camera.append((drot, a))

    player = []
    for a in world:
        idle = math.dist(pos(still[a]), pos(looked[a]))
        walk = math.dist(pos(looked[a]), pos(walked[a]))
        if idle < 0.3 and 5 < walk < 20:
            player.append((walk, a))

    for title, hits, sample in (("CAMERA candidates", camera, looked), ("PLAYER candidates", player, walked)):
        print(f"\n{title}: {len(hits)}")
        by_kind = collections.defaultdict(list)
        for score, a in hits:
            by_kind[kind(sample[a])].append((score, a))
        for k, items in sorted(by_kind.items(), key=lambda kv: -len(kv[1]))[:15]:
            score, a = max(items)
            print(f"  {k}  n={len(items):4d}  e.g. {a}  pos={pos(sample[a])}  score={score:.2f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 4:
        sys.exit(__doc__)
    main(*sys.argv[1:])

```

`tools/dinput_probe.py`:

```py
"""Addresses of DirectInput8's GetDeviceState / GetDeviceData for the mouse (read-only).

    python tools/dinput_probe.py

Far Cry 6 reads the mouse through DirectInput8 (FC_m64d3d12.dll imports DirectInput8Create, not
the raw input API). This creates a mouse device in this process and prints its vtable methods:
system DLLs are mapped at the same address in every process of a boot, so the same addresses can
be given to tools/watch.py --exec to catch the game's own calls (and the buffer it reads into).
"""

import ctypes
import ctypes.wintypes as wt

import mem


class GUID(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("d1", wt.DWORD), ("d2", wt.WORD), ("d3", wt.WORD), ("d4", ctypes.c_ubyte * 8)]


def guid(d1, d2, d3, *d4):
    return GUID(d1, d2, d3, (ctypes.c_ubyte * 8)(*d4))


IID_IDirectInput8W = guid(0xBF798031, 0x483A, 0x4DA2, 0xAA, 0x99, 0x5D, 0x64, 0xED, 0x36, 0x97, 0x00)
GUID_SysMouse = guid(0x6F1D2B60, 0xD5A0, 0x11CF, 0xBF, 0xC7, 0x44, 0x45, 0x53, 0x54, 0x00, 0x00)
DIRECTINPUT_VERSION = 0x0800
# IDirectInputDevice8 methods: 0 QueryInterface 1 AddRef 2 Release 3 GetCapabilities 4 EnumObjects
# 5 GetProperty 6 SetProperty 7 Acquire 8 Unacquire 9 GetDeviceState 10 GetDeviceData
METHODS = {9: "GetDeviceState", 10: "GetDeviceData"}


def vtable(obj):
    return ctypes.cast(ctypes.cast(obj, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p))[0], ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p))


def main():
    ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetModuleHandleW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p  # 64-bit handles
    dinput = ctypes.WinDLL("dinput8")
    di = ctypes.c_void_p()
    create = dinput.DirectInput8Create
    create.argtypes = [wt.HINSTANCE, wt.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(GUID), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p), ctypes.c_void_p]
    hr = create(ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetModuleHandleW(None), DIRECTINPUT_VERSION, ctypes.byref(IID_IDirectInput8W),
                ctypes.byref(di), None)
    if hr != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"DirectInput8Create failed: {hr & 0xFFFFFFFF:#x}")
    # IDirectInput8W::CreateDevice is method 3.
    create_device = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(ctypes.c_long, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.POINTER(GUID),
                                       ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p), ctypes.c_void_p)(vtable(di)[3])
    dev = ctypes.c_void_p()
    hr = create_device(di, ctypes.byref(GUID_SysMouse), ctypes.byref(dev), None)
    if hr != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"CreateDevice failed: {hr & 0xFFFFFFFF:#x}")
    base = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetModuleHandleW("dinput8.dll")
    g = mem.Game()
    game_base = mem._module(g.pid, "dinput8.dll")
    print(f"dinput8.dll here {base:#x}, in the game {game_base[0]:#x}" if game_base else "dinput8.dll not in the game")
    for k, name in METHODS.items():
        a = vtable(dev)[k]
        print(f"{name:15} {a:#x}  (dinput8 + {a - base:#x})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/disasm.py`:

```py
"""Disassemble part of the engine dump with llvm-objdump (no extra Python package needed).

    python tools/disasm.py <dump.bin> <hex offset> [length=0x200] [--back 0x40]
    python tools/disasm.py live <hex offset> ...      # read the code from the running game instead

Wraps the bytes into a minimal ELF64 .text section placed at the module offset, so addresses in
the listing are module offsets (FC_m64d3d12.dll + offset), then runs llvm-objdump on it.
RIP-relative targets are annotated with the string they point to when there is one.
"""

import os
import re
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

OBJDUMP = r"C:\Program Files\LLVM\bin\llvm-objdump.exe"


def make_elf(code, vaddr):
    shstr = b"\0.text\0.shstrtab\0"
    ehdr_size, shdr_size = 64, 64
    text_off = ehdr_size
    shstr_off = text_off + len(code)
    shoff = (shstr_off + len(shstr) + 7) & ~7
    ehdr = b"\x7fELF" + bytes([2, 1, 1, 0]) + bytes(8)
    ehdr += struct.pack("<HHIQQQIHHHHHH", 1, 62, 1, 0, 0, shoff, 0, ehdr_size, 0, 0, shdr_size, 3, 2)
    null = bytes(shdr_size)
    text = struct.pack("<IIQQQQIIQQ", 1, 1, 0x6, vaddr, text_off, len(code), 0, 0, 16, 0)
    strtab = struct.pack("<IIQQQQIIQQ", 7, 3, 0, 0, shstr_off, len(shstr), 0, 0, 1, 0)
    body = ehdr + code + shstr
    body += bytes(shoff - len(body))
    return body + null + text + strtab


def string_at(dump, off):
    if not (0 <= off < len(dump)):
        return None
    m = re.match(rb"[\x20-\x7e]{4,80}\x00", dump[off:off + 81])
    return m.group()[:-1].decode() if m else None


class LiveModule:
    """The engine module in the running game, sliced like the bytes of a dump (read-only)."""

    def __init__(self):
        from mem import Game
        self.g = Game()

    def __len__(self):
        return self.g.size

    def __getitem__(self, s):
        start, stop = max(0, s.start), min(self.g.size, s.stop)
        return (self.g.read(self.g.base + start, stop - start) or b"") if stop > start else b""


def main():
    path, start = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2], 16)
    length = int(sys.argv[3], 16) if len(sys.argv) > 3 and not sys.argv[3].startswith("--") else 0x200
    back = int(sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--back") + 1], 16) if "--back" in sys.argv else 0
    if path == "live":
        dump = LiveModule()
    else:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            dump = f.read()
    start -= back
    code = dump[start:start + length + back]
    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False, suffix=".o") as t:
        t.write(make_elf(code, start))
    out = subprocess.run([OBJDUMP, "-d", "--no-show-raw-insn", "-M", "intel", t.name],
                         capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    os.unlink(t.name)
    for line in out.splitlines():
        m = re.match(r"\s*([0-9a-f]+):\s+(.*)", line)
        if not m:
            continue
        addr, ins = int(m.group(1), 16), m.group(2)
        note = ""
        r = re.search(r"\[rip ([+-]) 0x([0-9a-f]+)\]", ins)
        if r:
            # objdump prints the target in a trailing comment as well; compute it to be safe
            size_hint = re.search(r"#\s*0x([0-9a-f]+)", ins)
            if size_hint:
                tgt = int(size_hint.group(1), 16)
                s = string_at(dump, tgt)
                note = f'   ; "{s}"' if s else f"   ; +{tgt:x}"
        print(f"{addr:9x}  {ins}{note}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/esp.py`:

```py
"""Skeleton ESP: a debug overlay showing what the aimbot reads (offline single-player only).

    python tools/esp.py              # skeletons, chosen aim point, source labels
    python tools/esp.py --all-bones  # also every bone of the body pose as a dot
    python tools/esp.py --max-dist 80   # default 300 m
    python tools/esp.py --hud-only      # only the aimbot status, no skeletons or labels

The aimbot's status is shown on the left: held weapon and its ballistics profile, current target,
and a verdict for each shot fired while aiming. It comes from fc6internal.dll v13 (shared memory,
the aimbot inside the DLL) or else from tools/aim.py (captures/aim_status.json).

F9 in game saves a capture for analysis: captures/esp_<time>.png (screen with the overlay) and
captures/esp_<time>.json (every NPC: source, bone count, check result, key bone positions).

Draws over the game window (borderless/windowed mode), click-through, read-only. For each NPC:
  - the body skeleton read from memory (lines between named bones) and the head point the aimbot
    would pick (targets.aim_points), coloured by the aimbot's decision: red = target
    (targets.is_hostile, alive), green = Libertad (attitude 4), grey = not a target, dark = dead;
  - a yellow square instead of the circle when the head point does not come from the skeleton
    (ragdoll or fixed height);
  - a label: distance, attitude (hostile / calm / ally), health or DEAD, bone count, and why
    the skeleton was rejected if it was.

Projection (docs/reversing.md "Camera"): the camera matrix is followed by the projection matrix,
x scale at +0x40 and y scale at +0x54 (they change when aiming down sights).
"""

import argparse
import ctypes
import ctypes.wintypes as wt
import json
import math
import mmap
import os
import struct
import time
import tkinter as tk

import mouse
import skeleton as sk
from mem import Game
from targets import aim_points, camera_with_projection, faction, health, is_dead, is_hostile, targets

user32 = ctypes.WinDLL("user32", use_last_error=True)
KEY = "#010203"  # transparent colour of the overlay window

CHAINS = [
    ("Hips", "Spine", "Spine1", "Spine2", "Neck", "Head", "HeadEnd"),
    ("Spine2", "LeftShoulder", "LeftArm", "LeftForeArm", "LeftHand"),
    ("Spine2", "RightShoulder", "RightArm", "RightForeArm", "RightHand"),
    ("Hips", "LeftUpLeg", "LeftLeg", "LeftFoot"),
    ("Hips", "RightUpLeg", "RightLeg", "RightFoot"),
]
CHAIN_BONES = sorted({n for c in CHAINS for n in c} | {"Neck1"})
TARGET, ALLY, OTHER, DEAD, NOT_SKELETON = "#ff4040", "#40ff60", "#b0b0b0", "#606060", "#ffd000"
ATTITUDE = {1: "calm", 2: "hostile", 4: "ally"}
# Written by tools/aim.py: its weapon profile, current target and last shots, shown top left.
STATUS = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "captures", "aim_status.json")
HUD_FONT = ("Consolas", 11, "bold")


kernel32 = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32")
kernel32.GetTickCount64.restype = ctypes.c_uint64


def dll_status(state):
    """Status of the aimbot inside fc6internal.dll (v13 HUD block), or None if it isn't running."""
    from port_check import VIEW_CHAR, VIEW_CHARS_OFF, VIEW_MAX
    from rays import MAGIC, NAME
    hud_off = VIEW_CHARS_OFF + VIEW_MAX * VIEW_CHAR.size
    head, event = struct.Struct("<QII200s200s"), struct.Struct("<Q200s")
    try:
        if "dll" not in state:
            state["dll"] = mmap.mmap(-1, hud_off + head.size + 5 * event.size, tagname=NAME)
        m = state["dll"]
        magic, version = struct.unpack_from("<II", m, 0)
        if magic != MAGIC or version < 13:
            return None
        tick, aim_on, _pad, weapon, target = head.unpack_from(m, hud_off)
        now_tick, now = kernel32.GetTickCount64(), time.time()
        if not tick or now_tick - tick > 5000:
            return None
        text = lambda b: b.split(bytes(1))[0].decode(errors="replace")
        events = []
        for k in range(5):
            t, e = event.unpack_from(m, hud_off + head.size + k * event.size)
            if t:
                events.append([now - (now_tick - t) / 1000.0, text(e)])
        return {"t": now, "weapon": text(weapon) + ("" if aim_on else "   [aimbot OFF: F6]"),
                "target": text(target), "events": events}
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None


def read_status(state):
    dll = dll_status(state)
    if dll:
        return dll
    try:
        mtime = os.stat(STATUS).st_mtime
        if mtime != state.get("status_mtime"):
            with open(STATUS) as f:
                state["status"] = json.load(f)
            state["status_mtime"] = mtime
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        pass  # missing, or being replaced right now
    return state.get("status")


def draw_hud(c, status, y=30):
    now = time.time()
    if not status or now - status.get("t", 0) > 5:
        lines = [("aimbot: not running", "#ff8080")]
    else:
        lines = [("weapon  " + status.get("weapon", ""), "#ffffff")]
        if status.get("target"):
            lines.append(("target  " + status["target"], TARGET))
        lines += [(text, "#40ff60" if " HIT" in text else "#ffd000")
                  for t, text in status.get("events", []) if now - t < 20]
    for text, colour in lines:
        c.create_text(9, y + 1, anchor="nw", fill="#000000", font=HUD_FONT, text=text)  # shadow
        c.create_text(8, y, anchor="nw", fill=colour, font=HUD_FONT, text=text)
        y += 19


def game_rect(pid):
    """(hwnd, (x, y, w, h)): the game's main window and its client area in screen pixels, or None."""
    found = []

    @ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(wt.BOOL, wt.HWND, wt.LPARAM)
    def cb(hwnd, _):
        p = wt.DWORD()
        user32.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, ctypes.byref(p))
        if p.value == pid and user32.IsWindowVisible(hwnd):
            r = wt.RECT()
            user32.GetClientRect(hwnd, ctypes.byref(r))
            if r.right * r.bottom > 0:
                found.append((r.right * r.bottom, hwnd, r.right, r.bottom))
        return True

    user32.EnumWindows(cb, 0)
    if not found:
        return None
    _, hwnd, w, h = max(found)
    pt = wt.POINT(0, 0)
    user32.ClientToScreen(hwnd, ctypes.byref(pt))
    return hwnd, (pt.x, pt.y, w, h)


class Projector:
    def __init__(self, cam, sx, sy, w, h):
        self.r, self.f, self.u, self.eye = cam
        self.sx, self.sy, self.w, self.h = sx, sy, w, h

    def __call__(self, p):
        d = [p[k] - self.eye[k] for k in range(3)]
        z = sum(d[k] * self.f[k] for k in range(3))
        if z < 0.1:
            return None
        x = sum(d[k] * self.r[k] for k in range(3)) / z * self.sx
        y = sum(d[k] * self.u[k] for k in range(3)) / z * self.sy
        return (1 + x) * self.w / 2, (1 - y) * self.h / 2


def decision_colour(g, entity, name, dead):
    """Colour of what the aimbot does with this character."""
    if dead:
        return DEAD
    if is_hostile(g, entity, name):
        return TARGET
    return ALLY if faction(g, entity) == 4 else OTHER


def make_overlay():
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.overrideredirect(True)
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    root.attributes("-transparentcolor", KEY)
    root.config(bg=KEY)
    canvas = tk.Canvas(root, bg=KEY, highlightthickness=0)
    canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    root.update_idletasks()
    hwnd = user32.GetAncestor(root.winfo_id(), 2)  # GA_ROOT
    GWL_EXSTYLE, WS_EX_LAYERED, WS_EX_TRANSPARENT = -20, 0x80000, 0x20
    WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW, WS_EX_NOACTIVATE = 0x80, 0x8000000
    style = user32.GetWindowLongW(hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE)
    user32.SetWindowLongW(hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE,
                          style | WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW | WS_EX_NOACTIVATE)
    return root, canvas


def draw_npc(g, c, proj, entity, pos, name, eye, all_bones, record=None):
    dead = is_dead(g, entity)
    colour = decision_colour(g, entity, name, dead)
    sel, reason = sk.select_pose(g, entity, tuple(CHAIN_BONES))
    world, idx = (sel["world"], sel["idx"]) if sel else (None, {})
    why = "ok" if sel else reason.split(" ")[0]
    if world:
        if all_bones:
            for p in world:
                s = proj(p)
                if s:
                    c.create_rectangle(s[0], s[1], s[0] + 1, s[1] + 1, outline=colour)
        for chain in CHAINS:
            prev = None
            for n in chain:
                s = proj(world[idx[n]]) if n in idx and idx[n] < len(world) else None
                if s and prev:
                    c.create_line(prev[0], prev[1], s[0], s[1], fill=colour, width=2)
                prev = s or prev
    label, head = aim_points(g, entity, pos)[0]
    src = label.split("~")[1] if "~" in label else ""
    dist = math.dist(eye, head)
    if record is not None:
        s = sk.skeleton_instance(g, entity)
        record.append({
            "name": name, "dist": round(dist, 2), "source": src or "skeleton", "bones": len(world or []),
            "pose": sel and sel["state"], "reason": reason, "check": why,
            "head_rig_animated": g.u32(s + 0x4B8) if s else None,
            "entity": [round(v, 3) for v in pos], "aim_head": [round(v, 3) for v in head],
            "on_screen": proj(head),
            "bones_world": {n: [round(v, 3) for v in world[i]] for n, i in idx.items() if world and i < len(world)},
        })
    s = proj(head)
    if not s:
        return
    r = max(3.0, min(14.0, 120.0 / max(dist, 1.0)))
    if src:  # not from the skeleton: flag it
        c.create_rectangle(s[0] - r, s[1] - r, s[0] + r, s[1] + r, outline=NOT_SKELETON, width=2)
    else:
        c.create_oval(s[0] - r, s[1] - r, s[0] + r, s[1] + r, outline=colour, width=2)
    what = f"{len(world)}b" + ("" if sel["state"] == "live" else " new") if sel else src
    hp = health(g, entity)
    life = " DEAD" if dead else (f" {hp[0]:.0f}/{hp[1]:.0f}hp" if hp else "")
    mood = "" if "_Neutral" in name and faction(g, entity) == 1 else f" {ATTITUDE.get(faction(g, entity), '?')}"
    text = f"{dist:.0f}m{mood}{life} {what}" + ("" if why == "ok" else f" [{why}]")
    c.create_text(s[0], s[1] - r - 8, text=text, fill=colour, font=("Consolas", 9))


CAPTURES = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "captures")


def save_capture(root, cam, sx, sy, record):
    """Screen (with the overlay) and NPC data, for offline analysis."""
    from PIL import ImageGrab  # only needed for captures
    root.update()
    os.makedirs(CAPTURES, exist_ok=True)
    stem = os.path.join(CAPTURES, time.strftime("esp_%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"))
    ImageGrab.grab(include_layered_windows=True).save(stem + ".png")
    with open(stem + ".json", "w") as f:
        json.dump({"camera": [list(r) for r in cam], "proj": [sx, sy], "npcs": record}, f, indent=1)
    print("capture", stem, flush=True)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--max-dist", type=float, default=300.0)
    ap.add_argument("--all-bones", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--hud-only", action="store_true", help="only the aimbot status, no skeletons or labels")
    ap.add_argument("--fps", type=float, default=30.0)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    ctypes.windll.shcore.SetProcessDpiAwareness(2)  # screen pixels, same as the game window
    g = Game()
    found = game_rect(g.pid)
    if not found:
        raise SystemExit("game window not found")
    root, canvas = make_overlay()
    x, y, w, h = found[1]
    root.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{x}+{y}")
    root.update()
    # Creating the overlay took the focus: hand it back so the game keeps keyboard and mouse.
    user32.SetForegroundWindow(found[0])
    state = {"rect": found[1], "rect_t": time.time(), "frame_ms": 0.0}

    def tick():
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        canvas.delete("all")
        if time.time() - state["rect_t"] > 1.0:
            found = game_rect(g.pid)
            rect = found[1] if found else None
            if rect and rect != state["rect"]:
                x, y, w, h = rect
                root.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{x}+{y}")
            state["rect"], state["rect_t"] = rect, time.time()
        cp = camera_with_projection(g) if state["rect"] else None
        f9 = bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x78) & 0x8000)
        capture = f9 and not state.get("f9")
        state["f9"] = f9
        if cp and mouse.foreground_pid() in (g.pid, os.getpid()):
            cam, sx, sy = cp
            _, _, w, h = state["rect"]
            proj = Projector(cam, sx, sy, w, h)
            record = [] if capture else None
            _, pts = targets(g)
            for _i, _p, entity, pos, name in [] if args.hud_only else pts:
                if "NPC" in name and math.dist(cam[3], pos) <= args.max_dist:
                    try:
                        draw_npc(g, canvas, proj, entity, pos, name, cam[3], args.all_bones, record)
                    except (TypeError, IndexError, ValueError):
                        pass  # entity freed mid-read
            # Left side at 40 % of the height: the game's mission tracker sits in the top left.
            top = int(h * 0.4)
            canvas.create_text(8, top, anchor="nw", fill="#ffffff", font=("Consolas", 9),
                               text=f"ESP {state['frame_ms']:.0f} ms  fov x{sx:.2f} y{sy:.2f}  F9 = capture")
            draw_hud(canvas, read_status(state), top + 20)
            if capture:
                save_capture(root, cam, sx, sy, record)
        state["frame_ms"] = 1000 * (time.perf_counter() - t0)
        root.after(max(1, int(1000 / args.fps - state["frame_ms"])), tick)

    tick()
    root.mainloop()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass

```

`tools/find_angles.py`:

```py
"""Find the view angles the game aims with (read-only).

    python tools/find_angles.py scan      # player still: list every float equal to the current yaw/pitch
    python tools/find_angles.py filter    # after turning the mouse: keep candidates that followed

Angles are derived from the camera matrix (forward = row 1) under several conventions, because
the engine's own convention is unknown: yaw = atan2 of the 8 sign/axis combos, pitch = +/-asin(z),
each in radians and degrees (and yaw also wrapped to [0, 2pi) / [0, 360)).
"""

import json
import math
import os
import subprocess
import sys

from mem import Game
from targets import camera

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
PROBE = os.path.join(ROOT, "build", "Release", "fc6probe.exe")
STATE = os.path.join(os.environ.get("TEMP", "."), "fc6_angles.json")
TOL = 2e-3


def conventions(fwd):
    fx, fy, fz = fwd
    out = {}
    combos = {"atan2(y,x)": (fy, fx), "atan2(x,y)": (fx, fy), "atan2(-x,y)": (-fx, fy), "atan2(x,-y)": (fx, -fy),
              "atan2(-y,x)": (-fy, fx), "atan2(y,-x)": (fy, -fx), "atan2(-x,-y)": (-fx, -fy), "atan2(-y,-x)": (-fy, -fx)}
    for name, (a, b) in combos.items():
        r = math.atan2(a, b)
        out["yaw " + name] = r
        out["yaw " + name + " wrapped"] = r % (2 * math.pi)
        out["yaw " + name + " deg"] = math.degrees(r)
        out["yaw " + name + " deg wrapped"] = math.degrees(r) % 360
    p = math.asin(max(-1.0, min(1.0, fz)))
    out["pitch asin(z)"] = p
    out["pitch -asin(z)"] = -p
    out["pitch asin(z) deg"] = math.degrees(p)
    out["pitch -asin(z) deg"] = -math.degrees(p)
    return out


def scan(g):
    cam = camera(g)
    conv = conventions(cam[1])
    names = list(conv)
    # Values near 0 match far too much memory: skip them, the user should not look straight ahead.
    vals = [conv[n] for n in names]
    out = os.path.join(os.environ.get("TEMP", "."), "fc6_angles.tsv")
    subprocess.run([PROBE, "values", out, *[f"{v:.6f}" for v in vals], "--tol", str(TOL)], check=True)
    cands = []
    with open(out) as f:
        for line in f:
            addr, k, _ = line.split("\t")
            if abs(vals[int(k)]) > 0.05:
                cands.append((int(addr, 16), names[int(k)]))
    json.dump(cands, open(STATE, "w"))
    print(f"{len(cands)} candidates; forward {tuple(round(v, 3) for v in cam[1])}")


def filter_(g):
    cam = camera(g)
```
