```py
    conv = conventions(cam[1])
    cands = json.load(open(STATE))
    kept = []
    for addr, name in cands:
        v = g.f32(addr)
        if v is not None and abs(v - conv[name]) <= TOL * 5:
            kept.append((addr, name))
    json.dump(kept, open(STATE, "w"))
    print(f"{len(kept)} candidates left (from {len(cands)}); forward {tuple(round(v, 3) for v in cam[1])}")
    for addr, name in kept[:40]:
        print(f"  {addr:x}  {name:28} = {g.f32(addr):.5f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    g = Game()
    {"scan": scan, "filter": filter_}[sys.argv[1]](g)

```

`tools/find_faction.py`:

```py
"""Looks for the live faction field: values equal within each faction and different between
enemies and allies (exploration helper, read-only).

    python tools/find_faction.py [--size 0x400]

Groups human NPCs by the archetype suffix (_Hostile / _Friendly / _Neutral), then compares the
entity and each component (matched by vtable) across groups, at 4-byte and 8-byte granularity.
Prints offsets where all enemies share one value, all allies share another, plus what the
civilians have there. An 8-byte pointer shared by a whole group (a faction descriptor) counts too.
"""

import argparse
import collections
import struct

from mem import Game
from targets import targets

GROUPS = (("_Hostile", "H"), ("_Friendly", "F"), ("_Neutral", "N"))


def group_of(name):
    return next((tag for key, tag in GROUPS if key in name), None)


def objects(g, entity):
    """{label: address}: the entity itself and its components keyed by vtable."""
    out = {"entity": entity}
    arr = g.u64(entity + 0xA8)
    raw = g.read(arr, 96 * 8) if arr else None
    for k in range(96 if raw else 0):
        c = struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, k * 8)[0]
        vt = g.vt(c) if c else None
        if vt is not None:
            out.setdefault(f"comp vt+{vt:x}", c)
    return out


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--size", type=lambda s: int(s, 0), default=0x400)
    ap.add_argument("--min", type=int, default=2, help="minimum enemies and allies")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    _, pts = targets(g)
    members = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for _i, _p, e, _pos, name in pts:
        tag = group_of(name) if "NPC" in name else None
        if tag:
            members[tag].append((e, name))
    print({k: len(v) for k, v in members.items()})
    if len(members["H"]) < args.min or len(members["F"]) < args.min:
        raise SystemExit(f"need at least {args.min} enemies and {args.min} allies in the aim-assist list")

    # label -> group -> [bytes]
    dumps = collections.defaultdict(lambda: collections.defaultdict(list))
    for tag, ms in members.items():
        for e, _name in ms:
            for label, addr in objects(g, e).items():
                raw = g.read(addr, args.size)
                if raw:
                    dumps[label][tag].append(raw)

    found = 0
    for label, by_group in sorted(dumps.items()):
        hs, fs, ns = by_group.get("H", []), by_group.get("F", []), by_group.get("N", [])
        if len(hs) < len(members["H"]) or len(fs) < len(members["F"]):
            continue  # component missing on some NPCs: not a faction carrier
        for width, fmt in ((4, "<I"), (8, "<Q")):
            for off in range(0, args.size - width + 1, width):
                hv = {struct.unpack_from(fmt, b, off)[0] for b in hs}
                fv = {struct.unpack_from(fmt, b, off)[0] for b in fs}
                if len(hv) != 1 or len(fv) != 1 or hv == fv:
                    continue
                h, f = next(iter(hv)), next(iter(fv))
                nv = sorted({struct.unpack_from(fmt, b, off)[0] for b in ns})
                kind = "engine ptr" if g.in_engine(h) else ("ptr" if h > 0x10000000000 else "value")
                print(f"{label:22} +{off:#05x} u{width * 8:<2} H={h:#x} F={f:#x} N={[hex(v) for v in nv][:4]} ({kind})")
                found += 1
    print(f"{found} candidates")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/find_skeleton.py`:

```py
"""Breadth-first walk from a character entity looking for bone arrays (exploration helper).

Looks, in every buffer reachable within a few pointer hops, for runs of either
  - unit quaternions followed by a small translation (QT bones, stride 0x20 or 0x30), or
  - 3x4 / 4x4 rotation matrices with small translations (model-space bone matrices).
"""

import math
import struct
import sys

from mem import Game
from targets import camera, targets


def is_unit(q):
    return abs(sum(x * x for x in q) - 1) < 2e-3


def runs_in(buf):
    n = len(buf) // 4
    f = struct.unpack(f"<{n}f", buf[: n * 4])
    found = []
    # QT: quat (4) + pos (3 or 4) [+ scale], stride 8 or 12 floats
    for stride in (8, 12):
        for start in range(0, 16, 4):
            k = 0
            while start + k * stride + 7 <= n:
                q = f[start + k * stride: start + k * stride + 4]
                t = f[start + k * stride + 4: start + k * stride + 7]
                if not (is_unit(q) and all(math.isfinite(x) and abs(x) < 2.5 for x in t)):
                    break
                k += 1
            if k >= 20:
                found.append(("QT", stride * 4, start * 4, k))
    # matrices with small translation
    for stride in (12, 16):
        for start in range(0, 16, 4):
            k = 0
            while start + k * stride + 12 <= n:
                o = start + k * stride
                r = (f[o:o + 3], f[o + 4:o + 7], f[o + 8:o + 11])
                t = (f[o + 3], f[o + 7], f[o + 11]) if stride == 12 else f[o + 12:o + 15]
                if not (all(is_unit(v) for v in r) and all(abs(x) < 2.5 for x in t)):
                    break
                k += 1
            if k >= 20:
                found.append(("MAT", stride * 4, start * 4, k))
    return found


def walk(g, root, depth=3, size=0x600):
    seen = {root}
    frontier = [(root, ())]
    for d in range(depth):
        nxt = []
        for addr, path in frontier:
            b = g.read(addr, size)
            if not b:
                continue
            for o in range(0, len(b) - 7, 8):
                v = struct.unpack_from("<Q", b, o)[0]
                if not (0x10000000000 < v < 0x7F0000000000) or g.in_engine(v) or v in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(v)
                p = path + (o,)
                big = g.read(v, 0x1000)
                if big:
                    for hit in runs_in(big):
                        print(f"  path +{' +'.join(hex(x) for x in p)}  -> {v:x}  {hit}")
                nxt.append((v, p))
        frontier = nxt[:4000]
        print(f"depth {d + 1}: {len(frontier)} buffers")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    g = Game()
    cam = camera(g)
    _, pts = targets(g)
    e = min((t for t in pts if "_Hostile" in t[4]), key=lambda t: math.dist(t[3], cam[3]))
    print("enemy", e[4], f"entity {e[2]:x}")
    walk(g, e[2], depth=int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 3)

```

`tools/fps_ab.py`:

```py
"""Game frame rate with the overlay's ESP on and off (presses F7, the DLL's ESP key).

    python tools/fps_ab.py [--cycles 3] [--seconds 6]

Reads the game's frame time (double at [+0x6a5eaa8] + 0x88, tools/view_lag.py) while switching
the ESP every few seconds, then prints the median and the 1 % low frame rate of each state. Best
where many NPCs are in view, standing still. The ESP ends as it started.
"""

import argparse
import configparser
import ctypes
import os
import statistics
import struct
import time

import mouse
from mem import Game

FRAME_GLOBAL = 0x6A5EAA8
VK_F7 = 0x76
INI = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "build", "Release", "fc6hack.ini")
user32 = ctypes.windll.user32


def esp_on():
    c = configparser.ConfigParser()
    c.read(INI)
    return c.getfloat("overlay", "esp", fallback=0) != 0


def press_f7():
    user32.keybd_event(VK_F7, 0, 0, 0)
    time.sleep(0.08)
    user32.keybd_event(VK_F7, 0, 2, 0)


def frames(g, frame, seconds):
    """Frame times (s) seen during this many seconds (one value per frame)."""
    out, last = [], None
    end = time.perf_counter() + seconds
    while time.perf_counter() < end:
        b = g.read(frame + 0x88, 8)
        v = struct.unpack("<d", b)[0] if b else None
        if v is not None and v != last and 0 < v < 1:
            out.append(v)
        last = v
        time.sleep(0.0005)
    return out


def summary(times):
    if not times:
        return "no frames (paused?)"
    fps = sorted(1 / t for t in times)
    low = fps[max(0, len(fps) // 100 - 1)] if len(fps) >= 100 else fps[0]
    return f"{statistics.median(fps):5.1f} fps median, 1% low {low:5.1f} ({len(times)} frames)"


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--cycles", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--seconds", type=float, default=6)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    frame = g.u64(g.base + FRAME_GLOBAL)
    print("switch to the game and stand still where NPCs are in view...", flush=True)
    while mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
        time.sleep(0.2)
    time.sleep(1.0)
    start = esp_on()
    print(f"ESP {'on' if start else 'off'} at start; switching it every {a.seconds:.0f} s", flush=True)
    res = {True: [], False: []}
    for _ in range(2 * a.cycles):
        state = esp_on()
        time.sleep(0.5)  # let the switch settle before counting
        seg = frames(g, frame, a.seconds)
        print(f"  ESP {'on ' if state else 'off'}: {summary(seg)}", flush=True)
        res[state] += seg
        if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
            print("the game lost focus: stopped")
            break
        press_f7()
        time.sleep(0.3)
    if esp_on() != start:
        press_f7()
    print(f"ESP on : {summary(res[True])}")
    print(f"ESP off: {summary(res[False])}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/hp_hunt.py`:

```py
"""Finds the health value by snapshotting one NPC between shots (exploration helper, read-only).

    python tools/hp_hunt.py

In game: aim at a healthy NPC and press F8 (the NPC nearest the crosshair becomes the target,
first snapshot, one beep). Wound it, F8; wound it again, F8; kill it, F8 (two beeps per
snapshot after the first). After 4 snapshots, or on F7, prints the fields of that NPC that went
down at every wound (health) or up at every wound (damage taken), skipping fields that moved the
same way on a control NPC nobody shot.

Snapshots cover the entity, every component (0x1000 bytes) and one level of heap pointers out of
the damage (+0x5a74d68), death (+0x59336e8) and +0x5951dd8 components.
"""

import ctypes
import math
import struct
import time
import winsound

import mouse
from mem import Game
from targets import camera, targets

user32 = ctypes.WinDLL("user32")
VK_F7, VK_F8 = 0x76, 0x77
SIZE = 0x1000
FOLLOW = (0x5A74D68, 0x59336E8, 0x5951DD8)


def objects(g, entity):
    """{label: (address, size)} for the entity, its components and selected pointees."""
    out = {"entity": (entity, SIZE)}
    arr = g.u64(entity + 0xA8)
    raw = g.read(arr, 96 * 8) if arr else None
    for k in range(96 if raw else 0):
        c = struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, k * 8)[0]
        vt = g.vt(c) if c else None
        if vt is None:
            continue
        label = f"comp+{vt:x}"
        out.setdefault(label, (c, SIZE))
        if vt in FOLLOW:
            body = g.read(c, 0x600) or b""
            for off in range(0, len(body), 8):
                p = struct.unpack_from("<Q", body, off)[0]
                if 0x10000000000 <= p < 0x7FF000000000 and not g.in_engine(p):
                    out.setdefault(f"{label}->[{off:#x}]", (p, 0x200))
    return out


def snapshot(g, objs):
    return {label: g.read(addr, size) for label, (addr, size) in objs.items()}


def pick(g):
    cam = camera(g)
    _, pts = targets(g)
    best, ctrl = None, None
    for _i, _p, e, pos, name in pts:
        if "NPC:" not in name:
            continue
        d = [pos[k] + 1.2 * cam[2][k] - cam[3][k] for k in range(3)]
        n = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in d)) or 1.0
        ang = math.acos(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(d[k] * cam[1][k] for k in range(3)) / n)))
        row = (ang, e, name, n)
        if best is None or ang < best[0]:
            best, ctrl = row, best or ctrl
        elif ctrl is None or ang < ctrl[0]:
            ctrl = row
    return best, ctrl


def key_pressed(vk, state):
    down = bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(vk) & 0x8000)
    hit = down and not state.get(vk)
    state[vk] = down
    return hit


def series(snaps, label, off, fmt):
    vals = []
    for s in snaps:
        b = s.get(label)
        if not b or off + 4 > len(b):
            return None
        vals.append(struct.unpack_from(fmt, b, off)[0])
    return vals


def analyse(target_snaps, control_snaps):
    found = 0
    for label in target_snaps[0]:
        size = len(target_snaps[0][label] or b"")
        for off in range(0, size - 3, 4):
            for fmt in ("<f", "<i"):
                v = series(target_snaps, label, off, fmt)
                if not v or not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in v):
                    continue
                # With 4 snapshots the last one is the kill: health may hit 0 or stay put there.
                core = v[:-1] if len(v) >= 4 else v
                down = all(b < a for a, b in zip(core, core[1:])) and v[-1] <= core[-1]
                up = all(b > a for a, b in zip(core, core[1:])) and v[-1] >= core[-1]
                if not ((down and 1 <= v[0] <= 1e5) or (up and 0 <= v[0] and v[-1] <= 1e5)):
                    continue
                c = series(control_snaps, label, off, fmt) if control_snaps else None
                if c and len(set(c)) > 1:
                    continue  # moved on the control NPC too: a timer, a position...
                kind = "HEALTH?" if down else "DAMAGE?"
                shown = [round(x, 2) if fmt == "<f" else x for x in v]
                print(f"  {label:34} +{off:#06x} {'f32' if fmt == '<f' else 'i32'} {shown}  {kind}")
                found += 1
    print(f"{found} candidates")


def main():
    g = Game()
    print("aim at a healthy NPC in game and press F8...", flush=True)
    state, snaps, ctrl_snaps = {}, [], []
    target = control = None
    while True:
        time.sleep(0.02)
        if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
            continue
        if key_pressed(VK_F7, state) and len(snaps) >= 2:
            break
        if not key_pressed(VK_F8, state):
            continue
        if target is None:
            best, ctrl = pick(g)
            if not best:
                print("no NPC found")
                continue
            target = objects(g, best[1])
            control = objects(g, ctrl[1]) if ctrl else None
            print(f"target {best[2].split('Base.NPC.')[-1]} at {best[3]:.1f} m"
                  + (f", control {ctrl[2].split('Base.NPC.')[-1]}" if ctrl else ""), flush=True)
        snaps.append(snapshot(g, target))
        if control:
            ctrl_snaps.append(snapshot(g, control))
        print(f"snapshot {len(snaps)}", flush=True)
        for _ in range(1 if len(snaps) == 1 else 2):
            winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
        if len(snaps) >= 4:
            break
    analyse(snaps, ctrl_snaps)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/inv_hunt.py`:

```py
"""Find where the player's item counts live: integer scan, then narrow down after a change in game
(offline only, read-only).

    python tools/inv_hunt.py scan metal=245 bois=60 cire=12    # counts read in the inventory screen
    python tools/inv_hunt.py next metal=250                     # after picking up / spending some
    python tools/inv_hunt.py show [name]                        # candidates left, with their neighbours

`scan` runs `fc6probe ints` over the heap (a minute or so) and keeps every address holding one of
the counts; `next` keeps the addresses of that item whose value is now the new count. Candidates
are marked "id" when a 64-bit item id (0x002007........, like the ammo ids) sits in the 32 bytes
before them: an inventory entry {id, count}. State in captures/inv_hunt.json.
"""

import json
import os
import struct
import subprocess
import sys

from mem import Game

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
STATE = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "captures", "inv_hunt.json")
PROBE = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "build", "Release", "fc6probe.exe")
SCAN_OUT = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "captures", "inv_hunt_scan.tsv")


def id_before(g, addr):
    """The item id in the 32 bytes before addr (8-byte aligned), or None."""
    base = (addr - 32) & ~7
    raw = g.read(base, addr - base)
    if not raw:
        return None
    for k in range(len(raw) // 8 - 1, -1, -1):
        v = struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, k * 8)[0]
        if (v >> 40) == 0x2007:  # 0x002007xx_xxxxxxxx
            return base + k * 8, v
    return None


def parse_pairs(args):
    out = {}
    for a in args:
        name, _, value = a.partition("=")
        out[name] = int(value)
    return out


def load():
    with open(STATE, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def save(state):
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(STATE), exist_ok=True)
    with open(STATE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(state, f)


def describe(g, name, cands, limit=20):
    print(f"{name}: {len(cands)} candidate(s)")
    shown = 0
    for a in cands:
        if shown >= limit:
            break
        found = id_before(g, a)
        tag = f"  id {found[1]:#018x} at -{a - found[0]:#x}" if found else ""
        raw = g.read(a - 16, 48)
        words = " ".join(f"{w:08x}" for w in struct.unpack_from("<12I", raw)) if raw else "?"
        print(f"  {a:#x} = {g.u32(a)}{tag}   [{words}]")
        shown += 1


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print(__doc__)
        return
    g = Game()
    cmd = sys.argv[1]
    if cmd == "scan":
        pairs = parse_pairs(sys.argv[2:])
        names = list(pairs)
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(SCAN_OUT), exist_ok=True)
        subprocess.run([PROBE, "ints", SCAN_OUT] + [str(pairs[n]) for n in names], check=True)
        state = {n: [] for n in names}
        with open(SCAN_OUT, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                addr, k, _v = line.split("\t")
                state[names[int(k)]].append(int(addr, 16))
        save({"values": pairs, "cands": state})
        for n in names:
            with_id = [a for a in state[n] if id_before(g, a)]
            print(f"{n}={pairs[n]}: {len(state[n])} addresses, {len(with_id)} after an item id")
            describe(g, n + " (after an item id)", with_id, 10)
    elif cmd == "next":
        state = load()
        for name, value in parse_pairs(sys.argv[2:]).items():
            before = state["cands"][name]
            state["cands"][name] = [a for a in before if g.u32(a) == value]
            state["values"][name] = value
            describe(g, f"{name}={value} ({len(before)} before)", state["cands"][name])
        save(state)
    elif cmd == "ids":
        # The entry may move when the count changes (a rebuilt list): follow the item id instead.
        # Each round keeps the (id, offset of the count after it) pairs seen with the new count.
        pairs = parse_pairs(sys.argv[2:])
        names = list(pairs)
        subprocess.run([PROBE, "ints", SCAN_OUT] + [str(pairs[n]) for n in names], check=True)
        found = {n: set() for n in names}
        with open(SCAN_OUT, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                addr, k, _v = line.split("\t")
                a = int(addr, 16)
                hit = id_before(g, a)
                if hit:
                    found[names[int(k)]].add(f"{hit[1]:x}:{a - hit[0]:x}")
        state = load() if os.path.exists(STATE) else {}
        rounds = state.setdefault("ids", {})
        for n in names:
            prev = rounds.get(n)
            keep = found[n] & set(prev) if prev is not None else found[n]
            rounds[n] = sorted(keep)
            print(f"{n}={pairs[n]}: {len(found[n])} (id, offset) pairs now, {len(keep)} kept"
                  + (f" of {len(prev)}" if prev is not None else ""))
            for p in rounds[n][:30]:
                iid, off = p.split(":")
                print(f"  id 0x{int(iid, 16):016x}, count at +{off} from it")
        save(state)
    elif cmd == "show":
        state = load()
        for name, cands in state["cands"].items():
            if len(sys.argv) < 3 or name == sys.argv[2]:
                describe(g, name, cands)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/inventory.py`:

```py
"""The player's stackable items through the game's own functions, run on the game's thread by
fc6internal.dll (version 15; offline only).

    python tools/inventory.py list                    # items (names, max stack); reads only
    python tools/inventory.py count Metal             # count, read by the game's getter
    python tools/inventory.py add Metal 10            # +10 through the game's add (pickups' path)
    python tools/inventory.py add Metal -10           # -10 through the game's remove
    ... [--hook 0x24d0580]                            # function the game's thread runs each frame

The item is matched by the end of its name ("Metal", "LookMaterial") or by its index in `list`.
The DLL arms a hardware execute breakpoint on --hook, runs the call there (gamethread.hpp) and
reports the thread and the time it took; the game must have the focus (it pauses without it).
"""

import argparse
import mmap
import os
import re
import struct
import time

import mouse
from mem import Game
from rays import MAGIC, NAME

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
LOG = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "build", "Release", "fc6hack.log")
HEAD = struct.Struct("<IIIiQQiIIIdII")  # invSeq .. invPad
ENTRY = struct.Struct("<QQII96s")
MAX = 64


def block_offset():
    """Offset of the inventory block, as the DLL logged it at load."""
    off = None
    with open(LOG, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
        for line in f:
            m = re.search(r"inventory block at \+0x([0-9a-f]+)", line)
            if m:
                off = int(m.group(1), 16)
    if off is None:
        raise SystemExit("fc6internal.dll v15 not loaded (no 'inventory block' line in fc6hack.log)")
    return off


class Inventory:
    def __init__(self, hook):
        self.off = block_offset()
        self.m = mmap.mmap(-1, self.off + HEAD.size + MAX * ENTRY.size, tagname=NAME)
        magic, version = struct.unpack_from("<II", self.m, 0)
        if magic != MAGIC or version < 15:
            raise SystemExit("fc6internal.dll version 15+ is not loaded")
        self.hook = hook

    def command(self, cmd, item=0, delta=0, timeout=5.0):
        seq, resp = struct.unpack_from("<II", self.m, self.off)
        struct.pack_into("<IiQQ", self.m, self.off + 8, cmd, delta, item, self.hook)
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, self.off, (seq + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
        end = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < end:
            if struct.unpack_from("<I", self.m, self.off + 4)[0] == (seq + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF:
                break
            time.sleep(0.01)
        else:
            raise SystemExit("no answer from the DLL")
        (_s, _r, _c, _d, _i, _h, result, value, ok, thread, ms, count, _p) = HEAD.unpack_from(self.m, self.off)
        return {"result": result, "value": value, "ok": ok, "thread": thread, "ms": ms, "count": count}

    def items(self):
        r = self.command(1)
        out = []
        for k in range(r["count"]):
            item, iid, mx, _pad, name = ENTRY.unpack_from(self.m, self.off + HEAD.size + k * ENTRY.size)
            out.append({"item": item, "id": iid, "max": mx, "name": name.split(b"\0")[0].decode(errors="replace")})
        return out


def find(items, key):
    if key.isdigit():
        return items[int(key)]
    hits = [it for it in items if it["name"].endswith(key)]
    if len(hits) != 1:
        raise SystemExit(f"{len(hits)} items match {key!r}")
    return hits[0]


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=("list", "count", "add"))
    ap.add_argument("item", nargs="?")
    ap.add_argument("n", nargs="?", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--hook", type=lambda s: int(s, 16), default=0x24D0580)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    inv = Inventory(args.hook)
    items = inv.items()
    if args.cmd == "list":
        for k, it in enumerate(items):
            print(f"{k:3d}  {it['name']:70s} max {it['max']:7d}  item {it['item']:#x}")
        return
    it = find(items, args.item)
    if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
        print("waiting for the game to have focus (it pauses without it)...", flush=True)
        while mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
            time.sleep(0.2)
        time.sleep(0.5)
    r = inv.command(2 if args.cmd == "count" else 3, it["item"], args.n)
    if not r["ok"] and r["thread"] == 0:
        print("not run: the hook function didn't run within 2 s (game paused? wrong --hook?)")
        return
    what = f"applied {r['result']:+d}, " if args.cmd == "add" else ""
    print(f"{it['name']}: {what}count {r['value']} ({'read' if r['ok'] else 'not read'}), "
          f"on thread {r['thread']} in {r['ms']:.3f} ms")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/life_log.py`:

```py
"""Logs, for every human NPC, each change of the fields that may tell wounded from dead
(exploration helper, read-only).

    python tools/life_log.py [--seconds 120] [--max-dist 80]

Wound a soldier (e.g. a foot), wait a few seconds, then kill him: the field that changes at the
kill and not at the wound is the death indicator; one that drops at the wound is health.
Only runs while the game has focus (the game pauses without it).
"""

import argparse
import math
import time

import mouse
import skeleton as sk
from mem import Game
from targets import PHYS_COMPONENT_VT, camera, component, faction, targets

FIELDS = (  # label, component vtable, offset, kind
    ("hp298", 0x5A74D68, 0x298, "f"),
    ("hpmax", 0x5A74D68, 0x29C, "f"),
    ("ratio", 0x5951DD8, 0x2D0, "f"),
    ("f50", 0x59336E8, 0x50, "u"),
    ("f54", 0x59336E8, 0x54, "u"),
    ("f98", 0x59336E8, 0x98, "u"),
    ("hp60", 0x59336E8, 0x60, "f"),
    ("rag9c", PHYS_COMPONENT_VT, 0x9C, "u"),
)


def sample(g, e, pos):
    out = {}
    for label, vt, off, kind in FIELDS:
        c = component(g, e, vt)
        v = (g.f32(c + off) if kind == "f" else g.u32(c + off)) if c else None
        out[label] = round(v, 2) if isinstance(v, float) else v
    out["att"] = faction(g, e)
    sel, _ = sk.select_pose(g, e)
    out["head"] = round(sel["points"]["head"][2] - pos[2], 1) if sel else None
    return out


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--seconds", type=float, default=120)
    ap.add_argument("--max-dist", type=float, default=80)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    last = {}
    t0 = None
    while True:
        if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
            time.sleep(0.2)
            continue
        t0 = t0 or time.perf_counter()
        t = time.perf_counter() - t0
        if t > args.seconds:
            break
        cam = camera(g)
        _, pts = targets(g)
        for _i, _p, e, pos, name in pts:
            if "NPC:" not in name or math.dist(cam[3], pos) > args.max_dist:
                continue
            s = sample(g, e, pos)
            prev = last.get(e)
            key = {k: v for k, v in s.items() if k != "head"}
            if prev is None or prev[0] != key:
                changed = {k: v for k, v in s.items() if prev is None or prev[1].get(k) != v}
                tag = name.split("Base.NPC.")[-1][:34]
                print(f"{t:6.1f}s {math.dist(cam[3], pos):5.1f}m {tag:34} {'NEW ' if prev is None else ''}{changed}", flush=True)
                last[e] = (key, s)
        time.sleep(0.1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/mem.py`:

```py
"""Access to the running Far Cry 6 process from Python (offline single-player only).

Reads by default; `write()` opens a separate write handle only when called.

    from mem import Game
    g = Game()
    g.u64(addr), g.f32(addr), g.mat(addr), g.vt(addr), g.read(addr, n)

Offsets printed by `vt()` are relative to FC_m64d3d12.dll, like everywhere else in the repo.
"""

import ctypes
import ctypes.wintypes as wt
import struct

ENGINE = "FC_m64d3d12.dll"
PROCESS_VM_READ = 0x0010
PROCESS_VM_WRITE = 0x0020
PROCESS_VM_OPERATION = 0x0008
PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION = 0x1000
TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS = 0x2
TH32CS_SNAPMODULE = 0x8 | 0x10

k32 = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)


class PROCESSENTRY32W(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("dwSize", wt.DWORD), ("cntUsage", wt.DWORD), ("th32ProcessID", wt.DWORD),
                ("th32DefaultHeapID", ctypes.c_void_p), ("th32ModuleID", wt.DWORD), ("cntThreads", wt.DWORD),
                ("th32ParentProcessID", wt.DWORD), ("pcPriClassBase", ctypes.c_long), ("dwFlags", wt.DWORD),
                ("szExeFile", ctypes.c_wchar * 260)]


class MODULEENTRY32W(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("dwSize", wt.DWORD), ("th32ModuleID", wt.DWORD), ("th32ProcessID", wt.DWORD),
                ("GlblcntUsage", wt.DWORD), ("ProccntUsage", wt.DWORD), ("modBaseAddr", ctypes.c_void_p),
                ("modBaseSize", wt.DWORD), ("hModule", wt.HMODULE), ("szModule", ctypes.c_wchar * 256),
                ("szExePath", ctypes.c_wchar * 260)]


k32.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot.restype = wt.HANDLE
k32.OpenProcess.restype = wt.HANDLE
k32.ReadProcessMemory.argtypes = [wt.HANDLE, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_size_t,
                                  ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_size_t)]
k32.WriteProcessMemory.argtypes = [wt.HANDLE, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_size_t,
                                   ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_size_t)]


def _module(pid, name):
    snap = k32.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPMODULE, pid)
    me = MODULEENTRY32W()
    me.dwSize = ctypes.sizeof(me)
    ok = k32.Module32FirstW(snap, ctypes.byref(me))
    found = None
    while ok:
        if me.szModule.lower() == name.lower():
            found = (me.modBaseAddr, me.modBaseSize)
            break
        ok = k32.Module32NextW(snap, ctypes.byref(me))
    k32.CloseHandle(snap)
    return found


class Game:
    def __init__(self, exe="FarCry6.exe"):
        snap = k32.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0)
        pe = PROCESSENTRY32W()
        pe.dwSize = ctypes.sizeof(pe)
        ok = k32.Process32FirstW(snap, ctypes.byref(pe))
        self.pid = None
        while ok:
            if pe.szExeFile.lower() == exe.lower() and _module(pe.th32ProcessID, ENGINE):
                self.pid = pe.th32ProcessID
                break
            ok = k32.Process32NextW(snap, ctypes.byref(pe))
        k32.CloseHandle(snap)
        if not self.pid:
            raise RuntimeError("Far Cry 6 is not running")
        self.h = k32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_VM_READ | PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, False, self.pid)
        self.base, self.size = _module(self.pid, ENGINE)

    def read(self, addr, n):
        buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(n)
        got = ctypes.c_size_t()
        if not k32.ReadProcessMemory(self.h, ctypes.c_void_p(addr), buf, n, ctypes.byref(got)) or got.value != n:
            return None
        return buf.raw

    def write(self, addr, data):
        """Writes raw bytes. Opens a write handle on first use: reading stays the default."""
        if not getattr(self, "hw", None):
            self.hw = k32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_VM_WRITE | PROCESS_VM_OPERATION, False, self.pid)
        done = ctypes.c_size_t()
        return bool(k32.WriteProcessMemory(self.hw, ctypes.c_void_p(addr), data, len(data), ctypes.byref(done)))

    def write_f32(self, addr, *values):
        return self.write(addr, struct.pack(f"<{len(values)}f", *values))

    def u64(self, addr):
        b = self.read(addr, 8)
        return struct.unpack("<Q", b)[0] if b else None

    def u32(self, addr):
        b = self.read(addr, 4)
        return struct.unpack("<I", b)[0] if b else None

    def f32(self, addr, n=1):
        b = self.read(addr, 4 * n)
        if not b:
            return None
        v = struct.unpack(f"<{n}f", b)
        return v[0] if n == 1 else v

    def mat(self, addr):
        """R44 transform -> (right, forward, up, pos)."""
        f = self.f32(addr, 16)
        return None if f is None else (f[0:3], f[4:7], f[8:11], f[12:15])

    def in_engine(self, v):
        return v is not None and self.base <= v < self.base + self.size

    def vt(self, addr):
        """Engine-relative vtable offset of the object at addr, or None."""
        v = self.u64(addr)
        return v - self.base if self.in_engine(v) else None

    def ptr(self, addr, *offsets):
        """CE-style chain: p = [addr]; p = [p + o] for all but the last; returns p + last."""
        p = self.u64(addr)
        for o in offsets[:-1]:
            if not p:
                return None
            p = self.u64(p + o)
        return p + offsets[-1] if p and offsets else p

```

`tools/mod_hunt.py`:

```py
"""Finds what an attachment or ammo change alters in the held weapon (exploration, read-only).

    python tools/mod_hunt.py [--weapon MS16_S]

In game, holding the weapon: F8 with configuration A (one beep), change one mod (ammo switches in
game, attachments at the workbench), F8 (two beeps), back to A and F8, B and F8. Prints the fields
that alternate A, B, A, B in the held weapon's object graph: the weapon object and the objects it
points to (2 levels), the weapon entity's components and their pointees, and the player's
components and their pointees (P.*). Objects are named by their pointer path, so it still works
if the game rebuilds them after a change. Round values come first; snapshots go to captures/.
"""

import argparse
import os
import pickle
import struct
import time
import winsound

import mouse
from mem import Game
from targets import archetype, held_weapon_object, player_entity

SIZE = 0x800
SNAPSHOTS = 4


def graph(g, w):
    """{path label: address} below the held weapon object w and its entity."""
    out = {"W": w}
    frontier = [("W", w)]
    for _depth in range(2):
        nxt = []
        for label, a in frontier:
            b = g.read(a, SIZE) or g.read(a, 0x200) or b""
            for o in range(0, len(b) - 7, 8):
                p = struct.unpack_from("<Q", b, o)[0]
                if 0x10000000000 <= p < 0x7FF000000000 and not g.in_engine(p) and p not in out.values():
                    child = f"{label}[{o:#x}]"
                    out[child] = p
                    nxt.append((child, p))
        frontier = nxt
        if len(out) > 6000:
            break
    h = g.u64(w + 0x8)
    e = g.u64(h + 0x8) if h else None
    if e:
        out["E"] = e
        arr = g.u64(e + 0xA8)
        raw = g.read(arr, 96 * 8) if arr else None
        for k in range(96 if raw else 0):
            c = struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, k * 8)[0]
            vt = g.vt(c) if c else None
            if vt:
                out[f"E.comp+{vt:x}"] = c
                body = g.read(c, SIZE) or b""
                for o in range(0, len(body) - 7, 8):
                    p = struct.unpack_from("<Q", body, o)[0]
                    if 0x10000000000 <= p < 0x7FF000000000 and not g.in_engine(p):
                        out.setdefault(f"E.comp+{vt:x}[{o:#x}]", p)
    return out


def entity_graph(g, e, prefix):
    """{label: address}: the entity, its components and the objects they point to."""
    out = {prefix: e}
    arr = g.u64(e + 0xA8)
    raw = g.read(arr, 96 * 8) if arr else None
    for k in range(96 if raw else 0):
        c = struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, k * 8)[0]
        vt = g.vt(c) if c else None
        if not vt:
            continue
        out[f"{prefix}.comp+{vt:x}"] = c
        body = g.read(c, SIZE) or b""
        for o in range(0, len(body) - 7, 8):
            p = struct.unpack_from("<Q", body, o)[0]
            if 0x10000000000 <= p < 0x7FF000000000 and not g.in_engine(p):
                out.setdefault(f"{prefix}.comp+{vt:x}[{o:#x}]", p)
    return out


def key_pressed(vk, state):
    down = mouse.user32.GetAsyncKeyState(vk) & 0x8000 != 0
    hit = down and not state.get(vk)
    state[vk] = down
    return hit


def nice(x):
    """A designer's number (0.9, 6.5, 3600...), not a pointer half or a coordinate."""
    return 0.001 <= abs(x) <= 20000 and abs(x * 100 - round(x * 100)) < 1e-3


def analyse(snaps):
    labels = set.intersection(*(set(s) for s in snaps))
    rows = []
    for label in sorted(labels):
        bufs = [s[label][1] for s in snaps]
        addrs = [s[label][0] for s in snaps]
        if addrs[0] == addrs[2] and addrs[1] == addrs[3] and addrs[0] != addrs[1]:
            rows.append(f"  {label:40} pointer A={addrs[0]:#x} B={addrs[1]:#x}")
        n = min(len(b) for b in bufs)
        for o in range(0, n - 3, 4):
            v = [b[o:o + 4] for b in bufs]
            if v[0] == v[2] and v[1] == v[3] and v[0] != v[1]:
                fa, fb = (struct.unpack("<f", x)[0] for x in (v[0], v[1]))
                ia, ib = (struct.unpack("<i", x)[0] for x in (v[0], v[1]))
                flag = ""
                if 1e-3 < abs(fa) < 1e5 and 1e-3 < abs(fb) < 1e5:
                    ratio = fa / fb if fb else 0
                    flag = f"  ratio A/B {ratio:.3f}" + ("  <== multiplier?" if 1.05 < abs(ratio) < 10 or 0.1 < abs(ratio) < 0.95 else "")
                rows.append(f"  {label:40} +{o:#05x} f32 {fa:.6g} / {fb:.6g}  i32 {ia} / {ib}{flag}")
    print(f"{len(rows)} alternating fields; round values on both sides first:")
    first = [r for r in rows if " f32 " in r and all(nice(float(v)) for v in r.split(" f32 ")[1].split()[0:3:2])]
    for r in first + [r for r in rows if r not in first][:200]:
        print(r)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--weapon", default="", help="part of the weapon's name to insist on (e.g. MS16_S)")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    print("hold the weapon, configuration A, and press F8 in game...", flush=True)
    state, snaps = {}, []
    while len(snaps) < SNAPSHOTS:
        time.sleep(0.02)
        if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid or not key_pressed(0x77, state):
            continue
        p = player_entity(g)
        w = held_weapon_object(g, p) if p else None
        e = g.u64(g.u64(w + 8) + 8) if w and g.u64(w + 8) else None
        name = archetype(g, e) if e else None
        if not w or not name or args.weapon not in name:
            print(f"not holding the weapon ({name}): F8 ignored", flush=True)
            winsound.Beep(400, 300)
            continue
        objs = {**graph(g, w), **entity_graph(g, p, "P")}
        snaps.append({label: (a, g.read(a, SIZE) or g.read(a, 0x200) or b"") for label, a in objs.items()})
        config = "A" if len(snaps) % 2 else "B"
        print(f"snapshot {len(snaps)} ({config}): {name}, weapon object {w:#x}, {len(objs)} objects", flush=True)
        for _ in range(1 if config == "A" else 2):
            winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "captures",
                        time.strftime("mod_hunt_%Y%m%d_%H%M%S.pickle"))
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "wb") as f:
        pickle.dump(snaps, f)
    print(f"snapshots saved to {os.path.normpath(path)}")
    analyse(snaps)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/mouse.py`:

```py
"""Relative mouse input for the aim prototype (Windows SendInput).

Input only reaches the foreground window, so nothing happens unless Far Cry 6 has focus.
"""

import ctypes
import ctypes.wintypes as wt

user32 = ctypes.WinDLL("user32", use_last_error=True)

INPUT_MOUSE = 0
MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE = 0x0001


class MOUSEINPUT(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("dx", wt.LONG), ("dy", wt.LONG), ("mouseData", wt.DWORD), ("dwFlags", wt.DWORD),
                ("time", wt.DWORD), ("dwExtraInfo", ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_ulong))]


class INPUT(ctypes.Structure):
    class _U(ctypes.Union):
        _fields_ = [("mi", MOUSEINPUT), ("pad", ctypes.c_byte * 32)]

    _anonymous_ = ("u",)
    _fields_ = [("type", wt.DWORD), ("u", _U)]


def move(dx, dy):
    """Relative move in mouse counts (same as moving the physical mouse)."""
    inp = INPUT(type=INPUT_MOUSE)
    inp.mi = MOUSEINPUT(int(dx), int(dy), 0, MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE, 0, None)
    return user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT)) == 1


def foreground_pid():
    hwnd = user32.GetForegroundWindow()
    pid = wt.DWORD()
    user32.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, ctypes.byref(pid))
    return pid.value


VK = {"lalt": 0xA4, "ralt": 0xA5, "x1": 0x05, "x2": 0x06, "lbutton": 0x01, "rbutton": 0x02, "capslock": 0x14,
      "f6": 0x75, "f8": 0x77}


def key_down(name):
    return bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(VK[name]) & 0x8000)

```

`tools/npc_targets.py`:

```py
"""Who is each NPC after? Reads the AI's aim target of every living NPC (offline only, read-only).

    python tools/npc_targets.py [--s 120]

The NPC fire direction (+0x28485f0, the non-player path of +0x2848c40) asks the shooter's pawn for
its AI aim data: +0x23d1dd0 returns A = [pawn + 0x2ae8] + 0xd40, then
    A + 0xe8   entity handle of the target ([handle + 8] = entity)
    A + 0xf0   bone index aimed at on it (-1: none)
    A + 0xf4   float time, compared with the game time ([[+0x6a5eaa8] + 0x60], double)
    A + 0x34   aim point (vec3)
The NPCs' pawn component has vtable +0x5fcd820 (the player's is +0x5d53d88).

Prints every change: "<NPC> -> PLAYER" when an NPC takes the player as its target.
"""

import argparse
import math
import struct
import time

from mem import Game
from targets import archetype, component, faction, is_dead, player_entity, targets

NPC_PAWN_VT = 0x5FCD820
AIM_DATA = 0xD40


def aim_target(g, entity):
    """(target entity, bone, until) of the NPC's AI aim, or None."""
    c = component(g, entity, NPC_PAWN_VT)
    data = g.u64(c + 0x2AE8) if c else None
    if not data:
        return None
    a = data + AIM_DATA
    h = g.u64(a + 0xE8)
    target = g.u64(h + 8) if h and h > 0x10000 else None
    raw = g.read(a + 0xF0, 8)
    if not target or not raw:
        return None
    bone, until = struct.unpack("<if", raw)
    return target, bone, until


def short(name):
    return name.split("Base.NPC.")[-1][:40]


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--s", type=float, default=120.0)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    prev = {}
    end = time.time() + args.s
    counts = {}
    while time.time() < end:
        player = player_entity(g)
        if not player:
            time.sleep(0.5)
            continue
        pm = g.mat(player + 0x30)
        clock = g.u64(g.base + 0x6A5EAA8)
        now = struct.unpack("<d", g.read(clock + 0x60, 8))[0] if clock else 0.0
        _, pts = targets(g)
        stamp = time.strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        on_player = 0
        for _i, _p, e, pos, name in pts:
            if not e or "NPC" not in name or is_dead(g, e):
                continue
            t = aim_target(g, e)
            if t and t[0] == player:
                label = "PLAYER"
                on_player += 1
            elif t:
                label = short(archetype(g, t[0]))
            else:
                label = None
            if prev.get(e, "?") != label:
                dist = math.dist(pos[:3], pm[3][:3]) if pm else 0.0
                extra = f" (bone {t[1]}, until {t[2] - now:+.1f} s)" if t else ""
                print(f"{stamp} {short(name)} {dist:.0f} m, attitude {faction(g, e)}: -> {label}{extra}", flush=True)
                prev[e] = label
        counts[on_player] = counts.get(on_player, 0) + 1
        time.sleep(0.25)
    print("NPCs targeting the player per sample:", dict(sorted(counts.items())))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/port_check.py`:

```py
"""Checks the DLL's C++ game reads (src/internal/game.cpp) against the Python tools (read-only).

    python tools/port_check.py [--rounds 20]

fc6internal.dll v12 publishes what it reads (camera, held weapon, every character of the
aim-assist list with its aim point, attitude, death and health, hostile / dead verdicts) while
viewOn is set. Each round reads the same things with targets.py / skeleton.py right after the
DLL's update and prints the differences: verdicts must match exactly, positions within a few cm
(the two reads are a few ms apart and characters move).
"""

import argparse
import math
import mmap
import struct
import time

from ballistics import MONITOR, MONITOR_OFF
from mem import Game
from skeleton import select_pose
from rays import MAGIC, NAME
from targets import aim_points, camera_with_projection, faction, health, held_weapon, is_dead, is_hostile, \
    player_entity, targets, weapon_ballistics

VIEW_OFF = MONITOR_OFF + MONITOR.size
VIEW_HDR = struct.Struct("<IIII3f3fff128s4f")  # on, seq, count, camera, eye, fwd, sx, sy, weapon, stats
VIEW_CHAR = struct.Struct("<Q128s3f3fiiffIII3f20sI")
VIEW_CHARS_OFF = VIEW_OFF + VIEW_HDR.size
VIEW_MAX = 64
SOURCES = {0: "skeleton", 1: "ragdoll", 2: "estimate"}


def text(b):
    return b.split(b"\0")[0].decode(errors="replace")


def dll_view(m):
    on, seq, count, has_cam, *rest = VIEW_HDR.unpack_from(m, VIEW_OFF)
    eye, fwd, sx, sy, weapon, stats = rest[0:3], rest[3:6], rest[6], rest[7], text(rest[8]), rest[9:13]
    chars = {}
    for i in range(min(count, VIEW_MAX)):
        e, name, *v = VIEW_CHAR.unpack_from(m, VIEW_CHARS_OFF + i * VIEW_CHAR.size)
        chars[e] = {"name": text(name), "head": tuple(v[0:3]), "chest": tuple(v[3:6]), "attitude": v[6],
                    "death": v[7], "hp": (v[8], v[9]), "hostile": bool(v[10]), "dead": bool(v[11]),
                    "source": SOURCES.get(v[12], v[12]), "origin": tuple(v[13:16]), "reason": text(v[16])}
    return seq, (eye, fwd, sx, sy) if has_cam else None, weapon, stats, chars


def python_view(g):
    chars = {}
    _, pts = targets(g)
    for _i, _p, entity, pos, name in pts:
        points = aim_points(g, entity, pos)
        label = points[0][0]
        chest = next((p for lab, p in points if lab.startswith(("chest", "spine1"))), None)
        hp = health(g, entity)
        a = faction(g, entity)
        m = g.mat(entity + 0x30)
        _, reason = select_pose(g, entity)
        chars[entity] = {"name": name, "head": points[0][1], "chest": chest, "origin": m[3][:3] if m else None,
                         "reason": reason.split(" ")[0],
                         "source": label.split("~")[1] if "~" in label else "skeleton",
                         "attitude": -1 if a is None else a, "hp": hp or (-1.0, -1.0),
                         "hostile": is_hostile(g, entity, name), "dead": is_dead(g, entity)}
    return chars


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--rounds", type=int, default=20)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    m = mmap.mmap(-1, VIEW_CHARS_OFF + VIEW_MAX * VIEW_CHAR.size, tagname=NAME)
    magic, version = struct.unpack_from("<II", m, 0)
    if magic != MAGIC or version < 12:
        raise SystemExit(f"fc6internal.dll v12 is not loaded (version {version})")
    struct.pack_into("<I", m, VIEW_OFF, 1)
    problems, compared, worst = {}, 0, {}
    try:
        seq = dll_view(m)[0]
        for r in range(args.rounds):
            deadline = time.perf_counter() + 2.0
            while dll_view(m)[0] == seq and time.perf_counter() < deadline:
                time.sleep(0.002)
            seq, cam, weapon, stats, dll = dll_view(m)
            py = python_view(g)
            cp = camera_with_projection(g)
            if r == 0:
                p = player_entity(g)
                base = weapon_ballistics(g, p) if p else None
                print(f"weapon: DLL {weapon or '-'} {stats[0]:.0f} m/s g {stats[1]:.2f} drop {stats[2]:.0f}"
                      f" rpm {stats[3]:.0f} | Python {held_weapon(g, p) if p else '-'} {base}")
                if cam and cp:
                    print(f"camera: eye moved {math.dist(cam[0], cp[0][3][:3]) * 100:.1f} cm between reads,"
                          f" sx {cam[2]:.4f} / {cp[1]:.4f}, sy {cam[3]:.4f} / {cp[2]:.4f}")
                print(f"{len(dll)} characters in the DLL view, {len(py)} in Python")
            for e in set(dll) | set(py):
                a, b = dll.get(e), py.get(e)
                if not a or not b:
                    k = f"only in {'DLL' if a else 'Python'} (left or joined meanwhile): {(a or b)['name']}"
                    problems[k] = problems.get(k, 0) + 1
                    continue
                compared += 1
                short = b["name"].split(".")[-1]
                for key in ("name", "hostile", "dead", "attitude", "source"):
                    if a[key] != b[key]:
                        extra = f" ({a['reason']} / {b['reason']})" if key == "source" else ""
                        k = f"{key} differs for {short}: DLL {a[key]} / Python {b[key]}{extra}"
                        problems[k] = problems.get(k, 0) + 1
                if a["source"] != b["source"] or not b["origin"]:
                    continue
                # Relative to each read's own entity position: the character may have moved between.
                rel = lambda v, o: [v[k] - o[k] for k in range(3)]
                worst.setdefault(short, []).append(math.dist(rel(a["head"], a["origin"]), rel(b["head"], b["origin"])))
                if abs(a["hp"][0] - b["hp"][0]) > 0.01:
                    k = f"health differs for {short}: DLL {a['hp']} / Python {b['hp']}"
                    problems[k] = problems.get(k, 0) + 1
    finally:
        struct.pack_into("<I", m, VIEW_OFF, 0)
    print(f"{compared} character reads compared over {args.rounds} rounds")
    for k, n in sorted(problems.items()):
        print(f"  {n:3}x {k}")
    # The Python read of a round ends tens of ms after the DLL's: animation moves the head
    # meanwhile, so the median says whether the reads agree, the maximum is motion.
    print("head gaps relative to the entity, same source on both sides (cm): median / max, reads")
    for k, v in sorted(worst.items(), key=lambda kv: -sorted(kv[1])[len(kv[1]) // 2]):
        v = sorted(v)
        print(f"  {k:28} {v[len(v) // 2] * 100:6.1f} / {v[-1] * 100:6.1f}  {len(v):4}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/ray_probe.py`:

```py
"""What the game's bullet ray query returns on different surfaces (exploration, offline only).

    python tools/ray_probe.py [--seconds 120] [--out file]

Casts a bullet-filter ray from the camera along the crosshair. Each time a numpad key is pressed
with the game focused, records the hit under that key's label:
    1 vitre (car or building window)   2 carrosserie (car body)   3 mur      4 feuillage / buisson
    5 tronc / arbre                    6 sol                      7 grillage / barriere
    8 ennemi (crosshair on an NPC)     9 autre
Prints per sample the distance, the result record's small fields (+0x18, +0x28...) and the object
pointed to by +0x50 (vtable, first fields), then a table of which fields differ by label. The
record layout (DLL castOne): +0x00 fraction (x4), +0x10 fraction; the rest is unknown.
"""

import argparse
import ctypes
import struct
import time

import mouse
from mem import Game
from rays import BULLET_FILTER, Rays
from targets import camera

LABELS = {1: "vitre", 2: "carrosserie", 3: "mur", 4: "feuillage", 5: "tronc", 6: "sol", 7: "grillage",
          8: "ennemi", 9: "autre"}
VK_NUMPAD0 = 0x60
user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
LENGTH = 300.0


# The same numpad keys with NumLock off: End, Down, PgDn, Left, Clear, Right, Home, Up, PgUp.
NUMLOCK_OFF = {1: 0x23, 2: 0x28, 3: 0x22, 4: 0x25, 5: 0x0C, 6: 0x27, 7: 0x24, 8: 0x26, 9: 0x21}


def pressed():
    """Numpad digit going down now (NumLock on or off), or None."""
    for k in LABELS:
        if user32.GetAsyncKeyState(VK_NUMPAD0 + k) & 1 or user32.GetAsyncKeyState(NUMLOCK_OFF[k]) & 1:
            return k
    return None


def describe(g, raw):
    w = struct.unpack("<24I", raw)
    q = struct.unpack("<12Q", raw)
    obj = q[0x50 // 8]
    info = {"w18": w[0x18 // 4], "w1c": w[0x1C // 4], "w14": w[0x14 // 4], "w20": w[0x20 // 4],
            "w28": w[0x28 // 4], "w2c": w[0x2C // 4], "obj": obj}
    vt = g.vt(obj) if obj else None
    info["obj_vt"] = vt
    fields = []
    if obj:
        for off in range(0, 0x40, 8):
            v = g.u64(obj + off)
            fields.append(f"+{off:02x}={v:#x}" + (f"(vt+{g.vt(v):#x})" if v and g.vt(v) else ""))
    info["obj_fields"] = " ".join(fields)
    return info


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--seconds", type=float, default=120)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    r = Rays()
    r.set_filter(BULLET_FILTER)
    for k in LABELS:  # clear the "pressed since last call" bits
        user32.GetAsyncKeyState(VK_NUMPAD0 + k)
        user32.GetAsyncKeyState(NUMLOCK_OFF[k])
    print("aim the crosshair at a surface and press its numpad key:", flush=True)
    print("  " + "  ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in LABELS.items()), flush=True)
    samples = []
    end = time.time() + a.seconds
    while time.time() < end:
        time.sleep(0.02)
        if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
            continue
        k = pressed()
        if k is None:
            continue
        m = camera(g)
        if not m:
            continue
        eye, fwd = m[3], m[1]
        res = r.cast([(eye, tuple(eye[i] + fwd[i] * LENGTH for i in range(3)))])
        if not res or not res[0]["hit"]:
            print(f"{LABELS[k]}: nothing hit within {LENGTH:.0f} m", flush=True)
            continue
        info = describe(g, res[0]["raw"])
        dist = res[0]["fraction"] * LENGTH
        samples.append((LABELS[k], dist, info))
        print(f"{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} {LABELS[k]:11s} {dist:6.1f} m  +14 {info['w14']:08x} +18 {info['w18']:08x}"
              f" +1c {info['w1c']:08x} +20 {info['w20']:08x} +28 {info['w28']:08x} +2c {info['w2c']:08x}"
              f"  obj {info['obj']:#x} vt {info['obj_vt'] and hex(info['obj_vt'])}", flush=True)
        print(f"      {info['obj_fields']}", flush=True)
    print(f"{len(samples)} samples")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/rays.py`:

```py
"""Client for fc6internal.dll: Havok ray casts inside the game (offline only).

    build/Release/fc6inject.exe          # load the DLL into the running game first
    python tools/rays.py --status        # DLL state and captured hknpWorld
    python tools/rays.py --test          # cast from the camera 50 m forward and print the hit
    python tools/rays.py --unload        # ask the DLL to unload itself

Layout must match src/internal/shared.hpp.
"""

import ctypes
import mmap
import struct
import sys
import time
from ctypes import wintypes

NAME = "Local\\fc6hack"
REQUEST_EVENT, RESPONSE_EVENT = "Local\\fc6hack_req", "Local\\fc6hack_resp"
MAGIC = 0x36434646
MAX_RAYS = 64
# Collision filter of the game's bullet traces, sampled while the player fired (docs/reversing.md).
BULLET_FILTER = 0x11FBB
# magic, version, queryObject, status, quit, reqSeq, respSeq, count, served, queryVtable, filterInfo,
# entryFlags, filterOverride, sampleMs, sampleDistinct, sampleTotal
HEADER = struct.Struct("<IIQIIIIIIQIIIIII")
SAMPLE = struct.Struct("<IIIf")
MAX_SAMPLES = 64
RAY = struct.Struct("<8f")
HIT = struct.Struct("<IIfI4f4fQ96s")
SAMPLES_OFF = HEADER.size
RAYS_OFF = SAMPLES_OFF + MAX_SAMPLES * SAMPLE.size
HITS_OFF = RAYS_OFF + MAX_RAYS * RAY.size
MSG_OFF = HITS_OFF + MAX_RAYS * HIT.size
SIZE = MSG_OFF + 128
STATUS = {0: "starting", 1: "waiting for a game ray query", 2: "ready", 3: "no query captured", 4: "stopped"}

kernel32 = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
kernel32.OpenEventW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
kernel32.OpenEventW.argtypes = (wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.BOOL, wintypes.LPCWSTR)
kernel32.SetEvent.argtypes = (wintypes.HANDLE,)
kernel32.WaitForSingleObject.restype = wintypes.DWORD
kernel32.WaitForSingleObject.argtypes = (wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.DWORD)
SYNCHRONIZE, EVENT_MODIFY_STATE = 0x00100000, 0x0002


class Rays:
    def __init__(self):
        self.m = mmap.mmap(-1, SIZE, tagname=NAME)
        magic = struct.unpack_from("<I", self.m, 0)[0]
        if magic != MAGIC:
            raise RuntimeError("fc6internal.dll is not loaded (run build/Release/fc6inject.exe)")
        # None with a DLL older than the events: cast() then polls.
        self.request = kernel32.OpenEventW(EVENT_MODIFY_STATE, False, REQUEST_EVENT)
        self.response = kernel32.OpenEventW(SYNCHRONIZE, False, RESPONSE_EVENT)

    def header(self):
        return HEADER.unpack_from(self.m, 0)

    def status(self):
        h = self.header()
        msg = self.m[MSG_OFF:MSG_OFF + 128].split(b"\0")[0].decode(errors="replace")
        return {"status": STATUS.get(h[3], h[3]), "queryObject": hex(h[2]), "queryVtable": hex(h[9]),
                "filterInfo": hex(h[10]), "entryFlags": h[11], "served": h[8], "message": msg}

    def ready(self):
        return self.header()[3] == 2

    def cast(self, rays, timeout=0.2):
        """rays: list of (from_xyz, to_xyz). Returns a list of dicts (or None on timeout)."""
        rays = rays[:MAX_RAYS]
        for i, (a, b) in enumerate(rays):
            RAY.pack_into(self.m, RAYS_OFF + i * RAY.size, *a, 0.0, *b, 0.0)
        h = self.header()
        seq = (h[5] + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, 32, len(rays))   # count
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, 24, seq)         # reqSeq last: the DLL starts on this
        if self.request:
            kernel32.SetEvent(self.request)
        deadline = time.perf_counter() + timeout
        while struct.unpack_from("<I", self.m, 28)[0] != seq:
            left = deadline - time.perf_counter()
            if left <= 0:
                return None
            if self.response:
                # Auto-reset: a signal left over from an abandoned request just loops once more.
                kernel32.WaitForSingleObject(self.response, max(1, min(50, int(left * 1000))))
            else:
                time.sleep(0.0005)
        out = []
        for i in range(len(rays)):
            hit, fault, frac, _pad, *rest = HIT.unpack_from(self.m, HITS_OFF + i * HIT.size)
            out.append({"hit": bool(hit), "fault": bool(fault), "fraction": frac,
                        "position": tuple(rest[0:3]), "normal": tuple(rest[4:7]), "object": rest[8],
                        "raw": rest[9]})
        return out

    def set_filter(self, value):
        """Force the collision filter info of our rays (0 = the captured game query's)."""
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, 56, value)

    def sample_filters(self, ms=3000):
        """Counts the collision filters of the game's own ray queries for `ms` milliseconds."""
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, 60, ms)
        while struct.unpack_from("<I", self.m, 60)[0]:
            time.sleep(0.05)
        distinct, total = struct.unpack_from("<II", self.m, 64)
        out = [SAMPLE.unpack_from(self.m, SAMPLES_OFF + i * SAMPLE.size) for i in range(distinct)]
        return total, sorted(out, key=lambda s: -s[2])

    def unload(self):
        struct.pack_into("<I", self.m, 20, 1)
        if self.request:
            kernel32.SetEvent(self.request)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    r = Rays()
    if "--sample" in sys.argv:
        i = sys.argv.index("--sample")
        ms = int(sys.argv[i + 1]) if len(sys.argv) > i + 1 else 3000
        total, samples = r.sample_filters(ms)
        print(f"{total} game ray queries in {ms} ms")
        for f, flags, n, length in samples:
            print(f"  filter {f:#010x} flags {flags}  x{n:6}  mean length {length:7.2f} m")
    elif "--unload" in sys.argv:
        r.unload()
        print("unload requested")
    elif "--test" in sys.argv:
        from mem import Game
        from targets import camera
        g = Game()
        cam = camera(g)
        eye, fwd = cam[3], cam[1]
        start = tuple(eye[i] + 0.6 * fwd[i] for i in range(3))
        end = tuple(eye[i] + 50.0 * fwd[i] for i in range(3))
        print(r.status())
        res = r.cast([(start, end)])
        print(res)
        if res and res[0]["hit"]:
            p = res[0]["position"]
            print(f"hit at {res[0]['fraction'] * 49.4 + 0.6:.2f} m: ({p[0]:.2f}, {p[1]:.2f}, {p[2]:.2f})")
        elif res:
            print("nothing hit within 50 m")
    else:
        print(r.status())

```

`tools/skeleton.py`:

```py
"""Animated skeleton of a character, in world space (read-only).

Layout (docs/reversing.md "Skeleton ownership", build 11359732):
    S = [[entity + 0x28] + 0xe0]       the character's own skeleton instance (vtable +0x5951dc8);
                                       the same pointer sits at [body + 0xa0] and [comp9 + 0x40]
    S + 0x68 / 0x78 / 0x88 : pose outputs published by the animation job (+0xb22120) every frame:
                             local pose, **model-space pose** (stride 0x20: quat +0x00,
                             position +0x10) and per-bone mask
    S + 0xe0 : bone name table {u32 crc32(name), u32 index}, count u32 at S + 0xec; the count is
               the model's bone count (428, 431, 438, 446, 553...) and matches the size of the
               pose buffers ([S + 0x78] - [S + 0x68] = count * 0x20)
    Human models: Root 1, Hips 4, Spine 5, Spine1 6, Spine2 7, Neck 8, Head 9 (base of the skull),
    HeadEnd 10 (top of the head); other models look bones up by name.

Not the character's own data (all read by earlier versions, which put skeletons on the wrong NPCs):
    [body + 0x1c0 .. 0x1e8] : a link to *another* character's skeleton and its pose buffers
    [S + 0x4e8 / 0x4f8], count S + 0x4b8 : a second, partial output (162 bones near, fewer with
                             LOD) whose bone order does not follow the name table

select_pose() keeps the pose only while it is anatomically plausible and still moving (a pose that
stopped changing is a dead body or an NPC whose animation is no longer updated).
"""

import math
import struct
import time
import zlib

SKELETON_VT = 0x5951DC8
BODY_COMPONENT_VT = 0x595F6E8
AIM_BONES = ("Head", "HeadEnd", "Neck", "Spine2", "Hips")
REQUIRED_BONES = ("Hips", "Neck", "Head")


def skeleton_instance(g, entity):
    """The character's own skeleton instance, or None. [[entity + 0x28] + 0xe0] is sometimes
    null while [body + 0xa0] holds the same pointer, so the body component is the fallback."""
    c = g.u64(entity + 0x28) if entity else None
    s = g.u64(c + 0xE0) if c else None
    if not s:
        b = body_component(g, entity)
        s = g.u64(b + 0xA0) if b else None
    return s if s and g.vt(s) == SKELETON_VT else None


def body_component(g, entity):
    arr = g.u64(entity + 0xA8) if entity else None
    for k in range(96):
        c = g.u64(arr + 8 * k) if arr else None
        if c and g.vt(c) == BODY_COMPONENT_VT:
            return c
    return None


def bone_indices(g, s, names):
    """{name: bone index} for the names found in skeleton instance s's crc32 name table."""
    count = g.u32(s + 0xEC) if s else 0
    table = g.read(g.u64(s + 0xE0), count * 8) if s and 0 < count < 4096 else None
    if not table:
        return {}
    wanted = {zlib.crc32(n.encode()) & 0xFFFFFFFF: n for n in names}
    out = {}
    for k in range(count):
        h, idx = struct.unpack_from("<II", table, k * 8)
        if h in wanted:
            out[wanted[h]] = idx
    return out


_tables = {}  # (table address, count, names) -> {name: index}


def table_indices(g, s, names):
    """bone_indices(), cached by table (tables belong to the model and don't change)."""
    n = g.u32(s + 0xEC) if s else 0
    t = g.u64(s + 0xE0) if s else None
    if not t or not 0 < n < 4096:
        return {}
    key = (t, n, names)
    if key not in _tables:
        if len(_tables) > 2048:
            _tables.clear()
        _tables[key] = bone_indices(g, s, names)
    return _tables[key]


def own_pose(g, entity):
    """(pose address, bone count, skeleton instance) of the character's model-space pose, or None."""
    s = skeleton_instance(g, entity)
    if not s:
        return None
    local, model, count = g.u64(s + 0x68), g.u64(s + 0x78), g.u32(s + 0xEC) or 0
    # The buffers are allocated back to back: their spacing confirms the bone count.
    if not (model and local and 0 < count <= 1024 and model - local == count * 0x20):
        return None
    return model, count, s


def to_world(g, entity, pts):
    m = g.mat(entity + 0x30)
    if not m:
        return None
    r, f, u, t = m
    return [tuple(t[k] + p[0] * r[k] + p[1] * f[k] + p[2] * u[k] for k in range(3)) for p in pts]


def check_bones(world, idx, origin=None):
    """(aim points, reason): points from world bone positions with anatomical checks, or ({}, why
    they were rejected). The checks use bone lengths only, so standing, crouching, kneeling and
    lying bodies all pass. origin: the entity position, to reject poses away from their owner."""
    w = {n: world[i] for n, i in idx.items() if i < len(world)}
    missing = [n for n in REQUIRED_BONES if n not in w]
    if missing:
        return {}, "missing " + ",".join(missing)
    hips, neck, head = w["Hips"], w["Neck"], w["Head"]
    if "HeadEnd" in w:
        centre = tuple((head[k] + w["HeadEnd"][k]) / 2 for k in range(3))
    else:  # extend the neck -> head direction to the middle of the skull
        centre = tuple(head[k] + (head[k] - neck[k]) * 0.7 for k in range(3))
    chest = w.get("Spine2") or tuple((hips[k] + neck[k]) / 2 for k in range(3))
    spine, skull = math.dist(hips, neck), math.dist(neck, head)
    top = math.dist(head, w["HeadEnd"]) if "HeadEnd" in w else 0.1
    gaps = f"hips-neck {spine:.2f} neck-head {skull:.2f} head-top {top:.2f}"
    if not (0.3 < spine < 0.8 and skull < 0.3 and top < 0.3):
        return {}, "anatomy " + gaps
    if origin and math.dist(hips, origin) > 1.5:
        return {}, f"away {math.dist(hips, origin):.1f} m"
    return {"head": centre, "neck": neck, "chest": chest, "pelvis": hips}, "ok " + gaps


LIVE_WINDOW = 0.3  # seconds without change before a pose counts as frozen
_seen = {}  # pose address -> [digest, last change, first seen]


def liveness(addr, digest, now):
    """'live' if the pose changed within LIVE_WINDOW, 'frozen' if not, 'new' while unknown."""
    st = _seen.get(addr)
    if st is None:
        if len(_seen) > 4096:
            _seen.clear()
        _seen[addr] = [digest, now, now]
        return "new"
    if digest != st[0]:
        st[0], st[1] = digest, now
        return "live"
    if now - st[1] < LIVE_WINDOW:
        return "live"
    return "new" if now - st[2] < LIVE_WINDOW else "frozen"


def select_pose(g, entity, names=AIM_BONES, now=None):
    """(pose, reason): pose = {'state', 'world', 'idx', 'points', 'check'} for the character's own
    animated skeleton, or None with the reason it was not used."""
    now = time.perf_counter() if now is None else now
    found = own_pose(g, entity)
    m = g.mat(entity + 0x30)
    if not found or not m:
        return None, "no skeleton"
    addr, count, s = found
    buf = g.read(addr, count * 0x20)
    if not buf:
        return None, "unreadable"
    pts = [struct.unpack_from("<3f", buf, i * 0x20 + 0x10) for i in range(count)]
    if not all(math.isfinite(c) and abs(c) < 10 for p in pts for c in p):
        return None, "garbage"
    idx = {k: v for k, v in table_indices(g, s, tuple(names)).items() if v < count}
    # Digest of the aim bones only, whatever `names` is, so callers don't disturb each other.
    used = sorted(v for k, v in idx.items() if k in AIM_BONES)
    state = liveness(addr, zlib.crc32(b"".join(buf[i * 0x20:i * 0x20 + 0x20] for i in used)), now)
    world = to_world(g, entity, pts)
    aim, check = check_bones(world, {k: v for k, v in idx.items() if k in AIM_BONES}, m[3])
    if not aim:
        return None, check
    if state == "frozen":
        return None, "frozen"
    return {"state": state, "world": world, "idx": idx, "points": aim, "check": check}, check


def aim_bones(g, entity):
    """World positions {'head', 'neck', 'chest', 'pelvis'} from the live animated skeleton, or {}.
    'head' is the centre of the skull."""
    sel, _ = select_pose(g, entity)
    return sel["points"] if sel else {}

```

`tools/skeleton_lab.py`:

```py
"""Skeleton lab: checks the aim points against what the player sees (offline, read-only).

    python tools/skeleton_lab.py            # every human: faction, bone count, aim source, checks
    python tools/skeleton_lab.py --aim      # put the crosshair on a character's head by hand:
                                            # prints how far each computed point is from it, in cm

Allies (`Cuban_Friendly`) and civilians (`Cuban_Neutral`) use the same NPC classes and skeleton
as enemies (`Cuban_Hostile`), so they are safe test subjects: stand next to them in a camp, aim at
their head, crouch, walk around them. Nothing is written into the game.
"""

import math
import sys
import time

import skeleton as sk
from mem import Game
from targets import aim_points, bodies, camera, faction, targets


def faction_tag(g, entity):
    return {1: "NEUTR", 2: "HOSTL", 4: "ALLY "}.get(faction(g, entity), "?    ")


def off_axis(cam, p):
    d = [p[k] - cam[3][k] for k in range(3)]
    n = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in d)) or 1.0
    return math.degrees(math.acos(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(d[k] * cam[1][k] for k in range(3)) / n))))


def crosshair_offset(cam, p):
    """(right, up) in metres from the crosshair ray to p, measured at p's distance."""
    eye, right, up = cam[3], cam[0], cam[2]
    d = [p[k] - eye[k] for k in range(3)]
    return sum(d[k] * right[k] for k in range(3)), sum(d[k] * up[k] for k in range(3))


def candidates(g, entity, pos):
    """{source: head point} from every source aim_points() can use, plus the chosen one."""
    out = {}
    sel, _ = sk.select_pose(g, entity)
    if sel:
        out["skeleton"] = sel["points"]["head"]
        for n in ("Head", "HeadEnd"):
            if n in sel["idx"]:
                out["bone:" + n] = sel["world"][sel["idx"][n]]
    head = bodies(g, entity).get("Ragdoll_Head")
    if head:
        out["ragdoll"] = head
    label, p = aim_points(g, entity, pos)[0]
    out["CHOSEN " + label] = p
    return out


def report(g):
    cam = camera(g)
    _, pts = targets(g)
    rows = []
    for _i, _p, entity, pos, name in pts:
        if "NPC" not in name:
            continue
        sel, why = sk.select_pose(g, entity)
        label = aim_points(g, entity, pos)[0][0]
        rows.append((math.dist(cam[3], pos), off_axis(cam, pos), len(sel["world"]) if sel else 0, label, why, faction_tag(g, entity),
                     name.split("Base.NPC.")[-1][:48]))
    for dist, ang, n, label, why, tag, short in sorted(rows):
        print(f"  {tag} {dist:6.1f} m {ang:5.0f} deg  bones {n:3}  {label:16} {why:52} {short}")


def aim_lab(g, rate=4.0):
    """Follows the character closest to the crosshair and prints the offsets of each source."""
    while True:
        cam = camera(g)
        _, pts = targets(g)
        best = None
        for _i, _p, entity, pos, name in pts:
            if "NPC" not in name:
                continue
            p = aim_points(g, entity, pos)[0][1]
            a = off_axis(cam, p)
            if best is None or a < best[0]:
                best = (a, entity, pos, name)
        if best:
            _a, entity, pos, name = best
            cands = candidates(g, entity, pos)
            dist = math.dist(cam[3], pos)
            parts = []
            for src, p in cands.items():
                r, u = crosshair_offset(cam, p)
                parts.append(f"{src} {100 * r:+5.0f},{100 * u:+5.0f}")
            print(f"{faction_tag(g, entity)} {dist:5.1f} m  (cm right,up of the crosshair)  " + "  ".join(parts), flush=True)
        time.sleep(1.0 / rate)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    game = Game()
    if "--aim" in sys.argv:
        try:
            aim_lab(game)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            pass
    else:
        report(game)

```

`tools/spread_check.py`:

```py
"""Checks the weapon spread model against the player's real shots (offline only, read-only).

    python tools/spread_check.py [--s 60]

Model (docs/reversing.md "Spread: the game's own model"): the bullet leaves at an angle of
    (CurrentBulletSpread / 2) x max(u1, u2) ^ p   degrees from the fire direction
with CurrentBulletSpread = [weapon + 0xb10] (degrees, live), p = [fire props + 0x2c4] and u1, u2
uniform in [0, 1): for p = 2 the ratio angle / (spread / 2) is uniform in [0, 1] (mean 0.5) and
never above 1.

The bullet monitor of fc6internal.dll (v11+) gives each bullet's first segment; this side samples
the camera and the spread state. Fire single shots, still, at hip and aiming, standing, crouched,
walking. Each shot prints its angle to the camera forward, the spread just before it and the ratio.
2026-10-06, FAL at hip (39 shots): ratio 0.00-0.97, mean 0.47, quartiles 0.25 / 0.49 / 0.67.
"""

import argparse
import collections
import math
import time

import mouse
from ballistics import BulletMonitor, qpc
from mem import Game
from targets import camera, held_weapon_object, player_entity


def angle(a, b):
    na = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in a)) or 1.0
    nb = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in b)) or 1.0
    return math.acos(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(a[k] * b[k] for k in range(3)) / (na * nb))))


def spread_state(g):
    p = player_entity(g)
    w = held_weapon_object(g, p) if p else None
    t = g.u64(w + 0x168) if w else None
    props = g.u64(t + 0x10) if t else None
    if not props:
        return None
    return {"s": g.f32(w + 0xB10), "max": g.f32(w + 0xB14), "min": g.f32(w + 0xB18), "ads": g.f32(w + 0xBE8),
            "p": g.f32(props + 0x2C4), "angle": g.read(props + 0x2B1, 1) != b"\0"}


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--s", type=float, default=60.0, help="seconds")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    monitor = BulletMonitor()
    eyes = collections.deque(maxlen=400)
    states = collections.deque(maxlen=400)
    ratios = []
    print("fire single shots in game (hip, aiming, crouched, walking)...", flush=True)
    end = time.time() + args.s
    last_state = 0.0
    try:
        while time.time() < end:
            if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
                time.sleep(0.1)
                continue
            cam = camera(g)
            if cam:
                eyes.append((qpc(), tuple(cam[3][:3]), tuple(cam[1][:3])))
            if time.time() - last_state > 0.004:
                st = spread_state(g)
                if st:
                    states.append((qpc(), st))
                last_state = time.time()
            for est in monitor.poll(eyes):
                chain = est["chain"]
                t0 = chain[0]["qpc"]
                # Camera samples over the 25 ms before the shot: after it, the recoil kicks it.
                near = [e for e in eyes if 0 <= t0 - e[0] <= 0.025 * monitor.freq]
                if len(near) < 2:
                    continue
                turn = math.degrees(angle(near[0][2], near[-1][2]))
                cam = near[-1]
                d = [chain[0]["to"][k] - chain[0]["from"][k] for k in range(3)]
                dev = math.degrees(angle(d, cam[2]))
                before = [s for s in states if s[0] < t0 - 0.004 * monitor.freq]
                after = [s for s in states if s[0] > t0 + 0.004 * monitor.freq]
                if not before:
                    continue
                st = before[-1][1]
                half = st["s"] / 2
                ratio = dev / half if half > 0 else float("nan")
                still = turn < 0.05
                if half > 0:
                    ratios.append((ratio, still))
                nxt = after[0][1]["s"] if after else float("nan")
                print(f"shot: {dev:6.3f} deg off the camera, spread {st['s']:.3f} deg (next {nxt:.3f},"
                      f" min {st['min']:.2f} max {st['max']:.2f}), ads {st['ads']:.2f}, p {st['p']:.1f}"
                      f"{' angle-spread' if st['angle'] else ''} -> ratio {ratio:.2f}"
                      f"{'' if still else f'  (camera turned {turn:.2f} deg)'}", flush=True)
            time.sleep(0.002)
    finally:
        monitor.close()
    for label, pick in (("all", lambda s: True), ("still", lambda s: s)):
        r = sorted(x for x, s in ratios if pick(s))
        if r:
            n = len(r)
            print(f"{n} {label} shots with spread: ratio max {r[-1]:.2f}, mean {sum(r) / n:.2f}, quartiles"
                  f" {r[n // 4]:.2f} {r[n // 2]:.2f} {r[3 * n // 4]:.2f} (model, p = 2: uniform in [0, 1])")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/targets.py`:

```py
"""Live list of the game's own aim-assist targets (characters), read-only.

    python tools/targets.py            print once
    python tools/targets.py --watch    refresh every second

Layout (docs/reversing.md, build 11359732):
    aimAssist = [FC_m64d3d12.dll + 0x6e3d2d0]       vtable +0x5bb5788
    aimAssist + 0x30 : SAimingPoint** array
    aimAssist + 0x3c : u32 count
    point + 0x00 : vtable +0x5bb2638
    point + 0x08 : entity handle {u64 id, entity*}
    point + 0x30 : R44 world transform of the aiming point
    entity + 0x00 : vtable +0x5c80b80 for humans (animals differ)
    entity + 0x18 : archetype name {u32 len, u32, u32, char[len]} e.g.
                    "NPC:FCX_NPC:Base.NPC.Assaulter.Cuban_Hostile"
    entity + 0x20 : back to the handle
    entity + 0x30 : R44 world transform of the entity
    entity + 0xa8 : component pointer array (null entries allowed)

Ragdoll bodies (hit zones), see docs/reversing.md "Ragdoll bodies":
    phys  = component with vtable +0x5933868 in entity + 0xa8
    X     = [[phys + 0x70] + 0x08]                 ragdoll instance (vtable +0x58a3bd0)
    C     = [[X + 0x88] + 0x128]
    C + 0xd8 : body* array      C + 0xe0 : u32 count (19 for humans)
    body + 0xb8  : name pointer ("Ragdoll_Head" minus the leading 'R')
    body + 0x170 : 3 rotation columns (16 bytes each), body + 0x1a0 : world position
    body + 0x13c : u32 index in the physics world, 0xffffffff when the ragdoll is inactive
                   (inactive bodies keep stale or model-space positions: don't use them)
"""

import math
import struct
import sys
import time

from mem import Game

AIM_ASSIST_STATIC = 0x6E3D2D0
AIM_ASSIST_VT = 0x5BB5788
POINT_VT = 0x5BB2638

CAMERA_CHAINS = [(0x6A66EA0, 0x2F8, 0x260), (0x6A678E8, 0x348, 0x260), (0x6B1B1B0, 0x38, 0x350)]


def camera(g):
    for static, o1, o2 in CAMERA_CHAINS:
        addr = g.ptr(g.base + static, o1, o2)
        m = g.mat(addr) if addr else None
        if m and abs(m[2][2]) <= 1.0001 and abs(sum(x * x for x in m[1]) - 1) < 1e-2:
            return m
    return None


def camera_with_projection(g):
    """(camera matrix, x scale, y scale): the projection follows the camera matrix, x scale
    1 / tan(hfov / 2) at +0x40 and y scale at +0x54. They grow when aiming down sights."""
    for static, o1, o2 in CAMERA_CHAINS:
        addr = g.ptr(g.base + static, o1, o2)
        m = g.mat(addr) if addr else None
        if m and abs(m[2][2]) <= 1.0001 and abs(sum(x * x for x in m[1]) - 1) < 1e-2:
            sx, sy = g.f32(addr + 0x40), g.f32(addr + 0x54)
            if sx and sy and 0.1 < sx < 50 and 0.1 < sy < 50:
                return m, sx, sy
    return None


def targets(g):
    mgr = g.u64(g.base + AIM_ASSIST_STATIC)
    if not mgr or g.vt(mgr) != AIM_ASSIST_VT:
        return None, []
    arr, count = g.u64(mgr + 0x30), g.u32(mgr + 0x3C)
    out = []
    for i in range(min(count or 0, 256)):
        p = g.u64(arr + 8 * i)
```
