```py
        if not p or g.vt(p) != POINT_VT:
            continue
        m = g.mat(p + 0x30)
        handle = g.u64(p + 8)
        entity = g.u64(handle + 8) if handle else None
        out.append((i, p, entity, m[3], archetype(g, entity)))
    return mgr, out


def archetype(g, entity):
    s = g.u64(entity + 0x18) if entity else None
    n = g.u32(s) if s else 0
    raw = g.read(s + 0xC, min(n, 200)) if n else None
    return raw.decode(errors="replace") if raw else "?"


PHYS_COMPONENT_VT = 0x5933868


_components = {}  # (entity, vtable) -> (archetype pointer, component)


def component(g, entity, vt):
    """The entity's component with this vtable, or None. Cached per entity: the cache entry is
    trusted while the component keeps its vtable and the entity its archetype pointer."""
    if not entity:
        return None
    key, arch = (entity, vt), g.u64(entity + 0x18)
    hit = _components.get(key)
    if hit and hit[0] == arch and g.vt(hit[1]) == vt:
        return hit[1]
    arr = g.u64(entity + 0xA8)
    raw = g.read(arr, 96 * 8) if arr else None
    for k in range(96 if raw else 0):
        c = struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, k * 8)[0]
        if c and g.vt(c) == vt:
            if len(_components) > 4096:
                _components.clear()
            _components[key] = (arch, c)
            return c
    return None


# Attitude (docs/reversing.md "Faction and attitude"): u32 at +0x2c of the component with this
# vtable (a level/rank component). 4 = Libertad (allies, amigos, the player's vehicles),
# 2 = hostile to the player right now, 1 = neutral: civilians, hostages, and FND soldiers who are
# not hostile at the moment (they switch between 1 and 2). The player entity has none.
FACTION_COMPONENT_VT = 0x5BF9C58
ATTITUDE_HOSTILE, ATTITUDE_ALLY = 2, 4


def faction(g, entity):
    c = component(g, entity, FACTION_COMPONENT_VT)
    return g.u32(c + 0x2C) if c else None


# Death (docs/reversing.md "Health and death"): u32 at +0x50 of the component with vtable
# +0x59336e8 is 0 while alive (wounded included) and goes 1 -> 2 -> 3 as the character dies
# (+0x98 and the ragdoll component's +0x9c change at the same moment).
DEATH_COMPONENT_VT = 0x59336E8
# Damage component (strings near its vtable: SetInvincible, OnDeathStimData, SMaxHealthMultiplier).
# Its +0x48 points to the health object (vtable +0x5a49c68): current health at +0x18, max at +0x1c
# (100/100 on the NPCs seen; each leg shot took 18.2; negative after death).
# +0x298 of the component itself drops from 50 to 0 at the first wound: not the health.
DAMAGE_COMPONENT_VT = 0x5A74D68
HEALTH_OBJECT_VT = 0x5A49C68


def health(g, entity):
    """(current, max) health, or None."""
    c = component(g, entity, DAMAGE_COMPONENT_VT)
    h = g.u64(c + 0x48) if c else None
    if not h or g.vt(h) != HEALTH_OBJECT_VT:
        return None
    return g.f32(h + 0x18, 2)


def death_state(g, entity):
    c = component(g, entity, DEATH_COMPONENT_VT)
    return g.u32(c + 0x50) if c else None


def is_dead(g, entity):
    if death_state(g, entity):
        return True
    h = health(g, entity)
    return h is not None and h[0] <= 0.0


# The player's aiming component (vtable near the CAimingAssist* names) points to the held weapon
# object at +0x248 (also +0x358, +0x548); [weapon + 8] is a handle to the weapon entity, whose
# archetype names it ("weapons:Weapons:Rifles.FAL", "weapons:Weapons:Rifles.CUBAN-SRA").
AIM_COMPONENT_VT = 0x5BB55E8


def player_entity(g):
    """The player's own entity: the aim-assist list holds it too."""
    _, pts = targets(g)
    for _i, _p, entity, _pos, name in pts:
        if "player:" in name:
            return entity
    return None


def held_weapon(g, player):
    """Archetype name of the weapon the player holds, or None."""
    w = held_weapon_object(g, player)
    h = g.u64(w + 0x8) if w else None
    e = g.u64(h + 0x8) if h else None
    name = archetype(g, e) if e else None
    return name if name and name.startswith("weapons:") else None


def held_weapon_object(g, player):
    c = component(g, player, AIM_COMPONENT_VT)
    return g.u64(c + 0x248) if c else None


# The weapon object's live stats: fire rate (rounds per minute) at +0x170, magazine at +0x188 (the
# menu's "Cadence de tir 700 bpm" and "Chargeur 20" for the FAL), attachment modifiers at +0xec4
# (range, ?, speed, gravity, drop). Its fire properties, a reflected struct (fields registered by
# +0x29adc40), are at [[weapon + 0x168] + 0x10] (vtable +0x5c59958): bUseBulletPhysic +0x324,
# fBulletSpeed +0x328, fBulletGravity +0x32c (stored negative), fBulletDropDistance +0x330,
# spread fAngleYawBulletSpread +0x2b4 / fAnglePitchBulletSpread +0x2b8, SupportedAmmoList +0x6f0.
FIRE_PROPS_VT = 0x5C59958


def loaded_ammo(g, w, props):
    """(index, name, speed factor, gravity factor) of the ammo loaded in weapon object w: its id at
    [[w + 0x198] + 0xf98] looked up in SupportedAmmoList (props + 0x6f0, entries of 0xd8, id at
    +0x10), else iDefaultAmmoIndex (+0x700). Factors at entry + 0x3c (speed) and + 0x38 (gravity),
    as the game reads them when firing (+0x27d71e0, +0x2955120, +0x2910f20)."""
    lst, n = g.u64(props + 0x6F0), ((g.u64(props + 0x6F8) or 0) >> 32) & 0x7FFFFFFF
    if not lst or not 0 < n <= 64:
        return None
    fire = g.u64(w + 0x198)
    ammo_id = g.u64(fire + 0xF98) if fire else None
    ids = [g.u64(lst + k * 0xD8 + 0x10) for k in range(n)]
    k = ids.index(ammo_id) if ammo_id in ids else (g.u32(props + 0x700) or 0)
    k = k if k < n else 0
    e = lst + k * 0xD8
    gravity, speed = g.f32(e + 0x38, 2)
    desc = g.u64(e + 0x18)
    raw = g.read(g.u64(desc + 0x10), 96) if desc and g.u64(desc + 0x10) else b""
    i = raw.find(b"Ammo/") if raw else -1
    name = raw[i + 5:].split(bytes(1))[0].decode(errors="replace") if i >= 0 else f"#{k}"
    return k, name, speed, gravity


def weapon_ballistics(g, player):
    """The held weapon's ballistics as the game computes them when firing (+0x284ae60):
    {'speed', 'gravity', 'drop_distance', 'range', 'ammo', 'base_speed', 'base_gravity', 'speed_mul',
    'gravity_mul', 'ammo_speed', 'ammo_gravity', 'physics', 'rpm'}, or None. speed = fBulletSpeed x
    attachments [weapon + 0xecc] x ammo; gravity = -fBulletGravity x [weapon + 0xed0] x ammo; drop =
    fBulletDropDistance x [weapon + 0xed4]; range [weapon + 0xec4]."""
    w = held_weapon_object(g, player)
    t = g.u64(w + 0x168) if w else None
    p = g.u64(t + 0x10) if t else None
    if not p or g.vt(p) != FIRE_PROPS_VT:
        return None
    raw = g.read(p + 0x324, 0x10)
    rpm = g.f32(w + 0x170)
    if not raw:
        return None
    speed, gravity, drop = struct.unpack_from("<3f", raw, 4)
    if not 1.0 < speed < 100000.0:
        return None
    rng, _unknown, speed_mul, gravity_mul, drop_mul = g.f32(w + 0xEC4, 5) or (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
    ammo = loaded_ammo(g, w, p) or (0, "?", 1.0, 1.0)
    return {"speed": speed * speed_mul * ammo[2], "gravity": abs(gravity) * gravity_mul * ammo[3],
            "drop_distance": drop * drop_mul, "range": rng, "ammo": ammo[1], "base_speed": speed,
            "base_gravity": abs(gravity), "speed_mul": speed_mul, "gravity_mul": gravity_mul,
            "ammo_speed": ammo[2], "ammo_gravity": ammo[3], "physics": raw[0] == 1, "rpm": rpm}


def bodies(g, entity):
    """{name: world position} of the character's ragdoll bodies, or {} if inactive/missing."""
    phys = component(g, entity, PHYS_COMPONENT_VT)
    y = g.u64(phys + 0x70) if phys else None
    x = g.u64(y + 0x08) if y else None
    a = g.u64(x + 0x88) if x else None
    c = g.u64(a + 0x128) if a else None
    if not c:
        return {}
    arr, count = g.u64(c + 0xD8), g.u32(c + 0xE0)
    out = {}
    for k in range(min(count or 0, 64)):
        b = g.u64(arr + 8 * k)
        s = g.u64(b + 0xB8) if b else None
        raw = g.read(s, 32) if s else None
        if not raw or g.u32(b + 0x13C) == 0xFFFFFFFF:
            continue
        name = "R" + raw.split(bytes(1))[0].decode(errors="replace")
        out[name] = g.f32(b + 0x1A0, 3)
    return out


def head_position(g, entity, fallback_pos):
    """World position of the head: ragdoll body when active, else 1.6 m above the entity."""
    m = g.mat(entity + 0x30)
    parts = bodies(g, entity)
    head, pelvis = parts.get("Ragdoll_Head"), parts.get("Ragdoll_Pelvis")
    # An "active" ragdoll can still be parked somewhere else: trust it only on top of the entity.
    if head and pelvis and m and math.hypot(pelvis[0] - m[3][0], pelvis[1] - m[3][1]) < 1.0:
        return head, "ragdoll"
    base, up = (m[3], m[2]) if m else (fallback_pos, (0, 0, 1))
    return tuple(base[i] + 1.6 * up[i] for i in range(3)), "estimate"


AIM_PARTS = ("Ragdoll_Head", "Ragdoll_Neck", "Ragdoll_Spine1", "Ragdoll_Pelvis")
ESTIMATED_HEIGHTS = (("head", 1.6), ("chest", 1.25), ("pelvis", 0.9))


def aim_points(g, entity, fallback_pos):
    """Ordered aim points [(label, xyz)], best first. Sources, in order of preference:
    the animated skeleton (skeleton.py), the active ragdoll bodies, fixed heights above the
    entity. The label of the first point says which source was used."""
    from skeleton import aim_bones  # local import: no circular dependency
    bones = aim_bones(g, entity)
    if bones:
        return [("head", bones["head"]), ("neck", bones["neck"]), ("chest", bones["chest"]),
                ("pelvis", bones["pelvis"])]
    m = g.mat(entity + 0x30)
    parts = bodies(g, entity)
    pelvis = parts.get("Ragdoll_Pelvis")
    if pelvis and m and math.hypot(pelvis[0] - m[3][0], pelvis[1] - m[3][1]) < 1.0:
        pts = [(name.split("_", 1)[1].lower() + "~ragdoll", parts[name]) for name in AIM_PARTS if name in parts]
        if pts:
            return pts
    base, up = (m[3], m[2]) if m else (fallback_pos, (0, 0, 1))
    return [(label + "~estimate", tuple(base[i] + h * up[i] for i in range(3))) for label, h in ESTIMATED_HEIGHTS]


def is_hostile(g, entity, name):
    """Enemy human: hostile to the player right now (attitude 2), or an FND soldier by archetype
    name who is not hostile yet (for stealth). Never an ally. Vehicles and animals carry an
    attitude too but have no human skeleton, so only "NPC:" archetypes count.
    In some missions every NPC around reads attitude 5 (guerrillas and soldiers, alive and dead,
    "Rencontre avec les Montero"), so the archetype name decides there: FND elites
    (Base.NPC.Elite.*, e.g. Elite.AR_RPG) have no faction suffix and count as enemies."""
    if "NPC:" not in name or "_Friendly" in name or "_Neutral" in name:
        return False
    a = faction(g, entity)
    if a == ATTITUDE_ALLY:
        return False
    return a == ATTITUDE_HOSTILE or "_Hostile" in name or ".NPC.Elite." in name


def show(g):
    cam = camera(g)
    mgr, pts = targets(g)
    if mgr is None:
        print("aim-assist manager not found (menu or loading screen?)")
        return
    print(f"aimAssist {mgr:x}  targets {len(pts)}  camera {tuple(round(v, 1) for v in cam[3]) if cam else '?'}")
    for i, p, entity, pos, name in pts:
        d = math.dist(pos, cam[3]) if cam else float("nan")
        tag = "ENEMY" if is_hostile(g, entity, name) else ("YOU  " if "PawnPlayer" in name else "     ")
        head, src = head_position(g, entity, pos)
        hs = f"head ({head[0]:9.2f} {head[1]:9.2f} {head[2]:7.2f}) {src}"
        print(f"  [{i:2}] {tag} {d:6.1f} m  pos ({pos[0]:9.2f} {pos[1]:9.2f} {pos[2]:7.2f})  {hs}  {name}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    g = Game()
    if "--watch" in sys.argv:
        while True:
            show(g)
            time.sleep(1)
    else:
        show(g)

```

`tools/threat_hunt.py`:

```py
"""Which NPCs are after the player? Looks for references to the player in each NPC's components
(offline only, read-only).

    python tools/threat_hunt.py [--watch 30] [--depth 1]

For every living NPC of the aim-assist list, reads each component (entity + 0xa8, up to 96) and
reports the fields that hold the player: the entity itself, one of its components, or its entity
handle (the ref-counted object whose +8 is the entity, as in the aim-assist list). With --depth 1
the pointees of each component (0x400 bytes each) are searched too.

Prints, per (component vtable, offset[, inner offset]), how many NPCs point at the player there,
with their attitude: a field set on alerted soldiers and clear on calm ones is a target / threat.
--watch N repeats every second for N seconds and prints when a field appears or disappears.
--log FILE also writes every scan as a JSON line: per NPC its attitude, distance, the angle between
its facing and the direction to the player, and its fields; plus the player's health.
"""

import argparse
import collections
import json
import math
import struct
import time

from mem import Game
from targets import camera, faction, health, is_dead, player_entity, targets

COMPONENT_BYTES = 0x1000
INNER_BYTES = 0x400


def qwords(raw):
    return struct.unpack_from(f"<{len(raw) // 8}Q", raw)


def player_refs(g, player):
    """Addresses that stand for the player: entity, components, entity handle(s)."""
    refs = {player: "entity"}
    arr = g.u64(player + 0xA8)
    raw = g.read(arr, 96 * 8) if arr else None
    for c in qwords(raw) if raw else ():
        vt = g.vt(c) if c else None
        if vt:  # the array may be shorter than 96: skip what isn't a component
            refs.setdefault(c, f"component {vt:#x}")
    _, pts = targets(g)
    for _i, p, e, _pos, _name in pts:
        if e == player:
            refs.setdefault(g.u64(p + 8), "handle")
    return refs


def scan(g, depth):
    player = player_entity(g)
    if not player:
        return None, {}
    refs = player_refs(g, player)
    cam = camera(g)
    found = {}  # npc entity -> set of keys
    info = {}
    _, pts = targets(g)
    for _i, _p, e, pos, name in pts:
        if not e or "NPC" not in name or e == player or is_dead(g, e):
            continue
        keys = set()
        arr = g.u64(e + 0xA8)
        raw = g.read(arr, 96 * 8) if arr else None
        for c in qwords(raw) if raw else ():
            if not c:
                continue
            vt = g.vt(c)
            if not vt:
                continue
            body = g.read(c, COMPONENT_BYTES)
            if not body:
                continue
            words = qwords(body)
            for k, v in enumerate(words):
                if v in refs:
                    keys.add((vt, k * 8, None, refs[v]))
            if depth:
                for k, v in enumerate(words):
                    if v < 0x10000 or v > 0x7FFFFFFFFFFF or v in refs or v == c or v == e:
                        continue
                    inner = g.read(v, INNER_BYTES)
                    if not inner:
                        continue
                    for j, w in enumerate(qwords(inner)):
                        if w in refs:
                            keys.add((vt, k * 8, j * 8, refs[w]))
        found[e] = keys
        dist = math.dist(cam[3][:3], pos[:3]) if cam else 0.0
        info[e] = (name.split("Base.NPC.")[-1][:40], faction(g, e), dist, facing(g, e, player))
    return info, found


def facing(g, e, player):
    """Degrees between the NPC's forward and the direction to the player, on the ground plane."""
    m, p = g.mat(e + 0x30), g.mat(player + 0x30)
    if not m or not p:
        return None
    fwd, d = m[1], [p[3][k] - m[3][k] for k in range(2)]
    nf, nd = math.hypot(fwd[0], fwd[1]), math.hypot(d[0], d[1])
    if nf < 1e-6 or nd < 1e-6:
        return None
    c = (fwd[0] * d[0] + fwd[1] * d[1]) / (nf * nd)
    return math.degrees(math.acos(max(-1.0, min(1.0, c))))


def key_text(k):
    vt, off, inner, what = k
    return f"vt {vt:#x} +{off:#x}" + (f" -> +{inner:#x}" if inner is not None else "") + f" ({what})"


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--watch", type=float, default=0.0)
    ap.add_argument("--depth", type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument("--log")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    log = open(args.log, "w", encoding="utf-8") if args.log else None
    g = Game()
    info, found = scan(g, args.depth)
    if info is None:
        raise SystemExit("no player")
    per_key = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for e, keys in found.items():
        for k in keys:
            per_key[k].append(e)
    print(f"{len(found)} living NPCs")
    for e, (name, att, dist, _face) in sorted(info.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1][2]):
        print(f"  {dist:6.1f} m  attitude {att}  {name}  ({len(found[e])} fields)")
    for k, es in sorted(per_key.items(), key=lambda kv: -len(kv[1])):
        atts = collections.Counter(info[e][1] for e in es)
        print(f"{len(es):3d} NPCs  {key_text(k)}  attitudes {dict(atts)}")
    end = time.time() + args.watch
    prev = found
    while time.time() < end:
        time.sleep(0.3)
        info, found = scan(g, args.depth)
        if info is None:
            continue
        stamp = time.strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        if log:
            p = player_entity(g)
            hp = health(g, p) if p else None
            log.write(json.dumps({"t": time.time(), "player_hp": hp, "npcs": [
                {"e": hex(e), "name": info[e][0], "att": info[e][1], "dist": round(info[e][2], 1),
                 "facing": None if info[e][3] is None else round(info[e][3]), "keys": sorted(key_text(k) for k in keys)}
                for e, keys in found.items()]}) + "\n")
            log.flush()
        for e, keys in found.items():
            old = prev.get(e, set())
            name, att, dist, _face = info[e]
            for k in keys - old:
                print(f"{stamp} + {name} ({dist:.0f} m, attitude {att}): {key_text(k)}", flush=True)
            for k in old - keys:
                print(f"{stamp} - {name} ({dist:.0f} m, attitude {att}): {key_text(k)}", flush=True)
        prev = found


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/view_hunt.py`:

```py
"""Finds where the player's view angles live, and which of them turn the camera when written
(exploration; --test writes game memory, offline only).

    python tools/view_hunt.py [--test] [--vectors] [--extra 0xADDR ...]

Waits for the game to have focus, then takes five snapshots: at rest, after turning the camera
right (200 mouse counts), right again (100 counts), down (120 counts), then up (60 counts). Objects: the player
entity, its components, the pawn data P = [pawn component (+0x5d53d88) + 0x2ae8], the camera
object [[+0x6a66ea0] + 0x2f8], the --extra addresses, and every object those point to (one level).
A yaw field changes in proportion to the camera's yaw on both turns (same factor, any unit:
radians, degrees, mouse counts, either sign) and not on the pitch moves; a pitch field the other way.
Floats and doubles. With --test each candidate is written (+0.05 rad worth of its unit) and the
camera is read 0.3 s later: a field that turns the camera is an input; the value is put back.
--vectors looks instead for 3 floats equal to the camera's forward vector (or its horizontal part,
normalised) in every snapshot: a look direction stored as a vector isn't proportional to the angle.
With --test each is turned 0.05 rad about the up axis.
"""

import argparse
import math
import struct
import time

import mouse
from mem import Game
from targets import camera, player_entity

PAWN_VT = 0x5D53D88
SIZE = 0x1000


def angles(g):
    f = camera(g)[1]
    return math.atan2(-f[0], f[1]), math.asin(max(-1.0, min(1.0, f[2])))


def wrap(a):
    return (a + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi


def objects(g, player, extra):
    roots = {"player": player}
    arr = g.u64(player + 0xA8)
    for k in range(96):
        c = g.u64(arr + 8 * k)
        if c and g.vt(c):
            roots[f"comp+{g.vt(c):x}"] = c
    s = roots.get(f"comp+{PAWN_VT:x}")
    if s and g.u64(s + 0x2AE8):
        roots["P"] = g.u64(s + 0x2AE8)
    cam = g.u64(g.u64(g.base + 0x6A66EA0) + 0x2F8)
    if cam:
        roots["camobj"] = cam
    for a in extra:
        roots[f"extra{a:#x}"] = a
    out = dict(roots)
    seen = set(out.values())
    for name, a in roots.items():
        raw = g.read(a, SIZE) or g.read(a, 0x400) or b""
        for o in range(0, len(raw) - 7, 8):
            p = struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, o)[0]
            if 0x10000000000 <= p < 0x7FF000000000 and not g.in_engine(p) and p not in seen:
                seen.add(p)
                out[f"{name}[{o:#x}]"] = p
    return out


def snapshot(g, objs):
    return {name: g.read(a, SIZE) or g.read(a, 0x400) or b"" for name, a in objs.items()}


def turn(dx, dy, steps=10):
    for _ in range(steps):
        mouse.move(dx, dy)
        time.sleep(0.01)
    time.sleep(0.4)


def proportional(d, cam):
    """Factor k with d = k * cam, or None (cam must be a real move)."""
    return d / cam if abs(cam) > 0.02 else None


def scan(snaps, views, objs):
    """Candidates, one per address (an object reached through several parents is scanned once)."""
    dy1, dy2 = wrap(views[1][0] - views[0][0]), wrap(views[2][0] - views[1][0])
    dp3, dp4 = views[3][1] - views[2][1], views[4][1] - views[3][1]
    found = []
    done = set()
    for name in objs:
        if objs[name] in done:
            continue
        done.add(objs[name])
        raws = [s[name] for s in snaps]
        n = min(len(r) for r in raws)
        for kind, size, fmt in (("f32", 4, "<f"), ("f64", 8, "<d")):
            for o in range(0, n - size + 1, size):
                v = [struct.unpack_from(fmt, r, o)[0] for r in raws]
                if not all(math.isfinite(x) and abs(x) < 1e7 for x in v) or len(set(v)) == 1:
                    continue
                d = [v[i + 1] - v[i] for i in range(4)]
                k1, k2 = proportional(d[0], dy1), proportional(d[1], dy2)
                quiet = max(abs(d[2]), abs(d[3]))
                if k1 and k2 and abs(k1) > 1e-3 and abs(k1 - k2) < 0.03 * abs(k1) and quiet < 0.1 * abs(d[0]):
                    found.append((name, o, kind, "yaw", (k1 + k2) / 2, v[-1]))
                k3, k4 = proportional(d[2], dp3), proportional(d[3], dp4)
                quiet = max(abs(d[0]), abs(d[1]))
                if k3 and k4 and abs(k3) > 1e-3 and abs(k3 - k4) < 0.03 * abs(k3) and quiet < 0.1 * abs(d[2]):
                    found.append((name, o, kind, "pitch", (k3 + k4) / 2, v[-1]))
    return found


def plausible(k):
    """A unit the game could use: radians, degrees or mouse counts per radian (either sign)."""
    k = abs(k)
    return 0.3 <= k <= 3.0 or 30.0 <= k <= 120.0 or 300.0 <= k <= 3000.0


def still(g, seconds=0.3):
    """True if the camera doesn't move by itself (the player isn't touching the mouse)."""
    y0, p0 = angles(g)
    time.sleep(seconds)
    y1, p1 = angles(g)
    return abs(wrap(y1 - y0)) < 0.003 and abs(p1 - p0) < 0.003


def scan_vectors(snaps, fwds, objs):
    """(name, offset, kind) of 3 floats equal to the camera forward ("fwd") or to its normalised
    horizontal part ("flat", z ignored) in every snapshot, one per address."""
    found, done = [], set()
    for name in objs:
        if objs[name] in done:
            continue
        done.add(objs[name])
        raws = [s[name] for s in snaps]
        n = min(len(r) for r in raws)
        if n < 12:
            continue
        cols = [struct.unpack_from(f"<{n // 4}f", r) for r in raws]
        for k in range(n // 4 - 2):
            for kind in ("fwd", "flat"):
                ok = True
                for c, f in zip(cols, fwds):
                    if kind == "fwd":
                        want = f
                    else:
                        h = math.hypot(f[0], f[1]) or 1.0
                        want = (f[0] / h, f[1] / h, c[k + 2])
                    if any(abs(c[k + i] - want[i]) > 3e-3 for i in range(3)):
                        ok = False
                        break
                if ok:
                    found.append((name, 4 * k, kind))
                    break
    return found


def write_test_vectors(g, objs, found):
    """Turns each vector 0.05 rad about the up axis; reports the camera's yaw response."""
    c, s = math.cos(0.05), math.sin(0.05)
    for name, o, kind in found:
        if not still(g):
            print("  camera moving by itself (mouse touched?): test stopped", flush=True)
            return
        addr = objs[name] + o
        v = g.f32(addr, 3)
        y0, _p0 = angles(g)
        g.write_f32(addr, v[0] * c - v[1] * s, v[0] * s + v[1] * c, v[2])
        time.sleep(0.3)
        y1, _p1 = angles(g)
        moved = wrap(y1 - y0)
        now = g.f32(addr, 3)
        verdict = "TURNS THE CAMERA" if abs(moved) > 0.02 else "output (no effect)"
        print(f"  {name:34} +{o:#06x} {kind}: camera yaw {math.degrees(moved):+.2f} deg (expected {math.degrees(0.05):+.2f}),"
              f" field now {tuple(round(x, 4) for x in now)}: {verdict}", flush=True)
        if abs(moved) > 0.02:
            w = g.f32(addr, 3)
            g.write_f32(addr, w[0] * c + w[1] * s, -w[0] * s + w[1] * c, w[2])
            time.sleep(0.3)


def write_test(g, objs, found):
    """Writes each candidate +0.05 rad worth; reports the camera's response; puts it back."""
    for name, o, kind, axis, k, _last in found:
        if not plausible(k):
            continue
        if not still(g):
            print("  camera moving by itself (mouse touched?): test stopped", flush=True)
            return
        addr = objs[name] + o
        fmt = "<f" if kind == "f32" else "<d"
        raw = g.read(addr, struct.calcsize(fmt))
        if not raw:
            continue
        old = struct.unpack(fmt, raw)[0]
        y0, p0 = angles(g)
        g.write(addr, struct.pack(fmt, old + 0.05 * k))
        time.sleep(0.3)
        y1, p1 = angles(g)
        moved = wrap(y1 - y0) if axis == "yaw" else p1 - p0
        now = struct.unpack(fmt, g.read(addr, struct.calcsize(fmt)))[0]
        verdict = "TURNS THE CAMERA" if abs(moved) > 0.02 else "output (no effect)"
        print(f"  {name:34} +{o:#06x} {kind} {axis}: camera {math.degrees(moved):+.2f} deg"
              f" (expected {math.degrees(0.05):+.2f}), field {old:.5f} -> {now:.5f}: {verdict}", flush=True)
        if abs(moved) > 0.02:
            cur = struct.unpack(fmt, g.read(addr, struct.calcsize(fmt)))[0]
            g.write(addr, struct.pack(fmt, cur - 0.05 * k))
            time.sleep(0.3)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--test", action="store_true", help="write each candidate and check the camera")
    ap.add_argument("--extra", nargs="*", type=lambda s: int(s, 16), default=[])
    ap.add_argument("--vectors", action="store_true", help="look for the camera's forward vector instead")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    print("waiting for the game to have focus (don't touch the mouse for 5 s once it has)...", flush=True)
    while mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
        time.sleep(0.2)
    time.sleep(1.0)
    objs = objects(g, player_entity(g), args.extra)
    print(f"{len(objs)} objects", flush=True)
    snaps, views, fwds = [], [], []
    for dx, dy in ((0, 0), (20, 0), (10, 0), (0, 12), (0, -6)):
        if dx or dy:
            turn(dx, dy)
        views.append(angles(g))
        fwds.append(tuple(camera(g)[1][:3]))
        snaps.append(snapshot(g, objs))
        print(f"  yaw {math.degrees(views[-1][0]):8.3f}  pitch {math.degrees(views[-1][1]):7.3f}", flush=True)
    turn(-30, -6)
    time.sleep(1.5)  # the game smooths the view: let it settle before the write test
    if args.vectors:
        found = scan_vectors(snaps, fwds, objs)
        print(f"\n{len(found)} vectors followed the camera forward:")
        for name, o, kind in found:
            print(f"  {name:34} +{o:#06x} {kind:4} = {tuple(round(x, 4) for x in g.f32(objs[name] + o, 3))}"
                  f"   (address {objs[name] + o:#x})")
        if args.test and found:
            print("\nwrite test:", flush=True)
            write_test_vectors(g, objs, found)
        return
    found = scan(snaps, views, objs)
    print(f"\n{len(found)} fields followed the view (factor = field change / camera change in radians):")
    for name, o, kind, axis, k, v in sorted(found):
        print(f"  {name:34} +{o:#06x} {kind} {axis:5} x{k:+10.4f}  last {v:.5f}   (address {objs[name] + o:#x})")
    if args.test and found:
        print("\nwrite test:", flush=True)
        write_test(g, objs, found)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/view_lag.py`:

```py
"""How late does the camera follow the view angle accumulator? (writes game memory, offline only)

    python tools/view_lag.py [--trials 12] [--step 1.0]

The DLL's direct aim adds its correction to the accumulator (pawn component + 0x2f20) and reads
the error back from the camera. If the camera shows the accumulator only some frames later, a
correction made every tick is counted again before the camera shows it, and the aim overshoots.

With the game focused and the mouse still: first 2 s of watching (how often each copy of the
camera changes, the frame time), then trials that turn the accumulator's yaw by +-step degrees
and time when each copy shows it:
  matrix  camera object q + 0x260 (what the DLL reads, written on a render thread)
  source  q + 0x3d0 (what that matrix is copied from)
  angles  q + 0x6a0 / + 0x6a8 (view angles copied each frame)
Each trial undoes the previous one: the view ends where it started.
"""

import argparse
import math
import statistics
import struct
import time

import mouse
from mem import Game
from targets import CAMERA_CHAINS, component, player_entity

PAWN_VT = 0x5D53D88
VIEW_ANGLES = 0x2F20
FRAME_GLOBAL = 0x6A5EAA8  # [global] + 0x88: frame time (double), 0 while paused
Q_FROM, Q_SIZE = 0x260, 0x6B0 - 0x260


def wrap(a):
    return (a + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi


def fwd_yaw(raw, off):
    fx, fy = struct.unpack_from("<2f", raw, off - Q_FROM + 0x10)
    return math.atan2(-fx, fy)


class Sources:
    def __init__(self, g):
        self.g = g
        static, o1, _o2 = CAMERA_CHAINS[0]
        self.q = g.ptr(g.base + static, o1, 0)
        self.acc = component(g, player_entity(g), PAWN_VT) + VIEW_ANGLES
        self.frame = g.u64(g.base + FRAME_GLOBAL)
        if not self.q or not self.acc:
            raise SystemExit("camera object or player not found (in game, not in a menu?)")

    def read(self):
        raw = self.g.read(self.q + Q_FROM, Q_SIZE)
        if not raw:
            return None
        return {
            "matrix": fwd_yaw(raw, 0x260),
            "source": fwd_yaw(raw, 0x3D0),
            "angles": struct.unpack_from("<f", raw, 0x6A8 - Q_FROM)[0],
            "raw_matrix": raw[0:0x40],
        }

    def frame_time(self):
        b = self.g.read(self.frame + 0x88, 8) if self.frame else None
        return struct.unpack("<d", b)[0] if b else None

    def yaw(self):
        return self.g.f32(self.acc + 0x18)


def watch(src, seconds):
    end = time.perf_counter() + seconds
    last, changes, dts = {}, {k: 0 for k in ("matrix", "source", "angles")}, []
    last_dt = None
    while time.perf_counter() < end:
        s = src.read()
        if not s:
            continue
        for k in changes:
            v = s["raw_matrix"] if k == "matrix" else s[k]
            if last.get(k) is not None and v != last[k]:
                changes[k] += 1
            last[k] = v
        dt = src.frame_time()
        if dt is not None and dt != last_dt:
            dts.append(dt)
            last_dt = dt
    print(f"watched {seconds:.0f} s, the mouse still:")
    for k, n in changes.items():
        print(f"  {k:7s} changed {n / seconds:6.1f} times/s")
    good = [d for d in dts if 0 < d < 1]
    if good:
        print(f"  frame time ({len(dts)} values): median {statistics.median(good) * 1000:.1f} ms"
              f" = {1 / statistics.median(good):.0f} fps, max {max(good) * 1000:.1f} ms")
    else:
        print(f"  frame time: no plausible value at [+0x{FRAME_GLOBAL:x}] + 0x88 ({dts[:5]})")


def trial(src, step):
    base = src.read()
    before = {k: base[k] for k in ("matrix", "source", "angles")}
    frames, last_raw = 0, base["raw_matrix"]
    seen = {}
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    src.g.write_f32(src.acc + 0x18, src.yaw() + step)
    while time.perf_counter() - t0 < 0.5 and len(seen) < 3:
        s = src.read()
        if not s:
            continue
        if s["raw_matrix"] != last_raw:
            frames += 1
            last_raw = s["raw_matrix"]
        t = time.perf_counter() - t0
        for k in before:
            if k not in seen and wrap(s[k] - before[k]) * math.copysign(1, step) > 0.5 * abs(step):
                seen[k] = (t * 1000, frames)
    return seen


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=12)
    ap.add_argument("--step", type=float, default=1.0, help="degrees")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    g = Game()
    src = Sources(g)
    print("switch to the game and leave the mouse still (about 6 s)...", flush=True)
    while mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
        time.sleep(0.2)
    # Paused (menu, map): the camera doesn't change at all. Wait for it to move once.
    first = src.read()["raw_matrix"]
    while src.read()["raw_matrix"] == first:
        time.sleep(0.01)
    print("the game runs: measuring, leave the mouse still", flush=True)
    time.sleep(1.5)
    watch(src, 2.0)
    step = math.radians(a.step)
    results = {k: [] for k in ("matrix", "source", "angles")}
    for i in range(a.trials):
        seen = trial(src, step if i % 2 == 0 else -step)
        for k, v in seen.items():
            results[k].append(v)
        time.sleep(0.15)
    print(f"{a.trials} turns of {a.step:.1f} deg written into the accumulator; when each copy showed it:")
    for k, v in results.items():
        if not v:
            print(f"  {k:7s} never (within 0.5 s)")
            continue
        ms = [x[0] for x in v]
        fr = [x[1] for x in v]
        print(f"  {k:7s} {len(v)}/{a.trials}: after {statistics.median(ms):5.1f} ms median ({min(ms):.1f}..{max(ms):.1f}),"
              f" {statistics.median(fr):.0f} matrix updates median ({min(fr)}..{max(fr)})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/view_write.py`:

```py
"""Does writing a view field turn the camera? (exploration; writes game memory, offline only)

    python tools/view_write.py

For the candidates of tools/view_hunt.py: the pitch at pawn component (+0x5d53d88) + 0x2d4c and the
orientation quaternion at pawn data P = [S + 0x2ae8] + 0x330. With the game focused, adds 0.1 rad
to each in turn (the pitch directly, a 0.1 rad turn about the world up axis for the quaternion),
then reads the camera 0.3 s later and the field itself: a field that is only a per-frame output
is overwritten and the camera doesn't move. Undoes what moved.
"""

import math
import struct
import time

import mouse
from mem import Game
from targets import camera, component, player_entity

PAWN_VT = 0x5D53D88
DELTA = 0.1


def angles(g):
    f = camera(g)[1]
    return math.atan2(-f[0], f[1]), math.asin(max(-1.0, min(1.0, f[2])))


def qmul(a, b):
    ax, ay, az, aw = a
    bx, by, bz, bw = b
    return (aw * bx + ax * bw + ay * bz - az * by, aw * by - ax * bz + ay * bw + az * bx,
            aw * bz + ax * by - ay * bx + az * bw, aw * bw - ax * bx - ay * by - az * bz)


def report(g, name, before, addr, fmt):
    time.sleep(0.3)
    yaw, pitch = angles(g)
    now = struct.unpack(fmt, g.read(addr, struct.calcsize(fmt)))
    print(f"{name}: camera yaw {math.degrees(yaw - before[0]):+.2f} deg, pitch {math.degrees(pitch - before[1]):+.2f} deg"
          f" after the write; field now {tuple(round(v, 4) for v in now)}", flush=True)
    return yaw - before[0], pitch - before[1]


def main():
    g = Game()
    s = component(g, player_entity(g), PAWN_VT)
    p = g.u64(s + 0x2AE8)
    print("waiting for the game to have focus (don't touch the mouse for 2 s once it has)...", flush=True)
    while mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
        time.sleep(0.2)
    time.sleep(1.0)

    before = angles(g)
    old = g.f32(s + 0x2D4C)
    print(f"pitch field {old:.4f} (camera pitch {before[1]:.4f})")
    g.write_f32(s + 0x2D4C, old + DELTA)
    _dy, dp = report(g, "pitch +0.1 rad", before, s + 0x2D4C, "<f")
    if abs(dp) > 0.05:
        g.write_f32(s + 0x2D4C, g.f32(s + 0x2D4C) - DELTA)

    before = angles(g)
    q = struct.unpack("<4f", g.read(p + 0x330, 16))
    turn = (0.0, 0.0, math.sin(DELTA / 2), math.cos(DELTA / 2))
    print(f"quaternion {tuple(round(v, 4) for v in q)}")
    g.write_f32(p + 0x330, *qmul(turn, q))
    dy, _dp = report(g, "quaternion yaw +0.1 rad", before, p + 0x330, "<4f")
    if abs(dy) > 0.05:
        back = (0.0, 0.0, -math.sin(DELTA / 2), math.cos(DELTA / 2))
        g.write_f32(p + 0x330, *qmul(back, struct.unpack("<4f", g.read(p + 0x330, 16))))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/visibility.py`:

```py
"""Shows which enemies are exposed from the camera, part by part (needs fc6internal.dll).

    python tools/visibility.py            # once
    python tools/visibility.py --watch    # refresh every second
"""

import math
import sys
import time

from aim import BULLET_FILTER, RAY_START, visible_point
from mem import Game
from rays import Rays
from targets import aim_points, camera, is_dead, is_hostile, targets


def show(g, r):
    cam = camera(g)
    eye = cam[3]
    _, pts = targets(g)
    rows = []
    for _i, _p, entity, pos, name in pts:
        if not is_hostile(g, entity, name) or is_dead(g, entity):
            continue
        points = aim_points(g, entity, pos)
        m = g.mat(entity + 0x30)
        parts = []
        for label, p in points:
            seen = visible_point(r, eye, cam[1], [(label, p)], m[3] if m else None, m[2] if m else (0, 0, 1))
            parts.append(f"{label}:{'VU' if seen else 'caché'}")
        rows.append((math.dist(eye, points[0][1]), name.split(".")[-2:], parts))
    for dist, short, parts in sorted(rows):
        exposed = any(p.endswith("VU") for p in parts)
        print(f"  {'VISIBLE ' if exposed else 'dormant '} {dist:6.1f} m  {'.'.join(short):32} {' '.join(parts)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    g, r = Game(), Rays()
    r.set_filter(BULLET_FILTER)
    while True:
        show(g, r)
        if "--watch" not in sys.argv:
            break
        print()
        time.sleep(1)

```

`tools/watch.py`:

```py
"""Who writes this value? Hardware write watch through fc6internal.dll (offline only, read-only for
the game: a debug register is set, no code is patched).

    python tools/watch.py --weapon 0x328 --reads --ms 15000   # who reads the held weapon's fBulletSpeed

    python tools/watch.py --addr 0x1dd12345670 [--len 4] [--ms 2000]
    python tools/watch.py --npc crosshair --bone Head        # the NPC nearest the crosshair
    python tools/watch.py --npc nearest --buf partial --bone Head
    python tools/watch.py --exec 0xa5b733 0xbefca0 --show 0x328 --show 0x32c --ms 15000

--buf picks the pose buffer of the character's own skeleton S = [[entity + 0x28] + 0xe0]
(docs/reversing.md "Skeleton ownership"); the watched float is the x of the bone's position:
    model   : [S + 0x78], count S + 0xec (the model-space pose skeleton.py reads)
    partial : [S + 0x4f8], count S + 0x4b8 (second output, bone order unknown)

Prints each writing instruction (module offset of the instruction *after* the write), its count,
the registers at the first write (annotated when they point at the entity, the component or the
buffer) and the caller chain. Disassemble with tools/disasm.py <dump> <offset> --back 0x40.

--exec (DLL v8) puts execute breakpoints on up to 3 engine offsets instead: each distinct
register set seen there is printed, and --show OFF prints the f32 at register + OFF for every
register that points to memory (to see which object the code reads a field from).

--weapon OFF watches the held weapon's fire properties [[weapon + 0x168] + 0x10] + OFF (DLL v14 with
--reads: reads are caught too, the instruction listed is the one after the read). Starts on F8.
"""

import argparse
import math
import mmap
import struct
import time
import winsound
import zlib

import mouse
import skeleton as sk
from mem import Game
from ballistics import SIZE as EXEC_OFF
from rays import MAGIC, MSG_OFF, NAME
from targets import camera, held_weapon_object, player_entity, targets

WATCH_OFF = MSG_OFF + 128
WATCH_HDR = struct.Struct("<QIIII")  # addr, len, ms, distinct, total
HIT = struct.Struct("<QII16Q8Q")
HITS_OFF = WATCH_OFF + WATCH_HDR.size
MAX_HITS = 32
SIZE = HITS_OFF + MAX_HITS * HIT.size
REGS = ("rax", "rcx", "rdx", "rbx", "rsp", "rbp", "rsi", "rdi", "r8", "r9", "r10", "r11", "r12", "r13", "r14", "r15")


def table_index(g, s, name):
    n = g.u32(s + 0xEC) if s else 0
    raw = g.read(g.u64(s + 0xE0), n * 8) if s and 0 < n < 4096 else None
    want = zlib.crc32(name.encode()) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    for k in range(n if raw else 0):
        h, i = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, k * 8)
        if h == want:
            return i
    return None


def pick_npc(g, how):
    cam = camera(g)
    _, pts = targets(g)
    best = None
    for _i, _p, e, pos, name in pts:
        if "NPC" not in name:
            continue
        if how == "nearest":
            key = math.dist(cam[3], pos)
        else:
            d = [pos[k] + 1.2 * cam[2][k] - cam[3][k] for k in range(3)]
            n = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in d)) or 1.0
            key = math.acos(max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(d[k] * cam[1][k] for k in range(3)) / n)))
        if best is None or key < best[0]:
            best = (key, e, name, math.dist(cam[3], pos))
    return best[1:] if best else (None, None, None)


def bone_address(g, entity, buf, bone):
    """(address of the bone's position x, description, {address: label} for annotations)."""
    b = sk.body_component(g, entity)
    s = sk.skeleton_instance(g, entity)
    labels = {entity: "entity", b: "body", s: "skeleton"}
    if buf == "model":  # the character's own model-space pose (skeleton.py)
        base, count = (g.u64(s + 0x78), g.u32(s + 0xEC)) if s else (None, 0)
    else:  # "partial": the second output, kept for research
        base, count = (g.u64(s + 0x4F8), g.u32(s + 0x4B8)) if s else (None, 0)
    idx = table_index(g, s, bone)
    if not base or idx is None or idx >= (count or 0):
        return None, f"{buf}: no live '{bone}' (buffer {base and hex(base)}, count {count}, index {idx})", labels
    labels[base] = f"{buf} pose"
    return base + idx * 0x20 + 0x10, f"{buf} pose {base:#x}, {bone} = bone {idx} of {count}", labels


def all_labels(g):
    """{address: label} for every NPC's entity, components, skeleton instances and pose buffers."""
    cam = camera(g)
    _, pts = targets(g)
    labels = {}
    for _i, _p, e, pos, name in pts:
        if "NPC" not in name:
            continue
        tag = f"{name.split('.')[-1][:14]}@{math.dist(cam[3], pos):.0f}m"
        labels[e] = f"entity {tag}"
        labels[g.u64(e + 0x28)] = f"c28 {tag}"
        b = sk.body_component(g, e)
        if b:
            labels[b] = f"body {tag}"
            labels[g.u64(b + 0x1D8)] = f"body pose {tag}"
            own = g.u64(b + 0xA0)
            labels[own] = f"own skel {tag}"
            labels[g.u64(own + 0x4F8) if own else 0] = f"own skel pose {tag}"
    labels.pop(0, None)
    labels.pop(None, None)
    return labels


def annotate(g, v, labels):
    if v in labels and labels[v]:
        return labels[v]
    if g.in_engine(v):
        return f"+{v - g.base:x}"
    for base, name in labels.items():
        if base and name and 0 < v - base < 0x800:
            return f"{name}+{v - base:#x}"
    return ""


def print_hits(g, m, labels, show=None):
    """Prints the DLL's watch hits. show (execute mode): offsets to read as f32 from each register."""
    _a, _l, _ms, distinct, total = WATCH_HDR.unpack_from(m, WATCH_OFF)
    for i in range(distinct):
        rip, count, tid, *rest = HIT.unpack_from(m, HITS_OFF + i * HIT.size)
        regs, frames = rest[:16], rest[16:]
        where = f"+{rip - g.base:x}" if g.in_engine(rip) else f"{rip:#x}"
        print(f"\n  {'at' if show is not None else 'after'} {where}  x{count}  thread {tid}")
        for r, v in zip(REGS, regs):
            if not v:
                continue
            extra = ""
            if show and not g.in_engine(v) and 0x10000 <= v < 0x7FF000000000:
                vals = [g.f32(v + off) for off in show]
                if all(x is not None for x in vals):
                    extra = "  " + " ".join(f"[+{off:#x}]={x:.6g}" for off, x in zip(show, vals))
            print(f"      {r:3} = {v:#x}  {annotate(g, v, labels)}{extra}")
        chain = [f"+{f - g.base:x}" if g.in_engine(f) else f"{f:#x}" for f in frames if f]
        print("      callers: " + " <- ".join(chain))
    return distinct, total


def run_exec(g, m, args):
    offs = (args.exec + [0, 0, 0])[:3]
    if args.now:
        print("starting as soon as the game has focus...", flush=True)
        while mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
            time.sleep(0.02)
    else:
        print("in game, press F8 to start (one beep; two beeps at the end)...", flush=True)
        while not (mouse.foreground_pid() == g.pid and mouse.key_down("f8")):
            time.sleep(0.02)
    # Engine offsets, or absolute addresses (system DLLs) when beyond the engine's size.
    struct.pack_into("<3Q", m, EXEC_OFF, *[(o if o >= g.size else g.base + o) if o else 0 for o in offs])
    WATCH_HDR.pack_into(m, WATCH_OFF, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)
    struct.pack_into("<I", m, WATCH_OFF + 12, args.ms)  # watchMs last: the DLL starts on it
    winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    print(f"execute breakpoints on {', '.join(f'{o:#x}' if o >= g.size else f'+{o:x}' for o in args.exec[:3])}"
          f" for {args.ms} ms", flush=True)
    while struct.unpack_from("<I", m, WATCH_OFF + 12)[0]:
        time.sleep(0.05)
    for _ in range(2):
        winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    distinct, total = print_hits(g, m, all_labels(g), args.show)
    print(f"\n{total} executions, {distinct} distinct register sets")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--addr", type=lambda s: int(s, 16))
    ap.add_argument("--npc", choices=("crosshair", "nearest"))
    ap.add_argument("--buf", choices=("model", "partial"), default="model")
    ap.add_argument("--bone", default="Head")
    ap.add_argument("--len", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--ms", type=int, default=2000)
    ap.add_argument("--exec", nargs="+", type=lambda s: int(s, 16), help="engine offsets (up to 3) to break on")
    ap.add_argument("--show", action="append", type=lambda s: int(s, 16), default=[],
                    help="with --exec: print the f32 at register + this offset")
    ap.add_argument("--weapon", type=lambda s: int(s, 16), help="offset in the held weapon's fire properties")
    ap.add_argument("--reads", action="store_true", help="catch reads too (DLL v14)")
    ap.add_argument("--now", action="store_true", help="with --exec: start when the game has focus, no F8")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    g = Game()
    m = mmap.mmap(-1, MSG_OFF, tagname=NAME)
    magic, version = struct.unpack_from("<II", m, 0)
    need = 14 if args.reads else 8 if args.exec else 6
    if magic != MAGIC or version < need:
        raise SystemExit(f"fc6internal.dll (version {need}+) is not loaded: run build/Release/fc6inject.exe")
    m = mmap.mmap(-1, EXEC_OFF + 24 if args.exec else SIZE, tagname=NAME)
    if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
        print("waiting for the game to have focus (it pauses without it)...", flush=True)
        while mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid:
            time.sleep(0.2)
        time.sleep(1.0)
    labels = {}
    addr = args.addr
    if args.exec:
        run_exec(g, m, args)
        return
    if args.npc:
        entity, name, dist = pick_npc(g, args.npc)
        if not entity:
            raise SystemExit("no NPC in the aim-assist list")
        addr, desc, labels = bone_address(g, entity, args.buf, args.bone)
        print(f"NPC {name.split('Base.NPC.')[-1]} at {dist:.1f} m, entity {entity:#x}: {desc}")
        if not addr:
            return
    if args.weapon is not None:
        p = player_entity(g)
        w = held_weapon_object(g, p) if p else None
        t = g.u64(w + 0x168) if w else None
        props = g.u64(t + 0x10) if t else None
        if not props:
            raise SystemExit("no held weapon")
        addr = props + args.weapon
        labels = {w: "weapon", t: "weapon+0x168", props: "fire props", p: "player"}
        print(f"weapon {w:#x}, fire props {props:#x}: watching +{args.weapon:#x} = {g.f32(addr)}")
    if not addr:
        raise SystemExit("give --addr, --npc or --weapon")
    labels = {**all_labels(g), **labels}
    access_off = None
    if args.reads:
        from port_check import VIEW_CHAR, VIEW_CHARS_OFF, VIEW_MAX
        access_off = VIEW_CHARS_OFF + VIEW_MAX * VIEW_CHAR.size + 16 + 400 + 5 * 208  # after the HUD block
        m = mmap.mmap(-1, access_off + 8, tagname=NAME)
        struct.pack_into("<I", m, access_off, 1)
    if args.weapon is not None:
        print("in game, press F8 to start (one beep; two beeps at the end)...", flush=True)
        while not (mouse.foreground_pid() == g.pid and mouse.key_down("f8")):
            time.sleep(0.02)
        winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    before = g.read(addr, 4)
    WATCH_HDR.pack_into(m, WATCH_OFF, addr, args.len, 0, 0, 0)
    struct.pack_into("<I", m, WATCH_OFF + 12, args.ms)  # watchMs last: the DLL starts on it
    print(f"watching {addr:#x} ({args.len} bytes{', reads too' if args.reads else ''}) for {args.ms} ms..."
          " keep the game focused", flush=True)
    while struct.unpack_from("<I", m, WATCH_OFF + 12)[0]:
        time.sleep(0.05)
    if access_off is not None:
        struct.pack_into("<I", m, access_off, 0)
    if args.weapon is not None:
        for _ in range(2):
            winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    _a, _l, _ms, distinct, total = WATCH_HDR.unpack_from(m, WATCH_OFF)
    changed = g.read(addr, 4) != before
    print(f"{total} {'accesses' if args.reads else 'writes'} from {distinct} instructions"
          f" (value {'changed' if changed else 'unchanged'})")
    print_hits(g, m, labels)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/weapon_hunt.py`:

```py
"""Finds where the player's held weapon is referenced (exploration helper, read-only).

    python tools/weapon_hunt.py

In game: hold weapon A and press F8 (one beep), switch to weapon B and press F8 (two beeps),
back to A and F8, B and F8. Prints the fields of the player's entity and components (and one
level of heap pointers out of them) that alternate A, B, A, B: equal for the same weapon,
different between the two. The held weapon's pointer or id is among them.
"""

import ctypes
import struct
import time
import winsound

import mouse
from mem import Game
from targets import targets

user32 = ctypes.WinDLL("user32")
VK_F8 = 0x77
SIZE = 0x1000
SNAPSHOTS = 4


def player(g):
    _, pts = targets(g)
    for _i, _p, e, _pos, name in pts:
        if "player:" in name:
            return e
    return None


def objects(g, entity):
    """{label: address} for the entity, its components and the heap objects they point to."""
    out = {"entity": entity}
    arr = g.u64(entity + 0xA8)
    raw = g.read(arr, 96 * 8) if arr else None
    for k in range(96 if raw else 0):
        c = struct.unpack_from("<Q", raw, k * 8)[0]
        vt = g.vt(c) if c else None
        if vt is None:
            continue
        label = f"comp+{vt:x}"
        out.setdefault(label, c)
    for label, addr in list(out.items()):
        body = g.read(addr, 0x400) or b""
        for off in range(0, len(body), 8):
            p = struct.unpack_from("<Q", body, off)[0]
            if 0x10000000000 <= p < 0x7FF000000000 and not g.in_engine(p) and g.vt(p):
                out.setdefault(f"{label}->[{off:#x}]", p)
    return out


def key_pressed(vk, state):
    down = bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(vk) & 0x8000)
    hit = down and not state.get(vk)
    state[vk] = down
    return hit


def analyse(g, objs, snaps):
    found = 0
    for label, addr in objs.items():
        bufs = [s.get(label) for s in snaps]
        if any(b is None for b in bufs):
            continue
        n = min(len(b) for b in bufs)
        for off in range(0, n - 7, 8):
            v = [struct.unpack_from("<Q", b, off)[0] for b in bufs]
            if v[0] == v[2] and v[1] == v[3] and v[0] != v[1]:
                notes = []
                for x in (v[0], v[1]):
                    vt = g.vt(x) if 0x10000000000 <= x < 0x7FF000000000 else None
                    notes.append(f"obj vt +{vt:x}" if vt else ("engine" if g.in_engine(x) else ""))
                print(f"  {label:40} +{off:#06x} A={v[0]:#018x} B={v[1]:#018x}  {notes}")
                found += 1
    print(f"{found} alternating qwords")


def main():
    g = Game()
    e = player(g)
    if not e:
        raise SystemExit("player entity not found in the aim-assist list")
    objs = objects(g, e)
    print(f"player entity {e:#x}, {len(objs)} objects watched")
    print("hold weapon A in game and press F8...", flush=True)
    state, snaps = {}, []
    while len(snaps) < SNAPSHOTS:
        time.sleep(0.02)
        if mouse.foreground_pid() != g.pid or not key_pressed(VK_F8, state):
            continue
        snaps.append({label: g.read(addr, SIZE) for label, addr in objs.items()})
        print(f"snapshot {len(snaps)} ({'A' if len(snaps) % 2 else 'B'})", flush=True)
        for _ in range(1 if len(snaps) % 2 else 2):
            winsound.Beep(1200, 120)
    analyse(g, objs, snaps)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/xrefs.py`:

```py
"""Find strings and RIP-relative references in the engine dump (local analysis only).

    python tools/xrefs.py <dump.bin> str "GetBonePosition"     offsets of the string
    python tools/xrefs.py <dump.bin> lea 0x5e6a278 [0x...]       code doing lea/mov reg, [rip+X]
    python tools/xrefs.py <dump.bin> ptr 0x5e6a278               8-byte absolute pointers to X
                                                                 (needs --base, the module base)

Offsets are module offsets (FC_m64d3d12.dll + offset), as everywhere in the repo. The dump is
the unpacked module written by `fc6probe dump`, so file offset == module offset.
"""

import re
import struct
import sys

# REX.W lea / mov with a RIP-relative operand: 48|4C 8D|8B modrm(mod=00, rm=101)
RIP_OP = re.compile(rb"[\x48\x4c][\x8d\x8b][\x05\x0d\x15\x1d\x25\x2d\x35\x3d]", re.S)


def find_str(dump, s):
    return [m.start() for m in re.finditer(re.escape(s.encode()) + b"\x00", dump)]


def rip_refs(dump, targets):
    targets = set(targets)
    out = []
    for m in RIP_OP.finditer(dump):
        i = m.start()
        disp = struct.unpack_from("<i", dump, i + 3)[0]
        t = i + 7 + disp
        if t in targets:
            out.append((i, t, "lea" if dump[i + 1] == 0x8D else "mov"))
    return out


def main():
    dump = open(sys.argv[1], "rb").read()
    cmd, args = sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3:]
    if cmd == "str":
        for s in args:
            print(s, [hex(o) for o in find_str(dump, s)])
    elif cmd == "lea":
        for i, t, kind in rip_refs(dump, [int(a, 16) for a in args]):
            print(f"{i:#x}  {kind} -> {t:#x}")
    elif cmd == "ptr":
        base = int(next(a for a in args if a.startswith("--base=")).split("=")[1], 16)
        for a in args:
            if a.startswith("--"):
                continue
            needle = struct.pack("<Q", base + int(a, 16))
            print(a, [hex(m.start()) for m in re.finditer(re.escape(needle), dump)])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
